### [1] [Multimodal Road Closure Mapping via Cross-Modal Graph Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13192)
*Izzeddin Teeti,Vaibhav Kango,Nidhi Sridhar,Mohamed Moustafa*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出统一的多模态多任务框架，联合完成道路封闭分类（无/部分/完全）与受影响路段归属，并发布首个联合基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模导航与物流系统需准确及时识别道路封闭，但需同时推理视觉证据、空间上下文与路网拓扑；现有方法或依赖人工地图更新，或只检测封闭相关目标而无法确定受影响路段。

Method: 单一架构内联合执行封闭分类与路段关联：采用模态专属编码器处理图结构地图数据、叠加街景检测投影的卫星影像及街景图像，并通过跨模态Transformer注意力进行显式推理；引入边感知池化与图消息传递等地理空间归纳偏置，消融验证其作用。

Result: 在约2万来自大型商用车辆队的真实场景上，封闭分类准确率72.7%、路段关联准确率60%；封闭分类较前沿视觉语言模型高28个百分点以上，且模型规模小4000倍以上、推理快100倍。

Conclusion: 建立首个联合道路封闭分类与路段级归因基准，表明任务特定的架构归纳偏置显著优于通用多模态模型的结构化地理空间理解能力。

Abstract: Accurate and timely identification of road closures is critical for large-scale navigation and logistics systems, yet remains challenging due to the need to jointly reason over visual evidence, spatial context, and road network topology. Existing approaches either rely on manual map updates or focus on detecting closure-related objects without resolving which road segment is affected. In this work, we introduce a unified multimodal and multitask framework that jointly performs road closure classification (no, partial, complete) and road segment association within a single learning architecture. Our model integrates graph-structured map data, satellite imagery augmented with projected street-level detections, and street-view images using modality-specific encoders and cross-modal transformer attention, enabling explicit reasoning over visual cues and network topology. Evaluated on nearly 20K real-world scenarios collected from a large-scale commercial vehicle fleet, our approach achieves 72.7% closure classification accuracy and 60% road segment association accuracy, outperforming frontier vision-language models by over 28 percentage points on closure classification while being over 4,000x smaller and 100x faster at inference. Extensive ablations demonstrate the importance of cross-modal attention, edge-aware pooling, and graph message passing for accurate geospatial reasoning. This work establishes the first benchmark for joint road closure classification and segment-level attribution, and shows that task-specific architectural inductive biases substantially outperform general-purpose multimodal models for structured geospatial understanding.

</details>


### [2] [GTAGym: Real AAA Game Tasks Environment for Evaluation and Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13028)
*Jiajun Chen,Shicheng Fang,Jiahao Lu,Yuxin Wang,Xipeng Qiu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GTAGym：基于GTA V的任务评测与强化学习环境框架，含103个在线任务与47个图像射击任务的基准GTAVBench，最佳配置达70.9%，并可程序化生成任务用于LoRA RL，使Qwen3-27B从15%提升至23%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AAA游戏中任务结果难以自动验证，通常依赖人工标注，导致难以用于大规模评测，更难以直接支撑强化学习训练。作者希望构建一个可自动判定成败、可并行评测且可生成训练环境的GTA V平台。

Method: 构建GTAGym环境与框架：GTAVBench含从主线任务重构的9类共103个在线任务，以及47个基于游戏影像的图像射击/狙击任务；通过Web服务器提供并行评测接口，以截图+键鼠动作交互，并用引擎状态自动判定结果。另提供可扩展生成器，在固定模板下按用户指定参数范围与选项采样任务，批量生成3D与图像射击环境，附带自动奖励与终止信号，用于RL训练。

Result: 在GTAVBench（文本指令）与GTAVBench-AGI分支（指令仅出现在首帧截图、交互历史自动压缩）上评测6个前沿LLM，最佳配置总体准确率70.9%；空中导航、规避与拦截仍是难点。在生成任务上对Qwen3-27B做LoRA RL，域内导航性能提升并迁移到域外任务类别，原生GTA V任务成功率由15%升至23%。

Conclusion: AAA游戏可作为智能体感知、推理与控制的规模化测试平台；GTAGym同时提供可自动验证的基准与可程序化生成训练环境的能力，验证了“生成任务RL训练→迁移至真实游戏任务”的可行路径，并揭示空中导航等尚待攻克的难点。

Abstract: AAA games offer a rich testbed for evaluating agent perception, reasoning, and control. However, evaluating whether agents can complete tasks in AAA games is challenging and often human-annotated because task outcomes are often difficult to verify automatically, let alone applying it for Reinforcement Learning (RL). We introduce GTAGym, an environment and framework for evaluation and RL on GTA V tasks. Its evaluation benchmark, GTAVBench, derived from GTA V main-story missions, contains 103 online tasks reconstructed across nine task families, alongside 47 image-based shooting and sniping tasks using GTA V imagery. A web server provides access to the online environment and evaluation interface, supporting parallel testing through screenshots and keyboard-mouse actions with automatic engine-state outcome checks. We evaluate six frontier LLMs on GTAVBench with textual task instructions and on its GTAVBench-AGI branch, where instructions appear only in the first screenshot and a harness automatically compresses interaction history. The best configuration achieves 70.9% overall accuracy in GTAVBench-AGI, yet aerial navigation, evasion and interception remain difficult. GTAGym also provides a scalable generator that samples task parameters within user-specified ranges and option sets under fixed templates, creating batches of 3D and image-based shooting environments for RL with automatic reward and termination signals. LoRA RL of Qwen3.8-27B on generated on-foot navigation tasks improves the in-domain performance and transfers to out-of-domain task categories, and native GTA V tasks from 15% to 23%.

</details>


### [3] [Reasoning-Aware Relational Representation Learning for Open-Vocabulary Scene Graph Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13496)
*Jiawei Xiao,Boya Wang,Longtian Qiu,Shan Ning,Xuming He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RaRe框架，将MLLM生成的CoT理由转成结构化关系嵌入并直接优化，用于开放词汇场景图生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OV-SGG方法依赖数据集特定的物体-谓词共现模式，在长尾分布和噪声定位下导致有偏、纠缠的关系表示，限制罕见关系识别与未见谓词泛化。

Method: 将MLLM生成的链式推理理由编码为关系嵌入；通过自监督对比学习将理由嵌入与文本化关系描述对齐；再用面向特异性的强化学习目标精炼理由生成，促使证据具有物体对特异性；直接优化理由导出的表示空间而非仅作中间推理痕迹。

Result: 在VG150与PSG上相较基线实现更类别均衡的识别；学习带来的额外收益随数据集和proposal设置而变化。

Conclusion: 以理由驱动的表示学习可缓解共现偏置并提升类间可判别性，但增益具有数据集与设置依赖性。

Abstract: Open-Vocabulary Scene Graph Generation (OV-SGG) requires models to recognize visual relationships beyond the training vocabulary, yet existing methods often rely on dataset-specific object--predicate co-occurrence patterns. Under long-tailed distributions and noisy object localization, such reliance leads to biased, entangled relation representations, limiting both rare-relation recognition and generalization to unseen predicates. To address these challenges, we propose RaRe, a reasoning-aware relational representation learning framework that transforms MLLM-generated chain-of-thought (CoT) rationales into structured relation embeddings. Rather than using CoT only as an intermediate reasoning trace for final prediction, RaRe directly optimizes the rationale-derived representation space to improve inter-relation discriminability. Specifically, RaRe first aligns rationale embeddings with textualized relation descriptions through self-supervised contrastive learning, and then refines rationale generation with a specificity-oriented reinforcement learning objective that encourages semantically object-pair-specific relational evidence. Experiments on VG150 and PSG demonstrate improved category-balanced recognition over existing baselines, while the additional gains from learning vary across datasets and proposal settings. Code available at: https://artanic30.github.io/project_pages/RaRe.

</details>


### [4] [How Hyperbolic Are Hyperbolic Vision-Language Models?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13384)
*Freek Byrman,Partha Das,Stijn Verdenius,Pascal Mettes*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 实证发现主流双曲视觉语言模型(MERU、HyCoCLIP、ARGENT、PHyCLIP)的指数映射近似恒等变换,推理时去掉该映射评测分数不变;用相同层级损失在CC3M上从零训练的欧氏VLM与双曲版本训练统计几乎不可区分,评测分数与径向分布一致,说明当前实践中欧氏空间已足以支撑层级视觉-语言学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双曲VLM被广泛认为具备按 generality 排序的可解释嵌入空间与强零样本性能,但其"双曲性"实际程度未被检验;作者希望探究曲率究竟在何处、以何种方式真正发挥作用。

Method: 定位曲率进入网络的唯一环节——指数映射,检验其可被恒等函数近似程度(映射前后成对距离与夹角变化、推理时移除映射的评测影响);并在CC3M上从零训练采用相同层级损失(hierarchy losses)的欧氏VLM作为对照,比较训练统计、评测分数与径向分布。

Result: 指数映射前后成对距离与角度几乎不变,移除映射后评测分数不变;欧氏对照模型在约最后90%训练步中训练统计与双曲对应物几乎无法区分,评测分数相当且恢复相同径向分布。

Conclusion: 在当前实践下,欧氏空间已是层级视觉-语言学习的充分基底;双曲几何的理论优势尚未被现有方法兑现,需新范式才能真正发挥其潜力。

Abstract: Hyperbolic vision-language models (VLMs) have emerged as a rapidly growing area of research, reporting strong zero-shot performance alongside an interpretable embedding space in which radius orders representations by generality. But how hyperbolic are hyperbolic VLMs in practice? We investigate this at the exponential map, the single operation through which curvature enters an otherwise Euclidean network. We show that across MERU, HyCoCLIP, ARGENT, and PHyCLIP, the exponential map is well approximated by the identity function, i.e., pairwise distances and angles in the pre-map Euclidean space barely change under projection, and removing the map at inference leaves evaluation scores unchanged. Motivated by this observation, we train Euclidean VLMs on CC3M from scratch under the same hierarchy losses. Over roughly the final 90% of the training steps, the training statistics are nearly indistinguishable from those of their hyperbolic counterparts, and the resulting models match the evaluation scores and recover the same radial distributions. Our findings establish Euclidean space as a sufficient substrate for hierarchical vision-language learning as it is currently practiced, and motivate new paradigms in which hyperbolic geometry can deliver on its theoretical promise. Code is available at https://github.com/fbyrman/hyperbolic-vlms.

</details>


### [5] [Toward Foundation Detection Models via Example-Based Grounding in EM-DETR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14518)
*Sheethal Bhat,Bogdan Georgescu,Awais Mansoor,Florin C. Ghesu,Sasa Grbic,Andreas Maier*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种整合多源异构标注数据构建多域病灶检测基础模型的方法：采用学生-教师训练策略融合异构标签空间以缓解灾难性遗忘，并将EM-DETR扩展为多域联合学习；在CXR与乳腺摄影上取得SOTA（AUC提升约1个百分点，mAP50提升2-3个百分点）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学病灶检测的CAD系统性能高度依赖大规模、高质量标注数据，但标准化的综合标注稀缺、现有数据集异质性强，限制了鲁棒基础模型的构建。

Method: 1）构建同时含解剖结构与异常标注的多域医学影像数据集整合方案；2）提出学生-教师训练策略，有效融合标签空间异质的数据集，缓解灾难性遗忘并改善特征学习；3）在原有基于样例的EM-DETR框架基础上扩展为多域特定数据集联合学习，实现细粒度、样例驱动的跨模态检测。

Result: 达到SOTA：在CXR四项关键征象上图像级AUC较此前SOTA高约1个百分点；在CXR与乳腺摄影上取得领先的mAP50，较单数据集训练的EM-DETR高2-3个百分点。

Conclusion: 该工作为利用异构数据构建可扩展、鲁棒且泛化性强的医学影像基础模型提供了可行路径。

Abstract: DeepLearning (DL)-based Computer-Aided Diagnostic (CAD) systems have shown great promise in supporting clinicians, but their performance heavily depends on the availability of large, well-annotated datasets. In medical lesion detection, the scarcity of comprehensive, stan dardized annotations and the heterogeneity of existing datasets limit the development of robust foundation models. In this work, we introduce a novel approach to building a multi-domain foundation model for le sion detection by integrating diverse medical image datasets annotated for both anatomical structures and abnormalities. We first propose a student-teacher training strategy to effectively combine datasets with heterogeneous label spaces, mitigating catastrophic forgetting, and im proving feature learning. In addition, building on our previously pro posed example-based EM-DETR framework, we adapt it to jointly learn from multiple domain-specific datasets, enabling fine-grained, example driven detection across modalities. Our model achieves state-of-the-art (SOTA) results, with image-level AUC scores exceeding previous SOTA by 1% point on four key findings in CXRs. We achieve a leading mAP50 in CXRs and mammography, surpassing EM-DETR trained on a single dataset by 2-3% points. This approach paves the way for scalable foundation models in medical imaging that leverage heterogeneous data while maintaining robust and generalizable performance.

</details>


### [6] [Relaxing Cross-Modal Correspondence for Multimodal Remote Sensing Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13807)
*Xianping Ma,Yu Zhang,Mingzhe Jiang,Wanxin Xie,Qing Zhu,Nan Xu,Xiaokang Zhang,Yuanxin Ye*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出语义配对（SemPair）预训练策略，将跨模态对应关系从实例级松弛到语义级，证明严格实例配对并非多模态遥感预训练的必要前提。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态遥感预训练普遍依赖跨模态精确的实例级对应，隐含地将严格配对观测视为学习可迁移表示的前提。这种依赖导致数据获取成本高昂、难以规模化。作者希望检验这一假设是否必要。

Method: 提出SemPair：用类一致但跨实例的配对替代精确实例对。构建潜因子模型，将跨模态依赖分解为共享语义结构与实例特定相关，从理论上刻画对应松弛的影响；并引入MixPair作为语义配对与精确配对之间的受控插值，沿连续谱研究对应粒度。

Result: SemPair的迁移性能与精确配对（ExaPair）相当；MixPair实验揭示下游迁移性能与实例配对比例之间呈非单调关系。潜因子分析表明SemPair在期望意义上保留共享语义结构、边缘化实例特定相关。

Conclusion: 精确实例对应并非有效多模态遥感预训练的必要条件；跨模态对应粒度应作为可迁移多模态表示学习的重要设计维度，并为降低对昂贵实例对齐数据依赖、规模化多模态预训练提供可行路径。

Abstract: Multimodal remote sensing pretraining commonly relies on exact instance-level correspondence across modalities, implicitly treating strictly paired observations as a prerequisite for learning transferable representations. In this work, we revisit this assumption by relaxing cross-modal correspondence from the instance level to the semantic level. We introduce Semantic Pairing (SemPair), a pairing-relaxed pretraining strategy that replaces exact instance pairs with class-consistent cross-instance pairs. To characterize the effect of correspondence relaxation, we formulate a latent-factor model that separates cross-modal dependence into shared semantic structure and instance-specific correlation, showing that SemPair preserves the former while marginalizing instance-specific cross-modal correlations in expectation. We further introduce Mixed Pairing (MixPair) as a controlled interpolation between semantic and exact pairing to study correspondence granularity along a continuous spectrum. Extensive experiments consistently show that SemPair achieves transfer performance comparable to exact pairing (ExaPair), while MixPair reveals a non-monotonic relationship between downstream transfer and the instance-pairing ratio. These results demonstrate that exact instance correspondence is not a prerequisite for effective multimodal remote sensing pretraining and establish cross-modal correspondence granularity as an important design dimension for transferable multimodal representation learning. More broadly, this finding suggests a practical route toward scaling multimodal pretraining with reduced dependence on costly instance-aligned data.

</details>


### [7] [DisasterAgent: An Evidence-Driven Agent for Verifiable Disaster Report Generation From Remote Sensing Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13776)
*Zhijian Li,Jiawei Song,Chao Ren,Yidan Zhang,Peijin Wang,Xian Sun,Kun Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 DisasterAgent，采用 Perception-Augmentation-Generation 流程，从双时相光学影像生成带内联证据引用的可验证灾害报告，在 DisasterM3 与 RSCC 上显著优于通用 VLM、遥感专用模型与现有遥感智能体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遥感灾害评估正从简单损伤分类转向报告级综合分析，应急响应者不仅需要损伤描述，还需要可核验的证据以指导响应与恢复。现有 VLM 与工具增强智能体虽具备多模态推理与感知能力，但难以提供高风险核验所需的证据支撑。

Method: 三阶段流程：①感知阶段调用专用视觉专家工具集构建时空感知图（STPG），作为结构化记忆组织灾害引起的时空变化；②证据增强（EA）从 STPG 中抽取证据，为每条陈述附加内联引用，形成从陈述到视觉证据的闭环；③生成阶段产出可验证的灾害报告。

Result: 在 DisasterM3 与 RSCC 基准上，相较原模型，DisasterM3 灾害报告生成平均分提升 13.5%，RSCC 的 ST5-SCS 提升 15.34%，并一致优于通用域 VLM、遥感专用模型及现有遥感智能体。

Conclusion: 以结构化时空图记忆+证据闭环引用为核心的智能体范式，可有效弥合遥感灾害报告中"描述"与"可验证性"之间的差距，为灾害应急响应提供更准确、可核验的方案。

Abstract: Remote sensing disaster assessment is moving from simple damage classification toward comprehensive report-level analysis. In this setting, responders need not only damage descriptions but also verifiable evidence to guide response and recovery. Recent vision-language models (VLMs) and tool-augmented agents offer strong multimodal reasoning and perception, but they struggle to provide the evidence required for high-stakes verification. To address these limitations, we propose DisasterAgent, an evidence-driven agent that uses a Perception-Augmentation-Generation workflow to generate verifiable disaster reports from bitemporal optical imagery. DisasterAgent first invokes a specialized vision expert toolkit in the perception phase to build a Spatiotemporal Perception Graph (STPG), serving as a structured memory to store and organize disaster-induced changes across space and time. Evidence-based Augmentation (EA) then extracts evidence from the STPG, attaching inline citations that form a closed evidence loop from each statement to the supporting visual evidence. Extensive experiments on the DisasterM3 and RSCC benchmarks demonstrate that DisasterAgent consistently outperforms general-domain VLMs, remote sensing-specialized models, and existing remote sensing agents. Notably, compared with the original model, it improves the average score for disaster report generation on DisasterM3 by 13.5\% and ST5-SCS on RSCC by 15.34\%, providing a more accurate and verifiable solution for disaster emergency response. The code will be available at https://github.com/ZhijianLi2003/DisasterAgent.

</details>


### [8] [Satellite Pretraining Is Not One Thing: A Matched-Pair Study of Generic and Earth-Observation Foundation Models for Scene Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13849)
*Sandarbh Agrawal,Rakshith Sathish,Ashwathi Nambiar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文通过五组“匹配对”（共享目标函数与架构、仅预训练数据不同的EO与通用编码器）在14个冻结编码器、5个RGB场景分类数据集（0.06–30 m GSD）上的基准比较，证明"用卫星影像预训练"并非可靠选择准则：EO预训练对三组有益、对两组显著有害，且效应在1–5%标签下最大；同时发现冻结嵌入的轮廓系数几乎完美预测k-NN精度与检索mAP。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地理空间基础模型多用卫星/航拍影像预训练，但既往比较很少隔离预训练数据这一变量，因此"EO预训练编码器在少标签土地利用/土地覆盖制图上是否优于强通用编码器"仍不清楚。作者希望在控制架构与训练目标的前提下，干净地度量预训练数据来源（EO vs 通用）的净效应。

Method: 构建五组匹配对：DINO/SatDINO、DINOv3/DINOv3-SAT、MAE/SatMAE、MAE/Scale-MAE、CLIP/RemoteCLIP，共14个冻结编码器。在5个RGB场景分类数据集（GSD 0.06–30 m）上采用原生预处理，评估k-NN、线性探针、注意力探针与图像检索，覆盖6种低标签划分，并使用逐图像配对显著性检验；另做patch-token池化与注意力探针的鲁棒性分析，并计算冻结嵌入的轮廓系数与下游指标的相关性。

Result: EO预训练并非普遍有益：在多数数据集与指标上，SatDINO、Scale-MAE、RemoteCLIP（后者仅在未见数据集上评估）三组显著提升；而DINOv3-SAT（线性探针最多下降22.5点）与SatMAE（最多8.8点）两组显著变差。即便两个MAE在同一fMoW-RGB影像上预训练，结果也相反。上述效应在patch-token池化与注意力探针下稳健，主要集中于小型人造目标，并在标签比例为1–5%时最明显。跨编码器上，冻结嵌入的轮廓系数几乎完美预测k-NN精度与检索mAP（5个数据集中4个Spearman ρ≥0.96）。

Conclusion: 以卫星影像预训练作为编码器选择标准并不可靠，EO与通用编码器之间的优劣取决于具体数据集、探针类型与标签预算；候选checkpoint必须在目标任务数据上实测验证。冻结嵌入的轮廓系数可作为低成本、强预测性的代理指标用于筛选。

Abstract: Geospatial foundation models are pretrained on satellite and aerial imagery, but whether such an encoder beats a strong generic one for few-label land-use/land-cover mapping remains unclear, as prior comparisons rarely isolate the pretraining data. We study five matched pairs of Earth-observation (EO) and generic encoders sharing objective and architecture (DINO/SatDINO, DINOv3/DINOv3-SAT, MAE/SatMAE, MAE/Scale-MAE, CLIP/RemoteCLIP) within a benchmark of 14 frozen encoders on five RGB scene-classification datasets (0.06-30 m GSD). With native preprocessing, we evaluate k-NN, linear and attentive probes and image retrieval over six low-label splits, using paired per-image significance tests. EO pretraining is not uniformly helpful: on most datasets and metrics, it significantly improves three pairs (SatDINO, Scale-MAE, RemoteCLIP, the latter evaluated only on unseen datasets) and significantly hurts two (linear probe: DINOv3-SAT by up to 22.5 points, SatMAE by up to 8.8), with opposite outcomes even for two MAEs pretrained on the same fMoW-RGB imagery. These effects are robust to patch-token pooling and attentive probing, concentrate on small man-made objects, and are largest with 1-5% labels. Across encoders, the silhouette score of frozen embeddings predicts k-NN accuracy and retrieval mAP almost perfectly (Spearman $ρ\geq 0.96$ on four of five datasets). Pretraining on satellite imagery is thus not a reliable selection criterion; candidate checkpoints must be validated on target data.

</details>


### [9] [ZoomGain: Mining Global-to-Zoom Perception Gaps for Fine-Grained Visual Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14076)
*Haozhan Shen,Liangbo He,Jun Lan,Huijia Zhu,Weiqiang Wang,Tiancheng Zhao,Jianwei Yin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ZoomGain数据构建流水线，利用"全局-放大"感知差构建细粒度视觉监督，产出双语基准ZoomGainBench（1000题，28个VLM中最佳仅61.08），仅用9K样本训练即将4B/9B模型提升至超越数百B参数模型，9B在HR-Bench 4K达90.25。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 细粒度视觉感知要求VLM定位并解读复杂场景中的细微证据，但此类监督数据构建困难：问题需有正确且无歧义的答案，且答案必须依赖真实细粒度视觉细节而非粗粒度线索或语言先验。人工标注验证成本高，自动化生成又受限于模型自身细粒度感知能力弱。然而模型在区域放大后往往能识别这些细节，作者据此利用全局-放大感知差来生成监督。

Method: 提出ZoomGain数据构建流水线：基于仅在放大视图中获得的视觉信息，为全图构造问题；用多模型在放大视图上验证答案正确性；确保问题在全图上仍无歧义且无法仅凭语言先验作答；并专门为多选题生成高质量干扰项。仅做人工筛查、不做人工QA编辑，据此构建ZoomGainBench（1000题双语基准），并用9K样本进行训练。

Result: 在ZoomGainBench上28个VLM中最佳成绩仅61.08；失败分析发现模型答错多选题时偏好某一类特定干扰项。训练方面，仅用9K ZoomGain样本即显著提升4B/9B模型的视觉感知能力，在多项感知基准上超越若干数百B参数模型；9B模型在多项基准取得SoTA，如HR-Bench 4K达90.25。

Conclusion: 全局-放大感知差可作为规模化细粒度视觉监督的实用基础，同时服务于评测与训练，为构建高难度细粒度基准和高效提升VLM感知能力提供了可复用范式。

Abstract: Fine-grained visual perception requires Vision-Language Models (VLMs) to locate and interpret subtle evidence within complex scenes. Constructing supervision for this ability is challenging: questions must have correct, unambiguous answers that depend on actual fine-grained visual details rather than coarse cues or language priors. Verifying these properties makes manual annotation labor-intensive and automatic generation difficult for models that themselves struggle with fine-grained perception. Yet the same models can often recognize such details once the region is zoomed in. We exploit this global-to-zoom perception gap and introduce ZoomGain, a data construction pipeline that constructs questions about the full image from visual information gained only by zooming in. It verifies each answer on the zoomed view with multiple models, ensures that each question remains unambiguous in the full image and cannot be answered from language priors, and specifically generates high-quality distractors for multiple-choice questions. With this pipeline and human-only screening (no manual QA editing), we build ZoomGainBench, a 1,000-question bilingual benchmark on which the best of 28 VLMs achieves only 61.08. Failure analysis reveals that models favor a particular type of distractor when answering multiple-choice questions incorrectly. Beyond evaluation, training on just 9K ZoomGain samples substantially improves the visual perception capabilities of our 4B/9B models, enabling them to outperform several hundred-billion-parameter models across various perception benchmarks. The trained 9B model achieves SoTA performance on several benchmarks, such as 90.25 on HR-Bench 4K. These results establish global-to-zoom perception gaps as a practical basis for scaling fine-grained visual supervision for both evaluation and training. Our code is available at https://github.com/inclusionAI/ZoomGain.

</details>


### [10] [Obscured Wildfire Flame Detection by Spatio-temporal Analysis of Smoke Patterns Using Frame-wise Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14074)
*Uma Meleti,Abolfazl Razi,Fatemeh Afghah*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于连续RGB视频帧时空联合分析的逐帧Transformer架构，可同时检测可见火焰与被烟雾遮挡的火焰，在FLAME2数据集上取得前景分割Dice 92.61%、检测精确率93.21%/召回率91.73%、片段级分类准确率98.02%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法对图像和单帧视频独立分析，忽略了连续帧中烟雾运动的时间模式，而该模式恰可用于指示被遮挡的火源；同时方法需适配仅配备普通相机的低成本商用无人机。

Method: 对RGB视频进行空间与时间联合建模：设计逐帧Transformer，在连续帧之间施加注意力机制，同时保留帧内空间信息（区别于将时空信息压缩为单一向量的non-local block）；在不同空间分辨率下优化时间模式长度以兼顾性能与计算开销；在FLAME2数据集（双模RGB/IR视频）的筛选版本上验证，IR仅用于生成真值，模型仅用RGB训练。

Result: 前景分割Dice 92.61%；目标检测精确率93.21%、召回率91.73%；片段级分类准确率98.02%；并通过消融实验验证了方法的有效性与泛化性。

Conclusion: 时空联合的逐帧注意力建模优于逐帧独立分析，能够有效检测可见与烟雾遮挡火焰；仅用RGB训练即可在低成本无人机场景下应用，并可扩展至现有纯RGB数据集。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose a new paradigm in wildfire management for detecting both visible and obscured fire using Deep Learning (DL). The essence of our method is the joint spatial and temporal analysis of RGB videos, embracing the fact that temporal patterns of smoke motions in consecutive video frames can indicate obscured fire flames. This perspective contrasts the traditional approach of independently analyzing images and individual video frames. To this end, we propose a frame-wise transformer architecture that applies an attention mechanism between the consecutive frames while preserving spatial information within frames as opposed to regular attention mechanisms like the non-local block, which combines spatial and temporal information into a single vector. We also optimize the temporal pattern lengths under different spatial resolutions to achieve the highest performance while reducing the computational cost. To investigate the applicability of our method in aerial image processing, we applied it to a curated version of the FLAME2 dataset that includes dual-mode RGB/IR videos. The IR video feed is used to extract ground truth, while the model is trained only on RGB videos to maintain applicability to lowcost commercial drones (which are equipped only with regular cameras) and facilitate processing existing RGB-only datasets. Our proposed method achieves a Segmentation Foreground Dice score of 92.61%, an Object Detection Precision and Recall rate of 93.21% and 91.73%, and clip-level classification accuracy of 98.02%, respectively. We also investigated the efficacy and generalizability of our method through ablation study

</details>


### [11] [Trajectory-Regularized Distribution Distillation for One-Step Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12978)
*An Zhao,Yixiang Zhou,Zejian Li,Yi Guo,Pei Chen,Lingyun Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出轨迹正则化分布蒸馏（TRD），用单次前向预测的动量混合策略同时充当一步生成器、学生边缘速度估计器与闭式时间相关速度场，统一了分布级与轨迹级监督，在SANA-1.6B和SD 3.5上于质量与效率上优于一致性/分布匹配基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有两范式各有缺陷：轨迹对齐（trajectory alignment）以教师概率流ODE监督学生在极小采样预算下性能下降；分布匹配（distribution matching）直接优化生成分布，但估计逆向KL梯度通常需额外的可训练网络来追踪不断演变的学生得分。

Method: 提出轨迹正则化分布蒸馏（TRD）：通过一次学生前向即可预测的动量混合策略（momentum-mixture policy），同时承担三种角色——一步生成器、学生边缘速度的估计器（从而在分布匹配中省去第二个可训练网络）、以及沿教师ODE轨迹监督的闭式时间相关速度场，从而融合分布级与轨迹级监督。

Result: 在SANA-1.6B与Stable Diffusion 3.5上的实验表明，TRD在一步与少步生成的生成质量和训练效率上均优于代表性的一致性蒸馏与分布匹配基线，但在样本多样性上存在权衡。

Conclusion: TRD以单一策略统一分布匹配与轨迹正则，避免了辅助网络开销并克服极小步数下的轨迹对齐退化，实现了质量与效率的兼得；主要局限是多样性有所下降，需要在质量与多样性之间做权衡。

Abstract: Distilling flow matching models for one-step generation generally follows two paradigms. Trajectory alignment supervises the student with the teacher's probability flow ODE, but its performance degrades under very small sampling budgets. Distribution matching directly optimizes the generated distribution, but estimating its reverse Kullback-Leibler gradient typically requires a separate network to track the evolving student score. We propose trajectory-regularized distribution distillation (TRD), which combines distribution-level and trajectory-level supervision through a momentum-mixture policy predicted in a single student forward pass. This policy serves simultaneously as a one-step generator, an estimator of the student's marginal velocity that removes the need for a second trainable network in distribution matching, and a closed-form time-dependent velocity field supervised along the teacher ODE trajectory. Experiments on SANA-1.6B and Stable Diffusion 3.5 show that TRD outperforms representative consistency and distribution matching baselines in generation quality and training efficiency for both one-step and few-step generation, with a trade-off in sample diversity.

</details>


### [12] [Before Words, Beyond Speech: Evaluating Nonverbal Social Reasoning in Early Childhood](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13213)
*Marie Amale Huynh,Laura Bravo-Sánchez,Lauren Klein,Nick Haber,Philip Fisher,Serena Yeung-Levy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出首个以幼儿为中心的非言语社交推理基准NEST，含人工标注短视频与分级评估框架，揭示主流VLM在行为与人际线索理解上的显著性能塌陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 儿童早期是社交学习关键期，其意义建构依赖注视、手势与肢体接触等非言语线索，但现有多模态视频理解基准集中于成人对话驱动场景，且评估粒度粗糙（混淆基础感知与高阶推理），导致该领域数据与针对性改进均严重不足。

Method: 构建NEST基准：从自然场景、文化背景与发展阶段多样化的视频中筛选并人工标注约12秒片段（totalvids个）；设计组合式评估体系，由原子交互线索逐步构建语境、行为、人际与开放式描述四个递进推理层级；系统评测SOTA视觉语言模型，并分析其失败模式与偏差，进一步尝试领域自适应策略。

Result: 模型虽具备较强语境识别能力，但在精确行为与人际线索解码上出现显著性能下降；失败可归因于后果接地不足与时间敏感性等具体偏差。

Conclusion: NEST为幼儿社交意义上的多模态理解提供严格测试平台，推动社会性接地的人工智能研究，并为真实应用中的领域自适应提供实证依据。

Abstract: Children's earliest years are a period of intense social learning, during which meaning is built through gaze, gesture, and physical contact. Yet, this critical aspect of social interaction remains under-evaluated in multimodal video understanding, as existing benchmarks prioritize the conversation-driven interactions of adults. We address this gap with \bench ($\textbf{N}$aturalistic $\textbf{E}$arly-childhood $\textbf{S}$ocial in$\textbf{T}$eractions), the first child-centric benchmark for nonverbal social reasoning. \bench comprises \totalvids manually annotated clips ($\sim$12s) curated for diversity across naturalistic settings, cultural contexts, and developmental stages. Beyond a lack of data, targeted model improvement in this domain is hindered by coarse evaluation schemas that conflate basic perception with higher-order reasoning. To resolve this, \bench introduces a compositional schema that builds atomic interaction cues into progressive levels of social reasoning: contextual, behavioral, interpersonal, and open-ended descriptions. Extensive analysis of state-of-the-art Vision-Language Models (VLMs) reveals a stark performance collapse: despite strong contextual recognition, models exhibit substantial gaps in decoding precise behavioral and interpersonal cues. We trace these failures to specific biases, including poor consequence grounding and temporal sensitivity. Finally, we ground \bench in real-world applications by evaluating VLMs using domain adaptation strategies. Ultimately, \bench provides a rigorous testbed for advancing socially grounded artificial intelligence in early childhood. Project page: https://laubravo.github.io/nest_website/.

</details>


### [13] [Just Image Transformers with Late-Stage Pixel Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13120)
*Denis Lukovnikov,Andreas Müller,Asja Fischer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 通过在后训练阶段加入简单的卷积像素通路，并仅在去噪末期（最后20%）激活，显著提升无VAE潜变量的JiT扩散Transformer图像生成质量，ImageNet@256上FID从2.34降至1.78。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无潜变量（latent-free）扩散Transformer（如JiT）避免了VAE压缩伪影并简化训练流程，且训练效率可比拟潜空间模型；但JiT因将图像建模为大幅块（large patches）而导致生成质量下降。

Method: 重新审视JiT，在后训练（post-training）阶段为其加入简单的卷积像素通路（pixel-pathway）层；并发现该像素通路无需在去噪早期激活，可在去噪过程最后20%才激活；不引入额外损失。

Result: 在ImageNet@256上的实验相比原始JiT结果有所提升，FID达到1.78（原始为2.34，采用修正采样），且未使用额外损失。

Conclusion: 轻量级卷积像素通路作为后训练增强手段，可有效弥补无潜变量扩散Transformer因大块建模造成的质量损失；其只需在去噪末期激活，即可在低额外开销下取得显著质量提升。

Abstract: Recent work on latent-free diffusion transformers has demonstrated the feasibility of training image generators without a latent VAE. This avoids compression artifacts from the VAE and simplifies the training pipeline. In particular, JiT showed that even a standard transformer can be trained to generate images when the model is trained to predict the final image ($x$-prediction). Even though JiT unlocks training efficiency comparable to latent models, it still suffers from reduced image quality likely arising from modeling the image as a collection of large patches. In this work, we revisit JiT and show that adding simple convolutional pixel-pathway layers in a post-training phase significantly improves generation quality. Moreover, we show that the added pixel-pathway does not need to be active in the early denoising stages, and can be activated as late as in the last $20\%$ of the denoising process. Our experiments on ImageNet@256 show improvements compared to the original JiT results, achieving an FID of $1.78$ (from $2.34$ with corrected sampling), without using additional losses.

</details>


### [14] [Few-Step Streaming Video Generation via Condition Shifting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14033)
*Deyuan Liu,Jungang Li,Kaiwen Zheng,Kai Jiang,Jintao Chen,Yaole Wang,Ming Lin,Tao Lin,Jianfei Chen,Jun Zhu,Jintao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: APEX 将条件平移（condition shifting）引入流式视频生成，在生成器内部用同一套参数估计生成分布的速度场，从而去掉 DMD 所需的独立 fake-score 网络，同时保留 S2-Avatar 的冻结参考教师、Self-Replay Forcing 与感知正则化，在几乎不损失质量的前提下降低显存与迭代耗时。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 少步采样可降低流式视频生成成本，但 Distribution Matching Distillation（DMD）依赖独立的 fake-score 网络来监督少步生成器：这既增加训练显存与计算开销，又必须持续跟踪不断演化的生成器。因此需要一个无需额外 fake 网络的少步蒸馏方案。

Method: 把 APEX 扩展到流式视频：采用条件平移，在原文本条件下做生成，在平移后的条件下用同一组参数做 fake 估计，从而在生成器内部估计生成分布的速度场；移除独立 fake 网络，保留 S2-Avatar 的冻结参考教师、Self-Replay Forcing 和感知正则化；块级三次骨干评估，并配一步空间 Refiner。

Result: 在 180 秒视频上评测：Progressive 轨的视频指令遵循度 3.856 对比 3.831，两条轨道的视觉美学 0.641898 对比 0.632606，均优于 S2-Avatar；70 例人类研究中，指令遵循净偏好 +30.0%，一致性与视频质量各 +4.3%，运动质量略偏向 S2-Avatar，视听同步多为持平，可用性无变化；320 例 StreamAV-Bench 对比。匹配训练设置下，每 GPU 峰值分配显存降低 4.4%，平均迭代时间缩短 9.2%（相对 DMD）。

Conclusion: 条件平移可在生成器内自洽地替代独立 fake-score 网络，在保持甚至提升指令遵循与美学质量的同时显著降低训练显存与时间开销；运动质量与视听同步未获提升，是后续改进方向。

Abstract: Few-step sampling reduces the cost of streaming video generation. Distribution Matching Distillation (DMD) supervises few-step generators with a separate fake-score network, which adds training memory and computation and must track the evolving generator. We extend APEX to streaming video by using condition shifting to estimate the velocity field of the generated distribution within the generator. The original text condition selects generation, while a shifted condition selects fake estimation using the same parameters. This removes the independent fake network while retaining S2-Avatar's frozen reference teacher, Self-Replay Forcing, and perceptual regularization. We evaluate APEX on 180-second videos using three backbone evaluations per block and a one-step spatial Refiner, with a 70-case human study and a 320-case StreamAV-Bench comparison. Compared with S2-Avatar, APEX achieves higher video instruction fulfillment on the Progressive track (3.856 versus 3.831) and higher visual aesthetics across both tracks (0.641898 versus 0.632606). Human raters favor APEX most strongly on instruction following, with +30.0% net preference, followed by consistency and video quality at +4.3% each. Motion quality favors S2-Avatar, audiovisual synchronization is predominantly tied, and usability is unchanged. Separate measurements under matched training settings show 4.4% lower peak allocated GPU memory per GPU and 9.2% shorter mean iteration time than DMD.

</details>


### [15] [Specify Privacy Yourself: Assessing Inference-Time Personalized Privacy Preservation Ability of Large Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13997)
*Xingqi Wang,Xiaoyuan Yi,Xing Xie,Jia Jia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出"推理时个性化隐私保护"(ITP³)新任务，构建SPY-Bench基准（32,700样本、67类、24场景），评估21个SOTA LVLM，发现最强模型合规准确率仅约24%，并提出含Noise Contrastive Alignment变体的对齐方法达到96.88%准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LVLM能从图像高精度推断敏感个人信息，现有对齐仅保护信用卡等通用隐私，但隐私本质上是个人化、情境依赖的，需要用户通过语言动态指定隐私边界。

Method: 提出ITP³任务；构建SPY-Bench：含图像-问题对与个性化隐私指令的32,700个样本，覆盖67类隐私与24个真实场景，并设计基于用户规范与情境感知的新指标；对21个SOTA LVLM进行评测；实现初始ITP³对齐方法，包括一种新的Noise Contrastive Alignment变体。

Result: 多数模型表现差，即使最强的o4-mini合规准确率仅约24%；模型的情境隐私理解能力有限；所提对齐方法达96.88%准确率，同时保持合理的通用性能。

Conclusion: ITP³是对齐LVLM可控性的重要方向，SPY-Bench与所提对齐方法为更可控、更符合伦理部署的LVLM迈出第一步，代码与数据已开源。

Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities but raise significant privacy concerns due to their abilities to infer sensitive personal information from images with high precision. While current LVLMs are relatively well aligned to protect universal privacy, e.g., credit card data, we argue that privacy is inherently personalized and context-dependent. This work pivots towards a novel task: can LVLMs achieve Inference-Time Personalized Privacy Protection (ITP$^3$), allowing users to dynamically specify privacy boundaries through language specifications? To this end, we present SPY-Bench, the first systematic assessment of ITP$^3$ ability, which comprises (1) 32,700 unique samples with image-question pairs and personalized privacy instructions across 67 categories and 24 real-world scenarios, and (2) novel metrics grounded in user specifications and context awareness. Benchmarking the ITP$^3$ ability of 21 SOTA LVLMs, we reveal that: (i) most models, even the top-performing o4-mini, perform poorly, with only $\sim$24% compliance accuracy; (ii) they show quite limited contextual privacy understanding capability. Therefore, we implemented initial ITP$^3$ alignment methods, including a novel Noise Contrastive Alignment variant which achieves 96.88% accuracy while maintaining reasonable general performance. These results mark an initial step towards the ethical deployment of more controllable LVLMs. Code and data are available at https://github.com/achernarwang/specify-privacy-yourself

</details>


### [16] [Textual Response-Aware Guidance for Zero-Shot Adversarial Robustness of Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40309)
*Yubo Cui,Xianchao Guan,Zheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出文本响应感知引导（TRAG），利用预训练文本编码器对输入嵌入局部变化的响应构建锚点级约束并按其图像相关性聚合，实现图像自适应的视觉表征漂移约束，在保持零样本能力的同时达到SOTA的干净与鲁棒性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言模型对抗微调依赖预训练模型引导以维持图文对齐，但两类代表性引导各有局限：匹配视觉特征对各方向等权约束，覆盖广但无语义侧重；匹配预测分布侧重语义相关方向，却受候选文本集合与预测概率限制。两者均难以充分保持预训练图文关系，导致零样本性能下降。

Method: 先分析视觉表征漂移，指出上述两类引导的互补缺陷；进而提出TRAG：以预训练文本编码器对输入嵌入局部扰动的响应为依据，围绕语义锚点从响应的方向与幅度构造锚点级约束，再用预训练的图像-锚点相关性进行加权聚合，得到图像自适应约束项用于对抗微调。

Result: 在多种架构与任务上的大量实验表明，该方法在干净性能与鲁棒性能上均达到当前最优；进一步分析显示TRAG有助于保持预训练的图像-文本关系。

Conclusion: 以文本编码器局部响应替代特征匹配或预测分布匹配，可提供更具语义针对性且不受候选文本限制的引导信号，是缓解对抗微调中零样本性能退化的有效途径。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) exhibit strong zero-shot generalization but remain vulnerable to adversarial attacks. Existing adversarial fine-tuning methods often use pre-trained model guidance to preserve image--text alignment while improving robustness. Our analysis of visual representation drift reveals complementary limitations of two representative forms of guidance. Matching visual features constrains all directions equally, providing broad coverage without semantic emphasis. Matching prediction distributions prioritizes semantically relevant directions, but remains limited by the candidate texts and prediction probabilities. These limitations can hinder preservation of pre-trained image--text relationships and degrade zero-shot performance. We therefore propose Textual Response-Aware Guidance (TRAG), which constrains visual representation drift using the pre-trained text encoder's responses to local variations in input embeddings. TRAG constructs anchor-level constraints from the response directions and magnitudes around semantic anchors, then aggregates them using pre-trained image--anchor relevance, yielding an image-adaptive constraint. Extensive experiments across architectures and tasks demonstrate state-of-the-art clean and robust performance. Further analysis shows that TRAG helps preserve pre-trained image--text relationships.

</details>


### [17] [Looking One Step Ahead: An Anticipation-guided Agent for Object State Readout in Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14113)
*Yuanhe Tian,Yan Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 LASER：一种以“预测下一步变化”为引导的对象状态读出智能体，通过将预期变化转为针对性视觉问题来检查当前帧、建立属性值，并用后续帧独立观测进行校验与记录精化，在 OSCaR 与 PLM RCap 上稳定优于强基线，且全程冻结模型参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解活动进展需跟踪对象随时间的变化，对象状态读出即描述目标对象在视频帧间的状态与转移。现有 MLLM 生成的视觉描述虽详尽，但往往未充分指定区分相邻状态所需的属性，导致状态刻画不足、区分度不够。

Method: LASER 为 anticipation-guided agent：给定按时间排序的帧与目标对象，先把“可能的下一步变化”转化为有针对性的视觉问题，在当前帧上检查以确定属性取值；再以对后续帧的独立观测检验状态读出与预测，迭代精化状态记录并生成最终描述。流程由可复用的文本“观察技能”引导，通过将输出与参考字幕的差异回溯到具体指令来改进技能；候选修订在其他视频上依据“字幕质量提升且不与参考事实冲突”进行选择。适应与推理阶段模型参数冻结，评估阶段修订后的技能保持固定。

Result: 在 OSCaR 与 PLM RCap 上的实验结果及进一步分析显示，LASER 相对强基线与现有方法取得一致提升，验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 以预期变化驱动属性检查、并结合后续帧独立观测进行校验的状态读出范式，可在不更新模型参数的前提下有效提升对象状态描述的充分性与可靠性；可自改进的文本观察技能为冻结 MLLM 提供了可迁移的适配途径。

Abstract: Understanding the progress of an activity requires tracking how the objects involved change over time. Object state readout provides this information by describing a target object's states and transitions across video frames. Although multimodal large language models (MLLMs) generate detailed visual descriptions, these descriptions may leave the attributes needed to distinguish successive states insufficiently specified. In this paper, we propose look-ahead state readout (LASER), an anticipation-guided agent for object state readout that uses possible next-step changes to determine which attributes to inspect in the present. Given temporally ordered frames and a target object, LASER converts anticipated changes into targeted visual questions and inspects the current frame to establish attribute values. It then checks state readouts and forecasts against independent observations of subsequent frames, refining a state record used to generate the final description. Reusable textual observation skills guide this process and are refined by tracing discrepancies with reference captions to the responsible instructions. Candidate revisions are selected on separate videos based on improved caption quality without increased contradictions with reference facts. Model parameters remain frozen throughout adaptation and inference, and the revised skills remain fixed during evaluation. Experiment results and further analysis on OSCaR and PLM RCap show consistent improvements of LASER over strong baselines and existing approaches, which demonstrates the effectiveness of our approach.

</details>


### [18] [Dark Matters: VAE-Aware HDR Encoding for SDR-to-HDR Video Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13504)
*Donghoon Cha,Bomseok Park,Hansl von Kwon,Ku DoHyung,Hanwool Sul*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 EquivFlow：一种紧凑、可逆、曝光等变的 HDR 信号编码，将曝光变化映射为加性平移并保留尺度无关的色度分量；它无需修改预训练 VAE，即可缓解对数类编码在冻结 VAE 潜空间中造成的暗部色度塌陷，并在 SDR-to-HDR 视频扩散中带来稳定色度保真度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SDR-to-HDR 重建是病态问题，需同时恢复高光截断、阴影压暗与量化伪影。潜扩散模型的生成先验虽有帮助，但其效果受限于 HDR 信号通过预训练 VAE 的方式：常见对数类编码会在生成前压低低亮度区域的色度差异，导致冻结 VAE 潜空间出现"暗侧塌陷"。

Method: 提出 EquivFlow——一种紧凑、可逆的曝光等变编码：把曝光变化转换为加性平移，同时保留一个尺度无关的色度分量，且不修改 VAE 本身。在不同预训练图像与视频 VAE 上进行编码层面的测试，并将其集成到 SDR-to-HDR 视频扩散流程中。

Result: 编码层面测试表明，其曝光结构与深暗部色度传输特性在所测试的主机 VAE 上均能保持；集成到 SDR-to-HDR 视频扩散后，在所测试设置下取得一致的色度保真度增益，同时保持有竞争力的重建保真度。

Conclusion: HDR 信号进入冻结 VAE 的编码方式本身是关键瓶颈；曝光等变、可逆的编码设计可在不改动 VAE 的前提下改善暗部色度恢复，实现色度保真与重建保真度的较好权衡。

Abstract: SDR-to-HDR reconstruction is an ill-posed problem in which clipped highlights, crushed shadows, and quantization artifacts must be recovered while preserving the original content. Latent diffusion models provide powerful generative priors for restoring missing detail, but their effectiveness is constrained by how HDR signals pass through the pretrained VAE. We find that common log-like encodings suppress low-luminance chromatic differences before generation, producing a dark-side collapse in the frozen-VAE latent. We introduce EquivFlow, a compact, invertible exposure-equivariant encoding that maps exposure changes to additive shifts while retaining a scale-independent chromatic component, without modifying the VAE. Encoding-level tests across pretrained image and video VAEs show that its exposure structure and deep-dark chroma transport persist across the tested hosts. Integrated into SDR-to-HDR video diffusion, EquivFlow delivers consistent chromatic-fidelity gains across the tested settings while retaining competitive reconstruction fidelity.

</details>


### [19] [TIB: Sample-wise Tempered Information Bottleneck for Multimodal Attribution beyond Alignment Assumption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12997)
*Qiming Huang,Peixi Liu,Jianbo Jiao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文指出多模态归因中图像-文本对语义对齐假设常被违背，导致信息瓶颈(IB)归因目标出现后验过度收缩与强制解释；提出样本级温度化信息瓶颈(TIB)框架，按跨模态可靠性自适应调节归因强度，无需对齐假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态归因方法（含IB类方法）把成对模态当作代理监督，隐含假设图文语义对齐；但真实数据集中部分语义错配与不可靠的跨模态对应普遍存在，该假设被频繁违背。

Method: 理论分析表明：在错配条件下，IB类归因目标因假设所有代理监督信号可靠性相同，产生后验过度收缩(posterior over-contraction)与强制解释(forced explanations)。据此提出TIB——样本级Tempered Information Bottleneck框架，以跨模态可靠性自适应调节归因强度，不依赖语义对齐假设。

Result: 在受控基准与大规模数据集上的大量实验验证了方法有效性，同时验证了所识别的失效模式。

Conclusion: 多模态归因需摆脱语义对齐假设；样本级可靠性加权的温度化信息瓶颈可有效缓解过度收缩与强制解释问题。

Abstract: Multimodal (e.g. vision-language, in this work) attribution aims to interpret the models without access to ground-truth explanatory supervision. Existing multimodal attribution methods typically rely on paired modalities as proxy supervision, implicitly assuming that image-text pairs are semantically aligned. Information bottleneck (IB)-based attribution methods follow this general paradigm and instantiate it through a global sufficiency-compression objective. In realistic multimodal datasets, however, this assumption is frequently violated due to partial semantic misalignment and unreliable cross-modal correspondences. Through theoretical analysis, we show that, under such misalignment, information-bottleneck-based attribution objectives exhibit a structural limitation: by assuming equal reliability across all proxy supervision signals, they induce posterior over-contraction and forced explanations. To address this issue, we propose TIB, a sample-wise Tempered Information Bottleneck framework that adaptively modulates attribution strength according to cross-modal reliability, without assuming semantic alignment. Extensive experiments on controlled benchmarks and large-scale datasets show the effectiveness of the proposed approach, while also validating the identified failure mode.

</details>


### [20] [MicroBT: Centroid-Guided Synergistic Learning for Multi-Modal Micro Brain Tumor Segmentation and Counting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13912)
*Zhenye Lou,Qing Xu,Stefanie Thust,Dorothee P. Auer,Zhen Chen,Wenting Duan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MicroBT统一多模态框架，利用病灶质心的尺度不变性，将质心预测作为共享锚点同时驱动稠密分割与实例级定位，并配合体积校准损失与尺寸感知课程优化，实现微脑肿瘤（≤3mm）分割与计数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖基于重叠度的目标函数，天然偏向大区域，导致小于3mm的微病灶在训练中被系统性抑制；同时像素级分割框架缺乏显式实例级建模，病灶计数不可靠，影响临床分期与治疗决策。

Method: 核心思想是病灶质心这一几何属性具有尺度不变性。方法包括：(1) 质心锚定的协同范式，用预测质心作为共享锚点，同时条件化稠密分割与实例级定位；(2) 体积校准损失（VC-Loss），自适应放大微病灶的梯度贡献以缓解尺寸不平衡；(3) 尺寸感知课程优化（SACO），先建立全局表示再逐步解锁细粒度定位，以稳定多任务训练。

Result: 大量实验表明MicroBT优于现有SOTA方法，尤其在转移瘤上，微病灶（≤3mm）定位精度提升约7%，计数估计误差降低约26%。

Conclusion: 以尺度不变的质心为统一锚点，并结合损失重加权与课程式优化，可有效解决微脑肿瘤分割被抑制与计数不可靠的问题，为临床分期与治疗决策提供更可靠的微病灶定量信息。

Abstract: Multi-modal brain tumor segmentation is essential for clinical diagnosis, radiotherapy planning, and longitudinal monitoring. While recent studies have achieved remarkable overall segmentation performance, they rely on overlap-based objectives that inherently favor large regions, causing sub-3 mm micro-lesions to be systematically suppressed during training. Moreover, existing pixel-level segmentation frameworks lack explicit instance-level modeling, leading to unreliable lesion counting that directly impacts clinical staging and therapeutic decisions. To address these challenges, we propose MicroBT, a unified multi-modal framework for micro brain tumor segmentation and counting. Our core insight is that a fundamental geometric property is inherently scale-invariant: regardless of size, each lesion possesses a well-defined centroid. Leveraging this, we first devise a centroid-anchored synergistic paradigm that utilizes predicted centroids as shared anchors to simultaneously condition dense segmentation and instance-level localization. To counteract the tumor size imbalance, we further devise a Volume-Calibrated Loss (VC-Loss) that adaptively amplifies the gradient contribution of micro-lesions, alongside a Size-Aware Curriculum Optimization (SACO) that stabilizes multi-task training by establishing global representations before progressively unlocking fine-grained localization. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MicroBT outperforms state-of-the-art methods, particularly on metastases, where micro-lesion ($\leq 3$ mm) localization precision improves by approximately 7% and counting estimation errors decrease by approximately 26%. The code is available at https://github.com/leonlou921/MicroBT.

</details>


### [21] [CASCADE: Constraint-Aware SCAffolded DEcomposition for Logical Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13820)
*Jinchan Kim,Jinhee Im,Hyunsouk Cho*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文指出LVLM-based LAD的checklist分解为纯文本分解，导致"约束特定感知鸿沟"及两类失败模式，并提出免训练的CASCADE/CASCADE++，用约束特定的视觉支架（CASCADE++进一步在object层面自适应）替代纯文本推理引导，仅用3个参考样本即超过全样本SOTA 3.9 AUROC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LAD需在仅有少量正常参考下验证查询图像是否满足各类别特定约束（计数、配对关系、测量范围）。现有基于LVLM的方法将其表述为checklist验证、分解为独立文本检查，但该分解是文本-only的：每个条目仍读取同一张未修改的查询图像，迫使LVLM从单一原始图像中提取不同形式的视觉证据。作者将此文本-图像错配识别为"约束特定感知鸿沟"，并归因出两种失败模式：binding约束下的instance conflation与calibration约束下的scale drift。

Method: 在免训练设置下对比文本推理引导与视觉感知引导以诊断失败来源；据此提出免训练框架CASCADE，用约束特定的视觉支架扩展checklist分解；进一步提出CASCADE++，依据约束结构与参考分布推导支架配置，使其在object层面自适应。

Result: 分析显示对同一图像进行推理收益甚微，而针对每个约束适配视觉输入可显著提升验证准确率。在MVTec-LOCO与VID-AD上，CASCADE++仅用三个参考即比全样本SOTA高3.9 AUROC点，比最强VID-AD基线提升11.4，并可免训练迁移到多个LVLM骨干。

Conclusion: LAD中的核心瓶颈在于感知而非推理：纯文本checklist分解不足以应对多样约束，需按约束定制视觉输入。CASCADE/CASCADE++以constraint-specific视觉支架实现这一思路，在少样本下达到并超越全样本SOTA，且具备跨LVLM骨干的免训练可迁移性。

Abstract: Logical Anomaly Detection (LAD) requires verifying whether all category-specific constraints, such as object counts, pairing relations, and measurement ranges, are satisfied by a query image given only a few normal references. Recent LAD methods based on Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) formulate this as checklist verification, decomposing the decision into independent textual checks. This decomposition, however, is text-only. Every item still reads the same unmodified query image, so the LVLM must extract different forms of visual evidence from one raw image. We identify this text-image mismatch as a constraint-specific perception gap, the source of two failure modes: instance conflation for binding constraints and scale drift for calibration constraints. To diagnose whether these failures stem from insufficient reasoning or insufficient perception, we compare textual reasoning guides and visual perception guides in a training-free setting. Our analysis shows that reasoning over the same image provides little benefit, whereas adapting the visual input to each constraint substantially improves verification accuracy. Based on this finding, we propose CASCADE, a training-free framework that extends checklist decomposition using constraint-specific visual scaffolds. We further introduce CASCADE++, which adapts these scaffolds at the object level by deriving their configurations from the constraint structure and reference distribution. Across MVTec-LOCO and VID-AD, CASCADE++ surpasses the full-shot state of the art by 3.9 AUROC points with only three references, and improves over the strongest VID-AD baseline by 11.4, transferring across multiple LVLM backbones without any training.

</details>


### [22] [The Case for AI-Enabled Gate Monitoring as Farm Security in the UK: A PRISMA-Guided Review of the Evidence on Rural Crime, Livestock Predation and Farm Access](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13933)
*Steve Symes,Paul Fergus,Mark Carr,Omar McIver,Arwyn Jones*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过PRISMA系统综述指出英国农场安全证据基础薄弱（英国同行评议文献仅16条），成本数据依赖保险理赔，且缺乏对监控、传感、标记等技术的独立评估；作者推断Fieldmark AI式门级连续AI监控将大有益处，但明确承认尚无实证，并提出所需评估设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 英国农场安全投入与证据不匹配：成本估算主要来自保险理赔与行业调查，官方数据无法单独识别农业犯罪，现有防控技术（摄像头、传感器、追踪器、标记）缺乏独立效果评估。作者意在系统梳理证据缺口，并为一项新技术主张确定评估路径。

Method: PRISMA指南综述：2026年11月2–4日在Web of Science与Scopus执行12次检索，获1,183条记录（去重后761条），灰色文献与手工检索补充35条；最终对96条数据库记录和24条灰色来源作叙述性综合。证据分层呈现：英国同行评议记录16条；英国犯罪与农场主心理健康主要调查仅80份回复；成本来自NFU Mutual理赔数据（2025年农村犯罪£41.5M，牲畜盗窃升近30%至约£4.5M，犬袭牲畜£1.95M）；内政部称中央数据无法单独识别农业犯罪。技术效果证据：未发现摄像头/传感器/追踪器/标记的独立评估；防捕食干预系统综述显示光声驱避在1–5个月内失效；无研究考察农场门开启频率及后果；相机陷阱AI论文仅在生态数据集报告精度，一条对比中机器学习检出41%野生动物，人工分析为81%。

Result: 证据缺口明确且系统：英国高质量证据规模极小，现有调查样本量低（80），成本估算依赖单一保险来源，官方统计无法分离农业犯罪，技术有效性缺乏独立验证，而驱避手段效果随时间衰减。相机陷阱AI在农场门口场景缺乏验证，且部分研究显示其检出率显著低于人工。

Conclusion: 作者以推断（非已证实效应）形式主张：门级连续AI介导监控（如Fieldmark AI）可生成官方缺失的农场级时间戳记录、核验周期性人工巡查遗漏的事件、厘清狐、犬或人所致事件，并为其他安防措施提供可检验基准。但尚无研究显示其降低损失，论文据此提出验证减损效果所需的评估方案。

Abstract: Farm security in the UK rests on thinner evidence than its cost suggests. We ran a PRISMA guided review: twelve searches of Web of Science and Scopus on 2nd -4th November 2026 returned 1,183 records (761 unique), and grey-literature and hand-searches added 35 assessed sources. We synthesised 96 database records and 24 grey sources narratively. UK peer-reviewed evidence is small (16 records), and the main UK survey of crime and farmer mental health has 80 responses. Cost estimates rest on insurer claims: NFU Mutual put UK rural crime at £41.5 million in 2025, with livestock theft up almost 30% to about £4.5 million and dog attacks on livestock costing £1.95 million. The Home Office states that agricultural crime cannot be separately identified in its central data. We found no independent evaluation of cameras, sensors, trackers or marking; light and sound deterrents lost effect within one to five months in a systematic review of anti-predator interventions; and no study examined how often farm gates are left open or what follows. Camera-trap AI papers report accuracy on ecological datasets, none at a farm gate in the records we found, and in one comparison machine learning detected 41% of wildlife against 81% for analysts. We argue, as an inference from these gaps and not as a demonstrated effect, that continuous gate-level AI-mediated monitoring, as implemented in Fieldmark AI, would be highly beneficial for UK farm security. It would create the farm-level, time-stamped record that no official source holds, verify events that periodic human checks miss, resolve whether foxes, dogs or people were responsible, and give every other security measure something to be tested against. No study has yet shown that it reduces loss, and we set out the evaluation needed to show it.

</details>


### [23] [Interleaving anatomical inspection and diagnostic reasoning for explainable radiology report generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14018)
*Chentao Huang,Runlong Lin,Zhijun Wang,Yunxiang Li,Yuanyuan Sun,Jian Wang,Hongfei Lin,Ling Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ARIVE：解剖引导、交错视觉证据的可解释放射报告生成框架


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动放射报告生成虽能产出临床合理的报告，但"视觉证据→报告发现"的推理路径不透明，报告诊断依据无法解释，也难以审阅和修改。

Method: 提出ARIVE框架，在最终报告撰写前将解剖区域检查与诊断推理交错进行；从MIMIC-CXR构建159,416条多模态思维链（MCoT）轨迹（解剖区域视觉证据驱动后续临床解读）；在此基础上训练，并用解剖奖励与临床内容奖励进一步优化。

Result: 在MIMIC-CXR与以异常为中心的MIMIC-ABN上，临床效能优于强基线；盲评专家打分与领先模型报告相当；MCoT轨迹在诊断可追溯性上获高评分；配对审阅评估显示MCoT有助于识别报告中的错误。

Conclusion: 将解剖检查与诊断推理显式交错的MCoT范式可提升报告的可解释性与可审阅性，在保持临床效能的同时增强诊断可追溯性并辅助错误识别。

Abstract: Automated radiology report generation can draft clinically plausible reports, yet the pathway from visual evidence to reported findings remains opaque, leaving the diagnostic basis of these reports unexplained and the generated reports difficult to review or revise. We introduce ARIVE (Anatomy-guided Reasoning with Interleaved Visual Evidence), an explainable report generation framework that interleaves anatomical inspection with diagnostic reasoning prior to final report composition. To support this process, we constructed 159,416 multimodal chain-of-thought (MCoT) trajectories from MIMIC-CXR, wherein visual evidence from inspected anatomical regions informs subsequent clinical interpretation. Trained on these trajectories and further optimized using anatomical and clinical-content rewards, ARIVE achieved superior clinical-efficacy performance compared with strong baselines on MIMIC-CXR and the abnormality-centered MIMIC-ABN. ARIVE's reports were competitive with those of leading models in blinded expert grading, and its MCoT trajectories earned high ratings for diagnostic traceability. Furthermore, a paired review evaluation suggests that access to MCoT can aid in identifying errors within generated reports.

</details>


### [24] [A Stage-Wise Explainable Pipeline for Arteriolar-to-Venular Ratio Estimation and Retinopathy Screening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14013)
*Sharan Sharmeela Karuppannan,Yoga Sri Varshan,Subin Sahayam,Umarani Jayaraman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一个五阶段可解释流水线用于从眼底图像估计AVR并做探索性视网膜病变筛查；在30张HRF图像上达96.7%准确率、F1=0.968、ROC-AUC=0.978，但受小样本与代理标签限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AVR是临床上具有意义的视网膜血管健康标志物，但现有方法多为端到端黑箱，中间过程不可检验，错误难以定位到具体处理阶段。

Method: 五阶段流水线：血管及动静脉掩膜、视盘定位、基于最小弦边界游走的血管口径估计、由主要动脉/静脉分支计算AVR，并以显式阈值规则生成筛查决策，各阶段输出可检查；辅以消融实验及与VascX的探索性对比，考察血管配对、口径测量与聚合方式的影响。

Result: 在30张HRF图像的探索性队列上取得96.7%准确率、F1为0.968、ROC-AUC为0.978；消融显示血管配对、口径测量与聚合策略对结果有影响。

Conclusion: 该流水线为研究视网膜血管生物标志物提供了透明的可追溯框架，但小队列、代理疾病标签及缺乏独立参考口径测量限制了结论解释，需在更大规模、具临床分级的数据集上进一步验证。

Abstract: The arteriolar-to-venular diameter ratio (AVR) is a clinically motivated marker of retinal vascular health. We present a five-stage pipeline for AVR estimation and exploratory retinopathy screening from fundus images, with inspectable intermediate outputs at each stage. The pipeline combines vessel and artery-vein masks, optic-disc localization, vessel-calibre estimation using a minimal-chord boundary walk, and AVR computation from dominant arterial and venular branches. An explicit threshold-based rule produces the screening decision, enabling errors to be traced to individual processing stages. On an exploratory cohort of 30 HRF images, we report 96.7% accuracy, an F1-score of 0.968 and ROC-AUC of 0.978. Ablation studies and an exploratory comparison with VascX examine the effects of vessel pairing, calibre measurement and aggregation. Interpretation is limited by the small cohort, proxy disease labels and absence of independent reference calibre measurements. The pipeline provides a transparent framework for investigating retinal vascular biomarkers, with further validation needed on larger, clinically graded cohorts.

</details>


### [25] [CAppleNet: A Coordinate Attention-Based YOLO11 for Real-Time Apple Stem Cavity and Calyx Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13920)
*Alican Güneş,Robert Selim Hill,Gökhan Hasanoğlu,Murat Kurt*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文构建苹果关键形态地标（果体、果柄腔、萼片）自采集数据集，以YOLO11s为基线做单因素消融，发现仅C2PSA→CA的同槽替换有效：F1持平（0.9038 vs 0.9047）、参数降10.2%、mAP@0.5最优（0.935），部署置信阈值为0.15，且架构与增强增益不可叠加。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水果自动分级、分选与定向系统需要实时、准确地检测果实级的形态地标，而不仅是果实边界；同时需明确在高品种间颜色与纹理变异条件下，哪些训练因素真正提升细粒度地标检测性能。

Method: 构建并自标注1506张苹果图像数据集（1054/301/151划分），固定相机受控采集，标注果体、果柄腔、萼片三类；以默认超参的YOLO11s为基线，围绕输入分辨率、数据增强策略（6种变体）、优化器配置与轻量化架构改造开展单因素逐一（OFAT）消融；进一步做置信度阈值扫描与CA替换+最佳增强的组合验证实验。

Result: 提升分辨率、六种增强变体与手动优化器调参均未超过未调优基线（F1=0.9047）；仅将骨干C2PSA块同槽替换为Coordinate Attention后总体F1持平（0.9038），参数减少10.2%，取得最佳mAP@0.5（0.935）与最佳果柄腔F1（0.8217）；阈值扫描确定0.15为部署工作点；组合实验显示架构与增强增益不具可加性，反而相互抵消（架构增益反转）。

Conclusion: 面向定向感知的工业分选与对齐场景，推荐方案为仅采用CA同槽替换架构、置信度阈值取0.15的实时苹果地标检测模型；研究亦提示在细粒度小目标任务中，常规"加大分辨率/堆叠增强/精调优化器"未必有效，且单因素独立验证的增益在组合时可能失效，需以联合实验确认。

Abstract: Automated fruit grading, sorting, and orientation systems require real-time, accurate detection of fruit-level morphological landmarks rather than the fruit boundary alone. This paper presents a custom, fully self-collected and self-annotated dataset of 1506 apple images (1054 training, 301 validation, 151 test) captured under controlled, fixed-camera conditions and annotated for three target classes: fruit body, stem cavity, and calyx. A YOLO11s detector trained with default hyperparameters is adopted as the baseline, and a systematic, one-factor-at-a-time ablation study is conducted across input resolution, data augmentation policy, optimizer configuration, and lightweight architectural enhancement to identify the factors that most improve detection of these fine-grained landmarks under high inter-cultivar color and texture variability. Neither increased input resolution, nor any of six tested augmentation variants, nor manual optimizer tuning improved on the untuned baseline (F1 = 0.9047); only a same-slot architectural substitution of the backbone's C2PSA block with Coordinate Attention (CA) matched the baseline's overall F1 (0.9038) while reducing parameter count by 10.2% and achieving the study's best mAP@0.5 (0.935) and best stem-cavity F1 (0.8217). A confidence-threshold sweep identified 0.15 as the deployment operating point for this model, and a confirmatory experiment combining the CA-swap architecture with the best-performing augmentation variant showed the two effects do not combine additively, reversing the architectural gain rather than compounding it. The resulting design recommendations --- the CA-swap architecture alone, at a 0.15 confidence threshold --- target real-time apple landmark detection for orientation-aware industrial sorting and alignment systems.

</details>


### [26] [Region-Wise Logit Fusion and Utility-Gated Refinement for Brain Metastasis Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13884)
*Zhenye Lou,Qing Xu,Wenting Duan,Zhen Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: COMPASS-METS 通过多训练器概率集成与结构化概率锚定，在 BraTS-METS 2026 Task 1 的脑转移瘤与术后腔分割中兼顾几何精度与小病灶检出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多参数 MRI 上脑转移瘤包含大量小强化灶，且需同时满足 ET/TC/WT/RC 的层级几何一致性；单一模型难以同时兼顾层级正确性与小病灶检测。

Method: 将三个五折 nnU-Net 配置集成为对齐的区域概率集成；采用结构化概率锚点做区域特定组件决策、TC 边界补全、RC 过滤与保层级转换；对被遗漏的 ET 候选以学习到的组件置信度加确定性多训练器支持进行加性召回，并由效用门控仅接纳满足病灶存在性与几何安全性概率的孤立候选。

Result: 179 例复用开发集上 Small F1 0.354、病灶级 DSC 0.712、NSD 0.714、All F1 0.779、HD95 56.6 mm；相对 ResEncXL 提升了 DSC、NSD、All F1 并降低 HD95，Small F1 由 0.367 变为 0.354。

Conclusion: 几何收益主要来自结构化概率构建，受门控的 ET 精修可挽回大部分小病灶检测损失；但 179 例结果为存在选择偏差的开发集估计，需外部验证。

Abstract: BraTS-METS 2026 Task 1 evaluates segmentation of brain metastases and postoperative resection cavities on heterogeneous multiparametric MRI, requiring detection of small enhancing foci while preserving the geometry and hierarchy of enhancing tumor (ET), tumor core (TC), whole tumor (WT), and resection cavity (RC). We present COMPASS-METS (Component-Aware Multi-Trainer Probability Assembly with Structured Segmentation), which combines three five-fold nnU-Net configurations into aligned regional probability ensembles. A structured-probability anchor applies region-specific component decisions, TC boundary completion, RC filtering, and hierarchy-preserving conversion. Omitted ET candidates are recovered additively using learned component confidence and deterministic multi-trainer support for ET and its TC/WT parents; a final utility gate admits only disconnected candidates with sufficient lesion-existence and geometry-safety probabilities. On the reused 179-case challenge development cohort, COMPASS-METS (Team: MicroBT) achieved Small F1 0.354, lesion-wise DSC 0.712, NSD 0.714, All F1 0.779, and HD95 56.6 mm. Relative to ResEncXL, it improved DSC, NSD, and All F1 and reduced HD95, while Small F1 changed from 0.367 to 0.354. Stagewise analysis attributes the principal geometric gains to structured probability construction and shows that guarded ET refinement recovers most of the associated small-lesion detection loss. These 179-case results remain selection-aware development estimates. The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/leonlou921/COMPASS-METS.

</details>


### [27] [PhysMoE: Fast Spatial Grounding with Single-Token Coordinate Representation in Modality-Decoupled VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13000)
*Ruimeng Liu,Denan Liang,Ruofei Bai,Fuming Zhang,Kuan Xu,Shenghai Yuan,Lihua Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PhysMoE 通过模态路由的 MoE、单 token 坐标编码与可微 SDF 监督，解决具身 VLM 坐标预测中的空间不连续、解码慢及多接触/不规则目标回归失效问题，在 9 个 grounding 基准上平均提升 15.7%、吞吐快 8.31 倍，并保持 11 个文本基准的理解能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身 VLM 是机器人规划与操作的主干，但 grounding 任务的坐标预测存在两类瓶颈：离散 token 化破坏空间连续性且解码延迟高；标准点回归无法处理多接触 affordance 与不规则形状目标。

Method: 三项核心设计：(1) 低层共享以建模多模态上下文，高层按物理模态路由到空间（像素）与语言（文本）专家，消除梯度干扰；(2) 复用视觉编码器已学的位置嵌入，将每个坐标编码为单 token，注入 2D 空间先验，使坐标与视觉特征原生对齐并降低训练难度；(3) 对 pointing 施加可微 Signed Distance Field 监督，无需昂贵的 mask 解码器即可适配 affordance 与不规则几何。

Result: 相比基座模型，在 9 个基于坐标的 grounding 基准（轨迹、affordance、定位）上平均提升 15.7%，吞吐提升 8.31 倍；同时在 11 个文本型具身基准上保持多模态理解与推理能力。

Conclusion: 通过模态分离的专家路由、位置嵌入复用的坐标 token 化与 SDF 式几何监督，可在显著提速的同时提升坐标级 grounding 精度，且不牺牲通用多模态理解，为具身 VLM 的空间输出提供了一种高效范式。

Abstract: Embodied Vision-Language Models (VLMs) are backbones for robotic planning and manipulation. However, for coordinate prediction in grounding tasks, existing architectures suffer from two distinct bottlenecks: discrete tokenization disrupts spatial continuity with high decoding latency, while standard point regression fails on multi-contact affordances and irregularly shaped targets. To resolve these challenges, we propose PhysMoE, introducing three core contributions: (1) sharing lower layers for multimodal context while routing upper layers to specialized spatial (pixel) and linguistic (text) experts by physical modality, eliminating gradient interference; (2) encoding each coordinate as a single token by reusing the visual encoder's learned positional embeddings, injecting 2D spatial priors to natively align coordinates with visual features and ease training; and (3) differentiable Signed Distance Field (SDF) supervision on pointing, accommodating affordances and irregular geometries without costly mask decoders. Compared to the base model, PhysMoE achieves a 15.7% average gain and an 8.31x throughput speedup across 9 coordinate-based grounding benchmarks (trajectory, affordance, and localization), while preserving multimodal understanding and reasoning across 11 text-based embodied benchmarks.

</details>


### [28] [PhyRubric: Rubric-Grounded Diagnostic Evaluation of Physical Commonsense in Text-to-Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13576)
*Huiyi Chen,Weitai Kang,Shicheng Fan,Xinshuo Wang,Zhixiang Shen,Yan Yan,Philip S. Yu,Baoxin Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 PhyRubric——一个基于评分准则(rubric)的诊断式框架，用于评估文生视频(T2V)模型的物理常识；并构建 PhyRubric-Bench(5 大物理域、34 类)、PhyRubric-Train(3,788 视频)与开源评判模型 PhyRubric-Judge。十个 T2V 模型的实验显示物理常识远未解决，且 PhyRubric-Judge 与人类判断的相关性优于现有开源评判器并可与 Gemini 4 Argon 持平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: T2V 模型日益被定位为"世界模拟器"，因此需评估其视频是否符合物理常识。现有基准由标注者对每个视频凭直觉给单一分数，结果主观，且无法指出违反了哪条物理原理。

Method: 提出 rubric 驱动的诊断框架 PhyRubric：在观看任何视频之前，先针对每条 caption 构建专属 rubric，将 caption 分解为语义项与物理项；分数由基于带时间戳证据的条目级判定推导得出。实例化为跨 5 个物理域、34 个类别的 PhyRubric-Bench，并扩展为含 3,788 个视频的 PhyRubric-Train，用于微调开源评判模型 PhyRubric-Judge。

Result: 在十个 T2V 模型上的实验表明物理常识仍远未解决；PhyRubric-Judge 与人类判断的相关性高于现有开源评判器，并与前沿专有模型 Gemini 4 Argon 相当。进一步分析为 T2V 模型提供了可操作的改进启示。

Conclusion: 以 rubric 为核心的条目级、证据化评估可替代直觉式单一打分，为 T2V 物理常识提供更可解释、更贴近人类判断的诊断手段。

Abstract: As text-to-video (T2V) models are increasingly positioned as world simulators, it is essential to evaluate whether their videos conform to physical commonsense. However, existing benchmarks rely on annotators who rate each video with a single score based on intuition, yielding ratings that are subjective and do not tell which physical principle is violated. We introduce PhyRubric, a rubric-grounded diagnostic framework for evaluating physical commonsense in T2V generation. For each caption, PhyRubric constructs a caption-specific rubric before any video is seen, decomposing the caption into semantic and physical items, and derives scores from item-level verdicts grounded in timestamped evidence. We instantiate this framework as PhyRubric-Bench, which spans 34 categories across five physical domains, and scale it up to PhyRubric-Train with 3,788 videos, on which we fine-tune the open-source PhyRubric-Judge. Empirical results across ten T2V models show that physical commonsense remains far from solved, and that PhyRubric-Judge correlates more closely with human judgments than existing open-source judges while matching the frontier proprietary Gemini 4 Argon. Further analysis provides actionable insights for T2V models.

</details>


### [29] [Medical Image Segmentation: Foundation Model Adaptation versus Task-Specific Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14519)
*Emre Kutlusan,Yongmin Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 将DINOv2基础模型以从仅调偏置到全量微调等多种策略适配于结肠镜、乳腺超声和皮肤镜分割任务，并在跨机构外部数据上比较其与从头训练的U-Net/ResU-Net的泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型被认为可替代任务专用模型，但现有比较多局限于训练分布内数据、且仅采用配置缺乏论证的单一任务基线，难以说明新临床站点上的表现；同时"需适配多少容量、由什么决定"尚不清楚。

Method: 以DINOv2为基础模型，采用从仅微调偏置项到全参数微调的连续光谱策略进行适配；与从头训练的U-Net和ResU-Net对比；覆盖结肠镜、乳腺超声、皮肤镜三个域；在训练来源的留出数据和来自其他机构的独立数据集上测试；并核查基准数据的重复性。

Result: 适配后的基础模型在所有外部数据集上泛化更优；在训练分布内，数据充足时任务专用模型仍具竞争力且边界勾画更精确，而基础模型更易漏检小病灶；仅需适配基础模型的一小部分参数，所需容量由成像域而非适配方法决定；发现一个广泛使用的息肉测试集与其他基准存在图像重复。

Conclusion: 在模型需跨站点工作时应优先采用基础模型适配，并按目标域选择适配容量；域内高数据量场景下任务专用模型仍有价值。

Abstract: Foundation models pretrained on large natural-image collections are increasingly adapted for medical image segmentation, in the expectation that they will replace models trained for each task. Most comparisons, however, use data from the training distribution and a single task-specific baseline whose configuration is rarely justified, and therefore provide limited evidence of performance at new clinical sites. It also remains unclear how much of a foundation model must be adapted, and what determines this. We adapted a foundation model, DINOv2, with strategies ranging from tuning only its bias terms to full fine-tuning, and compared it with U-Nets and ResU-Nets trained from scratch. Experiments covered colonoscopy, breast ultrasound and dermoscopy, with testing on held-out data from the training source and on independent datasets from other institutions. The adapted foundation model generalised better on every external dataset. Within the training distribution, the task-specific models remained competitive when training data were abundant, and delineated boundaries more precisely, while the foundation model more often missed small lesions. Only a small fraction of the foundation model required adaptation, and the capacity needed was determined by the imaging domain rather than by the adaptation method. A widely used polyp test set was also found to duplicate images from another benchmark. Foundation-model adaptation is therefore warranted where models must work across sites, with capacity selected on the target domain.

</details>


### [30] [Selective Surgical Action-Center Localization:Retaining Localization Gains through Supervised Scoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12977)
*Yanchong Xue,Wanting Zhang,Lingtao Yu,Guochao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出“选择性定位”流程：热图+坐标监督定位器配合逻辑回归评分器，在混合有无可报告中心的帧中判别可报告且误差在容差内的预测；以每100输入保留的正确输出数衡量改进，优于基线定位器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手术动作中心定位既要给出准确参考点，也要决定是否报告。当含可报告中心与不含的帧混合时，定位精度提升能否转化为实际收益，取决于哪些正确候选能在选择环节被保留；仅看定位误差指标无法反映这一点。

Method: 将热图监督与坐标监督结合的定位器，外加逻辑回归评分器，学习识别“存在可报告中心且定位误差在给定容差内”的预测。内部评估使用来自50个手术来源的5000帧，采用5折按来源分组交叉验证、2个随机种子；进行定位器与评分方式的匹配对照，追踪正确候选及其被拒情况。

Result: 在每来源80%接受配额、容差为图像对角线0.10倍的设置下，本方法每100个输入保留46.08个正确输出，同评分基线定位器为44.20，增益1.88（来源配对bootstrap描述性95%区间0.94–2.79）。基于熵的选择会因正确预测被拒增多而抵消其供给优势；对坐标监督定位器，监督评分将正确预测被拒数从每100输入6.15降至2.99。

Conclusion: 评估“最终保留的正确输出”才能判断选择规则是保留还是侵蚀了定位改进；监督评分优于熵式选择，选择性定位流程在混合帧场景下更有效。

Abstract: Surgical action-center localization requires both an accurate reference point and a decision about whether to report it. When frames with and without a reportable center are mixed, the benefit of improved localization depends on which correct candidates survive selection. We present a selective localization pipeline combining heatmap and coordinate supervision with a logistic-regression scorer. The scorer learns to identify predictions with a reportable center and a localization error within a specified tolerance. The internal evaluation uses 5,000 frames from 50 surgical sources, five source-grouped folds and two random seeds. Matched locator and scoring comparisons track correct candidates and their rejection. At an 80% acceptance quota per source and a tolerance of 0.10 times the image diagonal, our method retains 46.08 correct outputs per 100 inputs. The baseline locator with the same scoring approach retains 44.20. The gain is 1.88 (descriptive 95% source-paired bootstrap interval, 0.94 to 2.79). Under entropy-based selection, increased rejection of correct predictions offsets the higher supply of correct predictions on average. For the coordinate-supervised locator, supervised scoring reduces these rejections from 6.15 to 2.99 per 100 inputs. Evaluating retained correct outputs reveals whether a selection rule preserves or erodes an improvement in localization.

</details>


### [31] [Adaptive Residual Quantization for Memory-Efficient Temporal Action Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13262)
*Gerard Donahue,Guven Gergerli,Ayush Gupta,Reza Ghoddoosian,Enna Sachdeva,Faizan Siddiqui*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ARQ-VAE：对帧级视频特征做离散残差量化并按帧自适应截断残差链，得到无标签、紧凑的特征记忆表示，在TAS及增量/无监督设定下大幅改善存储-性能权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TAS依赖大规模预提取帧特征，长视频下存储与回放成本高；增量TAS还需保留旧任务数据以防灾难性遗忘。既有replay与condensation方法依赖稠密帧级标注，并用固定段级结构分配存储，对帧复杂度随时间变化的长流程视频缺乏灵活性。

Method: ARQ-VAE学习帧级特征的离散残差量化，并对每帧自适应截断残差链，仅保留最能重建该帧特征的残差前缀；辅以轻量微调阶段提升实际保留截断码的重建质量；表示不依赖帧级标签，同一存储可支持多种TAS设定。

Result: 在标准TAS基准上优于既有replay与condensation基线。Breakfast上训练集存储由原始I3D特征库的28 GB降至10 MB且保持强分割性能；配合ASFormer，监督设定下达71.5 Edit、51.2 F1@50；同一无标签记忆在增量与无监督TAS中依然有效。

Conclusion: ARQ-VAE可作为TAS的实用记忆表示，并为更广泛的数据集condensation应用提供有前景的方向。

Abstract: Temporal action segmentation (TAS) is commonly trained on large banks of pre-extracted frame features, but storing and replaying those features becomes a practical bottleneck for long-form video, especially in incremental TAS where past-task data must remain available to mitigate catastrophic forgetting. Recent TAS replay and condensation methods reduce this burden, but they rely on dense frame-wise annotations and allocate storage through fixed segment-level structures, limiting their flexibility for long procedural videos whose frame complexity varies substantially over time. We introduce Adaptive Residual-Quantized Variational AutoEncoder (ARQ-VAE), a compact and label-free memory representation for TAS features that learns a discrete residual quantization of frame-level video features and then adaptively truncates the residual chain on a per-frame basis, retaining only the residual prefix that best reconstructs each frame feature. This yields a practical compact feature memory that does not depend on frame-wise labels and can therefore support multiple TAS settings with the same stored representation, while a lightweight fine-tuning stage further improves reconstruction quality for the truncated codes that are actually retained. Across standard TAS benchmarks, ARQ-VAE achieves a substantially improved storage-performance trade-off over prior replay and condensation baselines. On Breakfast, our final representation reduces training-set storage from 28 GB for the original I3D feature bank to 10 MB while retaining strong downstream segmentation performance; with ASFormer, it achieves 71.5 Edit and 51.2 F1@50 in the supervised setting. We further show that the same label-free memory remains effective for incremental and unsupervised TAS. We position ARQ-VAE as a practical memory representation for TAS and a promising direction for broader dataset condensation applications.

</details>


### [32] [Attribute-Evidence Learning with Multimodal Prompt for Generalized Category Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12975)
*Wei Zhang,Zhu Teng,Zhixiang Chi,Junnan Zou,Wenxin Luo,Linfeng Ye,Jianping Fan,Konstantions N. Plataniotis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 AptM-GCD 多模态框架，通过属性证据学习（AEL）与多模态提示学习（MPL）将文本属性作为局部视觉证据而非全局类别描述，在六个数据集上取得细粒度与通用 GCD 基准的 SoTA，并在单模态与类不平衡设定下保持稳定增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义类别发现（GCD）仅依赖视觉线索，忽略了人类感知的多模态性；现有引入类别标签与属性的方法将全局标签与局部属性纠缠在统一的全局图像表示中，导致属性不可靠并产生误导性线索，驱动模型错误预测。

Method: 1) Attribute-Evidence Learning (AEL)：将属性视为局部视觉证据而非全局类别描述符，通过属性嵌入与 patch 级视觉 token 交互，定位可靠的属性相关区域并提取判别性局部证据，抑制噪声/无关属性线索；2) Multimodal Prompt Learning (MPL)：将细粒度文本证据转化为视觉提示并注入多模态提示 Transformer，通过上下文推理增强类别判别，而不强制模态间全局对齐。

Result: 在六个数据集的细粒度与通用 GCD 基准上均一致达到 SoTA；在单模态与类不平衡 GCD 设定下也表现出一致增益，具备较强泛化能力。

Conclusion: 以"属性作为局部证据 + 提示化推理"替代全局跨模态对齐，可有效缓解属性噪声并提升新旧类别发现性能，为多模态 GCD 提供了可靠且通用的范式。

Abstract: Generalized category discovery (GCD) aims to discover novel categories from unlabeled data by leveraging limited labeled samples from old classes. Existing methods that rely solely on visual cues often neglect the multimodal nature of human perception, prompting recent approaches to incorporate textual information such as category tags and attributes. However, these approaches typically entangle global tags and local attributes within a shared global image representation, rendering attributes unreliable and potentially providing misleading cues that can drive the model toward incorrect predictions. To this end, we propose AptM-GCD, a novel multimodal framework that exploits apt textual guidance through attribute evidence learning and multimodal prompt reasoning. Specifically, we design an Attribute-Evidence Learning (AEL) mechanism that treats attributes as localized visual evidence rather than global category descriptors. By interacting attribute embeddings with patch-level visual tokens, AEL identifies reliable attribute-related regions and extracts discriminative local evidence, thereby reducing the impact of noisy or irrelevant attribute cues. Building upon the extracted evidence, we further develop a Multimodal Prompt Learning (MPL) mechanism to bridge local attribute semantics and global visual understanding. MPL converts fine-grained textual evidence into visual prompts and injects them into a multimodal prompt transformer, enabling attribute information to enhance category discrimination through contextual reasoning without enforcing direct global alignment between modalities. Extensive experiments on six datasets demonstrate that AptM-GCD consistently achieves SoTA performance across both fine-grained and generic benchmarks. Moreover, the proposed framework exhibits strong generalization ability, delivering consistent gains under unimodal and class-imbalanced GCD settings.

</details>


### [33] [URL-Sim: Unified and Regulated LiDAR Simulation for High-Fidelity Reconstruction of Dynamic Driving Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12932)
*Kefei Qian,Xipeng Feng,Jie Li,Long Cao,Yuhan Chen,Xiangfei Huang,Guofa Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 URL-Sim，一个统一且受调控的动态 LiDAR 仿真框架，通过动态高斯场景图解耦异质交通参与者建模，并用多维度重要性的受调控高斯稠密化策略控制图元数量，在 Waymo 与 KITTI-360 上以相当图元预算取得更高精度与视觉质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实驾驶日志重建高保真 LiDAR 仿真场景对自动驾驶低成本安全验证至关重要。NeRF 类仿真器渲染慢，难大规模部署；现有 3DGS 方法多局限于刚性车辆，且无约束稠密化产生冗余图元。

Method: 构建动态高斯场景图，解耦车辆、行人、骑行者等交通参与者的建模，在单一框架内重建异质动态对象；引入受调控高斯稠密化策略，以多维度重要性评分对图元排序，将高斯分配到最关键区域并保持图元总数有界。

Result: 在 Waymo 和 KITTI-360 上，于可比图元预算下，定量精度与视觉质量优于现有基线，并能准确恢复多样化动态交通参与者的几何与位姿。

Conclusion: URL-Sim 为动态 LiDAR 仿真提供统一、图元受控的 3DGS 方案，兼顾渲染效率与重建保真度，扩展了对异质动态对象的支持。

Abstract: Reconstructing high-fidelity LiDAR simulation scenes from real-world driving logs is essential for cost-effective safety validation of autonomous driving systems. While simulators based on Neural Radiance Fields (NeRF) achieve realistic scene reconstruction, their large-scale adoption is limited by slow rendering speeds. Recent 3D Gaussian splatting (3DGS) approaches have improved rendering efficiency through explicit primitive representation and differentiable rasterization. However, existing 3DGS-based methods remain largely restricted to rigid vehicles and produce redundant primitives through unconstrained densification. To address these limitations, we propose URL-Sim, a unified and regulated dynamic LiDAR simulation framework. URL-Sim constructs a dynamic Gaussian scene graph that decouples the modeling of traffic participants, including vehicles, pedestrians, and cyclists, enabling accurate reconstruction of heterogeneous dynamic objects within a single framework. It further introduces a regulated Gaussian densification strategy that ranks primitives with multi-dimensional importance scores and allocates Gaussians to the most critical regions while keeping the total primitive count bounded. Experiments on the Waymo and KITTI-360 datasets demonstrate that URL-Sim achieves higher quantitative accuracy and visual quality than existing baselines under comparable primitive budgets, and accurately recovers the geometry and poses of diverse dynamic traffic participants.

</details>


### [34] [Where Do Vision-Language Models Get Left and Right? Position-Code Counterfactuals in Qwen2.5-VL, Qwen3-VL and InternVL3](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14060)
*Enhao Cui,Junzhi Yang,Yaoshun Lin,Yuhan Feng,Zijian Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文通过"仅镜像ViT位置编码"的充分性实验，证明视觉Transformer的宽度位置编码是VLM左右方向判断的主要来源，而语言模型侧的token位置/顺序作用微弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
