Motivation: VLM判断图像左右方向可能依赖三种线索：patch内图像内容的朝向、ViT的位置编码、LLM中的token位置与顺序。此前消融仅证明ViT编码"必要"，其"充分性"及各线索的相对贡献尚不清楚。

Method: 在Qwen2.5-VL-7B/3B、Qwen3-VL-4B、InternVL3-2B上做受控消融：(1)仅镜像ViT宽度位置编码（图像内容不变）；(2)仅镜像LLM侧id与token顺序而保留ViT编码；(3)半网格平移；(4)在Qwen2.5-VL-7B中定位到全局注意力ViT块（尤其最后一层）；(5)What'sUp上分别翻转内容+token顺序与位置编码；(6)事后分析：CV-Bench上对已定位对象做随机排列、检验随机排列命中镜像关系的比例、以及错误中"同一关系被赋予两对象"的比例。

Result: 仅镜像ViT宽度位置即可在CV-Bench左右题上复现翻转图像答案的比例达84.3–93.2%（Qwen2.5-VL-3B为87.5%，部分源于left回退）；两选项设置下同样计为干扰；半网格平移也翻转多数答案。ViT编码完好时，LLM侧id/顺序镜像在三系列中几乎不改变左右答案（Qwen2.5-VL的id检验较弱）。效应集中于全局注意力ViT块，主要在最后一层。What'sUp上，翻转内容+token顺序（而非位置编码）比左右题更能保持on/under答案。

Conclusion: 左右方向判断主要由ViT位置编码驱动，而非语言模型中的token顺序；但事后分析对结论有所限定——随机排列在反转对象顺序时多翻转18.9–31.0个百分点的答案，随机排列命中镜像关系的比例多低于随机水平，且What'sUp上"支撑关系优于左右关系"仅在InternVL3-2B中清晰。多数残余错误对两对象给出同一关系，提示修复应针对关系绑定、对象定位或参照对象的使用。

Abstract: A vision-language model may take left-right direction from the orientation of image content within patches, its vision transformer's (ViT) position code, or token positions and order in its language model (LLM). Ablations show the ViT code is necessary; we test sufficiency by mirroring only a position code. In Qwen2.5-VL-7B, Qwen3-VL-4B and InternVL3-2B, mirroring ViT width positions reproduces the flipped-image answer on 84.3-93.2% of CV-Bench left-right questions (Qwen2.5-VL-3B: 87.5%, partly a left fallback). With two options, this also counts disruption; a half-grid shift flips most answers too. With the ViT code intact, LLM-side id and token-order mirrors change few left-right answers in all three series (a weak id test in Qwen2.5-VL). In Qwen2.5-VL-7B the effect sits in the global-attention ViT blocks, mostly the last. On What'sUp, flipping content and token order, not the position code, keeps more on/under than left-right answers at the flipped-image answer. Post hoc analyses qualify these results. On CV-Bench items with located objects, random permutations flip 18.9-31.0 points more answers when reversing the objects' order than when keeping it. On What'sUp, in Qwen2.5-VL and Qwen3-VL, the share of the left-right changes made by random permutations that land on the mirror relation is mostly below chance. Counted as returns to the original answer, the What'sUp contrast of support with left-right relations is clear only in InternVL3-2B. Most remaining errors give the same relation whichever object is asked about, suggesting that repair may target relation binding, object localization or use of the reference object.

</details>


### [35] [HiPFuse: Real-Time LiDAR Semantic Segmentation for Robotic Perception on Edge Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14025)
*Haiyang Wu,George Vosselman,Ville Lehtola*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HiPFuse是一个点-BEV融合框架，通过跨水平尺度交换点特征与高度划分的BEV特征，在有限资源下实现实时精确的LiDAR语义分割，并在Jetson AGX Orin上超过10Hz扫描率，同时提升下游里程计与回环检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时机器人感知需要在有限资源下获得准确的LiDAR语义。BEV聚合效率高，但在垂直列内会混合标签不同的表面，造成语义混淆。

Method: 提出点-BEV融合框架HiPFuse：在水平尺度间交换点特征与高度划分的BEV特征，融合点级细节与2D空间上下文；采用固定高度分配以保持分离的垂直聚合组，并用同一3D体素目标对初始BEV特征进行辅助监督；引入双尺度BEV上下文模块，从更粗网格补充更广上下文。

Result: 在SemanticKITTI和MCD上分别达到63.7%和62.5% mIoU，在Jetson AGX Orin上分别达14.2和22.4 FPS，超过10Hz扫描率；室内适用性经独立训练评估验证；仅替换基线语义前端即将里程计ATE RMSE平均降低10.6%，SemanticKITTI上接受回环召回率提升8.7个百分点；定性语义图显示道路标注更连续。

Conclusion: HiPFuse在资源受限平台上兼顾精度与实时性，并可即插即用地提升固定机器人系统的里程计与回环性能，具有良好的室内外适用性与工程价值。

Abstract: Real-time robotic perception requires accurate LiDAR semantics under limited resources. Bird's-eye-view (BEV) aggregation is efficient but can mix differently labeled surfaces in columns. We present HiPFuse, a point-BEV fusion framework that exchanges point and height-partitioned BEV features across horizontal scales, combining point-wise detail with 2D spatial context from one LiDAR scan. Fixed height assignments preserve separate vertical aggregation groups, with auxiliary supervision of initial BEV features using targets from the same 3D cells. A dual-scale BEV context module adds broader context from a coarser grid. On SemanticKITTI and MCD, HiPFuse achieves 63.7% and 62.5% mIoU at 14.2 and 22.4 FPS on Jetson AGX Orin, respectively, exceeding the 10 Hz scan rate. Separate training and evaluation show indoor applicability. Replacing only the baseline semantic front end with HiPFuse in fixed robotic systems reduces odometry absolute trajectory error RMSE by 10.6% on average across two outdoor sequences and increases accepted-loop recall by 8.7 percentage points on SemanticKITTI. Qualitative semantic maps show more continuous road labeling. Our code is available at https://github.com/haiyang2022/HiPFuse.

</details>


### [36] [Pulse or Provenance? What rPPG-Based Deepfake Detectors Learn](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13992)
*Othmane Harraq,Tamer Aldwairi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文系统性地检验了基于rPPG（远程光电容积描记）线索的Deepfake检测器，发现其高判别力并非源于真实生理信号，而主要来自合成过程引入的高频伪迹与视频源域偏差；同时揭示了Celeb-DF/Celeb-DF++官方数据划分存在身份与源视频泄漏，会显著虚高弱检测器性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: rPPG作为Deepfake检测的生理线索被广泛采用，其前提假设是"合成人脸缺乏真实心脏信号"。作者质疑这一前提，希望厘清rPPG式检测器在Celeb-DF++（22种伪造方法）与FF++上究竟学到了什么，并检验现有评测协议是否存在数据泄漏导致性能虚高。

Method: 1）在Celeb-DF++与FF++上，对每个真实/伪造视频采样一个完全相同的窗口做受控对比；2）比较简单色度轨迹与专用rPPG提取器的AUC；3）消融实验：背景区域提取、无人脸检测的全帧提取、频域分析（0.7–4 Hz心率带之外）、帧顺序打乱、分辨率/帧率/编码归一化、独立重实现复现；4）高频内容的删除与注入实验、合成脉搏注入实验；5）双生成器下的自重现（self-reenactment）受控实验，区分"单帧驱动动画"与"逐帧源重建"；6）跨数据集迁移测试（Celeb-DF++训练→FF++测试）及CLIP检测器对比、CLIP与rPPG/频域特征融合对比；7）审计Celeb-DF与Celeb-DF++官方划分的身份与源视频重叠情况，并用自定义协议量化泄漏带来的AUC膨胀。

Result: 在说话人脸伪造上，简单色度轨迹AUC达0.998，高于专用rPPG提取器的0.87；该信号在背景区域或无检测全帧上同样具有判别力，且集中于0.7–4 Hz心率带以上；打乱帧序后信号基本消失，而归一化分辨率、帧率与编码后仍存在；删除高频使多数合成视频被判为真，添加高频使真实视频被判为假，注入合成脉搏影响甚微。自重现实验中，单帧驱动动画会引入相同高频信号，逐帧源重建则不会。基于人脸色度、在Celeb-DF++上训练的检测器将超过99%的真实FF++视频判为伪造，说明其编码的是视频源而非篡改痕迹；CLIP检测器表现一致；冻结CLIP骨干即可匹敌或超过CLIP与rPPG、频域特征的融合。官方划分在训练集与测试集间共享身份与源视频内容，复用测试视频与身份的自定义协议可使弱检测器AUC虚高至0.30。

Conclusion: rPPG式Deepfake检测器所依赖的高判别特征并非生理来源，而是生成管线引入的高频伪迹与数据源域捷径；现有基准的高AUC在很大程度上被数据泄漏与域偏差所放大。因此需重新审视rPPG作为Deepfake取证依据的有效性，并采用无身份/源内容重叠的严格协议与更审慎的基线（如冻结CLIP）进行评估。

Abstract: Remote photoplethysmography (rPPG) has been proposed as a physiological cue for deepfake detection, based on the premise that synthesized faces lack a genuine cardiac signal. We examine what rPPG-style detectors learn on Celeb-DF++ (22 forgery methods) and FaceForensics++ (FF++), using one identically sampled window per real and fake video. On talking-face forgeries, simple chrominance traces reach an AUC of 0.998, exceeding a dedicated rPPG extractor (0.87). Controlled experiments indicate that this signal is not physiological in origin. On fully synthesized forgeries, it is equally discriminative when extracted from background regions or from full frames without face detection, and it is concentrated above the 0.7 to 4 Hz heart-rate band. It is largely removed by shuffling frame order but persists after normalizing resolution, frame rate and encoding, and an independent reimplementation reproduces these findings. Removing high-frequency content makes most synthesized videos appear real, adding it makes real videos appear fake, and injecting a synthetic pulse has little effect. In controlled self-reenactment experiments with two generators, animating a face from a single frame introduces the same high-frequency signal, whereas reconstructing each frame from its own source does not. Face-based chrominance detectors trained on Celeb-DF++ classify over 99% of real FF++ videos as fake, indicating that they largely encode video source rather than manipulation, and a CLIP-based detector exhibits the same behavior. A frozen CLIP backbone matches or exceeds fusions of CLIP with rPPG and frequency features. Finally, we show that the official Celeb-DF and Celeb-DF++ splits share identities and source-video content between training and test, and that custom protocols reusing test videos and identities in training inflate weaker detectors by up to 0.30 AUC.

</details>


### [37] [DroneSegEdit: Adapting Pretrained Generative Editors for UAV Segmentation with Language-Grounded Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13890)
*Dehua Chen,Long Xin,Jiangtao Wei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 DroneSegEdit，将 RGB 提案渲染与语言引导的提案约束验证相结合，解决掩码渲染与指代目标选择之间的“Grounding-Rendering Gap”，在 RIS-LAD 达到 52.78 mIoU 的新 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练图像生成器虽可用于多种视觉任务，但直接用于语言引导的无人机分割时存在关键缺陷：生成的合理掩码可能对应错误实例，尤其在小目标与重复目标场景中。作者将这种“掩码渲染”与“表达式级目标选择”之间的错配定义为 Grounding-Rendering Gap。

Method: 基于开放权重 LongCat-Image-Edit 模型，用任务特定 LoRA 与固定颜色到标签的解码进行适配，实现 RGB 提案渲染。指代分割中引入 Language-Grounded Spatial Verifier (LGSV)，从冻结的多模态大语言模型骨干读取层级状态，推导表达式条件化的空间证据，并将其映射回渲染提案内部，不引入提案外的前景。另设无验证器实验，用任务适配器从单一任务级指令联合渲染全类别，无需枚举类名或调色板颜色。

Result: RIS-LAD 上达到新 SOTA：52.78 mIoU，较此前最佳提升 7.16 点；在 UAVid-RIS 与 VDD-RIS 上整体保持竞争力，在紧凑且应用相关的类别（尤其车辆与行人）上取得最明显的类别级增益。

Conclusion: 以“渲染+提案约束验证”的解耦范式可有效弥合 Grounding-Rendering Gap，兼顾几何渲染质量与语言级目标选择；该 RGB 接口还可扩展至无需枚举类别的全场景解析任务。

Abstract: Recent work has shown that pretrained image generators can support diverse visual tasks. Yet directly applying them to language-guided unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) segmentation exposes a key limitation: a plausible mask may correspond to the wrong instance among small and repeated objects. We call this mismatch between mask rendering and expression-level target selection the Grounding-Rendering Gap. We present DroneSegEdit, which combines RGB proposal rendering with language-grounded, proposal-constrained verification to select supported foreground while retaining rendered geometry. DroneSegEdit adapts the open-weight LongCat-Image-Edit model using task-specific LoRA and fixed color-to-label decoding. For referring segmentation, a Language-Grounded Spatial Verifier (LGSV) reads hierarchical states from the frozen multimodal large language model backbone to derive expression-conditioned spatial evidence, which is mapped back to the rendered proposal without introducing foreground outside it. On RIS-LAD, DroneSegEdit reaches a new state of the art at 52.78 mIoU, 7.16 points above the previous best. It remains competitive overall on UAVid-RIS and VDD-RIS, with its clearest class-wise gains on compact, application-relevant categories, notably vehicles and humans. A separate verifier-free experiment extends the RGB interface to full-scene parsing: a task-specific adapter jointly renders all classes from one task-level instruction without enumerating class names or palette colors.

</details>


### [38] [Rethinking Evaluation for Video Capsule Endoscopy: Validation Protocol, Source-Video Diversity, and Cross-Dataset Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13245)
*Smriti Regmi,Debesh Jha*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DMSNet（可变形自适应多尺度边缘引导注意力网络），并在源视频级划分与外部数据集下系统评估VCE分类模型的可靠性，揭示评估协议与视频多样性显著影响模型排序与性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动视频胶囊内镜（VCE）分类面临严重类别不平衡、源视频多样性不足、跨数据集体貌差异等挑战；已有基准上的深度模型表现虽好，但在不同验证协议与外部数据集偏移下的可靠性尚不明确。

Method: 构建DMSNet，融合自适应多尺度特征融合、可变形病灶细化、边缘引导注意力与稀疏特征选择；在Kvasir-Capsule上与18种代表性CNN、Transformer及预训练视觉架构对比，采用随机帧级与源视频不相交两种验证协议；识别原始训练/测试划分中的源视频重叠，并在完整测试集与源视频不相交子集上评估；在CV2024外部数据集上测试；以每类上限200帧固定训练规模，考察源视频覆盖数（4→8）的影响。

Result: 随机帧级验证下，DMSNet在完整测试集MCC为0.442±0.020，在源视频不相交子集为0.456±0.037；外部CV2024上最优为ConvNeXt-Tiny，MCC仅0.207，各架构性能较域内评估大幅下降；训练集总规模固定时，将最大源视频覆盖数从4提升至8，使19种架构中17种的MCC提升。

Conclusion: 模型排序与分类性能显著依赖评估协议、训练源视频多样性与跨数据集偏移，VCE分类系统亟需源视频感知的验证方案与严格的外部评估。

Abstract: Automated video capsule endoscopy (VCE) classification remains challenging due to severe class imbalance, limited source-video diversity, and variations in image appearance across datasets. Although deep learning models have demonstrated promising performance on established benchmarks, their reliability under different validation protocols and external dataset shift remains insufficiently understood. In this work, we introduce DMSNet, a deformable adaptive multi-scale edge-guided attention network integrating adaptive multi-scale feature fusion, deformable lesion refinement, edge-guided attention, and sparse feature selection. We benchmark DMSNet against 18 representative convolutional, transformer-based, and pretrained vision architectures on Kvasir-Capsule under random frame-level and source-video-disjoint validation protocols. We further identify source-video overlap between the original training and test splits and evaluate model performance on both the complete test set and a source-video-disjoint subset. Under random frame-level validation, DMSNet achieves an MCC of 0.442 $\pm$ 0.020 on complete test set and 0.456 $\pm$ 0.037 on the source-video-disjoint subset. On the external CV2024 dataset, ConvNeXt-Tiny achieves the highest MCC of 0.207, while performance degrades substantially across architectures relative to in-domain evaluation. We further investigate source-video diversity using a per-class cap of 200 training frames. With the total training-set size held fixed, increasing the maximum source-video coverage from four to eight improves MCC for 17 of 19 evaluated architectures. Overall, these findings demonstrate that model rankings and classification performance depend substantially on evaluation protocol, training-source diversity, and cross-dataset shift, emphasizing the need for source-aware validation and rigorous external evaluation of VCE classification systems.

</details>


### [39] [Comparative Evaluation of Deep Learning Models for Geographic Atrophy Segmentation in En Face OCT Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13191)
*Utkarsh Doshi,Adarsh Gadari,Sharat Chandra Vupparaboina,Joseph DeCicco,Giulia Gregori,Nicola Valsecchi,Nasiq Hasan,Elham Sadeghi,Josè-Alain Sahel,Jay Chhablani,Kiran Kumar Vupparaboina,Sandeep Chandra Bollepalli*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究系统比较了五种深度学习架构（Nested U-Net、ResU-Net、nnUNet、Swin-Unet、MedSegDiff）及阈值法基线在地图状萎缩（GA）en-face OCT图像分割中的表现，nnUNet在跨设备留出验证集上取得最高Dice（0.8882±0.0033），在小数据条件下表现最稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GA是晚期AMD的不可逆致盲病变，准确分割对早期诊断、病程监测和个性化治疗至关重要；但现有深度学习研究多在单一架构、单一设备数据集上评估，缺乏相对性能比较与跨设备鲁棒性证据。

Method: 基于269个OCT体数据（来自Cirrus与Spectralis两种设备）生成en-face OCT图像，经归一化与图像修复（inpainting）三步预处理；训练并系统对比五种分割架构（Nested U-Net、ResU-Net、nnUNet、Swin-Unet、MedSegDiff）及阈值法基线，在包含双设备样本的固定留出验证集上评估。

Result: nnUNet平均DSC最高，为0.8882±0.0033；ResU-Net为0.8453±0.0074，Nested U-Net为0.7932±0.0423，MedSegDiff扩散模型为0.7959±0.0242，Swin-Unet最低为0.6886±0.0294。

Conclusion: 在有限数据条件下，nnUNet框架对en-face OCT中GA分割的表现最可靠，在准确性、稳定性与效率之间取得最佳平衡，适于GA定量分析。

Abstract: Background: Geographic atrophy (GA) is an advanced form of age-related macular degeneration (AMD). It causes irreversible vision loss due to progressive degeneration of the retinal pigment epithelium (RPE) and photoreceptors. Accurate segmentation of GA in en-face image is crucial for early diagnosis, monitoring disease progression, and developing personalized treatment strategies. However, existing deep learning approaches are typically evaluated using single architectures and single-device datasets, limiting insight into relative performance and cross-device robustness. Methods: We trained and systematically compared five deep learning architectures for GA segmentation: Nested U-Net, ResU-Net, nnUnet, Swin-Unet and MedSegDiff model, together with a thresholding based classical baseline. The models were trained on a dataset of en-face OCT images derived from 269 OCT volumes from two different devices, following a three-step preprocessing technique involving normalization and inpainting to enhance the quality of the en-face images. Results: On the fixed held-out validation set comprising OCT scans from both Cirrus (R) (Carl Zeiss Meditec, Inc., USA) and Spectralis (R) (Heidelberg Engineering, Germany) devices, nnUnet achieved the highest mean Dice similarity coefficient (DSC) of 0.8882 +/- 0.0033. The other models yielded the following Dice scores: Nested U-Net (0.7932 +/- 0.0423), ResU-Net (0.8453 +/- 0.0074), Swin-Unet (0.6886 +/- 0.0294), and Diffusion model (0.7959 +/- 0.0242). Conclusions: nnUnet framework, demonstrated most reliable performance for GA segmentation in en-face OCT under limited data conditions. It offers the best balance of accuracy, stability, and efficiency for GA quantification.

</details>


### [40] [VIGOR: Benchmarking Visual Rationale Correctness in Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13150)
*Yanlin Chen,Tang Li,Xi Peng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VIGOR基准，用显式概念+像素级定位表示视觉推理依据，评估MLLM的输出是否由其声称的概念所对应的正确视觉证据支撑，而非仅看答案正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM评测通常只判断答案是否正确，但正确性无法揭示输出是否由正确的视觉证据支撑，即模型可能"答对但看错"。

Method: 将视觉推理依据表达为概念-像素定位对，覆盖低层视觉属性、中间结构、物体、部件及高层运动与关系等多抽象层级；构建可规模化的自动标注框架：复用已有密集监督，缺失的概念定位通过基于候选提案的标注加保守的多模态验证生成；评测时将模型对各表达概念导出的视觉证据与标注真值依据比对。

Result: 当前MLLM的视觉推理依据正确性有限，并在不同概念层级上表现出各异的定位失败模式。

Conclusion: VIGOR提供了基准与可扩展的数据构建流程，用于研究MLLM输出是否获得恰当视觉证据支撑；代码已开源。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are typically evaluated by whether they produce correct answers, but correctness alone does not reveal whether their outputs are supported by the right visual evidence. We introduce VIGOR, a benchmark for evaluating VIsually GrOunded Rationales in MLLMs. VIGOR represents visual rationales as explicit concepts paired with pixel-level groundings, covering multiple abstraction levels including low-level visual properties, intermediate structures, objects, parts and high-level motion, relations. To construct VIGOR at scale, we develop an automatic annotation framework that reuses existing dense supervision when available and generates missing concept groundings through proposal-based annotation followed by conservative multimodal verification. Using VIGOR, we evaluate whether MLLMs "look at what they say" by comparing model-derived visual evidence for each expressed concept against annotated ground-truth rationales. Our results show that current MLLMs exhibit limited visual rationale correctness and reveal distinct grounding failures across concept levels. VIGOR provides a benchmark and scalable data construction pipeline for studying whether MLLM outputs are supported by appropriate visual evidence. Code is available at https://github.com/deep-real/VIGOR

</details>


### [41] [When Reconstruction Helps Event-Camera Person Triggers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13488)
*Yifan Shuai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文比较了将事件相机异步亮度变化编码为通用视觉模型可读形式的多种方案（灰度累积、两种时间编码、两种强度重建器），并用配对真实数据评估其作为"录制触发器"的检测性能与代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件相机输出异步亮度变化，通用视觉模型无法直接处理；若要用通用模型做录制触发，必须先将事件表示为其可解释的形式，而不同表示方式在检测收益、准备成本与误触发之间的权衡尚不清楚。

Method: 在来自四段留出 DSEC 录制的 192 个窗口的配对真实事件数据上，对比直接灰度累积、两种时间编码与两种强度重建器（E2VID、FireNet+）；阈值仅在开发数据上校准；并在相同窗口上用 Qwen3-VL-4B 评估三种渲染方式，另做 96 秒探索性回放。

Result: E2VID 得到 52 个行人中的 40 个检测、140 个背景中 12 个误检；最强直接时间编码在可接受固定阈值下为 14 检测/7 误检；在 10% 误检率上限下 E2VID 为 40 检测，任一直接编码至多 19；FireNet+ 准备成本更低，冻结阈值下 21 检测、0 误检。Qwen3-VL-4B 上 E2VID 为 33/52、2/140，最高召回直接渲染为 9/52、9/140。

Conclusion: 在受控输入历史下，重建式表示较直接编码带来明显检测收益，但须与连续触发的成本及误启动代价区分评估；分段捕获与录制起点对阈值选择高度敏感。

Abstract: Event cameras record asynchronous changes in brightness. To use a general visual model as a recording trigger, a system must represent these measurements in a form the model can interpret. We compare direct grayscale accumulation, two temporal encodings, and two intensity reconstructors on paired real event data. On 192 windows from four held-out DSEC recordings, thresholds calibrated only on development data yielded 40 of 52 person detections and 12 of 140 background positives with E2VID. The strongest direct temporal encoding at an admissible fixed cutoff yielded 14 detections and seven background positives. A retrospective comparison at a common 10% background-positive cap yielded 40 detections for E2VID and at most 19 for any direct encoding. FireNet+ had lower preparation cost and produced 21 detections with zero observed background positives at its frozen cutoff. On the same 192 windows, Qwen3-VL-4B yielded 33/52, 2/140 with E2VID and 9/52, 9/140 with the highest-recall direct rendering, the three-bin image (person detections, background positives). A 96-second exploratory replay illustrates the sensitivity of episode capture and recording starts to cutoff selection. The experiments identify a detection benefit from reconstruction under controlled input history and distinguish it from the cost and false starts of continuous triggering.

</details>


### [42] [Hand-Object Contact Transition Detection in Egocentric Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13486)
*Huy Anh Nguyen,Chang Dong,Feras Dayoub,Minh Hoai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出统一的接触过渡检测框架，联合建模手与物体，定位"接触（touch）"与"释放（release）"两类时序边界，并发布ContactTransition数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自我中心视频中手-物体接触过渡（接触开始与释放）的时序定位尚缺专门方法；现有方案依赖外部物体/手-物体交互检测器，且需分别处理两类事件。

Method: 单一统一框架：在以手为中心的时空表征上融合直接从视频学习的物体感知特征；引入面向接触过渡的transition-aware event spotting建模，将两类边界作为事件检测问题联合求解；推理阶段不依赖外部物体或HOI检测器。同时构建ContactTransition数据集（4,021段自我中心视频，8,498条touch与8,077条release标注，含感兴趣物体监督）。

Result: 在两类过渡上均稳定优于强两阶段与端到端event-spotting基线；检测出的接触过渡可作为下游自我中心手-物体推理的有效时序锚点。

Conclusion: 统一的手-物体联合建模与过渡感知事件检测可有效定位接触与释放边界，无需外部检测器；所提数据集与过渡锚点对下游任务具实用价值。

Abstract: This paper tackles the task of hand-object contact transition detection in egocentric video, which aims to localize the two temporal boundaries of a contact episode: the moment a hand first establishes contact with an object (touch) and the moment contact is released (release). We propose a unified framework that jointly models the interacting hand and object to accurately localize both transition types. The proposed framework augments hand-centric spatiotemporal representations with object-aware features learned directly from the video and introduces a transition-aware event spotting formulation tailored to contact transitions, without relying on external object or hand-object interaction detectors during inference. To support this task, we present ContactTransition, a dataset comprising 4,021 egocentric videos with 8,498 touch and 8,077 release annotations, together with object-of-interest supervision. Experiments demonstrate that the proposed method consistently outperforms strong two-stage and end-to-end event-spotting baselines on both transition types. Finally, we show that detected contact transitions provide effective temporal anchors for downstream egocentric hand-object reasoning.

</details>


### [43] [What Factors Matter for Collection-Level Counting with Human-AI Collaboration?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14380)
*Seoyun Jeong,Logan Lawrence,Owen L. Martin,Benjamin Koger,Daniel Sheldon,Subhransu Maji*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文在统一的人类参与式计数框架下系统比较了模型、估计器与适应策略的选择，覆盖12个计数任务，并给出基于小规模试点集选择策略的实用指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多科学与政策决策需要估计大规模图像集合中目标对象的总数，但穷尽人工标注不可行，原始模型预测又存在偏差（尤其在域偏移下）。现有"人-AI"估计方法虽能结合不完美预测与有限人工核验得到可靠的集合级估计，却很少指导实践者在新部署任务中如何选择模型、估计器与适应策略。

Method: 构建统一的人类在环（human-in-the-loop）计数框架，在12个任务上开展对照研究，任务涵盖生物医学显微图像、地面与航拍野生动物调查、城市构筑物俯视影像；比较现代多模态模型与微调检测器，比较均匀采样与模型预测优先采样的人工标注单元选择方式，并评估用小规模试点集估计各模型排序质量（ranking quality）的可行性。

Result: 现代多模态模型为许多任务提供强且免训练的起点；在标注预算更大、重复部署场景中微调检测器更具优势。用模型预测优先选择人工标注单元可进一步提升估计效率。每个模型的排序质量可用小规模试点集估计，从而为新任务提供策略选择的实用指导。在航拍叉角羚调查的实地部署中，任务特定的方法适配在中位仅审阅3.2%采集图像后即达到调查±5%的容差要求。

Conclusion: 模型、估计器与适应策略的最优选择依赖于标注预算、部署次数与任务特性；基于试点集估计排序质量可为新计数任务提供可操作的选择依据，并在真实野外调查中验证了显著降低人工成本的有效性。

Abstract: Many scientific and policy decisions require estimating the total number of target objects across a large image collection. Exhaustive human labeling is often infeasible, while raw model predictions can be biased, especially under domain shift. Recent human-AI estimation methods combine imperfect model predictions with limited human verification to obtain reliable collection-level estimates. However, existing work provides little guidance on how practitioners should choose among models, estimators, and adaptation strategies for a new deployment. We study these choices within a common human-in-the-loop counting framework across 12 tasks spanning biomedical microscopy, ground-level and aerial wildlife surveys, and overhead imagery of urban structures. Our results show that modern multimodal models provide a strong, training-free starting point for many tasks, while fine-tuned detectors become more advantageous at larger labeling budgets and across repeated deployments. Estimation efficiency improves further when model predictions are used to prioritize which units humans label, rather than sampling uniformly. We also find that each model's ranking quality can be estimated with a small pilot set, yielding practical guidance for selecting strategies on new counting tasks. In a field deployment on aerial pronghorn surveys, a task-specific adaptation of these methods meets the survey's $\pm 5\%$ tolerance after reviewing a median of only $3.2\%$ of collected images.

</details>


### [44] [From Video Frames to Robot Meta-Frames: A Unified Latent Interface for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14365)
*Guoqiang Liang,Yiqi Lin,Yanzhe Chen,Kevin Yuchen Ma,Mike Zheng Shou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 MetaWAM，将机器人信号注入视频扩散模型视为分布对齐问题，用频域打包把多路信号折叠进单一统一元帧，在 RoboCasa 达 68.9% 成功率，推理延迟降低 39%，真实 Franka 任务达 85.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: World Action Models 需把低维机器人信号（本体感知、动作、价值）注入由自然视频塑造的预训练视频扩散模型隐空间，而现有方案将"注入接口"视为格式细节——每路信号占用一个独立隐帧，或额外挂接专用编解码器，破坏模型先验并增加开销。作者主张信号注入本质是分布对齐问题：表征需在去噪过程中匹配预训练 VDM 的先验。

Method: 以冻结 VDM 去噪器的相对重建误差作为"先验距离"探针，诊断发现常规空间编码偏离先验很远，而频域打包得到的隐表征被去噪器修正的幅度显著更小。据此提出 MetaWAM：将每路机器人信号分配到互不重叠的频域通道频带，经逆 DCT 提升至空间隐表征，把所有信号折叠进单个统一 meta-frame，无需新增模块或改动架构；固定频带布局配合 slot-aware routing，使同一 meta-frame 在共享机器人变量上同时支持策略、世界模型与价值预测。

Result: RoboCasa 基准上成功率 68.9%，将逐信号多帧压缩为一帧，推理延迟降低 39%；三项真实世界 Franka 任务平均成功率 85.0%。

Conclusion: 信号注入的接口选择应由分布对齐准则决定而非格式化偏好；频域通道分带是匹配视频扩散先验的高效、可解码接口，可统一承载多类机器人信号并支撑多任务头预测，兼具性能与效率优势。

Abstract: Pretrained video diffusion models (VDMs) offer a powerful foundation for World Action Models (WAMs), which jointly predict robot actions and future observations. Building such models requires injecting low-dimensional robot signals (e.g. proprioception, actions, and values) into a latent space shaped by natural videos, and a key design choice is the interface through which these signals enter: existing schemes treat it as a formatting detail, spending a separate latent frame on each signal or bolting on dedicated encoders and decoders. We argue that signal injection is instead a distribution-alignment problem: how well a representation matches the prior of the pretrained VDMs during denoising. Using the relative reconstruction error of a frozen VDM denoiser as a probe, we find that conventional spatial encodings sit far off this prior, whereas frequency-domain packing yields latents that the denoiser corrects far less. Motivated by this diagnostic result, we propose MetaWAM, which folds all robot signals into a single unified meta-frame: each signal is assigned to a disjoint frequency-domain channel band and lifted to the spatial latent by an inverse DCT, yielding a decodable interface aligned with the pretrained VDM prior without any added module or architectural change. A fixed band layout together with slot-aware routing lets the same meta-frame support policy, world-model, and value prediction over a shared set of robot variables. On RoboCasa, MetaWAM reaches 68.9% success while collapsing the per-signal frames into one and cutting inference latency by 39%, and attains 85.0% success on three real-world Franka tasks. Code and models are available at https://github.com/showlab/MetaWAM

</details>


### [45] [UniFlow-RS: Unified Generative Prediction for Heterogeneous Remote-Sensing Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14281)
*Jianlong He,Liang Liao,Jing Xiao,Chaofeng Chen,Gui-Song Xia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UniFlow-RS 使用单一潜在流匹配预测器统一六类遥感任务与十三份数据集，通过将异构输出映射到公共潜空间视觉目标、采用统一 Flow Matching 目标并引入任务-模态自适应时间步采样（TMAS），在多基准上超越或媲美任务专用 SOTA，并具备强零样本迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遥感基础模型的表征逐渐统一，但预测机制仍高度任务专用：分离的解码器、预测头与目标函数导致预测过程在任务间割裂。作者由此提出核心问题——异构遥感任务能否共享同一预测过程。

Method: 两点关键设计：1）统一异构预测空间——将异构任务输出转化为公共潜空间中的结构化视觉目标，用同一预训练流预测器建模，并以任务相关条件（task-dependent conditioning）容纳输入配置差异；2）统一联合优化——所有任务共享同一 Flow Matching 目标，去除任务专用预测损失，并提出任务-模态自适应时间步采样（TMAS），依据各任务-模态组的在线学习状态动态分配训练时间步，缓解跨任务与跨模态的优化失配。

Result: 单一共享模型覆盖 6 个任务、13 个数据集，在若干基准上超过近期任务专用 SOTA，其余基准保持竞争力；同一 checkpoint 在未见数据集、不同任务设置、遮挡条件及近红外假彩色影像上展现强零样本迁移，且无需目标域适配。

Conclusion: 异构遥感任务可共享统一的生成式（流匹配）预测过程，验证了“单一预测器替代多任务专用预测头”的可行性，并为跨任务、跨模态的零样本泛化提供了实证支持。

Abstract: Remote-sensing foundation models increasingly unify representations across sensing modalities and downstream tasks, yet their prediction mechanisms remain largely task-specific. Separate decoders, prediction heads, and objectives leave prediction fragmented across tasks. This naturally raises a question: can heterogeneous remote-sensing tasks share the prediction process? UniFlow-RS answers this question with a single latent flow-matching predictor shared across tasks. This requires addressing two key challenges: unifying heterogeneous prediction spaces and harmonizing joint optimization across tasks. First, we cast heterogeneous task outputs into structured visual targets in a common latent space, allowing them to be modeled by the same pretrained flow predictor. Task-dependent conditioning further accommodates differences in input configurations. Second, we optimize all tasks with a common Flow Matching objective, eliminating task-specific prediction losses. To mitigate optimization mismatch across tasks and modalities, we introduce Task-Modality Adaptive Timestep Sampling (TMAS), which dynamically allocates training timesteps according to the online learning state of each task-modality group. Using a single shared model, UniFlow-RS handles six tasks and thirteen datasets, surpassing recent task-specific state-of-the-art methods on several benchmarks while remaining competitive on the others. Beyond these benchmarks, the same checkpoint demonstrates strong zero-shot transfer across unseen datasets, task settings, occlusion conditions, and near-infrared false-color imagery, without target-specific adaptation.

</details>


### [46] [FastWild-GS: Minutes-Scale In-the-Wild Gaussian Splatting via Compact Per-Image Appearance Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13808)
*Donghun Ryu,Seokhyun Kim,Minhyeok Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FastWild-GS 用 128 维 Photo Appearance Token 加每通道 6 参数变换建模照片外观差异，并以小型 MLP 预测高斯颜色替代球谐函数，在保证性能的同时将单场景训练压缩至约 9 分钟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 野外场景照片在曝光、光照与瞬态物体上差异大，单一高斯颜色无法解释全部照片；现有 in-the-wild 高斯 Splatting 方法通常需数小时的单场景优化，效率不足。

Method: 为每张照片引入 128 维 Photo Appearance Token 及作用于渲染图像的每通道 scale/offset 变换（共 6 参数）；用小型 MLP 由该 token 与每高斯特征预测高斯颜色，替代球谐函数；并将同一变换应用于用于致密化与剪枝评分的高斯渲染结果，避免把照片间外观差异误计为重建误差。

Result: PhotoTourism 上每场景训练 8.8 分钟，在对比方法中 LPIPS 最低，比 LPIPS 次优的 AsymGS 快 23 倍；NeRF-OSR 上每场景 9.7 分钟取得对比方法中最优的平均 PSNR、SSIM 与 LPIPS。

Conclusion: 轻量的外观参数化与颜色预测式高斯表示可在野外场景中实现数量级加速，同时保持或提升重建质量；关键是让外观变换与致密化/剪枝误差度量保持一致。

Abstract: Internet photos of a scene differ in exposure, illumination and transient objects such as passers-by, so one set of Gaussian colours cannot explain all photos (the in-the-wild setting). Recent in-the-wild Gaussian Splatting methods mostly report hours of per-scene optimization. We present FastWild-GS, which describes each photo by a 128-dimensional Photo Appearance Token and a per-channel transform, a scale and an offset applied to the rendered image (six parameters); a small MLP predicts the colour of each Gaussian from this token and a per-Gaussian feature, replacing the spherical-harmonics colour. The same transform is applied to the renders that score Gaussians for densification and pruning, so appearance differences between photos are not counted as reconstruction error. On PhotoTourism, FastWild-GS trains in 8.8 minutes per scene with the lowest LPIPS among the compared methods, 23x faster than AsymGS, the next best in LPIPS. On NeRF-OSR, it has the best average PSNR, SSIM and LPIPS among the compared methods in 9.7 minutes per scene.

</details>


### [47] [CJRR-Net: A Candidate-Joint Reprojection Ranking for Future-View Selection in Human Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13700)
*Huiting Xi,Marsha Mariya Kappan,Eduardo Benitez Sandoval,Francisco Cruz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CJRR-Net，用于八相机固定配置下的未来视角选择，通过候选视角特定的几何重投影特征提升单视角人体姿态估计精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多相机系统（表演录制、运动与人体动作分析）中，选取信息量最大的视角对可靠的人体姿态估计至关重要，需要为下一时刻选择最佳单视角。

Method: 构建Candidate-Joint Reprojection Ranking Network（CJRR-Net），融合当前视角的姿态观测、相机配置，以及将估计的三维姿态及其不确定性重投影到各候选视角所得的候选特定几何特征，在MPI-INF-3DHP的八个已标定相机间进行视角排序选择。

Result: 选中视角的2D MPJPE为105.18像素，优于共享输入MLP的114.64像素与适配版PoseErrNet的117.56像素；在各评估遮挡分组中均取得所列策略中最低平均误差；blender rollout演示了重复相机选择过程。

Conclusion: 结果支持在固定八相机设定下，候选特定几何特征对单视角选择的有效性。

Abstract: Multi-camera systems support performance recording, sports and human-movement analysis, where selecting an informative viewpoint is important for reliable human pose estimation. We propose the Candidate-Joint Reprojection Ranking Network (CJRR-Net) for future-view selection among eight calibrated cameras in MPI-INF-3DHP. The model combines current-view pose observations, camera configuration, and candidate-specific geometric features obtained by reprojecting the estimated 3D pose and its uncertainty into each candidate view. CJRR-Net achieves a selected-view 2D mean per-joint position error (MPJPE) of 105.18 pixels, compared with 114.64 pixels for the shared-input MLP and 117.56 pixels for adapted PoseErrNet. It also achieves the lowest mean error among the listed policies in each evaluated occlusion group. A blender rollout illustrates repeated camera selection. These findings support candidate-specific geometry for single-view selection in the evaluated fixed eight-camera setting.

</details>


### [48] [PUMA: Probability-Constrained Sequential Interaction for Forensic Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13621)
*Zhouli Yu,Mengyuan Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PUMA方法，将空间约束的非平衡最优传输与逐位置凸选择相结合，实现语义与噪声取证证据的跨源融合，用于图像篡改定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语义-取证融合方法虽提供互补表征，但在空间对应过程中未显式保留质量失配信息，也未自适应选择跨分支信息传播的顺序，导致空间对应、匹配质量与交互顺序三者的作用被混为一谈。

Method: PUMA（概率约束非平衡匹配与聚合）：1）采用空间约束的非平衡最优传输建模跨分支对应；2）在归一化前保留带符号的传输质量残差；3）构建行随机的跨分支算子与噪声分支内算子；4）通过共享位置接口在两个顺序算子间做逐位置凸选择；5）以门控残差路径更新噪声特征。

Result: 在四个测试集上取得较强定位效果，其中COVERAGE与NIST16上的得分超过所列举代表性方法的已发表结果；实验验证了局部匹配状态与自适应顺序交互对跨源定位及多种后处理条件的有效性。

Conclusion: 显式保留匹配质量残差并结合自适应顺序交互，可有效解耦并利用空间对应、匹配质量与交互顺序的作用，提升图像篡改定位的鲁棒性与精度。

Abstract: Image manipulation localization must jointly exploit semantic structure and noise-sensitive forensic evidence. However, the effects of spatial correspondence, matching quality, and interaction order are often conflated. Existing semantic-forensic fusion methods provide complementary representations, but do not explicitly preserve quality-mismatch information during spatial correspondence or adaptively select the order in which information is propagated across branches. We propose PUMA (Probability-constrained Unbalanced Matching and Aggregation), which combines spatially constrained unbalanced optimal transport with position-wise convex selection between two sequential operators through a shared positional interface. Before normalization, PUMA retains signed transport-mass residuals, constructs row-stochastic cross-branch and within-noise-branch operators, and updates noise features through a gated residual path. PUMA achieves strong localization results on four test sets, with scores on COVERAGE and NIST16 exceeding the published results of the representative methods listed in our comparison. The experiments support the utility of local matching states and adaptive sequential interaction for cross-source localization and several post-processing conditions.

</details>


### [49] [CroCoMAE: Cross-modal self-supervised collaborative representation learning for SAR-optical remote sensing data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14124)
*Vito Recchia,Giuseppe Guarino,Cássio Fraga Dantas,Giuseppina Andresini,Annalisa Appice,Dino Ienco*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CroCoMAE：面向SAR-光学遥感的跨模态自监督框架，在图像级用类令牌对比学习、在块级用掩码建模+跨模态块对比对齐，保留模态特有子空间，在场景分类与语义分割上优于单模态SSL与多数SOTA跨模态方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAR与光学传感器互补，但地面真值标注稀缺，限制监督方法；现有跨模态SSL多停留在全局图像级对齐，或把两模态视为基本冗余，忽视其细粒度互补结构。

Method: 基于ViT的双层跨模态SSL：图像级对类令牌施加模态内对比损失，获得全局判别性、增强不变表示；块级用掩码图像建模学习各模态细粒度表示，并用跨模态对比目标在SAR与光学块令牌间对齐共享子空间，同时不约束互补子空间以保留模态特有信息。

Result: 在两个覆盖场景分类与语义分割的SAR-光学基准上，CroCoMAE优于单模态SSL基线，并在多数配置下超过SOTA跨模态框架；在不同标签量设置下单模态与多模态评估均有提升，语义分割增益最大。

Conclusion: 图像级与块级双层设计、共享+互补子空间的分离式对齐，能有效利用SAR-光学细粒度互补性；跨模态SSL在标注稀缺的遥感任务中具有实用价值。

Abstract: Remote sensing increasingly captures the Earth's surface through complementary sensors such as SAR and optical, yet ground-truth annotations remain scarce, limiting supervised approaches. Self-supervised learning (SSL) addresses the label scarcity, and cross-modal SSL further exploits the complementarity between co-located sensors, but existing cross-modal frameworks align modalities mostly through global image-level representations or treat them as largely redundant, leaving their fine-grained complementary structure underexploited. We propose CroCoMAE, a cross-modal SSL framework for SAR-optical remote sensing that unifies contrastive learning and masked image modeling within a ViT-based architecture, operating at two complementary levels. At the image level, an intra-modality contrastive loss on the class token yields globally discriminative, augmentation-invariant representations. At the patch level, masked image modeling drives fine-grained per-modality representations, while a cross-modal contrastive objective aligns a shared subspace across SAR and optical patch tokens, leaving the complementary subspace unconstrained to retain modality-specific information. On two SAR-optical benchmarks covering scene classification and semantic segmentation, CroCoMAE improves over uni-modal SSL baselines and outperforms state-of-the-art cross-modal frameworks in most configurations, with gains in both single-modality and multi-modal evaluation across varying label regimes and the largest improvements on semantic segmentation.

</details>


### [50] [TMAL: Topology-preserving Multi-offset Affinity Learning for Curvilinear Structure Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13511)
*Suho Han,Soungsill Park,Kanghyun Ryu,Yoseob Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出拓扑保持的多偏移亲和力学习（TMAL），用软亲和力与多偏移方向亲和力结合解码器加权融合，在DRIVE与DeepCrack上取得最优Dice、IoU、clDice并显著降低连通分量误差Δβ₀。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 曲线结构（视网膜血管、表面裂缝）同时要求像素级精度与结构连续性。逐像素监督不建模像素间连接；基于拓扑的损失仍只输出普通掩码；基于连通性的方法虽直接预测连接，但使用固定局部范围内的二值连通目标，导致细段与粗区域取值相同，且无法刻画更大偏移处的连接。

Method: TMAL：1）采用强调边界附近细区域的软亲和力，替代二值连通标签；2）在多个偏移处预测方向性亲和力以覆盖更远距离；3）以解码器特征生成权重融合各亲和力，兼顾局部连续性与更广上下文；4）增加辅助分割分支提供区域级监督以保持重叠精度。

Result: 在视网膜血管数据集DRIVE与裂缝检测数据集DeepCrack上，Dice、IoU、clDice均为最高，并大幅降低连通分量误差Δβ₀。

Conclusion: 以软亲和力+多偏移方向亲和力+特征加权融合+辅助分割监督，可同时提升曲线结构的像素精度与拓扑连续性，优于逐像素监督与固定范围二值连通方法。

Abstract: Curvilinear structures such as retinal vessels and surface cracks need both pixel-level accuracy and structural continuity. Pixel-wise supervision does not model the connections between pixels, and topology-based losses still produce ordinary masks. Connectivity-based methods predict these connections directly, but they use binary connectivity targets over a fixed local range. Such labels give the same value to thin segments and to thick regions, and they cannot describe connections at larger offsets. To address these limitations, we propose Topology-preserving Multi-offset Affinity Learning (TMAL). TMAL uses soft affinities that emphasize thin regions near the boundary, and it predicts directional affinities at several offsets to reach further. It then combines these affinities with weights that come from the decoder features, so local continuity and wider context are used together. An auxiliary segmentation branch adds region-level supervision and keeps the overlap accuracy high. On the retinal vessel dataset DRIVE and the crack inspection dataset DeepCrack, TMAL reaches the highest Dice, IoU, and clDice scores, and it greatly reduces the connected-component error $Δβ_0$. Code is available at https://github.com/suho-han/TMAL.

</details>


### [51] [Camera3R: Feed-Forward 3D Reconstruction from Heterogeneous Camera Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13453)
*Ruxiao Duan,Yunwen Zhou,Erin Hong,Dongxu Zhao,Eric Turner,Federico Tombari,Alex Wong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Camera3R，一种基于低秩适配（LoRA）的框架，可将透视相机预训练的前馈三维重建网络高效扩展到任意相机模型，仅需0.5%额外参数与不到一天训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前馈三维重建模型虽在大规模透视数据集上训练，但难以泛化到非透视（如鱼眼、全景）图像，限制了其在未知、异构相机模型场景中的部署；而全量重训练成本过高。

Method: 以低秩适配（low-rank adaptation）方式对透视预训练的3R网络进行参数高效微调，用少量新增参数扩展至任意相机模型，同时保持原有透视输入的性能。

Result: 仅增加0.5%参数、训练不足一天，即可使基础三维重建模型适配非透视相机，并在透视输入上完整保持原始性能。

Conclusion: Camera3R以最小重训练代价实现了透视预训练3R模型向非透视相机的泛化，为异构相机场景部署提供了高效方案。

Abstract: Feed-forward 3D reconstruction (3R) from unconstrained multi-view images has recently emerged as a prominent research direction in 3D computer vision. Various foundational reconstruction models have been proposed with different architectures and reconstruction paradigms. Despite being trained on large-scale perspective datasets with substantial computational resources, these models generally struggle to generalize to non-perspective images. This limitation hinders their deployment in applications involving arbitrary, unknown, and potentially heterogeneous camera models. To build on the capabilities of existing reconstruction models with minimal retraining, we propose Camera3R, a low-rank adaptation framework that efficiently extends perspective-pretrained 3R networks to any other camera models. Extensive experiments demonstrate that, with only 0.5% additional parameters and less than a day of training, Camera3R adapts a foundational 3D reconstruction model to non-perspective cameras while fully preserving its original performance on perspective inputs. Code is available at https://github.com/android-xr/camera3r.

</details>


### [52] [EgoHMP: Achieving Precise Human Motion Prediction in 3D Scenes via Egocentric Cues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13654)
*Xin Zhao,Pengzhan Zhou,Ziyi Li,Riheng Jia,Zhida Qin,Yu Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EgoHMP 提出以第一人称视角与头部运动作为交互意图载体，通过视觉-运动对齐、意图预测与接触感知扩散解码，实现3D场景中的人体运动预测并取得SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人与3D场景的复杂交互依赖使意图预测困难；已有工作用注视信号推断意图，但存在歧义、漂移及依赖眼动硬件的问题。认知科学表明第一人称视角天然编码潜在交互，头部运动是运动规划的重要前兆，故以二者替代注视作为更稳健的意图线索。

Method: EgoHMP 含三部分：(1) 视觉-运动对齐编码器对齐视觉与运动特征空间；(2) 第一人称意图预测器，引入模态感知调制机制自适应抑制无效交互线索，输出交互概率图；(3) 接触感知运动解码器：先预测全局轨迹作为自提示以缓解扩散模型优化冲突，再用多阶段去噪器结合人体接触建模抑制运动伪影，最后由GCN运动解码器合成物理合理、语义一致的运动。

Result: 大量实验表明 EgoHMP 达到最先进性能（SOTA）。

Conclusion: 第一人称视角与头部运动可作为稳健的交互意图载体；将意图转化为未来交互的"设想"能有效提升3D场景人体运动预测的准确性与物理合理性，为摆脱眼动硬件依赖提供了可行路径。

Abstract: The complex interactive dependencies between human behavior and 3D scenes make accurately predicting human intent a major challenge for human motion prediction (HMP). While recent studies use human gaze to infer intent, gaze signals suffer from ambiguity, drift, and a reliance on eye-tracking hardware. Research in cognitive science indicates that egocentric views inherently encode potential interactions, while head motion serves as a crucial precursor in motion planning. Motivated by this, we propose EgoHMP, a novel framework that uses egocentric views and head motions as robust carriers of interaction intent. By translating this intent into an envisioning of future interactions, EgoHMP achieves precise HMP in 3D scenes. EgoHMP first employs a vision-motion alignment encoder to align the visual and motion feature spaces. Based on these aligned features, an egocentric intent predictor with a modality-aware modulation mechanism adaptively suppresses invalid interaction cues to predict an accurate interaction probability map. Furthermore, to enhance the stability and realism of the generated motions, we propose a contact-aware motion decoder. Specifically, inspired by the human cognitive process of planning trajectories prior to action execution, we first predict a global trajectory to serve as a self-prompt, effectively mitigating the optimization conflict in diffusion models. Subsequently, we introduce a multi-stage denoiser, which relieves motion artifacts by incorporating human contact modeling through a multi-stage optimization strategy. Finally, a GCN-based motion decoder is employed to synthesize physically plausible and semantically consistent motions. Extensive experiments demonstrate that EgoHMP achieves state-of-the-art performance.

</details>


### [53] [picoEmergencyNet : Attention-Enhanced Ultra-Small Deep Learning Model for Aerial Image Classification in Emergency Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13638)
*Christos Kyrkou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出超轻量CNN picoEmergencyNet，用于航拍图像灾害分类，仅23k参数、7M FLOPs、0.1MB存储，AIDER/AIDERv2上最高96.1%准确率，Jetson AGX Orin上GPU 258 FPS、CPU 46 FPS，激活内存降低约7倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 航拍图像实时灾害识别需在严格的内存、算力与延迟约束下运行，现有高精度方法计算开销大，难以部署于资源受限的UAV平台。

Method: 设计超轻量CNN：以深度可分离空洞卷积（depthwise dilated convolutions）结合高效通道注意力与通道混洗（channel shuffling），在极低计算成本下实现多尺度特征提取。

Result: 模型规模23k参数、7M FLOPs、0.1MB存储；在AIDER与AIDERv2上最高达96.1%准确率，可媲美甚至超越规模大数个数量级的SOTA轻量模型；在NVIDIA Jetson AGX Orin上未经硬件特定优化即达GPU 258 FPS、CPU 46 FPS，激活内存较对比模型低约7倍。

Conclusion: 精细的架构专门化（而非模型缩放）即可实现高效准确的TinyML级灾害识别，满足UAV实时应急监测部署需求。

Abstract: Real-time disaster recognition from aerial imagery requires deep learning models that operate under strict memory, compute, and latency constraints. While recent approaches achieve high accuracy, their computational cost limits deployment on resource-constrained UAV platforms. This work introduces picoEmergencyNet, an ultra-lightweight convolutional neural network designed for aerial image disaster classification. The proposed architecture combines depthwise dilated convolutions with efficient channel attention and channel shuffling to enable multi-scale feature extraction at minimal computational cost. picoEmergencyNet has 23k parameters and 7M FLOPs, requiring only 0.1 MB of model storage. Evaluated on the AIDER and AIDERv2 disaster datasets, it achieves up to 96.1% accuracy, matching or exceeding state-of-the-art lightweight models that are orders of magnitude larger. Deployment experiments on the NVIDIA Jetson AGX Orin show that, without hardware-specific optimizations, picoEmergencyNet achieves 258 FPS on GPU and 46 FPS on CPU with a 7x lower activation memory footprint compared to competing models. These results demonstrate that careful architectural specialization, rather than model scaling, enables highly efficient and accurate TinyML-ready disaster recognition for real-time UAV-based emergency monitoring.

</details>


### [54] [DepthGS: Depth-Guided Gaussian Splatting with Adaptive Regularization for High-Quality Surface Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13628)
*Xiao Ren,Yu Liu,Ning An,Jian Cheng,Xin Qiao,You-Fu Li,He Kong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DepthGS，从理论上建立深度差异与重建误差之间的关系并给出上界，据此设计单视图与多视图的自适应正则化策略，提升3DGS表面重建与新视图合成性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS已扩展至表面重建，但深度信息与重建误差之间的关系缺乏系统研究，导致几何优化策略的设计缺乏理论依据。

Method: 推导单视图与多视图下深度差异与重建误差的理论关系及误差上界；单视图引入深度差引导的自适应法向正则（以局部深度差计算几何可靠性权重调制法向约束强度），多视图引入跨视图深度一致性正则（约束邻视图深度图与当前视图深度预测经变换投影后的一致性）。

Result: 在DTU、Mip-NeRF360、Tanks and Temples上实验，表面重建与新视图合成均取得有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: 深度差异可作为重建误差的理论度量，据此构建的自适应正则化能有效指导3DGS几何优化。

Abstract: Recently, 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has emerged as an efficient representation for novel view synthesis, offering fast differentiable rendering and high visual fidelity, and has increasingly been extended to surface reconstruction. However, the relationship between depth information and reconstruction error remains insufficiently explored, limiting the principled design of geometric optimization strategies. To fill the above gap, we propose DepthGS, a depth-guided Gaussian splatting framework. We derive theoretical relations between depth discrepancies and reconstruction error under single-view and multi-view settings, and rigorously establish corresponding upper bounds on reconstruction error. The above analysis informs the design of adaptive regularization strategies in both single-view and multi-view optimization. More specifically, for single-view optimization, we propose a depth-difference-guided adaptive normal regularization strategy that modulates the normal constraint strength using a geometric reliability weight computed from local depth differences. For multi-view optimization, we introduce a cross-view depth consistency regularization strategy that promotes consistency between neighboring-view depth maps and the corresponding depth predictions obtained by transforming the current view's depth predictions into those neighboring views. Extensive experiments on DTU, Mip-NeRF360, and Tanks and Temples demonstrate that DepthGS achieves competitive performance in both surface reconstruction and novel view synthesis.

</details>


### [55] [FlashMotion: Ultra-Fast Long-Term 3D Motion Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13626)
*Kaixing Yang,Xulong Tang,Ronghui Li,Xiangyue Zhang,Ziqiao Peng,Mingshuang Luo,Chenxi Zhao,Haoyu Wu,Ju Fan,Hongyan Liu,Puwei Wang,Zijiao Zeng,Jun He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FlashMotion：面向严格推理预算的高质量长时程3D人体运动生成联合设计框架，通过3D运动空间MeanFlow少步生成与轻量非自回归BiMamba架构，兼顾质量与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时交互应用（游戏、XR、数字人、具身仿真）需要超快速长时程3D运动生成，但高质量长时程生成受两个耦合瓶颈限制：(1) 生成策略需大量采样步数；(2) 长序列下模型单步计算与内存开销大。

Method: 提出轨迹级与单步级协同设计：轨迹级引入3D运动空间MeanFlow公式，配合运动学与动力学一致性约束实现少步生成，并提出自适应软掩码（Adaptive Soft masking）实现时间连贯的运动编辑；单步级设计轻量非自回归BiMamba架构，含条件引导状态选择与AdaIN条件融合机制以增强跨模态对齐与长序列泛化。覆盖text-to-motion、music-to-dance、speech-to-gesture三类任务。

Result: 大量实验表明，在文本到运动、语音到手势、音乐到舞蹈生成中，FlashMotion在质量与效率两方面均达到整体最优（state-of-the-art）表现。

Conclusion: 通过生成范式与架构的联合设计，可在严格推理预算下实现高质量长时程3D运动生成，验证了少步流匹配与线性复杂度序列建模在实时交互管线中的有效性。

Abstract: 3D human motion generation plays a crucial role in game development, extended reality (XR), digital humans, and embodied simulation. Beyond motion generation, practical pipelines include rigging, retargeting, and rendering, making ultra-fast long-term 3D motion generation without compromising quality valuable for real-time interactive applications. Despite promising progress, high-quality long-horizon motion generation under tight inference budgets remains challenging, owing to 2 coupled bottlenecks: (1) generative strategies that require many sampling steps for high-quality 3D motion generation and (2) model architectures with substantial per-step computational and memory costs for long sequences. We present FlashMotion, a co-designed framework for high-quality long-horizon 3D motion generation under a strict inference budget, covering text-to-motion, music-to-dance, and speech-to-gesture tasks. To enhance trajectory-level efficiency, we introduce a 3D motion-space MeanFlow formulation with kinematic and dynamic consistency constraints for few-step generation, together with an Adaptive Soft masking mechanism for temporally coherent motion editing. To improve per-step efficiency, we design a lightweight non-autoregressive BiMamba architecture with condition-guided state selection and AdaIN-based condition fusion mechanism, enhancing cross-modal alignment and generalization to longer motion sequences. Extensive experiments demonstrate that FlashMotion achieves overall state-of-the-art performance in both quality and efficiency across text-to-motion, speech-to-gesture, and music-to-dance generation. Code is available at FlashMotion (https://github.com/Sun-Happy-YKX/FlashMotion).

</details>


### [56] [Multimodal Judges Flag, Humans Decide: A Review-and-Edit Framework for AI-Generated Product Video Ads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13178)
*Anoubhav Agarwaal,Sukesh Perla,Karthikeyan Mohanraj,Susmita Ghose*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出面向电商商品页自动生成短视频广告的"审查—编辑"操作化框架：将可用性判断拆解为六个逐场景问题，由视觉-语言、语音和语言模型构建的judge给出发现，人类审查者逐条接受/拒绝并补充遗漏，每条确认问题再路由到匹配的编辑工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成流水线已能把每个商品页转成短视频广告，但"广告是否可用"并非单一好/坏判断，需要细粒度、可随生成速度同步的审查与编辑流程。作者希望使审查与编辑的接口不依赖具体的生成模型。

Method: 将审查拆成围绕商品、场景质量、发音、脚本、声音、字幕的六个逐场景问题；用VLM、语音与语言模型构建judge，将发现归类到对应问题；审查者逐条接受/拒绝并补充judge遗漏；确认的问题打开对应编辑工具。实验：120条广告（562场景）带judge发现的人工标注，60条广告做judge显示/隐藏对照，120个盲标场景评估judge的AUC。

Result: 显示judge发现时，审查者在60%的场景标记商品错误，脚本/字幕/声音问题各低于10%；同一批60条广告，显示judge时商品错误58%，隐藏时仅26%；一位两种方式都做过的审查者分别为57%与19%。盲标120场景上，商品judge（结合片段与商品图）AUC 0.84，视频质量与照片相似度分数接近随机。judge按高召回调优，商品judge几乎捕获所有缺陷但误报较多。

Conclusion: judge与人类互补：judge拓宽审查者标记范围，人类补足judge遗漏并由人做最终裁决；judge发现还能抑制长时间审查中的标记衰减。作者开源审查门户模板与数据（120条广告/562场景及judge发现与人工标记，120个盲标场景及基线分数）。

Abstract: Generative pipelines can now turn every product page of an e-commerce catalog into a short video ad, but deciding whether such an ad is usable is not one good-or-bad call. We describe how we operationalized the review and editing of these ads so that both can keep pace with generation: reviewers go through each ad scene by scene and answer six questions, on the product, scene quality, pronunciation, script, sound and captions; judges built from vision-language, speech and language models file each finding under the matching question, and reviewers accept or reject each finding and add what the judges missed. Each confirmed issue then opens an edit tool matched to it. The questions and the routing from a finding to its fix do not depend on the models that generate the ad. Across 120 ads from our pipeline, reviewed with judge findings on screen, reviewers marked the product wrong on 60% of scenes, and script, caption or sound problems on under 10% each. On 60 of these ads, they marked the product wrong on 58% of scenes, far more than the 26% marked by two reviewers who later saw the ads with the judges hidden; one reviewer who saw the ads both ways marked 57% with judge findings and 19% without. On 120 blind-labeled scenes, a product judge that watches the clip with the catalog photos reached AUC 0.84, while video-quality and photo-similarity scores were close to chance. We tune the judges for recall: the product judge catches nearly all defects but also flags many clean scenes, so reviewers decide; together, the judges widen what reviewers mark and reviewers add what the judges miss. Without judge findings, one reviewer's marking faded over a session. We release a review-portal template and our review data (120 ads and 562 scenes with judge findings and human marks, plus 120 blind-labeled scenes with baseline scores) at https://github.com/anoubhav/ad-review-and-edit.

</details>


### [57] [SpatialSR: Spatial Resolution-Centric Pipeline for Arbitrary-Scale Remote Sensing Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13046)
*Yan Zhang,Runfeng Luo,Liangke Gao,Lin Yuan,Bin Xiao,Xinbo Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 SpatialSR，以目标空间分辨率(SRe，如0.1m/pixel)作为绝对先验进行遥感图像任意尺度超分，通过分辨率估计、SRe 驱动的扩散纹理先验与轨迹一致性损失实现单步高质量重建，在 RSISR 及下游分类/分割任务上达到 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有遥感任意尺度超分(RSI-ASSR)方法仅以相对缩放因子为条件，未充分利用绝对空间分辨率这一先验，忽视了地物的固有空间上下文，易产生空间相关失真并削弱下游任务性能。

Method: 1) 设计空间分辨率估计模块(SREM)，估计低分图像的 SRe 并动态确定所需缩放因子；2) 利用预训练的 SRe-to-image 扩散模型提取 SRe 指定的纹理风格码，经分辨率感知风格一致性模块(RSCM)约束重建图像的 SRe 感知纹理一致性；3) 将迭代扩散过程封装为轨迹一致性(TC)损失，使生成模型在单步内达到可比纹理质量以降低计算开销。

Result: 在常用 RSISR 数据集及下游图像分类、分割数据集上的大量实验表明，SpatialSR 取得 SOTA 性能。

Conclusion: 以绝对空间分辨率为核心先验的 RSI-ASSR 框架可有效缓解空间相关失真，并通过扩散先验+单步一致性损失兼顾纹理质量与效率，且更利于下游遥感任务。

Abstract: Although remote sensing image arbitrary-scale super-resolution (RSI-ASSR) methods support reconstruction at arbitrary or continuous scales, they predominantly condition on the relative scale factor and have not sufficiently investigated the absolute spatial resolution itself as a reconstruction prior, thereby disregarding the inherent spatial context of objects, which frequently leads to spatial-related distortions and compromised performance in downstream remote sensing applications. To address these challenges, we introduce SpatialSR, a SRe-centric arbitrary-scale RSISR method that leverages the spatial resolution (SRe) of remote sensing images (RSI) as an essential prior for super-resolution reconstruction. Distinct from existing SR methods, SpatialSR aims to restore high-resolution remote sensing images with a target SRe (typically set to 0.1m/pixel to align with the metadata of commonly used downstream task datasets). Specifically, we design a Spatial Resolution Estimation Module (SREM) to estimate the SRe of low-resolution (LR) images, thereby dynamically determining the required arbitrary scaling factor. Next, we employ a pretrained SRe-to-image diffusion model to extract SRe-specified texture-style codes, which are further incorporated into a carefully designed Resolution-aware Style Consistency Module (RSCM) to constrain the SRe-aware texture consistency of reconstructed images. Furthermore, to overcome the high computational expense inherent in diffusion-based SR methods, we wrap the iterative diffusion process as a Trajectory Consistency (TC) loss, which enables comparable reconstructed texture quality of the generative model in SpatialSR within a single step. Extensive experiments demonstrate that the proposed SpatialSR achieves state-of-the-art (SOTA) performance on commonly used RSISR and downstream (image classification and segmentation tasks) datasets.

</details>


### [58] [Adapting a Vision Foundation Model for Fracture Characterization in Acoustic Image Logs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13911)
*Guangyu Wang,Xiaodong Ma,Xinming Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究将卫星影像预训练的视觉基础模型DINOv3迁移至声波成像测井裂缝识别，在仅80个标注样本下其性能优于从零训练的U-Net、YOLOv8与Swin Transformer，但优势随训练样本减少而收窄。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 声波成像测井中的天然裂缝(NF)与钻井诱导张性裂缝(DITF)可揭示岩体结构与地应力，但人工刻画耗时；深度学习虽可自动化，却受限于专家标注数据稀缺。作者欲验证预训练视觉基础模型(VFM)的适配是否在小样本条件下优于任务专用模型。

Method: 使用80个标注声波成像样本，对基于卫星影像预训练的DINOv3进行微调，并在未参与微调的4口井上进行外部验证；与从零训练的U-Net、YOLOv8、Swin Transformer对比；同时评估解码器架构、微调策略与图像增强的影响。

Result: 适配后的模型对NF与DITF参数的估计与人工解释相当；相比三种从零训练模型，假阳性与假阴性更少，裂缝数量与走向估计更准确；但性能优势随训练样本量减少而普遍缩小。渐进上采样解码器配合全参数微调在DINOv3各配置中表现最佳，而面向裂缝的图像增强未带来一致的精度提升。

Conclusion: VFM适配在标注有限的声波成像测井裂缝刻画中具有潜力，并可推广为面向其他标注稀缺地球科学成像任务的可迁移工作流程。

Abstract: Natural fractures (NFs) and drilling-induced tensile fractures (DITFs) in acoustic image logs provide insights into rock mass structure and in situ stress, but manual characterization is time-consuming. Deep learning can automate this task, although expert-labeled data are scarce. We investigate whether adapting a pretrained vision foundation model (VFM) outperforms task-specific models trained from scratch with limited annotations. Using 80 annotated acoustic image samples, we fine-tuned DINOv3, pretrained on satellite imagery, and evaluated it on four boreholes excluded from fine-tuning. The adapted model estimated NF and DITF parameters comparably to human interpretation. Relative to U-Net, YOLOv8, and Swin Transformer models trained from scratch, it yielded fewer false positives and negatives and more accurate fracture counts and orientations. However, its performance advantage generally narrowed as training sample size decreased. We also assessed decoder architecture, fine-tuning strategy, and image enhancement. A progressive upsampling decoder with full fine-tuning performed best among the DINOv3 configurations tested, while fracture-focused image enhancement did not consistently improve accuracy. These results demonstrate the potential of VFM adaptation for fracture characterization in acoustic image logs with limited labeled data and suggest a transferable workflow for other annotation-scarce geoscientific imaging tasks.

</details>


### [59] [EVAThink: Evidence Sufficiency-Guided Training for Adaptive Thinking in Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13907)
*Changti Wu,Ruizhe Fan,Bin Wang,Lei Zhang,Yuxiang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出EVAThink，通过"证据充分性引导"的训练让视觉语言模型在视频理解中自适应地在直接回答与显式推理之间切换，仅用6.7K样本即在32/64帧预算下稳定提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定帧预算下，采样帧能否提供足够证据决定模型应答方式：证据充分时直接回答更高效，证据稀疏时需显式推理解释有限线索。现有VLM缺乏对这种"应答模式"的自适应选择能力。

Method: 1) Recoverable Evidence Gap Mining：在同一帧预算、同一问答下构造证据密集与证据稀疏视频视图，保留"稀疏视图直接回答准确率低于密集视图、但可通过显式推理获益"的样本对，作为应答模式选择监督。2) Evidence-Guided Two-Stage RL：用阶段特定奖励先学习基于证据的模式偏好，再优化响应效率；并用首token优势正则化缓解模式坍缩。

Result: 仅用6.7K精选样本训练，在32帧与64帧预算下于多个视频理解基准上一致优于基座模型，平均性能超过强基线。

Conclusion: 证据充分性可作为有效的自适应思考监督信号，小规模高质量数据结合两阶段强化学习即可显著提升固定帧预算下的视频理解表现；代码、模型与数据已开源。

Abstract: Under a fixed frame budget, video understanding with vision-language models (VLMs) depends on whether the sampled frames provide sufficient evidence for the question. Sufficient frame evidence supports direct answering, whereas less informative observations may benefit from reasoning that connects the available cues. We introduce EVAThink, which enhances video understanding through evidence sufficiency-guided training for adaptive thinking. First, Recoverable Evidence Gap Mining constructs evidence-dense and evidence-sparse video views that share the same question and answer under an identical frame budget. It retains pairs where sparse views yield lower direct-answer accuracy than dense views but benefit from explicit reasoning, providing supervision for response-mode selection. Second, Evidence-Guided Two-Stage Reinforcement Learning uses stage-specific rewards to guide training with evidence-based mode preferences and then refine response efficiency, while first-token advantage regularization mitigates mode collapse. Trained on only 6.7K curated samples, EVAThink consistently improves upon the base model across video understanding benchmarks under both 32- and 64-frame budgets, while outperforming strong baselines on average. The code, model, and datasets are available at \href{https://github.com/ChangtiWu/EVAThink}{EVAThink}.

</details>


### [60] [The BRIDGE dataset: paired brightfield whole-slide images of prostate core-needle biopsies in paraffin, after deparaffinization and H&E staining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13883)
*Julia A. Mielcarz,Justyna Durślewicz,Marek Zdrenka,Ahmad Abbadi,Martin Eklund,Mateusz Matusiak,Łukasz Szylberg,Andrea Camilloni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发布BRIDGE数据集：波兰Bydgoszcz肿瘤中心采集的前列腺穿刺活检全切片图像，869张玻片（169例患者）、经切片/脱蜡/H&E染色顺序扫描共2607张全片图像，支持免标记虚拟染色、虚拟去染色、配准与分割等方法的开发与外部验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习免标记虚拟染色等计算方法可减少对化学染色的依赖、优化病理工作流，但配对且带临床注释的全切片数据集稀缺，制约模型开发与验证。

Method: 构建配对全切片数据集：同一组织切片在切片、脱蜡及H&E染色前后顺序数字化，形成未染色—染色配对图像；每张玻片附Gleason评分；图像去标识，以扫描仪原生格式经BioImage Archive发布。

Result: 共869张玻片、169例患者、2607张全切片图像；支持虚拟染色/去染色、图像配准与分割；可独立用于颜色归一化和跨扫描仪鲁棒性研究，并支持癌症分类与分级一致性分析。

Conclusion: BRIDGE提供了规模可观、带Gleason注释的配对前列腺活检全切片资源，可缓解该领域数据稀缺问题，推动免标记虚拟染色的开发与多中心外部验证。

Abstract: Computational approaches are increasingly being explored to streamline histopathology workflows, including label-free methods that aim to reduce reliance on chemical staining. Among these, deep learning-based label-free virtual staining uses images of unstained tissue to computationally generate stained images. Despite growing interest, model development and validation remain constrained by the scarcity of paired, clinically annotated whole-slide imaging datasets. We present BRIDGE, a dataset of prostate core-needle biopsies collected at the Prof. Franciszek Lukaszczyk Oncology Centre in Bydgoszcz, Poland. The dataset comprises 869 glass slides from 169 patients, each digitized sequentially after sectioning, deparaffinization, and hematoxylin and eosin staining, yielding in total 2607 whole-slide images. The dataset supports the development and external validation of label-free virtual staining, virtual unstaining, image-registration and segmentation methods. Each slide is accompanied by a Gleason score, enabling downstream analyses of cancer classification and grading concordance. The stained images can be used independently for color normalization and cross-scanner robustness studies. All images are de-identified, distributed in their native scanner format and will be available through the BioImage Archive.

</details>


### [61] [C$^2$-Routing: Complementary Cropping and Compression for Efficient Vision-Language Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14469)
*Guorui Lu,Qinyu Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 C²-Routing，模型无关地自适应选择裁剪或压缩来减少视觉 token，并配以安全门控制精度损失：在同为1,024 token上限的全图基线之上，端到端 FLOPs 再降 36.53%、视觉 token 再降 42.36%，平均任务分数仅相对下降 0.77%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VQA 中视觉语言模型计算开销大，需要模型无关、可用于黑盒场景的输入缩减方法。单纯设定视觉 token 上限虽能降低计算，却会丢弃回答问题所需信息；且单一缩减策略（裁剪或压缩）难以适配不同场景。

Method: 关键观察：裁剪突出小目标与局部细节，全图压缩更能保留大目标或分散证据的上下文，二者互补。据此提出 C²-Routing，依据输入图像与问题自适应路由至裁剪或压缩；由于两种策略仍可能丢失关键信息，进一步设计安全门（safety gate），利用原始输入与缩减输入下预测的联合正确性分布来指导回退，保护精度。全部训练与评测在单张 RTX 3090 上完成，基线为同样1,024视觉 token 上限的全图输入。

Result: 在3个 VLM 与18个数据集（覆盖局部细节、全局场景、场景文字、文档）上评测：在视觉预算已被上限约束的前提下，C²-Routing 进一步将端到端 FLOPs 降低36.53%、视觉 token 降低42.36%，平均任务分数仅相对下降0.77%。

Conclusion: 裁剪与压缩的自适应互补路由可作为一种模型无关的二级压缩手段，在已设 token 上限的基础上继续显著削减计算与 token，并以安全门机制将精度损失控制在可接受范围，且适配消费级 GPU 资源。

Abstract: Visual Question Answering (VQA) imposes substantial computational overhead on vision-language models (VLMs), motivating model-agnostic input reduction methods that can be readily applied across diverse VLMs, including in black-box settings. Simply enforcing a visual-token cap reduces computation, but it can discard information needed to answer the question. A single reduction strategy also struggles to accommodate different scenarios. Our key observation is that cropping and compression are complementary: cropping highlights small targets and local details, whereas whole-image compression better preserves context for large targets or distributed evidence. Based on this observation, we propose C$^2$-Routing, which adaptively selects cropping or compression according to the input image and question. Since both strategies may still discard key information, we further design a safety gate that uses the predicted joint correctness distribution under original and reduced inputs to guide fallback and protect accuracy. To accommodate consumer GPU resource constraints, all training and evaluation experiments run on RTX 3090 GPUs. To measure gains beyond token capping, we use full-image baselines with the same cap of 1,024 visual tokens. We conduct extensive evaluation on three VLMs and 18 datasets spanning local details, global scenes, scene text, and documents. With the visual budget already capped, C$^2$-Routing further reduces end-to-end FLOPs by 36.53% and visual tokens by 42.36% on average, with a mean relative task-score decrease of only 0.77%.

</details>


### [62] [Evidence-Grounded Iterative Reasoning for the Diagnosis of Diffuse Cystic Lung Diseases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12981)
*Haoqing Li,Jun Shi,Tou Li,Xianmeng Chen,Rui Wang,Yating Peng,Qiwei Jia,Li Gao,Qianyi Chen,Hong An,Ruoyun Ouyang,Xiaowen Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DCLDs-EIR：一个证据锚定的迭代推理框架，将诊断假设细化与多模态证据获取/验证耦合，用于弥漫性囊性肺疾病（DCLDs）的可溯源鉴别诊断，并在多中心数据上稳定优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DCLDs各疾病的影像表现高度重叠，鉴别诊断困难；同时标注数据稀缺，限制了监督式模型的构建。多模态大模型（MLLMs）虽可融合影像与临床信息，但其诊断推理受限于体数据感知不完整以及缺乏疾病特异证据支撑。

Method: 多视图渲染：对预测的肺与囊腔分割结果做多视角渲染，刻画囊肿的整体分布并初始化鉴别诊断；竞争假设驱动轴位CT的定向检视，获取局部征象，与临床背景共同用于从疾病特异性知识图谱中检索；对累积的患者观察与检索知识进行联合评估（支持与反驳），以更新诊断状态；更新后的假设与未解决的鉴别点引导下一轮检视，形成"证据获取—假设验证"闭环。

Result: 多中心评估显示，DCLDs-EIR相对各基线持续提升诊断准确率，并通过诊断假设、患者观察与疾病特异性知识之间的显式关联实现可溯源的鉴别诊断。

Conclusion: 将迭代式假设驱动检视与知识图谱证据检索相结合，可有效弥补MLLMs在体数据覆盖与证据锚定上的不足，兼顾准确性与可解释性，适用于影像重叠、标注稀缺的DCLDs鉴别诊断场景。

Abstract: Overlapping imaging manifestations complicate the differential diagnosis of Diffuse Cystic Lung Diseases (DCLDs), while limited annotated data constrain supervised model development. Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) can integrate imaging and clinical information, but their diagnostic reasoning remains limited by incomplete volumetric perception and insufficient grounding in disease-specific evidence. We propose DCLDs-EIR, an evidence-grounded iterative reasoning framework coupling diagnostic hypothesis refinement with multimodal evidence acquisition and verification. Multiview renderings of predicted lung and cyst segmentations characterize global cyst distributions and initialize the differential diagnosis. Competing hypotheses guide axial CT inspection to identify local findings that, together with clinical context, inform retrieval from a disease-specific knowledge graph. Accumulated patient observations and retrieved knowledge are jointly assessed for diagnostic support and refutation to revise the diagnostic state. Updated hypotheses and unresolved distinctions guide subsequent inspection, forming a feedback loop between evidence acquisition and hypothesis verification. Multicenter evaluation demonstrates that DCLDs-EIR consistently improves diagnostic accuracy over baselines and enables traceable differential diagnosis through explicit links between diagnostic hypotheses, patient observations, and disease-specific knowledge.

</details>


### [63] [Evaluation protocols for historical script classification: the cost of manuscript-level leakage and the role of soft labels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12969)
*Daniil I. Naryzhnyi,Roman A. Popkov,Daniil A. Bakalin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文量化了古文字学书写类型分类器在页面级划分下的准确率虚高：VML-HP-ext 上页面级 97% 降至手稿级 59%、官方盲测 39%，差距 40–47 pp 且显著；未见手稿上模型过度自信（ECE 0.065→0.444）；专家软标签不改善准确率但输出分布更贴近专家、跨手稿分歧降低 22–30%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 古文字学中书写类型分类器常在训练手稿的其他页面上测试，导致准确率被高估，但这一"泄漏"效应的大小、稳健性及其对置信度校准的影响缺乏系统量化；同时需要评估专家软标签能否缓解该问题，并给出可复现的评估协议建议。

Method: 在 VML-HP-ext（715 页、171 份希伯来手稿、14 个书写类别）上对比页面级与手稿级划分；在 patch/页/手稿三个粒度上计算准确率落差并做显著性检验，用 ResNet18 复现、并做二值化 patch（仅墨迹与背景）消融。评估校准：patch 级 ECE、平均置信度 vs 准确率、分位数分组 triage 分析。对比三种标签/后处理策略：专家软标签、标签平滑（无专家输入）、温度缩放（训练后置信度修正），以与专家评估的分布距离（分歧）为指标。最后在 97 页/16 份专家评级混合的手稿上验证软标签效应。

Result: 页面级准确率由 97% 降至 54%（手稿级 59%），官方盲测 39%；patch、页、手稿三粒度落差 40–47 pp，统计显著且 ResNet18 可复现；二值化 patch 上落差 34–42 pp 仍显著。未见手稿上模型过度自信：平均置信度 70% 对实际 54%，patch 级 ECE 由 0.065 升至 0.444；二值化保留 patch 级过度自信但显著降低页面级过度自信。置信度可用于分诊：高置信一半页面 67% 正确，其余 41%。专家软标签未见准确率差异证据（页级小损失不可排除），但预测分布更接近专家，patch 级 ECE 降低（0.21–0.31），页级多数运行亦降低，手稿级不显著。标签平滑同样降低 ECE 但不更接近专家；温度缩放仅在 patch 级显著有效。混合评级子集上软标签使与专家分歧下降 22–30%（5 次运行中 4 次显著），准确率点估计低于硬标签 16.5–23.7 pp 但 16 份手稿下无法确证或排除。

Conclusion: 在古文字学书写类型分类中，页面级划分会严重高估性能（约 40–47 pp），手稿级划分与盲测才是可靠评估；未见手稿上模型显著过度自信，置信度仍具分诊价值。专家软标签不提升准确率但改善与专家判断的一致性和校准，标签平滑与温度缩放无法替代专家信息。作者据此提出评估协议建议并承诺公开代码。

Abstract: A paleographer applies a script type classifier to unseen manuscripts. We measure how much testing on other pages of training manuscripts inflates accuracy. On VML-HP-ext (715 pages, 171 Hebrew manuscripts, 14 script classes) we compare page-level and manuscript-level splits. Page-level accuracy drops from 97% to 54% (59% at manuscript level), and is 39% on the official blind test set. The gap of 40 to 47 percentage points (pp) at patch, page and manuscript level is statistically significant and reproduces with ResNet18; on binarized patches (ink and background only) it is 34 to 42 pp, also significant. On unseen manuscripts the model is overconfident: mean confidence 70% against 54% correct pages; patch-level expected calibration error (ECE, mean gap between confidence and accuracy) rises from 0.065 to 0.444. Binarization leaves patch-level overconfidence but cuts it markedly at page level. Confidence still helps triage: in the more confident half of pages 67% are correct, 41% in the rest. Training on expert soft labels showed no evidence of an accuracy difference, though a small page-level loss cannot be ruled out. The predicted distributions are closer to the expert's assessment. ECE is lower at patch level (0.21 to 0.31) and in most runs at page level, but not significantly at manuscript level. Label smoothing (blurring the label, no expert input) also lowers ECE but comes no closer to the expert. Temperature scaling (post-training confidence correction) helps significantly only at patch level. On 97 pages of 16 manuscripts with mixed expert assessment, soft labels cut divergence from the expert by 22 to 30% (significant in four of five runs); the point estimate of accuracy is 16.5 to 23.7 pp below hard labels, but with 16 manuscripts the loss can neither be confirmed nor ruled out. We propose recommendations for the evaluation protocol; code will be released with the paper

</details>


### [64] [UniWeaver: Harnessing Unified Multimodal Models for Interleaved Text-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13530)
*Hongxu Chen,Zixuan Zhong,Donghao Zhou,Haodong Li,Xiaomin Yu,Tianhao Li,Shiyao Yu,Zhongwei Wan,Pheng-Ann Heng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 UniWeaver，一个模型无关的结构化框架，将多模态规划、生成与视觉反馈整合，用于生成连贯的交错图文内容，并配套构建 UniWeaver-10K 数据集与 UniWeaver-Bench 基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一多模态模型（UMMs）虽能生成丰富的交错图文内容，但普遍缺乏对文本与图像两种模态的原生协调能力，且将粗粒度用户请求转化为连贯的多模态组合仍需显式规划。

Method: 提出模型无关的结构化 harness 框架 UniWeaver，集成多模态规划、生成与视觉反馈；构建 UniWeaver-10K 数据集（20 个任务族、1 万条任务，配以 UniWeaver 引导的执行轨迹）用于训练 UMMs；并设计 UniWeaver-Bench 任务导向基准，从文本覆盖度、写作质量、视觉覆盖度、图文对齐四个维度进行评估。

Result: 实验表明 UniWeaver 达到 state-of-the-art 性能，并在多个 UMM 骨干模型上均提升了交错图文生成质量。

Conclusion: 通过结构化规划与视觉反馈闭环及配套数据与评测体系，UniWeaver 有效缓解了 UMMs 的模态协调与规划缺陷，且具备良好的模型通用性。

Abstract: Unified multimodal models (UMMs) have opened up new possibilities for generating rich interleaved text-image content. However, they generally lack native support for coordinating the two modalities, and translating coarse user requests into coherent multimodal compositions also requires explicit planning. We propose UniWeaver, a structured, model-agnostic harness framework that integrates multimodal planning, generation, and visual feedback to produce coherent interleaved outputs. To train UMMs for this task, we construct UniWeaver-10K, a dataset of 10,000 tasks across 20 task families, paired with execution trajectories guided by UniWeaver. We also introduce UniWeaver-Bench, a task-oriented benchmark systematically assessing text coverage, writing quality, visual coverage, and image-text alignment. Experiments show that UniWeaver achieves state-of-the-art performance and improves interleaved text-image generation across multiple UMM backbones.

</details>


### [65] [MEOW: Medical Open-World Interactive Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13935)
*Philip Matthias Winter,Maria Wimmer,Bernhard Schäfl,David Major,Dimitrios Lenis,Astrid Berg,Theresa Neubauer,Ariharasudhan Muthusami,Johannes Novotny,Georg Zotti,Katja Bühler*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MEOW是首个将非交互式分割骨干网络转化为交互式开放世界学习器的框架，在不更新任何可学习参数的前提下，通过无参数转导推理与记忆巩固持续积累知识并提升预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交互式分割虽能获得用户反馈，但始终局限于开放集设定；而开放世界识别要求模型不仅对未知结构做预测（广义开放集），还需随时间从这些预测中持续学习与改进。该工作正是要弥合交互式分割与开放世界持续学习之间的空白。

Method: 提出MEOW框架，将非交互式分割骨干重构为交互式开放世界学习器。核心为两项设计的结合：一是无参数的转导推理（parameter-free transductive reasoning），利用用户反馈进行推理而不改动模型参数；二是记忆巩固（memory consolidation），将推理所得知识写入并固化于记忆中，从而在后续预测中复用。整个过程中不更新任何可学习模型参数。

Result: 以医学数据为主要实验对象，MEOW在训练中从未见过的类别上能持续自我改进预测；并可零样本迁移至不熟悉的数据域，包括微CT数据及其他成像模态。

Conclusion: 交互式分割可在不进行参数更新的条件下实现开放世界学习，说明"转导推理+记忆巩固"是一种有效的知识积累范式，为开放世界持续学习提供了一条免训练、可迁移的新路径。

Abstract: In open-world recognition, machine learning models not only make predictions on unknown structures ("generalized open-set"), but also continually learn and improve from those predictions over time. Interactive segmentation, despite its access to user feedback, has so far remained confined to the open-set setting. In this work we introduce MEOW, the first framework that turns non-interactive segmentation backbones into interactive open-world learners without updating learnable model parameters. MEOW combines parameter-free transductive reasoning with memory consolidation to accumulate knowledge and thereby improve future performance. Focusing on medical data, we show that MEOW consistently improves its own predictions on classes never seen during training, and transfers zero-shot to unfamiliar domains, including micro-CT data and other imaging modalities.

</details>


### [66] [Measuring Robustness and Efficiency in a Connectome-Constrained Fly Visual System Model: Linking an Adversarial and a Perceptual Illusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14498)
*Alexandre Garcia-Duran,Alejandro Rodriguez-Garcia,Manuel Molano-Mazón,Alexandre Hyafil,Srikanth Ramaswamy,Janne K. Lappalainen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文将受果蝇突触连接组约束的连续时间循环网络（FlyNet）与CNN、RAFT及无连接组约束的CTRNN在可微3D渲染的碰撞检测任务上对比，发现FlyNet对抗脆弱但对常见视频损坏的鲁棒性-算力比优于RAFT（FLOPs低至1/10^5），而CTRNN几乎全面更鲁棒，说明连接组约束主要带来效率而非鲁棒性；并揭示对抗纹理与reverse-phi错觉共享同一低层运动统计量的电路级脆弱机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物视觉系统在代谢与布线约束下仍能可靠计算，而人工视觉系统能耗高且易受对抗扰动影响。尚不清楚类脑布线网络是否在更低计算代价下更具鲁棒性，因此需要一个受真实连接组约束、任务优化的模型作为可控基准来检验"布线约束—效率—鲁棒性"之间的关系。

Method: 构建任务优化、连续时间循环神经网络FlyNet，并以果蝇视觉系统突触连接组为硬约束；在可微3D渲染管线中设置碰撞检测任务；与CNN、RAFT、以及无连接组约束的sCTRNN/CTRNN进行基准对比；评估常见视频损坏、白盒对抗攻击、反事实纹理（保持与训练集等距但恢复正确分类）以及reverse-phi刺激；并单独优化低层运动统计量进行无模型攻击。

Result: FlyNet对对抗扰动脆弱，但相对其算力对常见视频损坏比RAFT更鲁棒，且FLOPs低至五个数量级；CTRNN在几乎所有扰动下都比FlyNet更鲁棒；循环结构提升RAFT对白盒攻击的鲁棒性，时间上下文提升对常见损坏的鲁棒性；反事实纹理实验排除"陌生纹理"解释；reverse-phi与对抗纹理反转同一低层运动统计量，单独优化该统计量即可致分类失败。

Conclusion: 连接组约束主要贡献于效率（边数少）而非鲁棒性；受连接组约束的模型可作为可测试假设的来源，揭示生物感知脆弱性的对抗失效机制（共享的低层运动统计量电路缺陷）。

Abstract: Biological visual systems compute reliably under tight metabolic and wiring constraints, whereas artificial vision systems consume far more energy and can be sensitive to adversarial perturbations. Whether networks with brain-like wiring are more robust at lower computational cost is unclear. We address this by benchmarking a task-optimized, continuous-time recurrent neural network of the fly visual system constrained by its synaptic connectome (FlyNet) against artificial neural networks. We compare it against CNNs, RAFT, and continuous-time recurrent neural networks without detailed connectome constraints (sCTRNN, CTRNN) on a collision-detection task within a differentiable 3D rendering pipeline. FlyNet is adversarially vulnerable but, relative to its compute, more robust to common video corruptions than RAFT, while using up to five orders of magnitude fewer FLOPs. The CTRNNs remain more robust than FlyNet across almost all tested perturbations, suggesting that connectome constraints benefit efficiency, through their low edge count, over robustness. Recurrence improves robustness to white-box attacks in RAFT and temporal context improves robustness to common corruptions. FlyNet's position and velocity representations degrade under adversarial textures but recover under counterfactual textures that restore correct classification while staying equally far from training, ruling out unfamiliar textures as the cause of failure. The reverse-phi illusion similarly disrupts these representations and inverts the same low-level motion statistic as the adversarial textures. Optimizing this input statistic alone, without access to the network, suffices to induce classification failure, attributing both perturbations to the same circuit vulnerability. Connectome-constrained models can thus surface adversarial failure mechanisms as testable hypotheses about biological perceptual vulnerabilities.

</details>


### [67] [BoviShift: A Six-Year Multi-Attribute Audit of Visual Cattle Phenotyping Across Animals and Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14476)
*Mobasshir Bhuiya Shagor,Nilambar Halder Tonmoy,Md Saef Ullah Miah*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文提出BoviShift——由2021至2026年连续六个年度队列构建的牛只视觉数据集（2657头牛、10610张静态图、2566段绕行视频、9类记录品种），并系统评估图像随机划分与牛只不相交划分及未来队列迁移下的多任务评估表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算机视觉为牲畜性状估计提供可扩展的非接触手段，但相关图像与视频帧使得可靠评估困难：图像随机划分可能把同一动物的不同视角同时放入训练集与测试集，造成信息泄漏；实际部署还要求模型能迁移到未来年份捕获的动物。现有资源缺乏跨越多个连续年度队列、同时包含物理与视觉属性的评估基准。

Method: 构建BoviShift语料库，覆盖六个连续年度队列；设计四种配置评估三种划分策略：同一开发队列上的图像随机划分与合并的牛只不相交划分，以及独立的未来2025年评估与锁定的未来2026年评估。使用固定ResNet-50多任务基线，评估体重、年龄、身高、品种与毛色五个属性；并进行单种子消融（多任务训练）、静态视图聚合分析及零样本视频帧聚合分析。

Result: 图像随机划分与牛只不相交划分之间的差异是任务依赖的，而非普遍性的泄漏惩罚。未来队列表现亦随属性与队列而异：品种性能相对合并评估下降；2025年体重与年龄的均值性能未能超过常数基线；身高仍高于基线；毛色可较好迁移至2025年但在2026年退化。单种子消融中多任务训练提升了全部五项点估计，静态视图聚合具有选择性增益，零样本视频帧聚合揭示了静态到视频的域差距。

Conclusion: 据作者所知，BoviShift是时间跨度最长的牛只视觉资源，结合了连续年度队列及物理与视觉属性。研究表明牲畜视觉评估需采用牛只不相交与未来队列的严格划分协议，并按属性分别考量迁移性，图像随机划分带来的泄漏效应不能被一概而论。
