Abstract: Computer vision offers a scalable, non-contact means of estimating livestock traits, but correlated images and video frames make reliable evaluation difficult. Image-random splitting can place views of the same animal in both training and test sets, while deployment further requires transfer to animals captured in future years. We introduce BoviShift, a corpus reconstructed from six consecutive annual cohorts of an operational cattle marketplace collected from 2021 to 2026. It contains 2{,}657 cattle, 10{,}610 still images, and 2{,}566 walk-around videos across nine recorded breed categories. We evaluate three splitting strategies through four configurations: image-random and pooled cattle-disjoint splits over the same development cohorts, followed by separate future-2025 and locked future-2026 evaluations. Using a fixed ResNet-50 multi-task baseline, we assess weight, age, height, breed, and coat color. Differences between image-random and cattle-disjoint splitting are task-dependent rather than a universal leakage penalty. Future-cohort behavior is also attribute- and cohort-specific: breed declines relative to pooled evaluation; mean weight and age performance fails to beat constant baselines in 2025; height remains above baseline; and color transfers well to 2025 but degrades in 2026. Multi-task training improves all five point estimates in a single-seed ablation, still-view aggregation helps selectively, and zero-shot video-frame aggregation exposes a still-to-video domain gap. To our knowledge, BoviShift is the longest-spanning cattle-vision resource combining consecutive annual cohorts with physical and visual attributes.

</details>


### [68] [Partition, Refine, and Detect: Preserving Novel Category Capacity in Open-Vocabulary Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12976)
*Wei Zhang,Zhu Teng,Zhixiang Chi,Junnan Zou,Yi Liu,Jianping Fan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 PR-OVD 分区-精炼框架，为开放词汇检测中的新类显式保留表征容量，缓解基类主导导致的语义崩塌，新类平均提升约 6.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于 CLIP 式视觉-语言嵌入的 OVD 方法，其学到的嵌入空间偏向基类，为新概念保留的有效容量有限，造成新类语义被压缩、推理时出现语义崩塌与置信度失准。

Method: 1) 粗粒度分区：在视觉与文本两种模态上构建基类、新类和显式背景三个全局划分，防止与目标无关的嵌入泄漏到类特定表征中；2) 类级精炼：在各分区内部锐化决策边界，弥补 CLIP 缺乏细粒度区域级预训练的问题；3) 结构提示（SP）：受认知启发，编码自我中心属性与他者中心上下文，为新类提供可靠视觉锚点，稳定分区-精炼学习。可无缝集成到不同检测器架构。

Result: 在两个 OVD 基准上，新类相较 SOTA 方法平均获得 6.5% 的比例提升；同时在开放词汇分割与分类任务上也有增益。

Conclusion: 通过显式容量分配与类别分离，PR-OVD 有效缓解基类偏置，并具备良好的通用性与可插拔性。

Abstract: Open-vocabulary object detection (OVD) expects the model trained on base classes to recognize both base and novel classes at inference. Typical methods rely on CLIP-style vision-language embeddings, yet the learned space is often biased toward base categories, with limited effective capacity preserved for novel concepts. Such a base-dominated representation can compress novel semantics, leading to semantic collapse and mis-calibrated confidence at inference. To address this issue, we present PR-OVD, a partition-and-refine framework that explicitly reserves capacity for novel categories and strengthens category separation, while being seamlessly integrated into different detector architectures. Specifically, PR-OVD first performs coarse partitioning to build global divisions for base, novel, and an explicit background partition in both visual and textual modalities, preventing object-irrelevant embeddings from leaking into category-specific representations. It then applies category-level refinement within each partition to sharpen decision boundaries and alleviate the lack of fine-grained region-level pretraining in CLIP. To further provide reliable visual anchors for novel categories, we design a cognitively inspired Structure Prompt (SP) that encodes both egocentric attributes and allocentric context, facilitating stable partition-and-refine learning. Extensive experiments on two OVD benchmarks show that PR-OVD delivers an average 6.5\% proportional improvement on novel categories over the state-of-the-art methods. Notably, PR-OVD also benefits other open-vocabulary tasks, including segmentation and classification, further confirming its effectiveness and generality.

</details>


### [69] [The Target Is a Predicate: Predicate-State Grounding for Semantic-Level Video Reasoning Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13785)
*Yuhuan Hong,Yiming Ren,Zhiqiang Lin,Feize Wu,Xingyun Liu,Yitong Xie,Lei Zhou,Lisheng Wu,Junjie Wang,Yujiu Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出谓词-状态视频推理分割（PS-VRS）任务与PS-Bench基准，并设计PSG模型，通过显式预测[ACTIVE]/[VACANT]状态并仅对活跃状态输出掩码，减少空白区间的误报。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频推理分割把目标视为一旦识别便永久激活，导致谓词空缺区间过度输出掩码，且无法处理目标切换。

Method: 构建PS-Bench（2,077个视频、16,250条查询，标注活跃/空缺谓词状态、时间区间与像素掩码）；提出Predicate-State Grounding（PSG）的State-Bind-Emit流水线：预测[ACTIVE]/[VACANT]状态token、通过稠密跨模态相似度对齐到帧、仅对活跃状态用SAM2输出掩码。

Result: PSG在PS-Bench上较现有基线提升+9.32 J&F，并显著降低假阳性掩码输出。

Conclusion: 显式谓词-状态建模对具有动态视觉见证的视频推理分割有效。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce Predicate-State Video Reasoning Segmentation (PS-VRS), where a query defines a dynamic predicate $P(o,t)$ over objects and time rather than a fixed object identity. Existing methods typically treat the target as permanently active once identified, leading to over-emission of masks during vacant intervals and failure to handle target switching. To address this, we construct PS-Bench, a large-scale benchmark with 2,077 videos and 16,250 queries annotated with explicit active/vacant predicate states, temporal intervals, and pixel masks. We further propose Predicate-State Grounding (PSG), a State-Bind-Emit pipeline that: (1) predicts [ACTIVE]/[VACANT] state tokens; (2) aligns them to video frames via dense cross-modal similarity; and (3) emits masks via SAM2 only for active states. Experiments show that PSG outperforms existing baselines by +9.32 J&F on PS-Bench and significantly reduces false-positive mask emissions. Our results demonstrate the effectiveness of explicit predicate-state modeling for video reasoning segmentation with dynamic visual witnesses.

</details>


### [70] [RF3DGS: Reflection-Free 3D Gaussian Splatting with Self-Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13772)
*Qiang Wen,Guotao Meng,Junhong Ruan,Hongyu Liu,Chenyang Lei,Qifeng Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RF3DGS，仅用多视角光度先验（同一3D点跨视图最小像素强度）去除3DGS中的反射，通过深度引导3D warping构建传输伪标签，配合自适应孪生渲染损失实现自监督优化，并建立首个3D反射去除基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D反射去除对博物馆展览等高保真可视化应用至关重要，而现有方法尚未在3D高斯泼溅框架下有效利用多视角光度先验实现反射分离。

Method: 先用几何先验优化vanilla 3DGS得到各视图粗粒度无反射深度图；基于深度图与光度先验，通过3D warping从辅助视图聚合无反射信息，为参考视图构建干净透射伪标签；设计自适应孪生渲染（ATR）损失，在利用伪标签的同时保留原始含反射图像的高频细节，实现自监督优化。

Result: 在合成与真实场景下均达到state-of-the-art的反射去除性能；并基于Unity渲染引擎建立了首个3D反射去除基准，用于严格定量评估。

Conclusion: RF3DGS证明多视角光度先验可有效迁移至3D高斯泼溅，内部3D自先验与ATR损失的融合实现了高质量反射去除，所建基准为后续3D反射去除研究提供评估基础。

Abstract: 3D reflection removal is crucial in applications demanding high-fidelity visualization, such as museum exhibitions. In this work, we propose Reflection-Free 3D Gaussian Splatting (RF3DGS), a novel method for superior reflection removal. RF3DGS exclusively leverages a widely-adopted photometric prior for reflection removal: that the minimum pixel intensity across multi-view images corresponding to the same 3D point strongly correlates with reflection-free information. However, generalizing this principle to a 3D Gaussian Splatting within a multi-view context is non-trivial. To address this, RF3DGS first optimizes a vanilla 3DGS model using a geometric prior to estimate a coarse, reflection-free depth map for each view. Leveraging these depth maps and photometric prior, a 3D warping process aggregates reflection-free information from auxiliary views to construct a clean transmission pseudo-label for a given reference view. This pseudo-label provides a robust self-supervised signal for optimization. We further introduce an Adaptive Twin Rendering (ATR) loss to synergistically utilize this pseudo-label while preserving high-frequency details from the original, reflection-degraded images. By adeptly fusing these internal 3D self-priors with the ATR loss, RF3DGS demonstrates state-of-the-art reflection removal performance across both synthetic and real-world scenarios. Furthermore, we establish the first 3D reflection removal benchmark, based on the Unity rendering engine, for rigorous quantitative evaluation.

</details>


### [71] [Effects of Laser Beam Width and Measurement Noise on Tree Diameter Estimation in Laser Scanning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13739)
*Jesse Muhojoki,Nuutti Ervasti,Eric Hyyppä,Aapo Erkkilä,Eppu Honkanen,Tuomas Yrttimaa,Mikko Vastaranta,Juha Hyyppä*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究分析了移动激光扫描(MLS)中测量噪声的异方差性与激光束宽对树干曲线(直径)估计偏差的影响，提出基于传感器技术参数、无需额外标定的偏差补偿方法，将胸径大于10 cm树木的直径估计RMSE降至亚厘米级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激光扫描虽广泛用于树木结构与几何测量，但传感器特性（如激光束直径）与测量条件引起系统性和随机误差，尤其会使重建的树干几何产生偏差，此前缺乏对这些误差来源的系统量化与分析。

Method: 通过仿真与实验，识别MLS中测量噪声的主要误差来源；发现测距与扫描角噪声的异方差性及激光雷达束宽是关键因素，使直径偏差同时依赖于距离和树木直径；据此建立可预测的偏差模型，并利用传感器技术规格（不额外标定）进行补偿。

Result: 直径偏差可被可靠预测与抵消（对DBH>10 cm的树木）；在北方森林试验点，补偿方法将茎干直径估计的均方根误差降至亚厘米级。对DBH<10 cm的小树，直径偏差对树径这一未知因素敏感，并受树皮差异与茎干非圆度影响，限制了精度与方法适用性。

Conclusion: 测量噪声异方差性与激光束宽是MLS树干直径估计偏差的核心来源；所提偏差补偿方法无需标定即可有效提升中大径树木的直径估计精度，但对小径树木仍受未知直径及树皮、非圆度等因素制约。

Abstract: Laser scanning has been utilized for decades to measure tree structure and geometry. Despite continuous advances in sensor technology, uncertainties arising from scanner characteristics and measurement conditions has remained relevant sources of error. In particular, factors such as laser beam diameter can introduce both systematic and random errors in the reconstructed tree geometry. This study investigates the main sources of these errors in mobile laser scanning (MLS) and quantifies their effects on stem curve (stem diameter) estimation. We discovered through simulations and experiments that the heteroscedasticity (difference in variance in ranging and scan angle) in the measurement noise is a key factor along with the beam width of the lidar, which cause the bias of the diameter estimates to depend not only on distance, but also on the tree diameter. We demonstrated that this bias can be reliably predicted and negated for trees with a diameter at the breast height greater than 10 cm using technical specifications of the sensor without any additional calibration. In experiments, the proposed bias compensation method reduced the root-mean-square error of stem diameter estimates to sub-centimeter level on boreal forest test sites. However, for small trees with diameter below 10 cm, the diameter bias becomes sensitive to the diameter of the tree$\unicode{x2013}$an unknown factor$\unicode{x2013}$and other factors, such as different bark and out-of-roundness of the stem, limiting the precision of lidar for measuring geometry of trees and applicability of the presented method.

</details>


### [72] [Hint, Not Hole: Localization-Robust Video Overlay Removal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13711)
*Dongyang Zhang,Wei Zhang,Renjie Zou,Zhiwei Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HintErase将视频定位（检测/分割）结果视为"提示"而非"待填充的洞"，用带腐蚀提示通道的生成模型纠正定位的双向错误，并配流式管线实现全长视频的去叠加。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频叠加去除（烧录字幕、歌词、标题、片尾、台标、水印）中，生成式模型虽能可信填充已知区域，但该区域来自检测器/分割器，存在漏检（笔画、光晕、整帧、整目标）与误检（字符间背景、框周围、真实场景文字）双向错误；把mask当洞、洞内重建洞外拷贝的"橡皮擦"会继承全部错误。

Method: 提出HintErase：模型输入未遮挡的完整视频加一路提示通道（hint channel）；训练时按真实定位器的失败模式对提示进行腐蚀/扰动，而监督信号始终对齐真实叠加区域标签；从而在单一模型中同时实现去除漏检叠加、保留被误覆盖背景、并保留提示覆盖到的真实场景文字。

Result: 单个模型即可纠正漏检与误检两类定位错误，并具备真实场景文字保留能力；面向全长发布，流式管线处理隔行源、镜头切换、漏提示与抖动提示、paste-back回贴与音频，内存开销与视频长度无关；报告给出方法与管线说明及定性结果，项目页提供输入/提示/输出并排视频结果。

Conclusion: 定位在去除任务中应作为软提示而非硬洞约束；提示腐蚀训练+真值监督+与时长无关的流式管线，构成了可交付全长视频的实用叠加去除方案（摘要未给出定量指标）。

Abstract: Video overlay removal recovers the scene beneath burned-in subtitles, karaoke lyrics, titles, credits, logos and watermarks. Generative video models now fill a known region convincingly, but in practice the region comes from a detector or a segmenter, and it is wrong in both directions: it misses strokes, halos, whole frames and whole objects, and it covers background between characters, around boxes and on genuine scene text. Erasers that treat the mask as a hole, regenerating inside and copying outside, inherit every one of these errors. We present HintErase, which treats localization as a hint, not a hole. The model sees the full, unmasked video together with a hint channel; during training the hint is corrupted the way real localizers fail, while supervision follows the true overlay. The result is a single model that removes overlay the hint missed, keeps background the hint wrongly covered, and is trained to keep genuine scene text even when the hint lands on it. For full-length releases, a streaming pipeline handles interlaced sources, shot cuts, missed and jittering hints, paste-back and audio, with memory independent of the length of the video. This report describes the approach, the pipeline that turns it into full-length deliveries, and qualitative results; video results, with input, hint and output side by side, are on the project page.

</details>


### [73] [Predicting Single-Cell Spatial Gene Expression from H&E using Pathology Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13649)
*Ashley P. Tsang,Evan Liu,Conrad Foo,Bob Chen,Hassaan Maan,Donny Chan,Elisa Penna,Veronica Ibarra Lopez,Hector Corrada Bravo,Aicha BenTaieb,Lisa M. McGinnis,Runmin Wei,Kenneth T. Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文系统评估并提出领域知识与任务特定的适配策略，将病理基础模型用于从H&E图像预测单细胞空间基因表达，较零样本基线将平均基因Pearson相关提升2.67倍，达到该任务SOTA（AUROC=0.800，空间可变基因r=0.643）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从常规H&E切片直接预测基因表达可低成本、可扩展地获取空间分子信息；但病理基础模型多以tile级目标预训练，难以捕捉单细胞分子预测所需的细粒度细胞特征。

Method: 基于配对的H&E与Xenium 5K数据，系统评估并建立领域知识驱动、任务特定的病理基础模型适配策略，用于单细胞空间基因表达预测。

Result: 平均基因wise Pearson相关较标准零样本基线提升2.67倍，取得该任务SOTA；全基因panel基因检测AUROC=0.800；性能随生物学情境显著变化，空间可变基因上相关最高（r=0.643）。

Conclusion: 尽管单细胞测量稀疏带来挑战，H&E衍生表征仍可恢复具生物学意义的空间表达模式，揭示了细胞分辨率下基于形态的分子推断的潜力与局限。

Abstract: Predicting gene expression directly from hematoxylin and eosin (H&E) images offers a scalable and cost-effective approach to deriving spatial molecular insights from routine histology. Pathology foundation models, typically pretrained using tile-level objectives, may not adequately capture the fine-grained cellular features required for single-cell molecular prediction. In this work, we systematically evaluate and establish domain-informed and task-specific strategies for adapting pathology foundation models to single-cell spatial gene expression prediction. Using paired H&E and Xenium 5K data, our approach improves mean gene-wise Pearson correlation by 2.67x over the standard zero-shot baseline and achieves state-of-the-art performance on this task. We demonstrate strong gene-detection performance across the full gene panel (AUROC = 0.800) and importantly identify that predictive performance varies substantially across biological contexts, with our model achieving its highest Pearson correlation for spatially variable genes (r = 0.643). Our findings demonstrate that, despite the challenges of sparse single-cell measurements, H&E-derived representations can recover biologically meaningful spatial expression patterns, highlighting both the opportunities and limitations of morphology-based molecular inference at cellular resolution.

</details>


### [74] [Lightweight Deep Learning Framework for Astrocyte-Centric 3D Reconstruction across Normal and Stress-associated Ultrastructural States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13581)
*Bowen Deng,Gwang-Jin Jeong,Seulgi Noh,Ji Young Mun,Na Young Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出轻量级、以星形胶质细胞为中心的框架AstroSeg-vEM，实现vEM图像中保留形态的星形胶质细胞分割、三维重建与定量形态分析，在四套小鼠海马数据集上Dice/加权Dice达0.92/0.95。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 星形胶质细胞形态与其支持和调控神经环路的功能密切相关，但从体电子显微镜（vEM）数据重建其形态仍高度依赖人工、计算资源开销大，成为大规模应用与推广的主要瓶颈。

Method: AstroSeg-vEM将基于Transformer的二维分割与专门设计的后处理方法结合，直接在高分辨率EM切片上运行，保留原始数据的全部面内分辨率，适配各向异性vEM数据及下游校对（proofreading）流程；整体架构轻量，可在消费级硬件上高效重建。

Result: 在代表正常及形态学定义的应激相关星形胶质细胞超微结构状态的四个小鼠海马数据集上，方法表现出稳健性能与泛化能力，留出测试集平均Dice为0.92、加权Dice为0.95。

Conclusion: AstroSeg-vEM为大规模星形胶质细胞超微结构分析及未来星形胶质细胞—神经元相互作用研究提供了可扩展、易获取的平台，降低了对人工和算力的依赖。

Abstract: The morphology of astrocytes is closely associated with their ability to support and modulate neural circuit functions; however, its reconstruction from volume electron microscopy (vEM) data remains labor-intensive and computationally resource-intensive, placing substantial barriers on its broader adoption and scalability despite the high spatial resolution of vEM. We present AstroSeg-vEM, a lightweight, astrocyte-centric framework for morphology-preserving astrocyte segmentation, 3D reconstruction, and quantitative morphological analysis in vEM images. AstroSeg-vEM combines transformer-based 2D segmentation with dedicated post-processing methods and operates directly on high-resolution EM sections, making it well suited to anisotropic vEM data and downstream proofreading workflows while preserving the full in-plane resolution of the original EM data. Across four mouse hippocampal datasets representing normal and morphologically defined stress-associated astrocyte ultrastructural states, AstroSeg-vEM demonstrates robust performance and generalization, achieving average Dice and weighted Dice values of 0.92 and 0.95, respectively, on a held-out test dataset. Its lightweight architecture enables efficient, high-fidelity reconstruction on consumer-grade hardware, providing a scalable and accessible platform for large-scale astrocyte ultrastructure analysis and future astrocyte-neuron interaction studies.

</details>


### [75] [Persistent Metric-Semantic Maritime Scene Memory from Open-Vocabulary 3D Scene Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13903)
*Kim Alexander Christensen,Dina Zimmermann,Martin Steinert,Eleni Kelasidi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文将代表性户外开放词表三维场景图方法迁移到海事环境，经度量-语义整合与几何约束的多视角VLM推理后处理，构建持久的海事场景记忆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有三维场景图方法主要面向室内与陆地环境；户外开放词表方法在海事环境中的迁移性、所需适配尚不明确，而海上空间组织需求与陆地假设存在差异。

Method: 保留原映射方法并对其累积证据做后处理：先用度量-语义整合方法，再用几何接地（geometry-grounded）的多视角VLM推理将残片归并为物理实体，并按观测把实体聚合为局部地点；同时利用地图先验与船载水面/结构观测构建海事宏观区域，并与局部地点关联。

Result: 零样本户外3DSG在多数几何支撑物体上与标注真值一致，但存在明显物理实体碎片化，且陆地高层假设不适配海事空间组织；本方法在实体碎片化程度上优于基线与单独的几何-语义整合组件，支持重复航行中固定局部地点的对应，并在评审查询上提升上下文检索效果。

Conclusion: 通过面向物体/地点身份与空间抽象的方法，可将户外开放词表地图适配为持久的海事场景记忆。

Abstract: Three-dimensional scene graphs (3DSGs) can provide robots with persistent metric-semantic representations for spatial reasoning, but the existing methods have primarily been developed for indoor and terrestrial environments. In this paper we investigate how a representative outdoor open-vocabulary method transfers to maritime environments and which adaptations are needed. Our experiments show that zero-shot representative outdoor 3DSG descriptions agree with our labeled ground truth for a majority of geometry-supported objects on our data, but the resulting map exhibits considerable physical-entity fragmentation, and its terrestrial higher-level assumptions don't capture maritime spatial organization needs. We therefore keep the underlying mapping method and post-process its accumulated evidence. We show a metric-semantic consolidation method followed by geometry-grounded multi-view vision-language model (VLM) reasoning that reconciles remaining fragments into physical entities and groups entities through observations into local places. In parallel, we construct maritime macro regions using map priors and onboard water and structure observations, and associate them with the local places. The resulting representation reduces physical-entity fragmentation beyond both the baseline and our geometry-semantic consolidation component alone, supports correspondence of fixed local places across repeated maritime traversals, and improves contextual retrieval on our reviewed queries over tested baselines. These results show how an outdoor open-vocabulary map can be adapted into persistent maritime scene memory with our methods for object/place identity and spatial abstractions.

</details>


### [76] [A 10K drone image dataset with component-level annotations for rooftop photovoltaic inspection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13892)
*Penglei Zhang,Wan Zhang,Ruizhe Deng,Bo Chen,Wenhao Duan,Jinhe Zhang,Yanzhen Zhang,Qi Chen,Zhiling Guo,Haoran Zhang,Yuntian Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 PVIS-10K，一个面向屋顶光伏组件级巡检的无人机图像数据集，含 10,065 张高分辨率 RGB 图像、覆盖 22 座屋顶电站的 63,632 个物理光伏组件，提供 3,516,675 个图像级多边形标注，并给出实例匹配与前景覆盖的全分辨率基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组件级信息对屋顶光伏巡检至关重要，但在复杂场景下识别单个组件仍具挑战；现有数据缺乏组件级标注、多角度斜视观测与跨电站泛化评估能力。

Method: 无人机在宁波、绍兴、深圳采集多角度斜视高分辨率 RGB 图像；采用三维勾绘、重投影与逐图复核的流程生成标注，以 COCO 格式存储；划分电站不相交（station-disjoint）子集以评估站内与跨城市未见电站的泛化；使用四种监督模型与文本提示的零样本 SAM 3 建立实例匹配与前景覆盖基准。

Result: 构建 10,065 张图像、63,632 个物理组件、3,516,675 条图像级多边形标注的数据集，覆盖安装高度与尺度变化、密集排布、遮挡、阴影与反光等复杂情形，并提供全分辨率实例匹配与前景覆盖基准结果。

Conclusion: PVIS-10K 支持组件定位、轮廓描绘与基于图像的计数，为无人机屋顶光伏巡检研究提供数据与评测基础。

Abstract: Component-level information supports rooftop photovoltaic (PV) inspection, but recognizing individual components across complex scenes remains challenging. We present PVIS-10K, a dataset of 10,065 drone images with component-level annotations covering 63,632 physical PV modules across 22 rooftop installations. The high-resolution red-green-blue (RGB) images were acquired by unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs, also known as drones) in Ningbo, Shaoxing and Shenzhen, China. Multi-angle oblique observations capture installation-height and scale variation, dense arrangements, occlusion, shadows and reflections. Three-dimensional delineation, reprojection and image-by-image review produced 3,516,675 image-level polygon annotations, stored in Common Objects in Context (COCO) format. Station-disjoint partitions support evaluation on unseen stations within and across cities. Four supervised models and text-prompted zero-shot Segment Anything Model 3 (SAM 3) provide full-resolution benchmarks of instance matching and foreground coverage. PVIS-10K supports component localization, delineation and image-based counting for drone-based rooftop PV inspection research.

</details>


### [77] [AgenticConflictLens: From Long Traffic Videos to Verifiable Conflict Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13540)
*Boshra Khalili,Sonia Moshfeghi,Jinwoo Jang,Andrew W Smyth*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出AgenticConflictLens，一个智能体式、查询条件化的框架，用于从长时固定摄像头交通视频中高效、可审计地提取冲突事件，仅检查约10%视频即取得最高F1与tIoU。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交通摄像头持续记录道路使用者行为，可提供超越事故报告的前瞻性安全证据；但冲突、险情、不安全转向、急刹、人行横道交互等安全关键事件稀少且短暂，淹没在大量常规视频中。现有交通视频流水线虽能抽取检测、轨迹与风险分数，却均匀处理全部视频、不随分析者查询自适应；视觉-语言方法虽提升了场景理解，但往往缺乏针对交通参与者级冲突的专业验证。

Method: 给定自然语言安全查询，Traffic-Aware Controller将结构化交通记忆与专用冲突分析工具结合，决定搜索位置、检查哪些道路使用者、以及验证或拒绝哪些证据；采用由粗到细的搜索，先检索候选时间区间，再有选择地做细粒度验证。输出冲突记录，包含精化时间区间、参与者对、语义区域、轨迹、运动证据、不确定性、拒斥理由与支撑视频片段。

Result: 在Bellevue与NYC DOT共96小时标注数据上，两数据集冲突存在性F1与时间IoU均最高：Bellevue 0.84 F1/0.77 tIoU，NYC DOT 0.80 F1/0.73 tIoU，且分别仅检查8.7%与11.9%的视频。

Conclusion: 结果支持可扩展、证据支撑的城市交通安全分析，兼顾效率、可审计性与参与者级冲突验证。

Abstract: Traffic cameras continuously record urban road-user behavior, providing proactive safety evidence beyond crash reports. Yet safety-critical events - conflicts, near misses, unsafe turns, sudden braking, and crosswalk interactions - are rare and brief, buried in hours of routine footage. Existing traffic-video pipelines extract detections, trajectories, and risk scores but process footage uniformly, without adapting to an analyst's query. Vision-language methods improve scene understanding but often lack traffic-specific verification of participant-level conflicts. We introduce AgenticConflictLens, an agentic, query-conditioned framework for efficient, auditable conflict extraction from long fixed-camera traffic videos. Given a natural-language safety query, a Traffic-Aware Controller combines structured traffic memory with specialized conflict-analysis tools to choose where to search, which road users to examine, and which evidence to verify or reject. A coarse-to-fine search retrieves candidate intervals and applies detailed verification selectively. The framework produces conflict records with refined time intervals, participant pairs, semantic regions, trajectories, motion evidence, uncertainty, rejection reasons, and supporting clips. On 96 annotated hours from Bellevue and NYC DOT, AgenticConflictLens achieves the highest conflict-existence F1 and temporal IoU on both datasets: 0.84 F1/0.77 tIoU on Bellevue and 0.80 F1/0.73 tIoU on NYC DOT, while inspecting only 8.7% and 11.9% of video, respectively. These results support scalable, evidence-grounded urban traffic-safety analysis.

</details>


### [78] [GroundCascade: Selective Second Opinions for GUI Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13177)
*Adrian Phoulady,Hady Ahmady Phoulady*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练的 GroundCascade 控制器：通过比较 grounder 在全屏与放大视图下的预测一致性来决定是否调用互补模型，在 ScreenSpot-Pro 达到 85.33%，并以更少调用次数超越独立 two-pass grounder。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GUI 定位中放大视图能揭示小目标但会丢失识别所需的上下文；无条件同时获取两个模型的两遍预测代价高，需要判断"何时"才值得调用第二模型。

Method: 免训练控制器：利用 grounder 全屏预测与放大预测之间的一致性作为触发信号，选择放大预测同时保留被佐证的全屏位置；可选 Finalizer 在仍有分歧时基于无标记联合裁剪区域预测（Full 模式），省略则为两阶段 Selective 变体。

Result: ScreenSpot-Pro 85.33%（Indeed-UI-32B/8B/32B）、OSWorld-G-Refined 可见目标子集 88.43%（Qwen3.6-27B/3.8-27B/3.8-27B），分别比更强的独立 two-pass grounder 提升 2.66 和 2.35 个百分点；UI-Vision 类别均衡准确率 72.20%（Selective 72.28%）。Full 模式每请求 2.42–2.73 次调用，比无条件双模型两遍少 31–39%；匹配第二模型调用预算下选择性获取增益 1.52–2.56 点，分层随机获取仅 0.22–0.69 点。

Conclusion: 一致性驱动的按需模型调用可在提升 GUI 定位精度的同时显著降低调用成本，方法在官方 ScreenSpot-Pro 榜单领先，并在 OSWorld-G-Refined 与 UI-Vision 上超过已报告的 GUI 专用模型对比（受外部评测协议差异限制）。

Abstract: Graphical user interface (GUI) grounding maps a screenshot and a natural-language instruction to the screen location where an agent should act. Zoomed views can reveal small targets but may lose the context needed to identify them. We introduce GroundCascade, a training-free controller that uses agreement between a grounder's full-screen and zoomed predictions to decide when to consult a complementary model. It selects between zoomed predictions while preserving corroborated full-screen locations. An optional Finalizer predicts from an unmarked joint crop when disagreement remains (Full); omitting it yields the two-stage Selective variant. GroundCascade reaches 85.33% on ScreenSpot-Pro with Indeed-UI-32B/8B/32B and 88.43% on the visible-target OSWorld-G-Refined subset with Qwen3.6-27B/3.8-27B/3.8-27B, gains of 2.66 and 2.35 percentage points over the stronger standalone two-pass grounder. On UI-Vision, GroundCascade reaches 72.20% category-balanced accuracy, with Selective slightly higher at 72.28%. Across these three evaluations, GroundCascade in Full mode uses 2.42-2.73 calls per request, 31-39% fewer than unconditionally acquiring both models' two-pass predictions. At matched second-model call budgets, selective acquisition gains 1.52-2.56 points over standalone Two-pass, compared with 0.22-0.69 points from stratified random acquisition. GroundCascade leads the official ScreenSpot-Pro leaderboard and exceeds reported GUI-specialist comparisons on OSWorld-G-Refined and UI-Vision, subject to differences in external evaluation protocols.

</details>


### [79] [A Lightweight Differentiable Method for Real-Time Skin Rendering and Digitization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14451)
*Mario Marquez-Balduque,Carlos Rodriguez-Pardo,Jorge Lopez-Moreno*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出可微屏幕空间次表面散射(SSSS)框架，从单光单视角轻量采集直接估计适配实时渲染管线的次表面散射参数，实现可生产使用的皮肤外观获取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统皮肤逆渲染依赖离线路径追踪估计物理属性，其结果无法直接映射到游戏引擎等交互应用中使用的近似着色器，存在离线物理参数与实时渲染管线之间的鸿沟。

Method: 构建可微SSSS框架，联合优化空间变化的基础纹理与散射距离；按不同面部区域分别估计参数以刻画局部皮肤厚度差异，并生成可用于生产运行时引擎的混合profile map；采用低成本单光、单视角采集装置，并使用聚焦次表面散射主导区域的掩码损失函数驱动优化。

Result: 在覆盖Monk肤色量表的45名真实个体数据集上验证了人口学泛化性；辅以合成数据、验证单光假设的多光装置以及确认角度一致性的倾斜视角配置进行补充评估，结果表明该方法能从轻量输入中有效恢复散射参数，生成与标准实时着色器直接兼容的逼真皮肤外观。

Conclusion: 通过直接估计面向实时管线的次表面散射参数，弥合了离线逆渲染与交互式近似着色器之间的差距，为实用、易获取的皮肤外观采集提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Inverse rendering for skin typically relies on offline path tracing to estimate physical properties, which fail to map directly to the approximate shaders utilized in interactive applications, such as game engines. We bridge this gap by directly estimating subsurface scattering parameters tailored for real-time rendering pipelines. We introduce a differentiable Screen-Space Subsurface Scattering (SSSS) framework that enables the joint optimization of spatially varying base textures and scattering distances. To account for local variations in skin thickness, we estimate these parameters across distinct facial regions, generating a blended profile map ready for production runtime engines. Our pipeline utilizes a low-cost, single-light, single-view capture setup designed for accessible and practical appearance acquisition. Optimization is driven by a masked loss function targeting regions where subsurface scattering effects are dominant. We validate our approach on a real-world dataset of $45$ individuals across the Monk Skin Tone Scale, ensuring demographic generalization. Furthermore, we complement this evaluation with synthetic data, a multi-light setup validating our single-light assumption, and a tilted-view configuration confirming angular consistency. Results demonstrate that our method effectively recovers scattering parameters from lightweight inputs, yielding realistic skin appearance directly compatible with standard real-time shaders.

</details>


### [80] [Metric-Aligned Group Relative Policy Optimization for Explainable Deepfake Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14436)
*Luka Dragar,Peter Peer,Vitomir Štruc,Borut Batagelj*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出解耦的"先预测后解释"流水线用于可解释深度伪造检测：视觉基础模型集成分类，Qwen3-VL-8B生成决策条件解释，并用指标对齐的GRPO优化解释器，在XPlainVerse上取得复杂BERTScore-F1第一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度伪造检测器仅以真/假分类评估，而实际应用需要给出预测背后的视觉证据解释；联合视觉-语言模型在真实性判断上表现不佳，甚至为被篡改图像生成"真实"叙述。

Method: 解耦的predict-then-explain流水线：先用视觉基础模型集成进行分类，再由Qwen3-VL-8B基于决策生成视觉证据解释；对伪造图像，第二个Qwen3-VL-8B在不看图像的情况下将解释压缩为面向非专家的简短摘要；并对两个解释器施加指标对齐的GRPO优化以贴合评测标准。

Result: 在相同条件下，GRPO将复杂BERTScore-F1从0.686提升至0.712，同时改善接地性（实体与证据F1）；提交方案在复杂BERTScore-F1上排名第一，总排名第七。

Conclusion: 将分类与解释解耦，并以评测指标为奖励对齐解释器，可有效提升深度伪造解释的质量与接地性，验证了该流水线在可解释深度伪造检测挑战中的有效性。

Abstract: Deepfake detectors are commonly evaluated on real/fake classification alone, but real-world applications require explanations of the visual evidence behind each prediction. Joint vision-language models struggle with authenticity assessment, producing "real" narratives for manipulated images. To address this, we propose a decoupled predict-then-explain pipeline as our submission to the ACM Multimedia 2026 Explainable Deepfake Detection Challenge, with methods trained and evaluated on the XPlainVerse benchmark. A vision-foundation-model ensemble first classifies the image, then a Qwen3-VL-8B model generates a decision-conditioned explanation of the visual evidence. For fake images, a second Qwen3-VL-8B compresses that text into a short non-expert summary without using the image. We further apply metric-aligned Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) to optimize both explainers against the challenge criteria. Under matched conditions, GRPO raises complex BERTScore-F1 from 0.686 to 0.712 and also improves grounding (entity and evidence F1); our submission ranked first on complex BERTScore-F1 and seventh overall.

</details>


### [81] [OnlyAct: Extracting Action Representations from Foundation Models for World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14420)
*Runjia Li,Minghao Chen,Junyu Xie,Philip Torr,Andrea Vedaldi,Tomas Jakab*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种无标签对比适应框架，以预训练基础模型V-JEPA 2为起点，通过重塑预训练嵌入空间几何来提取动作信息、丢弃场景信息，替代传统"重建瓶颈"式潜在动作学习方法，并在操纵、导航、游戏、驾驶四类基准的统一评估协议中取得更强的动作表达力与跨场景迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频世界模型的训练受限于动作监督：动作标签稀缺、规格碎片化且无法随无标注视频预训练语料规模化。常见的"动作编码器+下一帧重建解码器"联合训练方案存在两点局限：(1) 从零学习动作空间限制了对未见域的泛化；(2) 重建瓶颈在表达力与解耦之间权衡——瓶颈过紧丢失细节，过松则在域偏移下发生场景信息泄漏。

Method: 不再从零学习动作，而以预训练基础模型V-JEPA 2为起点（其帧对嵌入经大规模多样视频预训练已含丰富动作信息）。核心是从嵌入中提取动作并剔除场景：设计无标签对比适应框架，将"不同场景、相同运动"的帧对拉近，"相同场景、不同运动"的帧对推远，从而重塑预训练空间几何，且不引入重建瓶颈。同时提出覆盖动作表达力、场景不变性、动作条件视频生成质量三轴，横跨机器人操纵、相机导航、游戏与驾驶基准的统一评估协议（多数基准在适应阶段未见）。

Result: 与无标注潜在动作方法相比，所提表示在动作表达力上显著更强，同时场景不变性与之相当；由此构建的世界模型能更忠实地将示范动作迁移到新场景。

Conclusion: 以预训练模型的帧对嵌入为先验、用无标签对比学习进行几何重塑，可绕开从零学习动作空间与重建瓶颈的两难，得到既具表达力又具场景不变性的潜在动作表示，为可扩展、可跨域迁移的动作条件视频世界模型提供了一条可行路径。

Abstract: Video world models promise general-purpose interactive simulators, but their training is limited by action supervision: action labels are scarce, fragmented across incompatible specifications, and do not scale with the unlabeled video pretraining corpus. A common workaround jointly trains an action encoder to compress consecutive frames into a latent action and a decoder to reconstruct the next frame from the previous frame and that latent, forcing the latent to capture only new information. This has two limitations: learning the action space from scratch can limit generalization to unseen domains, and the bottleneck trades expressiveness for separation, with tighter settings losing nuance and looser ones risking scene leakage under domain shifts. We argue for a different starting point: rather than learning actions from scratch, we start from a pretrained foundation model (V-JEPA 2), whose embeddings of a frame pair already carry substantial action information after large-scale, diverse video pretraining. The remaining challenge is to extract the action while discarding the scene. We propose a label-free contrastive adaptation framework that pulls together frame pairs sharing the same motion under a different scene and pushes apart pairs from the same scene with different motion, reshaping the geometry of the pretrained space without imposing a reconstruction bottleneck. We introduce a unified evaluation protocol along three axes (action expressiveness, scene invariance, and action-conditioned video generation quality) across robot manipulation, camera navigation, game, and driving benchmarks, most of which are unseen during adaptation. Compared with annotation-free latent-action methods, our representations are substantially more expressive while matching their scene invariance, and they yield world models that transfer demonstrated actions to new scenes more faithfully.

</details>


### [82] [Location Matters: Sparse Latent-Variance Attacks for Face Privacy against Diffusion Personalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14254)
*Shuting Yang,Shuxin Li,Linyan Dai,Xinwei Zhang,Dong Huang,Renyang Liu,See-Kiong Ng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SEAL：一种稀疏对抗保护框架，仅改动面部图像5%的空间位置，通过可微top-k掩码联合优化扰动位置与幅值，并配合面向潜变量方差的攻击目标，在保持高画质的同时有效阻止扩散模型的身份个性化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文生图扩散模型可仅凭少量参考照习得个体身份，带来未授权生成风险。现有主动防御依赖稠密 L∞ 有界扰动，累积失真在人脸结构处易被察觉；而提升画质的方法往往牺牲保护效果。因此需要兼顾高画质与强保护的新范式。

Method: 提出SEAL稀疏对抗保护框架：(1) 可微 top-k 掩码方案，联合优化扰动的位置与取值，仅修改5%空间位置；(2) 在冻结VAE编码器的潜分布上设计方差导向目标，破坏身份一致的表示；(3) 引入显著性引导的分散惩罚，抑制扰动在感知显著面部区域聚集。

Result: 在CelebA-HQ与VGGFace2上，SEAL在对比方法中取得最佳保护图像质量：PSNR超过41 dB、SSIM达0.97、LPIPS至多0.07；同时在扩散个性化后身份相似度降至至多0.15。

Conclusion: 稀疏位置优化能够在保持高视觉质量的前提下实现强身份保护，表明稀疏扰动是替代稠密 L∞ 防御的可行且更优的路线。代码与数据已开源。

Abstract: Text-to-image diffusion models can learn and reproduce an individual's identity from only a few reference photos, creating risks of unauthorized and misleading content generation. Proactive defenses address this risk by adding adversarial perturbations to facial images before release, thereby disrupting identity acquisition during downstream personalization. However, existing methods typically rely on dense $L_\infty$-bounded perturbations, whose accumulated distortions can become visible around facial structures. Recent methods improve protected-image quality, but often at the cost of reduced protection effectiveness. In this paper, we propose Sparse Encoder Attack on Latent Variance (SEAL), a sparse adversarial protection framework that modifies only 5% of the spatial locations in a facial image. SEAL combines a differentiable top-k masking scheme that jointly optimizes perturbation locations and values with a variance-oriented objective over the frozen VAE encoder's latent distribution to disrupt identity-consistent representations. A saliency-guided dispersion penalty further discourages perturbation concentration in perceptually salient facial regions. Experiments on CelebA-HQ and VGGFace2 show that SEAL achieves the best protected-image quality among the compared methods, exceeding 41 dB PSNR with 0.97 SSIM and at most 0.07 LPIPS, while reducing identity similarity to at most 0.15 after diffusion personalization. These results demonstrate that sparse location optimization enables strong identity protection while preserving high visual quality. Code and data are available at https://github.com/Lvxan/SEAL

</details>


### [83] [Continuous 3D Spatial Transcriptomics Reconstruction via Histology-Informed Implicit Neural Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14129)
*Kaito Shiku,Ryoma Bise,Ahtisham Fazeel Abbasi,Yuichiro Iwashita,Shumpei Takezaki,Andreas Dengel,Muhammad Nabeel Asim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出HIM-INR，一种组织学信息引导的多任务隐式神经表示方法，从稀疏3D空间转录组切片连续重建基因表达。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D ST测量由离散2D切片构成，因采集成本高导致采样稀疏，亟需从稀疏观测中恢复连续三维基因表达。

Method: HIM-INR采用共享骨干的隐式神经表示（INR），联合重建基因表达谱与组织学表示，并利用密集采样的组织学图像作为辅助空间监督进行多任务训练；训练完成后可在任意3D坐标预测基因表达，无需组织学图像输入。

Result: 在两个3D ST数据集上，重建效果优于现有方法，且缺失ST切片数量越多，优势越明显。

Conclusion: 组织学信息作为辅助监督可有效缓解3D ST切片稀疏问题，实现高质量连续三维基因表达重建。

Abstract: Three-dimensional spatial transcriptomics (3D ST) enables the analysis of gene-expression patterns across tissue depths. However, 3D ST measurements are acquired from discrete 2D tissue sections, which are often sparsely sampled due to high acquisition costs. To address this limitation, we propose a histology-informed multi-task implicit neural representation (HIM-INR) for continuous 3D gene-expression reconstruction from sparse ST observations. HIM-INR jointly reconstructs gene-expression profiles and histological representations using an INR with a shared backbone, leveraging densely sampled histological images as auxiliary spatial supervision. Once trained, HIM-INR predicts gene expression at arbitrary 3D coordinates without requiring histological images. Experiments on two 3D ST datasets demonstrate improved reconstruction over existing methods, particularly as the number of missing ST sections increases.

</details>


### [84] [Stylization as Geodesic Navigation on Diffusion Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14097)
*Yan Li,Zehao Li,Xinlong Sun,Fuchen Long,Xi Chen,Wenhan Luo,Yike Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练框架Stylus，将人脸风格迁移建模为预训练扩散模型弯曲潜流形上的测地线导航，通过内容-风格潜变量插值与自注意力query优化双控制，实现身份保真与风格强度的连续细粒度调节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有人脸风格化方法（免训练或指令驱动）难以在"艺术化外观"与"身份不变"之间取得控制：要么身份漂移，要么风格化效果微弱。作者希望获得连续、可调的风格化强度控制，同时严格保持身份关键的面部结构。

Method: 将人脸风格迁移视为预训练扩散模型弯曲潜流形上的导航问题，而非在平坦欧氏空间中直接插值。对插值得到的内容-风格点，在pullback度量下最小化比例加权离散测地线能量以细化该点，并用Jacobian的秩一幂迭代近似使计算可行。该原理衍生出两种互补控制：1）内容-风格潜变量插值，实现平滑语义融合；2）自注意力query优化，实现空间自适应风格注入。二者构成双控制机制，无需额外训练。

Result: 实验表明，Stylus在竞争性风格化强度下取得了最强的身份保持与内容保持效果，并支持对风格化强度的连续细粒度控制。

Conclusion: 通过将风格迁移重构为潜流形上的测地优化，Stylus以零训练代价实现了身份-风格的可控权衡，为免训练人脸风格化提供了一种几何驱动的双控制范式。

Abstract: Facial stylization asks for a delicate transformation: the image should look unmistakably artistic, yet the person should remain unmistakably the same. Existing training-free and instruction-driven methods often fail to expose such control, either drifting away from identity or producing only weak stylization. We introduce Stylus, a training-free framework that treats facial style transfer as navigation on the curved latent manifold of a pretrained diffusion model. Rather than interpolating in flat Euclidean space, \method refines the interpolated content--style point by lowering a ratio-weighted discrete geodesic energy under the pullback metric, made tractable by a rank-one power-iteration approximation of the Jacobian. This principle drives two complementary controls: content--style latent interpolation for smooth semantic fusion, and self-attention query optimization for spatially adaptive style injection. The resulting dual-control mechanism provides continuous, fine-grained control over stylization strength while preserving identity-critical facial structure, all without additional training. Experiments show that Stylus achieves the strongest identity and content preservation at competitive stylization strength.

</details>


### [85] [ViGA-FLAME: Aligning Latent Visual and Geometric Representations for Robust 3D Head Expression Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14218)
*Wen Hao He,Jiacheng Wu,Jie Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ViGA-FLAME两阶段框架：先用PG-VAE学习规范头部表面与FLAME参数的统一潜空间，再用非对称注意力的ViGA将预训练视觉编码器特征映射到该潜空间，实现鲁棒的单图3D头部表情估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从单张图像估计3D头部表情本质上是将视觉外观映射到面部几何。现有前馈方法在严重面部形变和大视角下鲁棒性不足，且缺乏语义视觉特征与结构化几何潜空间之间的有效对齐。

Method: 两阶段框架。第一阶段：Parametric-Geometric VAE（PG-VAE）联合学习规范头部表面与FLAME参数的统一表示，支持细节表面重建与可解释参数恢复；通过几何与参数监督以及两个解码分支间的一致性，将表面细节与参数结构耦合。第二阶段：可训练的Visual-Geometric Adapter（ViGA）将预训练语义视觉编码器的特征映射进该潜空间，采用非对称注意力对齐语义图像特征与结构化几何信息，且不改变原视觉流。

Result: 在严重面部形变与大视角条件下仍能稳健估计3D头部表情，相比竞争性前馈方法高效提升鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 以几何潜先验为桥梁、用非对称注意力做特征对齐，可将图像证据落地到结构化先验上，兼顾细节重建与参数可解释性，并提升极端条件下的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Estimating 3D head expression from a single image requires mapping visual appearance to underlying facial geometry. We propose ViGA-FLAME, a two-stage framework that aligns semantic visual features with a structured geometric latent space. First, a Parametric-Geometric Variational Autoencoder (PG-VAE) learns a unified representation of canonical head surfaces and FLAME parameters, supporting both detailed surface reconstruction and interpretable parameter recovery. Supervision on geometry and parameters, together with consistency between the two decoding branches, connects surface detail with parametric structure. Second, a trainable Visual-Geometric Adapter (ViGA) maps features from a pretrained semantic visual encoder into this latent space. Asymmetric attention enables ViGA to align semantic image features from the visual encoder with the structured geometric information represented in the PG-VAE latent space, without altering the original visual stream. By grounding image evidence in this latent prior, ViGA-FLAME enables robust 3D head expression estimation across severe facial deformations and large viewing angles, efficiently improving robustness over competing feed-forward methods.

</details>


### [86] [DailyWildfireCAN: A Dataset and Benchmark for Next-Day Wildfire Spread Prediction in Canada](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14207)
*Mahacine Ettahri,Hager Radi Abdelwahed,Toluwaleke Olutayo,Daniel Aloise,Gaétan Marceau Caron*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文提出 DailyWildfireCAN，一个面向加拿大全境次日野火蔓延预测的多模态、多时序数据集与基准，空间分辨率90米、时间分辨率24小时，覆盖2017–2024八个火季共2440起野火事件、33个特征，并建立单时序与多时序模型对比及多维评估框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习野火蔓延研究主要依赖以美国为主的公开数据集，加拿大（广袤针叶林、暖化气候下极端火季频发）的火灾环境代表性不足；同时该任务需刻画气象、可燃物与地形的复杂交互，依赖具有代表性、高分辨率的数据集与可靠评估。

Method: 构建 DailyWildfireCAN 数据集：以90米空间分辨率、24小时时间分辨率追踪多日火事件中的连续火线推进，保留火缘几何、局部可燃物变化与地形效应的细尺度信息；整合地形、气象、土地覆盖、植被与森林结构、人类影响指数、卫星影像等5类模态共33个特征；建立覆盖单时序与多时序预测模型的综合基准，配套多维评估框架，考察在野火特征、地理区域、时序条件下的稳健性，空间预测质量，以及对参考标签差异的敏感性。

Result: 数据集覆盖2017–2024年八个火季、2440起野火事件，含33个多模态特征；基准实验系统比较了单时序与多时序模型，并通过多维评估框架揭示模型在不同火行为、区域与时间条件下的表现差异、空间预测质量及对标签变化的敏感性。

Conclusion: DailyWildfireCAN 填补了加拿大野火环境下高分辨率、多模态、多时序数据与基准的空白，可作为推进每日野火蔓延预测研究的标准化平台，并支持对模型稳健性与评估可靠性的系统研究。

Abstract: Wildfires represent a critical ecological and socio-economic challenge in Canada, where vast boreal forests and a warming climate have amplified the frequency and severity of extreme fire seasons. Predicting next-day wildfire spread can support fire management, but requires capturing complex interactions among weather, fuels, and terrain. Deep learning has emerged as a promising approach to learning these relationships from heterogeneous environmental data, yet its development and evaluation depend on representative datasets. Existing deep learning research on wildfire spread relies predominantly on US-focused datasets, leaving Canadian fire environments underrepresented. To address this gap, we present DailyWildfireCAN, a multi-modal, multi-temporal dataset and benchmark for predicting next-day wildfire spread across Canada. The dataset tracks continuous fire progression over multi-day events at a high spatial and temporal resolution of 90-m and 24-hour, respectively. This resolution preserves fine-scale information relevant to fire-edge geometry, local fuel variations, and terrain effects, supporting research toward improved daily wildfire spread forecasting. DailyWildfireCAN covers 2,440 wildfire events across eight fire seasons 2017-2024 and integrates 33 features across diverse modalities, including topography, meteorology, land cover, vegetation and forest structure, human influence indices, and satellite imagery. We establish a comprehensive benchmark comparing mono-temporal and multi-temporal prediction models, accompanied by a multi-dimensional evaluation framework assessing robustness across wildfire characteristics, geographic regions, and temporal conditions, spatial prediction quality, and sensitivity to reference label variations.

</details>


### [87] [Sim(3)-TurboReg: Robust and Efficient Scale-Aware Registration via Tri-Scale Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14135)
*Zijun Zhou,Shaocheng Yan,Zhenjun Zhao,Yutong Hu,Ke Yang,Yingdong Gu,Pengcheng Shi,Jiayuan Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Sim(3)-TurboReg，用三尺度一致性（TSC）准则替代 TurboReg 中的绝对距离约束来构建兼容图，实现高离群率下高效准确的 Sim(3) 配准，并集成到流式系统中实现公里级高保真重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前馈式 3D 基础模型几何重建保真度高，但受推理显存限制无法原生处理长序列；现有流式系统用滑窗分块规避，却引入严重的单目尺度歧义，需在高离群污染下仍保持准确高效的 Sim(3) 配准。

Method: 将 TurboReg 的兼容图构建由绝对距离约束改为严格的 Tri-Scale Consistency（TSC）准则，理论分析表明 TSC 可同时抑制误匹配离群点与坐标畸变离群点；再把所得求解器嵌入现有流式系统。

Result: 在 90% 离群率的受控未知尺度配准中，精度与 RANSAC 相当，中位延迟降低 92.5%；在 KITTI 上的 DA3-Streaming 中，平均 ATE RMSE 改善 22.2%，平均端到端时间减少 16.6%。

Conclusion: TSC 准则使 Sim(3) 配准在高离群、未知尺度条件下兼顾精度与效率，可作为即插即用模块提升前馈 3D 模型的公里级流式重建能力。

Abstract: Feed-forward 3D foundation models recover geometry at high fidelity, yet inference memory constraints keep them from processing long sequences natively. Current streaming systems work around this limit with sliding-window chunking, which introduces severe monocular scale ambiguity. Resolving this ambiguity requires Sim(3) registration that stays accurate and efficient under outlier contamination. We introduce Sim(3)-TurboReg, which reformulates the compatibility graph construction of TurboReg by replacing absolute distance constraints with a strict Tri-Scale Consistency (TSC) criterion. Our analysis shows that TSC suppresses both mismatching and coordinate-distortion outliers. We integrate the resulting solver into existing streaming systems and obtain high-fidelity, kilometer-scale reconstruction for feed-forward models. On controlled unknown-scale registration at 90% outliers, ours matches RANSAC accuracy with 92.5% lower median latency. In DA3-Streaming on KITTI, ours improves mean ATE RMSE by 22.2% and reduces mean end-to-end time by 16.6%. Code: https://github.com/ivan-zzj/Sim3-TurboReg

</details>


### [88] [RS-JEPA: Remote Sensing Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture with Scan-Path Views for Cross-Sensor Image Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14496)
*Tanishq Rachamalla,Srishti Kaushik,Muhammad Attique Khan,Gustau Camps-Valls,Swalpa Kumar Roy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 RS-JEPA：基于共享 Vision Mamba 编码器的紧凑联合嵌入预测框架，用同一图像两种扫描顺序构造自监督视图，实现跨传感器遥感图像检索，F1@5 最高且参数量仅 3.88M。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨传感器（SAR-光学、PAN-MS）自监督检索方法存在缺陷：重建传感器特定像素细节、仅与共配准孪生图像对齐、依赖 118–173M 甚至 398M 参数的大 Transformer，效率与迁移性不足。

Method: 1) 双传感器共享 Vision Mamba 状态空间编码器；2) 扫描路径视图（scan-path views）：将同一图像两种读取顺序作为两个像素完全相同的视图，由一种顺序的可见 patch 预测另一顺序的隐藏 patch；3) 对抗视图耦合（AVC）学习最难的可见 patch 划分以保持预测难度；4) 邻域一致性损失对齐两传感器的场景相似度结构。

Result: 在 SAR-光学（BEN-14K、CBRSIR-VS）与 PAN-MS（DSRSID）基准上，十二种复现方法中 F1@5 最高（72.92、80.35、94.43）；检索网络仅 3.88M 参数（训练时 14.57M），比已有 JEPA 检索器少 8–16 倍。

Conclusion: 读取顺序可作为一种有效的自监督视图来源；紧凑状态空间框架可在不依赖大型 Transformer 的前提下取得领先的跨传感器检索性能，但仍如既有方法一样依赖共配准图像对训练。

Abstract: Cross-sensor remote sensing image retrieval aims to find scenes with the same content across heterogeneous sensors, such as SAR and optical or panchromatic (PAN) and multispectral (MS) imagery, without manual labels. Existing self-supervised methods either reconstruct sensor-specific pixel detail, align each image only with its co-registered twin, or rely on large transformers with 118-173M parameters (and up to 398M). We introduce RS-JEPA, a compact joint-embedding predictive framework built on a Vision Mamba encoder shared by both sensors. Since a state-space encoder reads an image as an ordered sequence of patches, we propose scan-path views, which treat two reading orders of the same image as two views with identical pixels and predict the hidden patches in one order from the visible patches in the other.To keep this prediction challenging, we propose Adversarial View Coupling (AVC), which learns the hardest split of the visible patches, and a neighbourhood consensus loss that aligns the scene similarities of both sensors. Extensive experiments on SAR-optical (BEN-14K, CBRSIR-VS) and PAN-MS (DSRSID) benchmarks demonstrate that RS-JEPA achieves the highest F1@5 among twelve re-implemented methods (72.92, 80.35 and 94.43) with a retrieval network of only 3.88M parameters (14.57M in training, 8-16times fewer than prior JEPA retrievers). Like prior methods, RS-JEPA trains on co-registered pairs. Our results highlight reading order as a source of self-supervised views. code: https://github.com/Tanishq251/RSJEPA.

</details>


### [89] [UGG: Unified Generative Geometry Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14440)
*Amani Kiruga,Zhiyi Li,Ruojin Cai,Hansen Lillemark,Xiaoshan Wu,Ayush Tewari,Yilun Du*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出统一框架UGG，在单一生成模型中联合建模RGB视频与深度、相机位姿等几何表示，用于4D场景理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法割裂为前馈几何预测模型与生成式视觉合成模型，难以从部分观测中联合捕捉几何、外观与动态；同时异构数据集的几何/视觉标注子集不同，训练数据难以统一利用。

Method: 对跨模态、跨时间分配不同噪声水平，将视觉生成与几何预测统一表述为单一生成模型内的条件补全任务；该形式支持从标注子集各异的多源异构数据中学习；并在推理阶段利用从观测RGB帧提取的几何证据进行多假设搜索，无需真值几何。

Result: 在相机可控视频生成、深度估计与相机位姿预测任务上均优于非统一基线，在稀疏观测等挑战性设置下同样有效。

Conclusion: 统一生成式建模是理解4D场景的有效途径，兼具数据灵活性与推理时几何自校正能力；代码与可视化见 https://ugg-model.github.io

Abstract: Understanding 4D scenes is a core challenge in visual intelligence, requiring models to jointly capture scene geometry, appearance, and dynamics from partial observations. However, existing approaches are subdivided into feedforward models for direct geometric prediction, and generative models for visual synthesis. We introduce UGG, a unified framework for 4D scene understanding that jointly models RGB video and geometric representations such as depth and camera pose. By assigning different noise levels across modalities and time, UGG formulates visual generation and geometric prediction as conditional completion within a single generative model. This flexible formulation enables the model to learn from diverse, heterogeneous datasets with different subsets of geometric and visual annotations. Furthermore, our generative formulation enables inference-time search over multiple plausible scene hypotheses, using geometric evidence extracted from observed RGB frames to improve geometric prediction without ground truth geometry. We demonstrate UGG's effectiveness over non-unified baselines across camera-controlled video generation, depth estimation, and camera pose prediction, including in challenging sparse observation settings. Code and visualizations are available at https://ugg-model.github.io/

</details>


### [90] [TaoMate-R1: Temporal Role Specialization for Streaming Multi-Reference Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14307)
*Qijun Gan,Chenwei Zhang,Xinyang Li,Meiguang Jin,Junfeng Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TaoMate-R1：按"时间角色"而非"操作类型"分配适配参数，提升流式多参考视频生成的边界一致性与时序稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式多参考视频生成需先从指定人物/物体建立场景，再在指令变化下持续延展。已有方案按"操作"（去噪 vs 上下文写入）划分参数共享，导致同一角色在不同阶段被拆散，难以维持场景与指令变化下的一致性。

Method: 在冻结主干上为"场景建立"与"场景延续"分别配置独立低秩适配器（同一双适配器预算）；每个适配器对自身角色同时承担去噪与上下文写入；提出 Self Gradient Forcing，通过角色自身构建的历史联合训练两个角色；并用固定回放梯度分析刻画两种共享方案所允许的更新方向。

Result: 32 个多参考案例对比；10 段约两分钟、指令变化且共享教师音频条件的流：平均边界亮度跳变由 1.96 降至 0.613，边界特征相似度更高；在短交互、长流及公开参考组合上平均 CLIP 与 DINO 时序相似度更高。

Conclusion: 结果支持以"场景建立/延续"为划分依据组织适应参数，优于按操作划分，适用于流式多参考生成；代码与模型已开源。

Abstract: Streaming multi-reference video generation establishes a scene from specified people and objects, then continues it under changing instructions. We introduce TaoMate-R1, a streaming multi-reference generator that organizes adaptation parameters through temporal role specialization. It assigns separate low-rank adapters to scene establishment and continuation on a frozen backbone. Each adapter performs both denoising and context writing for its role; Self Gradient Forcing jointly trains the roles through the history they construct. This allocation contrasts with operation-based specialization, which separates denoising from context writing across all chunks. A fixed-replay gradient analysis characterizes the update directions admitted by the two sharing schemes. On 32 multi-reference cases, we compare the schemes at the same two-adapter budget. Across ten approximately two-minute streams with changing instructions and shared teacher-audio conditioning, TaoMate-R1 reduces the mean boundary luminance jump from 1.96 to 0.613, with higher boundary feature similarity. It also achieves higher mean CLIP and DINO temporal similarity across short interactions, long streams, and public reference compositions. These results support grouping adaptation parameters by scene establishment and continuation for streaming multi-reference generation. Our code and model are released at https://github.com/TaoLiveAIGC/TaoMate-H3.

</details>


### [91] [AutoThreshold: Joint Confusion-Profile Learning for Adaptive Mask Binarization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14279)
*Shaokai Wu,Xunchu Zhou,Pinzhi Wang,Bo Dong,Xiao Cui,Qi Kong,Yue Ding,Hongtao Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出AutoThreshold：一种后验阈值策略，直接预测分数bin上正负像素的联合质量，从而无重训练地为任意F_β目标选取阈值，并在息肉分割与KITTI Road上优于Otsu与标量回归。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单一验证集调优的阈值在密集分数分布发生偏移时容易失效（brittle），需要一种无需重训练分类头、可对任意F_β目标重定向阈值的稳健后验策略。

Method: AutoThreshold预测各分数bin内正类与负类像素的联合质量（joint mass），该目标直接参数化阈值索引的混淆计数，避免分别估计阳性率与类条件率的乘积；采用softmax归一化与累积解码得到连贯、单调的混淆曲线，再由plug-in解码器对任意F_β目标选阈值，头部无需重训练。

Result: 3个独立训练的Kvasir流水线迁移至4个息肉数据集：Joint较连续Otsu外部macro Dice提升0.0080±0.0046，3/3次运行均为正增益；参考流水线上3个head种子得0.6324±0.0027，比Otsu高0.0085；直接效用Ridge为0.6342，配对差异区间跨零。同一Joint检查点在F_{0.5}、Dice、F_2上均优于Otsu。KITTI Road留一损坏族评估中，Joint较标量回归提升0.0082 [0.0064, 0.0101]，而直接效用高0.0022。

Conclusion: 联合混淆预测是一种有效且可按指标重定向的归纳偏置，可作为稳健后验阈值选择方案；但作者不主张其达到贝叶斯最优的F_β决策。

Abstract: A single validation-tuned threshold can become brittle when dense-score distributions shift. We introduce AutoThreshold, a post-hoc policy that predicts the joint mass of positive and negative pixels over score bins. This target directly parameterizes threshold-indexed confusion counts, avoiding products of independently estimated prevalence and class-conditional rates. Softmax normalization and cumulative decoding yield coherent, monotone confusion profiles; a plug-in decoder then selects a threshold for any $F_β$ objective without retraining the head. Across three independently trained Kvasir pipelines transferred to four polyp datasets, Joint exceeds continuous Otsu by $0.0080 \pm 0.0046$ external macro Dice, with positive gains in 3/3 runs. On the reference pipeline, three head seeds achieve $0.6324 \pm 0.0027$, 0.0085 above Otsu; direct-utility Ridge achieves 0.6342, with the paired difference interval spanning zero. The same Joint checkpoints outperform Otsu for $F_{0.5}$, Dice, and $F_2$. On developmental KITTI Road leave-one-corruption-family-out evaluation, Joint improves over scalar regression by 0.0082 [0.0064, 0.0101], while direct utility is 0.0022 higher. These results establish joint confusion prediction as a useful, metric-retargetable inductive bias, without claiming Bayes-optimal $F_β$ decisions.

</details>


### [92] [Image Quality and the Reliability of Fabric-Density Measurement: Counting Threads from Smartphone Photos Through a Pick Glass](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13200)
*Anoubhav Agarwaal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出PickGlass-TPI基准（76个工厂场景、44块织物），以任务为导向的图像质量标准评估"手机照片+放大镜"数经纬密度的可行性；结果显示所有方法均无法在±5%容差内与标注员参考一致，最佳方法误差约25%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 织物经纬密度（每英寸纱线数）是买家必查的基本规格，但无仪器时检验员需透过pick glass（折叠式10×放大镜，带一英寸窗口）用肉眼缓慢计数。若能通过该放大镜拍手机照片，可加快检验并留下记录。作者主张按"任务导向的图像质量"来评价这类照片：只要从中数出的纱线数在真值±5%内（织物规格常用容差）即为合格。

Method: 构建PickGlass-TPI数据集：76个工厂场景、44块织物，由受纺织训练的标注员在开放标注工具中逐块计数作为参考值。在该基准上评测前沿与开源视觉语言模型、CNN、计数专用模型，以及免训练的谱域计数器PG-Lattice；另以非专家标注员、猜测基线（用其余场景均值）及重拍一致性作为对照。

Result: 无任何方法与参考值在±5%内一致。最佳方法误差约四分之一：仅在合成图像上微调的小型开源模型Molmo2-4B误差25%，与最佳前沿模型GPT-6.1-sol（直接提问27%，给四张窗口放大特写时25%）持平。二者均优于非专家标注员（37%），但仅微调模型在两项误差度量上同时优于猜测基线。PG-Lattice重拍一致性最好（两次拍摄计数常在5%内），但其失误常为2倍因子误差，GPT-6.1-sol亦如此。参考值本身跨重拍复现的偏差中位数达9%，故单张照片尚无法证明满足±5%。预先选定的通用无参考质量分数无法排序PG-Lattice的误差；生成式增强虽提升感知分数，却不能可靠改善计数。

Conclusion: 当前技术尚不能满足±5%这一实际容差，真正关键的图像质量是"跨重拍的一致性/可靠性"，而非通用感知质量指标或生成式增强带来的观感提升；这为后续面向任务的采集与算法改进指明了方向。

Abstract: Thread density, the threads per inch along and across a woven fabric, is a basic specification that buyers check whenever fabric is sold. Without laboratory instruments, inspectors count it slowly, by eye, through a pick glass, a folding 10$\times$ magnifier with a one-inch window. A smartphone photo through that glass could make the check faster and leave a record. We judge such a photo by what it is used for, as in task-based image quality: it is good if the thread count from it is within $\pm$5% of the true count, a common tolerance in fabric specifications. We introduce PickGlass-TPI: 76 factory scenes of 44 fabric patches, each counted in an open annotation tool by a textile-trained annotator, whose counts are the reference. On it we benchmark frontier and open vision-language models, CNNs, counting specialists and a training-free spectral counter (PG-Lattice). No method agrees with the reference within the tolerance. The best methods are off by about a quarter: a small open model that we tuned only on synthetic images (Molmo2-4B, 25%) is level with the best frontier model, GPT-6.1-sol (27% when asked directly, 25% given four magnified close-ups of the window). Both err less than a non-expert annotator (37%), but only the tuned model also beats, on both error measures, a baseline that guesses each scene's density as the average of the others. PG-Lattice most often repeats its count within 5% across two photos of the same patch, but its misses, like GPT-6.1-sol's errors, are often a factor of two. Even the reference repeats a count across such re-shoots only to a median 9%, so one photo cannot yet be shown to meet $\pm$5%; the image quality that matters is reliability across re-shoots. Generic no-reference quality scores chosen before labeling do not rank PG-Lattice's error, and generative enhancement raises perceptual scores but does not reliably improve the count.

</details>


### [93] [MICRO: A Surgery-Phase Retinal Image Rigid Registration Benchmark and One-Step Frequency-Structure Similarity Registration Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13045)
*Mingdao Gong,Yan Hu,Mingzhen Yuan,Jialu Zhang,Junming Feng,Yiming Jiang,Jing Ma,Jinghua Liu,Huazhu Fu,Hai Lu,Xiaoqing Zhang,Jiang Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MICRO大规模多模态视网膜图像刚性配准数据集与一步式OFS2Reg框架，通过频域结构相似性构建关键点拓扑约束实现跨模态血管结构精确配准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确的多模态视网膜图像刚性配准（MRIRG）对跨模态血管结构对齐及眼科手术导航至关重要，但公开数据集缺乏覆盖术前、术中、术后各手术阶段的多模态配准基准；同时现有一体化方法采用"先分割再检测"两步框架，性能受分割质量严重制约，其根源在于关键点拓扑约束未被显式利用。

Method: 构建MICRO数据集，含1,365对跨模态视网膜图像及标准化标注，覆盖全部眼科手术阶段。提出One-step Frequency-Structure Similarity Registration（OFS2Reg）一步式框架：在共享检测网络下利用跨模态血管结构相似性构建关键点拓扑约束；设计频域引导的结构保持描述子，通过突出显著血管结构在描述子层面构建粗粒度关键点拓扑约束；并开发跨模态血管结构相似性学习方法，借助相似性先验细化关键点拓扑约束，实现像素级血管结构精确对齐。

Result: 论文提出覆盖多手术阶段的大规模MRIRG基准MICRO数据集，并构建OFS2Reg框架，在共享检测网络下以频域结构保持描述子和跨模态相似性学习实现粗到精的关键点拓扑约束，从而提升跨模态视网膜图像配准精度。

Conclusion: MRIRG的关键瓶颈在于关键点拓扑约束缺失与两步框架对分割质量的依赖；OFS2Reg以一步式频域-结构相似性学习显式建模拓扑约束，配合MICRO数据集，为跨模态视网膜刚性配准提供了新的基准与方法范式。

Abstract: Precise multimodal retinal image rigid registration (MRIRG) is essential for aligning cross-modal vessel structures, offering critical guidance for ophthalmic surgery. Despite its significance, the advancement of MRIRG is hindered by the lack of publicly available multimodal retinal image rigid registration datasets spanning various ophthalmic surgery phases (e.g., preoperative, intraoperative, and postoperative). To address this issue, we introduce the MICRO dataset, a large-scale MRIRG benchmark, comprising 1,365 cross-modal retinal image pairs with standardized annotations across all ophthalmic surgery phases. Moreover, existing deep registration methods achieve competitive MRIRG results via a two-step segmentation-then-detection framework, but segmentation quality heavily constrains their performance. We argue that this dependency stems from an implicit need for keypoint topology constraints, which have not been exploited for MRIRG through a one-step registration framework. Inspired by the above analysis, we propose a novel One-step Frequency-Structure Similarity Registration (OFS2Reg) learning framework for MRIRG that leverages the potential of cross-modal vessel structure similarities to construct keypoint topology constraints under a shared detection network. To be specific, OFS2Reg applies a well-designed frequency-guided structure preservation descriptor to construct coarse keypoint topology constraints in descriptors by highlighting significant vessel structures. Additionally, we develop a new cross-modal vessel structure similarity learning method that refines cross-modal keypoint topology constraints to precisely align pixel-level cross-modal vessel structures by leveraging cross-modal vessel structure similarity priors.

</details>


### [94] [Evidential Deep Learning with conditional entropy for glaucoma detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13513)
*Hai Siong Tan,Kuancheng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种以条件熵函数为基的Evidential Deep Learning（EDL）框架，用于视网膜眼底图像青光眼检测中的预测不确定性量化，并验证其校准性与可解释性优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 青光眼眼底筛查模型需可靠的预测不确定性估计；传统EDL以Dempster不确定性作正则，校准误差偏高，且不确定性方法的可解释性（与临床关键结构如视盘的对应关系）尚缺乏系统评估。

Method: 设计新的条件熵函数作为EDL正则项以量化预测不确定性；在EyePACS-AIROGS-light-V2与RIM-ONE-DL数据集上，与Deep Ensembles、MC Dropout、事后校准及基于Dempster不确定性的常规EDL进行对比；并引入表征空间集中度的focus index分析Grad-CAM热图，考察正确/错误分类样本指数分布的可分离性及假阴性的激活位置。

Result: 所提条件熵正则EDL的校准误差显著低于Deep Ensembles、MC Dropout、事后校准和常规EDL；EDL模型在正确与错误分类眼底图的focus index分布间分离度更大；假阴性样本的Grad-CAM激活呈局部化且偏离视盘，与青光眼评估中视盘的临床重要性相符。

Conclusion: 所提EDL框架在青光眼检测中同时实现了更优的不确定性校准与更具生理一致性的可解释性，条件熵正则是提升EDL不确定性与解释质量的有效手段。

Abstract: We propose a novel conditional entropy function as a basis for quantifying predictive uncertainty in Evidential Deep Learning (EDL) for glaucoma detection from retinal fundus images. Using the EyePACS-AIROGS-light-V2 and RIM-ONE-DL datasets for validation, we found that our proposed EDL model regularized by conditional entropy function produced uncertainty estimates with substantially lower calibration errors than Deep Ensembles, MC Dropout, post-hoc calibration and the conventional EDL framework based on Dempster uncertainty. To further probe our models' interpretability, we analyzed Grad-CAM heatmaps using a focus index representing spatial concentration. Compared with the other uncertainty quantification methods, EDL models exhibited greater separation between the index distributions of correctly and incorrectly classified fundus images. In particular, false negatives were associated with localized Grad-CAM activations that were displaced from the optic disc, consistent with its established clinical importance in glaucoma assessment. Overall, our findings suggest that our proposed EDL framework for glaucoma detection provides not only enhanced uncertainty calibration but also admits a more physiologically consistent interpretability.

</details>


### [95] [Distribution Shift Detection in Remote Sensing Imagery using Machine Learning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13510)
*Abdullah Almansour,Ozan K. Tonguz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文对Alibi-Detect、NannyML、PyTorch-OOD三种开源分布漂移检测工具在无人机遥感跨数据集漂移场景下进行了实证比较，权衡其检测效果与计算延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UAV遥感部署ML模型不仅关注预测精度，还面临部署数据与训练分布不一致的问题（地理、传感器、季节等视觉差异）。资源受限平台需具备漂移检测与响应能力，但缺乏对现有工具在实际遥感场景中效果与开销的系统认识。

Method: 开展实证案例研究：选取三个开源检测工具（Alibi-Detect、NannyML、PyTorch-OOD），构造跨数据集的遥感分布漂移，统一评估其检测有效性（detection effectiveness）与计算延迟（computational latency）。

Result: 不同方法与实现在检测行为和计算开销上存在差异，体现出检测能力与响应时间之间的权衡关系。

Conclusion: 现有工具在资源受限航空平台上的适用性需结合延迟考量；未来应发展轻量化、延迟感知（latency-aware）的监控机制。

Abstract: Remote sensing imagery acquired by Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) supports wide range of applications; however, deploying machine learning (ML) models on resource-constrained aerial platforms introduces challenges beyond predictive accuracy, particularly when deployment data differ from the distributions observed during training. Such shifts are common in remote sensing due to geographic, sensor, seasonal, and other visual variations. A practical solution is to equip deployed models with mechanisms that detect emerging distribution shifts and support subsequent responsive actions. This paper presents an empirical case study of three open-source detection tools: (i) Alibi-Detect; (ii) NannyML; and (iii) PyTorch-OOD. Using cross-dataset remote-sensing shifts, we evaluate both detection effectiveness and computational latency of these tools. Our results reveal differences in detection behavior and processing cost across methods and implementations, highlighting trade-offs between detection capability and response-time. These findings motivate further work toward lightweight, and latency-aware monitoring mechanisms for resource-constrained systems.

</details>


### [96] [Uni-Loc: Weakly Supervised 3D CT Pathology Localization via Spatially Grounded Vision-Language Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13330)
*Hao-Chih Lee,Zahi A. Fayad,Xueyan Mei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Uni-Loc 视觉-语言框架，仅用扫描级监督统一病理分类与 3D 定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D CT 中异常的精确定位对于将自动预测与临床相关病理发现相对应至关重要，但缺乏体素级标注是主要瓶颈。

Method: 基于病理条件化查询与图像特征间的空间相似度同时导出预测与定位图；token-to-voxel 上采样模块实现高分辨率定位；梯度引导阈值化无需额外可学习参数即可划分病理区域。

Result: 在 5 个定位任务、355 例标注 CT 上，宏观平均体素级 AUROC/AUPR 达 96.1%/34.5%，优于 relevance propagation（80.7%/13.9%）与梯度显著性（88.7%/8.1%）；分类 AUROC 在 CT-RATE 为 88.39%、RAD-ChestCT 为 78.03%，均超过匹配基线。

Conclusion: 无需病理特异性的体素级监督即可实现有效的 3D 病理定位。

Abstract: Accurate localization of abnormalities in three-dimensional (3D) CT scans is essential for linking automated predictions with clinically relevant pathological findings. We propose Uni-Loc, a vision-language framework that unifies pathology classification and 3D localization using scan-level supervision. Uni-Loc derives both predictions and localization maps from spatial similarities between pathology-conditioned queries and image features. A token-to-voxel upsampling module enables high-resolution localization, while gradient-guided thresholding identifies pathology regions without additional learned parameters. Evaluated on five localization tasks comprising 355 annotated CT scans, Uni-Loc achieves macro-averaged voxel-level AUROC/AUPR of 96.1\%/34.5\%, outperforming relevance propagation (80.7%/13.9%) and gradient saliency (88.7%/8.1%). Uni-Loc also achieves classification AUROC of 88.39% on CT-RATE and 78.03% on RAD-ChestCT, improving over a matched baseline. These results demonstrate effective 3D pathology localization without requiring pathology-specific voxel-level supervision.

</details>


### [97] [Domain-Adversarial Adaptation from MURA to In-House Radiographs for Improved Adult Elbow Fracture Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13308)
*M. Bolhassani,W. Furtado,D. Duvall,MJ Negahdar,J. Joshi,S. Contractor,A. Amini*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究针对成人肘部骨折X线分类从大型公开数据集（MURA）迁移到小型院内数据集（UofL）时的域偏移问题，采用域对抗神经网络（DANN）进行特征分布对齐，实验表明DANN优于仅源域训练与朴素合并基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公开数据集训练的模型部署到规模小、分布不同的院内数据时存在显著域偏移，需在目标域标签有限条件下提升跨域泛化能力。

Method: 构建DANN：预训练卷积骨干适配灰度输入；任务头预测骨折有无；基于梯度反转层的域判别头通过对抗学习促使特征域不变。对比四种设置：仅源域、仅目标域（有限标签）、朴素合并（联合训练）、DANN（MURA→UofL）。消融包括渐进式对抗权重、数据集规模感知的域损失、域平衡采样。以AUC-ROC与准确率在UofL测试集评估。

Result: DANN在降低域偏差的同时避免对小目标集过拟合，性能优于仅源域与朴素合并基线；三项消融均提升了训练稳定性与目标域表现。

Conclusion: 域对齐是从公开数据到本地数据实现稳健临床泛化的可行路径。

Abstract: We studied domain shift in adult elbow fracture classification from radiographs when training on a large public dataset and deploying on a small, institution-specific dataset (UofL). We implemented a domain-adversarial neural network (DANN) to align feature distributions across source (MURA) and target (UofL) domains while optimizing fracture classification. A pretrained convolutional backbone was adapted to grayscale inputs; the task head predicts fracture presence, and a gradient-reversal-based domain head encourages domain-invariant features via adversarial learning. We compared four settings: (1) Source-only, (2) Target-only (limited labels), (3) Naive merge (pooled training), and (4) DANN (MURA to UofL). Evaluation on UofL test data uses AUC-ROC, and accuracy. DANN outperformed Source-only and Naive merge baselines by reducing domain bias without overfitting to the small target set. Ablations showed that progressive adversarial weight, dataset-size-aware domain loss, and domain-balanced sampling improved stability and target performance. Results demonstrated that domain alignment is a practical path to robust clinical generalization from public to local data.

</details>


### [98] [Domain adaptation of a video Foundation model for Heart Failure Diagnosis from Echocardiography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13297)
*Mahyar Bolhassani,Tijana Geroski,Narayana S. Singam,Amir Amini*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该工作探索将视频基础模型TimeSformer微调用于四腔心超声视频的HFrEF自动诊断，采用相位感知片段生成与分块时空注意力，实验表明全量微调优于仅分类器微调及3D CNN基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型已革新计算机视觉，但面向医学影像（尤其是时空心脏超声视频）的专用基础模型仍有限，需验证通用视频基础模型迁移到超声心动图诊断的可行性。

Method: 在公开RVENet A4C数据集上微调预训练TimeSformer；采用相位感知片段生成策略捕获互补的收缩期与舒张期动态；使用分块时空注意力（divided space-time attention）、逐层学习率衰减与多片段推理以提升小数据下的鲁棒性；对比全量微调、仅分类器微调与3D CNN基线。

Result: 全量微调模型性能优于仅分类器微调与3D CNN基线。

Conclusion: 视频基础模型在超声心动图心力衰竭诊断中具潜力，值得进一步开展医学影像领域的域适应研究。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of foundation models has transformed computer vision, yet their adaptation to medical imaging, particularly purpose built foundation models for spatio temporal echocardiography videos, remains limited. In this work, we explore the feasibility of fine tuning a pretrained foundation model for video (TimeSformer) for automated diagnosis of reduced ejection fraction heart failure (HFrEF) from apical four chamber videos. The model is finetuned on the publicly available RVENet A4C dataset with a phase aware clip generation strategy that captures complementary systolic and diastolic dynamics. We employ divided space time attention, layer wise learning rate decay, and multi clip inference to enhance robustness under limited data. Experimental results show that full fine tuning of the proposed model outperforms classifier only tuning and a 3D CNN baseline. These findings highlight the potential of leveraging foundation video models for echocardiography based heart failure diagnosis and motivates further domain adaptation studies in medical imaging.

</details>


### [99] [RetinexGS: Retinex-Inspired Gaussian Splatting for Low-Light RAW Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13280)
*Yulang Fei,Huaiyuan Weng,Chul Min Yeum*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RetinexGS：一个 RAW 域 3DGS 框架，结合 Retinex 式外观建模与传感器噪声感知优化，实现低光照新视角合成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准 3DGS 在严重欠曝图像上出现颜色不稳定、漂浮伪影与暗区重建不完整；RAW 图像保留线性传感器测量与噪声元数据，可为噪声感知重建提供自然基础。

Method: 将每个高斯颜色分解为视角无关基色与共享的、依赖位置与视角的调制场，并对两部分分别正则化；利用 DNG 噪声元数据对信号相关噪声加权重建损失；采用畸变感知渲染避免对 RAW 目标做空间重采样；引入深度引导初始化缓解暗区几何稀疏。

Result: 在 3 个公开 RawNeRF 场景与 2 个自采集低光 RAW 场景上，所有方法均使用相同 RAW 输入训练，RetinexGS 在五场景平均 sRGB PSNR 上分别优于 LE3D 2.18 dB、RawNeRF 2.71 dB，并支持实时新视角渲染。

Conclusion: RAW 域外观分解、噪声感知损失与深度引导初始化相结合，可显著提升低光照新视角合成的稳定性与精度，同时保持实时渲染效率。

Abstract: Standard 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) pipelines struggle with severely underexposed images, producing unstable colors, floating artifacts, and incomplete reconstructions in dark regions. To better account for noise in these low-light observations, RAW images offer a natural basis for noise-aware reconstruction by preserving linear sensor measurements and providing access to noise metadata. We present RetinexGS, a RAW-domain 3DGS framework that combines Retinex-inspired appearance modeling with sensor-informed optimization for low-light novel-view synthesis. We factorize each Gaussian's color into a view-independent base color and a shared, position- and view-dependent modulation field, regularizing the two components separately to constrain appearance learning from noisy observations. We use DNG noise metadata to weight the reconstruction loss for signal-dependent noise, while distortion-aware rendering avoids spatially resampling RAW targets. To address sparse geometry in dark regions, we introduce depth-guided initialization. We evaluate RetinexGS on three public RawNeRF scenes and two custom-captured low-light RAW scenes. With all methods trained on the same RAW inputs for each scene, RetinexGS outperforms LE3D and RawNeRF in mean sRGB PSNR by 2.18 dB and 2.71 dB respectively, across the five scenes, while supporting real-time novel-view rendering.

</details>


### [100] [Uncovering the Blind Spots of OKS-mAP: A Unified Protocol for Occlusion, Joint-Angle, and Temporal Evaluation of 2D Pose Estimators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12911)
*Yifan Fan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一套补充OKS-mAP的三模块评估协议（按可见性分层的OKS-mAP、关节角度误差、时序稳定性），并在4个数据集、4个模型上验证：聚合mAP系统性高估遮挡场景性能，角度与时序指标提供mAP无法定位的独立信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OKS-mAP作为2D人体姿态估计的主导指标存在三个盲点：不区分可见与遮挡关键点、不度量关节角度误差、对无跟踪逐帧推理的时序稳定性无度量。因此需要一套互补而非替代的评估协议。

Method: 提出三模块协议：(M1) 按真值可见性分层的OKS-mAP，并以Delta_occ = mAP(v=1) − mAP(full)量化聚合指标对遮挡差异的掩盖程度；(M2) 关节角度误差（RMSE/MAE/P95）及膝外翻检测一致性；(M3) 帧间位移、中值滤波残差抖动与跳变率，并在OneEuro平滑前后对比。实验覆盖COCO val2017、CrowdPose、OCHuman、PoseTrack18四个数据集，以及两个架构族的四个模型（YOLO11s-pose、其遮挡域微调变体、ViTPose-B/L），采用统一的detector-free oracle协议与开源评测框架。

Result: Delta_occ在所有组合中均为负（−0.19至−0.47），说明聚合mAP普遍高估遮挡场景性能；遮挡层中角度误差被放大，角度指标揭示了mAP无法定位的差异（如较小的遮挡训练模型在遮挡肘部显著优于更大的Transformer模型）。PoseTrack18 val上，两个YOLO模型原始抖动相当；像素域OneEuro平滑在匹配范围内AP无损的前提下将跳变率降低9–10%；去除检测器后残差抖动仍存在；Transformer模型的跳变率显著更低；平滑收益可跨架构复现。分层mAP与全量mAP高度一致（12/12符号一致，符号一致性1.0），适合幅度诊断而非重排序；角度指标差异向量（尤其膝部）出现部分背离，表明其携带独立信息。

Conclusion: 该三模块协议与开源评测框架可作为OKS-mAP的补充而非替代：分层mAP用于遮挡幅度诊断，角度指标与时序指标提供独立诊断维度；建议在姿态估计评测中并行报告。

Abstract: The dominant metric for 2D human pose estimation, OKS-mAP, has three blind spots: it does not distinguish visible from occluded keypoints, does not measure joint-angle error, and is silent on the temporal stability of tracking-free per-frame inference. We propose a supplementary protocol with three modules: (M1) OKS-mAP stratified by ground-truth visibility, with Delta_occ = mAP(v=1) - mAP(full), quantifying how much the aggregate metric masks occlusion-specific differences; (M2) joint-angle error (RMSE/MAE/P95) plus knee-valgus detection consistency; (M3) frame-to-frame displacement, median-filter residual jitter, and jump rate, before and after OneEuro smoothing. We ran it on four datasets (COCO val2017, CrowdPose, OCHuman, PoseTrack18) and four models from two architecture families (YOLO11s-pose, an occlusion-domain fine-tuned variant, ViTPose-B/L), under a common detector-free oracle protocol and an open-source harness. Delta_occ is negative in every combination (-0.19 to -0.47): aggregate mAP overestimates occluded-scene performance in every tested combination. Joint-angle error is amplified in occluded layers; angle metrics reveal differences mAP cannot localize, e.g., the smaller occlusion-trained model significantly outperforming a larger transformer in occluded elbows. On PoseTrack18 val, raw jitter matched between the two YOLO models; pixel-domain OneEuro smoothing cut jump rates 9-10% at no matched-scope AP cost; residual jitter persists after detector removal; the transformer pair's jump rate is significantly lower; smoothing benefits replicate across architectures. Stratified mAP tracks full mAP closely (sign-consistency 1.0, 12/12), serving magnitude diagnosis rather than re-ranking; angle-metric difference vectors (especially knee) partially diverge, suggesting independent information. The protocol and harness are open-sourced, complementing rather than replacing OKS-mAP.

</details>


### [101] [Instance-preserving graded semantic reporting for surgical action triplets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13009)
*Yanchong Xue,Wanting Zhang,Lingtao Yu,Guochao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出"实例保留式分级语义报告"框架，将实例选择与语义粒度选择解耦，用效用增量规则DELTA在I/IV/IVT三种报告层级间权衡收益与错误代价，从而保留部分正确的手术动作预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统"完整报告或拒报"（complete-or-reject）范式在目标不确定时会丢弃已正确识别的器械与动作，造成信息浪费；需要一种能在保证实例正确的前提下按语义完整度分级输出的报告机制。

Method: 方法分两层：每个被选实例保留其预测掩码与标签，由分层正确性得分决定以I（器械）、IV（器械-动词）或完整IVT（器械-动词-目标）形式报告；采用效用增量规则DELTA，将更精细报告带来的期望收益与其错误代价相权衡。在8段手术视频的5,641帧测试集上，使用固定预测与得分比较三种决策规则。

Result: 在10%开发集校准目标下，DELTA对匹配的complete-or-reject基线所拒候选中的80.0%（5,329/6,662）保留了正确的部分报告。相较分别重校准的I/IVT对照，三种规则加入IV后源归一化加权正确报告产出提升19.13至23.13（每100帧）。加权报告风险变化为-0.77至+1.01个百分点，而完整正确三元组数量不变或下降。

Conclusion: 分级报告可将部分手术预测作为局部化描述加以利用，并在报告效用与语义完整性之间提供显式权衡；其代价是完整正确三元组数未获提升，需在下游应用中按需求选择报告粒度。

Abstract: Complete-or-reject reporting of surgical action triplets can discard a correctly identified instrument and action when the target is uncertain. We introduce instance-preserving graded semantic reporting, which separates instance selection from the choice of semantic detail. Each selected instance retains its predicted mask and labels while hierarchical correctness scores guide instrument (I), instrument-verb (IV), or complete instrument-verb-target (IVT) reporting. The utility-increment rule DELTA weighs the expected gain from finer reporting against its error cost. We compare three decision rules on 5,641 test frames from eight surgical videos using fixed predictions and scores. At the 10% development calibration target, DELTA retains correct partial reports for 80.0% of candidates rejected by a matched complete-or-reject baseline (5,329/6,662). Adding IV increases source-normalized weighted correct-report yield by 19.13 to 23.13 per 100 frames across all three rules relative to separately recalibrated I/IVT controls. Weighted reporting risk changes by -0.77 to +1.01 percentage points, with complete correct-triplet counts unchanged or reduced. Graded reporting makes partial surgical predictions available as localized descriptions, with an explicit trade-off between report utility and completeness.

</details>


### [102] [G-STAR: Group-Scaled Token Capacity for Deeply Compressed Autoregressive Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13004)
*Xu Huang,Ye Huang,Xiaotong Li,Zhengrong Yue,Jialv Zou,Minhao Jing,Haodong Li,Wenhao Yan,Yaxin Luo,Duomin Wang,Daquan Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文将乘积量化理论引入离散自回归图像生成，揭示"组大小应随每轴空间压缩因子的平方增长"这一标度律，并据此提出空间-组协同缩放框架GSTAR，实现32×乃至有效的64×压缩，配合半自回归解码器，在五个文本到图像基准上媲美领先模型，1024×1024下单张图仅需2.5秒（H200），较先前离散自回归模型提速20倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散自回归模型存在根本性权衡：长token序列保真度高但生成慢，激进的空间压缩虽缩短序列却削弱表征容量。作者希望在不牺牲重建、理解与生成质量的前提下大幅压缩token序列，从而同时获得高质量与低延迟。
