Method: 1）基于乘积量化的信息论分析，推导出在固定名义信息预算下组大小应随每轴空间压缩因子二次方增长的标度原则；2）提出GSTAR空间-组协同缩放框架，同时放大空间压缩与组大小，达到32×及有效64×压缩；3）分析大组带来的组内依赖对推理延迟与生成质量的影响；4）设计半自回归解码器，将并行特征计算与轻量因果建模相结合以高效建模组内依赖。

Result: 高度压缩的模型在GenEval、DPG-Bench、PRISM、LongText-Bench、Qwen-Image-Bench五个代表性文本到图像基准上与领先模型表现相当；1024×1024分辨率下，单张NVIDIA H200上生成耗时2.5秒，相比先前离散自回归模型实现20×加速。

Conclusion: 结果表明高度压缩的离散表征能够在低延迟下支撑高质量自回归生成，空间压缩与组大小的协同缩放是缓解离散自回归"质量-速度"权衡的有效途径。

Abstract: Discrete autoregressive models offer a scalable framework for image generation. However, they face a fundamental trade-off: long token sequences preserve representation quality but slow generation, while aggressive spatial compression reduces representation capacity. Based on product quantization, we show that, under a fixed nominal information budget, group size should scale quadratically with the per-axis spatial compression factor. Building on this principle, we propose GSTAR, a spatial--group co-scaling framework that enables \(32\times\) and even effective \(64\times\) compression while preserving strong reconstruction, understanding, and generation performance. Since larger groups introduce intra-group dependencies that must be modeled efficiently, we analyze their impact on inference latency and generation quality and introduce a semi-autoregressive decoder that combines parallel feature computation with lightweight causal modeling. With these designs, our highly compressed models perform competitively with leading models across five representative text-to-image benchmarks (GenEval, DPG-Bench, PRISM, LongText-Bench, and Qwen-Image-Bench). At \(1024\times1024\) resolution, our model delivers a \(20\times\) speedup over prior discrete autoregressive models, generating each image in 2.5 seconds on a single NVIDIA H200. Together, these results show that highly compressed discrete representations can enable high-quality autoregressive generation at low latency.

</details>


### [103] [Exploring Synchrony for Communication in Modular Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13759)
*Nikolas Willems,Qiyang Sun,Björn W. Schuller*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SyncNet将模块内容与动态相位状态分离，用Kuramoto动力学驱动相位、以相位对齐决定共享信道通信，在SQOOP上取得最佳留出准确率，但内容寻址变体相当，说明收益主要来自模块分解而非同步机制本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模块化网络希望各组件学到可复用的独立功能并重组以支持组合泛化，但结构本身不保证专精，且无约束的模块间通信会破坏已出现的专精，因此需要研究模块通信的组织方式；该问题可类比知觉绑定问题，同步性被认为可协调分布式表征。

Method: 提出SyncNet：模块内容与动力学相位状态解耦，用可学习的Kuramoto动力学演化相位，由相位对齐决定是否通过共享信道通信。在Sort-of-CLEVR（关系推理）与SQOOP（系统泛化，较低pair variety需更多测试期重组）上评估，并进行固定相位、模块-区域置换等测试期干预，以及从零训练的内容寻址变体对照。

Result: 在三个最低pair variety上取得对比模型中最优留出准确率，最难设置下领先5.1个百分点；Sort-of-CLEVR上具竞争力但低于最强注意力基线。固定相位性能等同无寻址，置换模块与区域的对应关系损害近乎相同；内容寻址变体从零训练可匹敌SyncNet。

Conclusion: 性能依赖演化中的相位，说明动态相位承担寻址功能；但因内容寻址变体同样有效，SyncNet的收益似乎主要源于模块分解，而非同步机制本身。

Abstract: One motivation for modular neural networks is to divide computation across components that learn distinct, reusable functions, which could then be recombined to support compositional generalisation. In practice, modular structure alone does not guarantee that such roles develop, and unrestricted communication between modules can undermine any specialisation that does emerge. This raises the question of how modular communication should be organised. The problem can be viewed as an analogue of the perceptual binding problem, where synchrony has been proposed as a mechanism for coordinating distributed representations. We introduce SyncNet, a modular architecture that separates module content from a dynamical phase state: learnt Kuramoto dynamics evolve the phases, while phase alignment determines communication through a shared channel. We evaluate SyncNet on Sort-of-CLEVR for relational reasoning and on SQOOP for systematic generalisation, where lower pair variety demands more recombination at test time. SyncNet gives the best held-out accuracy of the models compared at the three lowest pair varieties, by 5.1 percentage points at the hardest, and is competitive on Sort-of-CLEVR though below the strongest attention baselines. Test-time overrides show that performance depends on phases that evolve: fixed phases cost as much as no addressing at all, and permuting which module holds which region is nearly as damaging. A content-addressed variant trained from scratch nonetheless matches it, so the benefit appears to come from the modular decomposition rather than synchrony itself.

</details>


### [104] [When to Start Denoising? Adaptive Denoising Entry for Generative Video Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13620)
*Zifu Zhang,Yuqiao Li,Ding Ding,Shengxi Li,Mai Xu,Daowen Li,Kai Li,Ying Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 ADE-GVC，首个时间步感知的生成式视频压缩框架，通过轻量预测器从压缩潜变量自适应估计去噪起始时间步，并用时序适配器注入压缩感知条件到预训练视频扩散模型，在极低码率下同时提升保真度与真实感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于扩散的生成式视频压缩依赖压缩潜变量注入扩散过程的时机，该时机直接决定重建保真度与感知真实感的权衡，而现有方法未对去噪起始时间步进行针对性选择，限制了性能。

Method: 将去噪起始时间步选择建模为几何优化问题；设计轻量预测器从压缩潜变量估计自适应去噪起点；引入时序适配器提取压缩感知条件并注入预训练视频扩散模型；并观察到预测时间步与原始/压缩潜变量间失真存在强定量关系，为优化建模提供实证支持。

Result: 在感知指标上达到 SOTA，显著提升重建真实感；相较最新开源基线 GLC-Video，平均节省码率 40.99%（DISTS）与 95.24%（PSNR）。

Conclusion: 起始时间步是控制保真度—真实感权衡的关键因素，自适应选择可有效提升生成式视频压缩性能，且预测时间步与潜变量失真间的定量关系验证了该优化建

Abstract: Generative video compression offers a promising solution for compressing and reconstructing videos under extremely low bitrate constraints, among which the diffusion-based solutions still achieve the best performance by providing the powerful generative latent prior. However, their effectiveness critically depends on when the compressed latent is introduced into the diffusion process, which directly governs the trade-off between reconstruction fidelity and perceptual realism. To address this limitation, we identify denoising start-timestep selection as a key factor governing this trade-off and formulate it as a geometric optimization problem. Based on this formulation, we propose a novel adaptive diffusion entry method for generative video compression (ADE-GVC), the first timestep-aware generative video compression framework, featuring a lightweight predictor that estimates an adaptive denoising start point from the compressed latent. A temporal adapter further extracts compression-aware conditions and injects them into a pretrained video diffusion model, enabling the reconstruction of videos with both high fidelity and realism. More importantly, we observe a strong quantitative relationship between the predicted timesteps and the distortion between the original and compressed latents, providing empirical support for our optimization formulation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ADE-GVC achieves the state-of-the-art performance on perceptual metrics and substantially improves reconstruction realism over existing generative video compression methods. Notably, our ADE-GVC achieves an average bitrate savings of 40.99\% (on DISTS) and 95.24\% (on PSNR) compared to the latest open-source baseline GLC-Video.

</details>


### [105] [CURRENT: A Recurrent Neural Network for Concurrent Spatial and Temporal Super-Resolution of 4D Flow MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14058)
*Luuk Jacobs,Pietro Dirix,Sebastian Kozerke*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出统一的循环神经网络CURRENT，对重建后的主动脉4D flow MRI速度矢量场同时进行空间与时间超分辨率，性能优于专用模型并改善相对压估测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 4D flow MRI的空间与时间分辨率受限，需在采集后提升；已有方法多分别处理空间或时间超分辨，缺乏统一框架。

Method: CURRENT为单一架构循环神经网络，同时完成空间与时间SR；用CFD模拟的主动脉流场嵌入在体胸廓MRI背景并经受控下采样生成配对低/高分辨率数据监督训练；与4DFlowNet（空间/时间分别）及串行级联流程对比，采用图像域与k空间定量指标，以插值为基线，并评估对物理引导相对压估计的影响及从合成数据到在体数据的泛化性。

Result: 空间SR性能优于专用4DFlowNet，时间SR性能相当；同时空时SR的相对压估计精度高于单独空间或时间SR，并优于串行4DFlowNet方案；仅用合成数据训练即在在体4D flow数据上展现良好泛化。

Conclusion: CURRENT为4D flow MRI的空间与时间SR提供统一框架，有望增强物理分析并拓展加速4D flow MRI的适用性。

Abstract: Objective: To improve the spatial and temporal resolution of aortic 4D flow MRI through a unified deep learning framework for post-acquisition super-resolution (SR) of reconstructed velocity vector fields.
  Methods: We propose CURRENT, a recurrent neural network that performs spatial and temporal SR with a single network architecture. Supervised training was conducted using paired low- and high-resolution datasets generated by embedding computational fluid dynamics simulations of aortic flow into in vivo thoracic MRI backgrounds, followed by controlled spatial and temporal downsampling. CURRENT was evaluated for two-fold spatial and temporal SR against 4DFlowNet and for two-fold spatiotemporal SR against sequential spatial and temporal SR pipelines based on 4DFlowNet. Image-based and k-space-based quantitative metrics were considered and conventional interpolation were used as baselines. In addition, the impact of CURRENT's spatiotemporal SR on physics-informed relative pressure estimation was assessed. Finally, the ability of CURRENT to generalize from synthetic training data to in vivo acquisitions was investigated.
  Results: CURRENT achieved superior spatial SR performance and comparable temporal SR performance to dedicated 4DFlowNet models while providing a unified solution for both tasks. Simultaneous spatiotemporal SR using CURRENT yielded more accurate relative pressure estimates than either spatial or temporal SR alone and outperformed sequential 4DFlowNet-based spatial and temporal SR approaches. Furthermore, the proposed model demonstrated promising generalization to in vivo 4D flow MRI data despite being trained exclusively on synthetic datasets.
  Conclusion: CURRENT provides a unified framework for spatial and temporal SR of 4D flow MRI. The proposed approach has the potential to enhance physics-based analysis and broaden the applicability of accelerated 4D flow MRI.

</details>


### [106] [Spatial-Spectral Coupling as Attention: Scaling Shape Correspondence to High-Resolution Surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13870)
*Bence Demeter,Kilian Ehrenreich,Amelie Kleber,Ioana Oprisiu,Philipp Spohn,Christian Wachinger,Fabian Bongratz,Lennart Bastian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文揭示功能映射方法中的空间-谱耦合等价于缩放点积注意力，提出 Geo-FlashAttention 在几何选定的表面块上做精确 softmax，实现近线性扩展并将训练显存从 106.08 GiB 降至 5.67 GiB。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分辨率三维形状间的稠密对应仍具挑战：功能映射方法的核心——空间-谱耦合，其计算与内存开销随网格分辨率呈二次增长，限制了现代深度功能映射流程在大网格上的应用。

Method: 观察到空间-谱耦合等价于以学习描述子为 query/key、谱基函数为 value 的缩放点积注意力，从而可将高效注意力实现直接用于形状匹配；进一步提出 Geo-FlashAttention，将精确 softmax 限制在几何选定的表面 patch 上，兼顾精确性与近线性扩展。

Result: 在六个标准基准与三条功能映射流程上验证：高效耦合保持对应精度；优化后的二次注意力可扩展到大规模网格并改善时间与内存；亚二次方法在非等距形状对上引入误差。6万顶点时峰值训练显存由 106.08 GiB 降至 5.67 GiB，Geo-FlashAttention 每训练步比 FlashAttention 快 1.5 倍。

Conclusion: 高效空间-谱耦合在保持精度的同时显著降低开销，使大网格（如皮层表面）上的端到端对应学习成为可能，为高分辨率形状匹配提供了可扩展方案。

Abstract: Establishing dense correspondence between high-resolution 3D shapes remains challenging due to the spatial--spectral coupling at the core of many functional-map methods, which grows quadratically with mesh resolution. We observe that this coupling is equivalent to a scaled dot-product attention formulation, with learned descriptors serving as queries and keys and spectral basis functions as values. This connection makes efficient attention implementations directly applicable to shape matching and an effective mechanism for scaling spatial-spectral coupling. We show that optimized quadratic attention implementations can scale modern deep functional map pipelines to large mesh sizes, improving both runtime and memory consumption, while sub-quadratic methods introduce errors, particularly for non-isometric shape pairs, where an exact coupling proves most necessary. These insights motivate \textit{Geo-FlashAttention}, which restricts the exact softmax to geometry-selected surface patches and achieves near-linear empirical scaling while preserving correspondence accuracy of exact implementations. Across six standard benchmarks and three functional-map pipelines, rigorous analysis shows that efficient couplings preserve correspondence accuracy. At $60k$ vertices, these insights reduce peak training memory from 106.08 to 5.67\,GiB, with Geo-FlashAttention being $1.5\times$ faster per training step than FlashAttention. This enables end-to-end learning for correspondence problems on large meshes such as cortical surfaces, where high resolution is required to preserve disease-relevant intricacies of the surface geometry. Project page available https://spatial-spectral-attention.github.io/

</details>


### [107] [Towards Text-Only Remote Sensing Scene Classification via Semantic Proxy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13851)
*Xianping Ma,Kunlin Wu,Xiaofeng Liu,Xiaokang Zhang,Yuanxin Ye*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出纯文本遥感场景分类（text-only RSSC）新问题设定与SeProRS基准：用VLM将遥感图像转为语义代理文本，再由文本嵌入模型分类，并系统分析提示策略、代理生成器、迁移性与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RSSC被视为以视觉为中心的问题，依赖图像视觉表征。作者质疑该假设，探索仅凭文本语义表征能否识别遥感场景，以量化语义代理分类的可行性与边界。

Method: SeProRS解耦流水线：先用视觉语言模型（VLM）将遥感图像转换为文本描述（语义代理），再用文本嵌入模型编码并做场景分类；构建统一可复现基准，考察代表性提示策略与代理生成器对分类性能、文本嵌入行为、跨生成器可迁移性及效率的影响。

Result: 在三个知名RSSC数据集上的大量实验表明：语义代理可保留可观的判别信息，同时也暴露出纯文本场景理解的内在局限。

Conclusion: 本文不旨在取代基于图像的RSSC，而是通过量化语义代理场景识别的可行性与边界，开辟新的研究方向，为高效、降模态的遥感场景理解提供基准与分析基础。

Abstract: Remote sensing scene classification (RSSC) is traditionally formulated as a vision-centric problem, where inference relies on rich visual representations extracted from images. In this work, we revisit this assumption by introducing text-only RSSC, a new problem setting that investigates whether remote sensing scenes can be recognized using only textual semantic representations. To enable this study, we propose SeProRS, which reformulates RSSC as a text-only classification task through semantic proxy generation. Specifically, a vision-language model (VLM) is first employed to convert remote sensing images into textual descriptions, termed semantic proxies, which are then encoded by a text embedding model for scene classification. This decoupled pipeline provides a unified and reproducible benchmark for evaluating text-only scene understanding. Furthermore, we investigate representative prompting strategies and proxy generators, and analyze their impact on classification performance, text embedding behavior, cross-generator transferability, and efficiency. Extensive experiments on three well-known RSSC datasets show that semantic proxies can preserve substantial discriminative information, while also revealing inherent limitations of purely text-based scene understanding. This paper does not aim to replace image-based RSSC, but instead establishes a new research direction by quantifying the feasibility and boundaries of semantic proxy-based scene recognition. The proposed benchmark and analysis provide a foundation for future research on efficient and modality-reduced remote sensing scene understanding. The source code and datasets will be accessible at https://github.com/sstary/SSRS.

</details>


### [108] [X Marks the Spot: Diffusion for Image Geolocalization with Location Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13180)
*Brian Wei,Chih-Chuan Hsu,Tianyi Gao,Daniel Cher,Eric Xing,Danielle Beaulieu,Nathan Jacobs*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出位置自编码器（LAE）与潜在扩散模型DIG，在位置编码器的预训练表征空间中生成地理位置，避免坐标空间的语义不稳定问题，在OSV-5M、Im2GPS3k、YFCC4k三个基准上刷新参数化地理定位SOTA，并首次让生成式方法超越检索式方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确的图像地理定位需区分物理上与语义上相邻的区域。既有基于扩散的方法直接在坐标空间生成位置，导致坐标表征在空间上语义不稳定，难以刻画语义邻接关系。

Method: 1) 利用预训练位置编码器的表征空间（兼具位置与语义理解）作为生成目标；2) 提出Location Autoencoder (LAE)：冻结的位置编码器配一个学习得到的解码器，将嵌入映射回坐标，实现全球公里级解码精度；3) 在LAE空间中训练DIG——以图像特征为条件的潜在扩散模型，将噪声传输至位置嵌入。亦可将DIG作为先验用于检索-重排序系统。

Result: DIG在OSV-5M、Im2GPS3k、YFCC4k上稳定优于基于坐标的生成模型，取得参数化地理定位的新SOTA；为首个超越检索式方法的生成式模型，并能进一步提升检索-重排序系统的性能。

Conclusion: 在位置编码器的语义化表征空间中做生成式定位优于坐标空间生成；LAE使该空间可用于坐标预测，DIG验证了该范式的有效性，并可作为即插即用先验增强检索式定位。

Abstract: Image geolocalization predicts the location at which a photo was taken. Accurate geolocalization requires discriminating between both physically and semantically adjacent areas on Earth. However, prior diffusion-based approaches predict locations by generating coordinates, which are semantically unstable over space. We avoid this limitation by learning geolocalization behavior in the pretrained representation spaces of location encoders, which provide both positional and semantic understanding. To make this space usable for coordinate prediction, we introduce a Location Autoencoder (LAE), which pairs a frozen location encoder with a learned decoder mapping embeddings back to coordinates with kilometer-level accuracy worldwide. In the LAE space, we train DIG, a latent diffusion model that transports noise to location embeddings conditioned on image features. DIG consistently outperforms coordinate-based generative models and sets a new state of the art for parametric geolocalization on three benchmarks: OSV-5M, Im2GPS3k, and YFCC4k. Notably, DIG is the first generative model to outperform retrieval-based methods, and further improves retrieve-and-rerank systems when used as a prior. Code and models will be released.

</details>


### [109] [Seeing the Small: Deformable Duality Mamba for Retinal Lesion Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13514)
*Chang Yu,Siqing Li,Yuanchen Liu,Zhenyu Bu,Luming Yang,Alper Yilmaz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 D²Mamba，将非因果状态空间对偶与可变形 Mamba 扫描相结合以建模病灶区域的空间组织，提升糖尿病视网膜病变中小而不规则病灶（尤其是微动脉瘤）的分割性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 糖尿病视网膜病变病灶形状差异大，微动脉瘤（MAs）仅有几个像素。作者假设对病灶区域的空间组织建模（结合全局上下文与形状自适应扫描）能改善这类微小、不规则病灶的分割。

Method: 提出 D²Mamba，耦合非因果状态空间对偶（non-causal state space duality）与可变形 Mamba 扫描（deformable Mamba scanning）。在 DDR 数据集上进行五折交叉验证，与代表性分割基线在相同训练协议下对比，并对每个组件进行消融实验。

Result: D²Mamba 取得最佳整体性能，mIoU 为 33.87%，比最强基线高 2.99 个百分点；在最小、最易漏检的 MAs 上增益最大，Dice 提升 10.06 个百分点。

Conclusion: 结果验证了假设：空间自适应的状态空间建模有利于微小病灶分割。代码与预训练权重已公开。

Abstract: Diabetic retinopathy (DR) lesions vary widely in shape, and microaneurysms (MAs) span only a few pixels. We hypothesize that modeling the spatial organization of lesion regions, with global context and shape-adaptive scanning, improves the segmentation of such small and irregular lesions. To test this, we propose D$^2$Mamba, which couples non-causal state space duality with deformable Mamba scanning. We evaluate it with five-fold cross-validation on the DDR dataset, comparing against representative segmentation baselines under an identical training protocol, and conduct ablations on each component. D$^2$Mamba achieved the best overall performance, with 33.87% mIoU, 2.99 percentage points above the strongest baseline. The largest gain appeared on MAs, the smallest and most easily missed lesions, where Dice improved by 10.06 percentage points. These results confirm our hypothesis that spatially adaptive state space modeling benefits tiny-lesion segmentation. Code and pretrained weights are available at https://github.com/PUREPANEL1/D2Mamba.

</details>


### [110] [Prompt2Echo (P2Echo): Text-Prompted Multi-View Echocardiography Segmentation with One Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13425)
*Moein Heidari,Alvin Kimbowa,Mahdi Tabatabaei,Afshin Bozorgpour,Sina Ghorbani Kolahi,River Jiang,Wenjin Chen,David J. Foran,Jasmine Grewal,Dorit Merhof,Ilker Hacihaliloglu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出P2Echo统一框架，用结构化视图感知文本提示控制单一模型完成多视图超声心动图多元心脏结构分割。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多视图超声心动图自动分割对全面心脏评估至关重要，但不同标准声窗间解剖结构与外观差异大，难以用单一模型统一处理。

Method: 提出动态图文对齐模块DyITA，内含可微路由机制与token差分算子，依据所请求的视图与目标结构动态调制解码器特征，无需改动网络架构即可切换处理通路；区别于依赖隐式潜在先验的基础模型适配方式，显式以文本提示条件化分割。

Result: 在大型内部数据集及三个外部留出数据集上，P2Echo达到或超过视图专用专家模型与最先进通用模型的表现，且计算开销仅为其一小部分。

Conclusion: P2Echo为多平面心脏分析提供了一种高效、可控的统一分割方案，代码已开源。

Abstract: Automated segmentation in multi-view echocardiography is essential for comprehensive cardiac assessment, yet remains challenging due to substantial anatomical and appearance variability across standard acoustic windows. In this paper, we present P2Echo, a unified framework capable of segmenting diverse cardiac structures across multiple standard echocardiographic views using a single model controlled by structured view-aware text prompts. Unlike recent foundation model adaptations that rely on implicit latent priors, P2Echo explicitly conditions the segmentation process via a novel Dynamic Image-Text Alignment (DyITA) module. DyITA integrates a differentiable routing mechanism and token differential operators to dynamically modulate decoder features according to the requested view and target structure. This allows the network to adaptively switch its processing pathways for different views without architectural changes. Extensive experiments on a large-scale internal dataset (seen during training) and held-out evaluation on three external datasets demonstrate that P2Echo matches or exceeds both view-specific specialists and state-of-the-art universal models at a fraction of their computational cost, offering an efficient, controllable solution for multi-plane cardiac analysis. Code is available at: https://github.com/moeinheidari7829/P2Echo.

</details>


### [111] [ABRR: Transforming Temporal Sentence Grounding with Wasserstein-Adaptive Refinement and Precision Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13310)
*Xiang Fang,Wanlong Fang,Junhao Dong,Daizong Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出模型无关的后处理框架ABRR，通过时序分布平滑、基于最优传输的边界精修与质量驱动重排序，无需重训练即可提升任意TSG模型的定位与排序性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于Transformer的TSG方法存在两大缺陷：时序降采样导致量化误差使片段边界错位；预测排序基于语义匹配而非定位精度，排序次优。

Method: ABRR为模型无关后处理，含三部分：(1)时序分布平滑，用高斯核卷积抑制预测分数分布噪声；(2)基于最优传输的边界精修，利用2-Wasserstein距离与Sinkhorn迭代，以Monge-Kantorovich理论将粗边界对齐至真实时刻；(3)质量驱动重排序，用兼顾定位精度与语义相关性的复合分数重排预测。

Result: 在Charades-STA、ActivityNet Captions、QVHighlights基准上，于严格IoU阈值（如IoU=0.7）下取得一致提升，并改善排序性能。

Conclusion: ABRR可纠正量化误差并优先准确定位片段，计算高效、与现有模型兼容，是实用的通用TSG后处理方案。

Abstract: Temporal Sentence Grounding (TSG) aims to localize temporal segments in untrimmed videos that correspond to natural language queries, a critical task for video understanding with applications in video retrieval, summarization, and human-computer interaction. Despite significant progress driven by transformer-based models, existing TSG methods suffer from two key limitations: quantization errors due to temporal downsampling, which misalign segment boundaries, and suboptimal ranking of predictions based on semantic matching rather than localization precision. To address these challenges, we propose the Adaptive Boundary Refinement and Ranking (ABRR) framework, a novel, model-agnostic post-processing approach that enhances the performance of any TSG model without retraining. ABRR integrates three innovative components: (1) Temporal Distribution Smoothing, which mitigates noise in predicted score distributions using Gaussian kernel convolution; (2) Boundary Refinement via Optimal Transport, which leverages the 2-Wasserstein distance and Sinkhorn iterations to align coarse segment boundaries with true temporal moments, grounded in Monge-Kantorovich optimal transport theory; and (3) Quality-Driven Re-Ranking, which reorders predictions using a composite score balancing localization precision and semantic relevance. By correcting quantization errors and prioritizing accurately localized segments, ABRR achieves substantial improvements over state-of-the-art TSG models. Extensive experiments on benchmark datasets, including Charades-STA, ActivityNet Captions, and QVHighlights, demonstrate consistent gains at stringent IoU thresholds (e.g., IoU=0.7), alongside enhanced ranking performance. The framework's computational efficiency and compatibility with existing models make it a versatile and impactful solution for real-world TSG applications.

</details>


### [112] [DUO: Dual Readouts of Diffusion Models for OOD Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13304)
*Alireza Javid,Shirin Shoushtari,Nuria González Prelcic*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出DUO：一种无需训练、全无监督的扩散模型OOD检测器，将重构误差与中间去噪特征的"前导敏感谱"结合，作为互补信号，在重构误差失效时仍有效，平均AUROC提升约6个点并降低推理开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散模型的OOD检测普遍依赖重构误差，假设ID样本重构得更准确。但OOD输入可能被重构得同样好甚至更好，导致该评分不可靠，因而需要补充性的OOD信号。

Method: 提出以中间去噪特征的"前导敏感谱"（leading sensitivity spectrum）作为互补OOD信号，并设计DUO检测器：将重构误差与特征敏感度两个读数融合，完全无监督、无需训练，且推理成本较低。

Result: 相较最强基线平均AUROC提升约6个百分点，同时显著降低推理成本；在near-OOD与高分辨率基准上达到SOTA。

Conclusion: 特征敏感度可弥补重构误差在OOD检测中的失效，双读数融合的DUO在有效性、效率与泛化基准上均优于现有方法。

Abstract: Diffusion models are powerful generative methods for learning and sampling from complex data distributions. Safely deploying these models requires detecting inputs that fall outside their training distribution. Because diffusion models recover clean samples from noisy inputs, reconstruction error is often used for out-of-distribution (OOD) detection. This approach assumes that in-distribution (ID) inputs are reconstructed more accurately than OOD inputs. However, OOD inputs may be reconstructed equally well or even better, making this score unreliable. We therefore propose the leading sensitivity spectrum of intermediate denoising features as a complementary OOD signal and demonstrate that it remains informative when reconstruction error is misleading. We introduce DUal-readout of diffusion models for OOD detection (DUO), a fully unsupervised, training-free OOD detector that combines reconstruction error and feature sensitivity. DUO improves average AUROC by approximately 6% points over the strongest baselines while substantially reducing inference cost, and achieves SOTA on near-OOD and high-resolution benchmarks.

</details>


### [113] [Continuous Angular Control in Text-to-Image Generation via Geometric Token Encoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13172)
*Furkan Güzelant,Basavaraj Sunagad,Aysegul Dundar,Adam Kortylewski*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对扩散模型难以精确控制角度型属性（如时钟指针时间、圆的分割比例）的失效，提出几何token编码框架：将角度以正余弦周期特征编码为角色特定token注入提示词，并用随机定位松弛（SLR）实现无需推理期定位输入的空间接地，配合指针互换偏好目标，使时钟时间生成Acc@6°从<1%提升至63–78%，圆比例生成达89.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本到图像扩散模型虽能生成逼真的模拟时钟，却无法控制其显示的时间：四个预训练模型中生成时钟双针均落在请求时间±6°以内的比例不足1%，模型坍缩到视觉上占主导的10:10姿势；类似地，请求圆形的特定扇形比例时，模型只返回完整或常规切分的对象。作者观察到两类失败具有共同几何结构——目标量都可由角度变量参数化，因此需要一种直接以角度为条件的生成控制机制。

Method: 1) 几何token编码：将每个角度分量编码为周期性sine-cosine特征，投影为角色特定的token嵌入，插入提示词并送入冻结的文本编码器（training-free于主干）；2) 随机定位松弛（SLR）：训练中将注意力监督由组件级逐步随机松弛到对象级再到无约束定位，从而在推理时无需提供定位输入即可实现空间接地；3) 指针互换偏好目标：针对时钟任务，惩罚将正确角度分配给错误指针角色的情况。

Result: 在模拟时钟任务上，各评测方式下达到63–78%的Acc@6°，基线低于1%；在圆形比例生成任务上取得89.5% Acc@0.10，在未见物体类别上保持88.8%、未见目标值上84.9%，而预训练基线约20%。

Conclusion: 角度参数化是理解并修复扩散模型细粒度数值/几何属性控制失败的有效视角；通过周期特征token化、监督松弛与角色偏好约束，可在冻结文本编码器条件下大幅提升角度型属性的可控性，并具备对新类别与新目标值的泛化能力。

Abstract: Text-to-image diffusion models can render a photorealistic analog clock but cannot control the time it displays. Across four pretrained models, fewer than 1% of generated clocks place both hands within 6 degrees of the requested time; the models instead collapse onto visually dominant configurations such as the canonical 10:10 pose. The same failure appears when a specific fraction of a circular object is requested, where models return complete or conventionally sliced objects. We observe that these two failures share a geometric structure: both target quantities can be parameterized by angular variables.
  We introduce a geometric token encoding framework that conditions generation directly on these angles. Each angular component is encoded as a periodic sine-cosine feature and projected into a role-specific token embedding that is injected into the prompt before a frozen text encoder. To spatially ground these tokens without requiring localization input at inference, we introduce Stochastic Localization Relaxation (SLR), which stochastically relaxes attention supervision from component-level to object-level to unconstrained localization during training. For clocks, we additionally introduce a hand-swap preference objective that discourages assigning the correct angles to the wrong hand roles. On analog clocks our method reaches 63-78% Acc@6 degrees across evaluation methods, compared with below 1% for the baselines. On circular-fraction generation, it achieves 89.5% Acc@0.10, while maintaining 88.8% on unseen object categories and 84.9% on unseen target values, compared with roughly 20% for pretrained baselines.

</details>


### [114] [Preserving Cultural Records: A Large-Scale Benchmark and Visual-Prior Routing Network for Ancient Book Image Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13010)
*Shumin Fan,Tianyu Song,Hongming Chen,Xiaodong Zhou,Xiang Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出大规模合成配对古籍图像复原基准AncBench（10万对）与退化感知视觉先验路由网络AncRestore，实现复合退化下的全局外观与局部文字细节恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 古籍图像因长期老化、环境暴露与物理损伤存在复杂退化；现有基准规模有限且依赖简化的退化模拟，难以表征真实退化模式，限制了复原模型的泛化能力。

Method: 构建AncBench：10万对齐的干净-退化图像对，通过综合退化管线建模材质老化、纸张纹理变化、模糊、污渍、水致几何畸变与局部遮挡。提出AncRestore：先从退化图像学习退化表征，再依据退化特征动态选择并融合多种视觉先验；由动态路由机制结合专家级与区域级路由，自适应调节各先验在不同区域的贡献。

Result: 实验表明AncRestore能有效抑制复合退化，保持文字结构，复原质量优于对比方法，体现其在文化遗产保护中智能古籍图像复原的应用潜力。

Conclusion: 真实感复合退化基准与退化感知先验路由机制相结合，可提升古籍图像复原的泛化性与细节保真度，为文化遗产数字化修复提供可行方案。

Abstract: Ancient books preserve invaluable cultural heritage, yet their images often suffer from complex degradations caused by long-term ageing, environmental exposure, and physical damage. Existing ancient book image restoration benchmarks are limited in scale and rely on simplified degradation simulations, which cannot fully represent real-world degradation patterns and restrict the generalization ability of restoration models. To address these challenges, we introduce AncBench, a large-scale synthetic paired benchmark containing 100K aligned clean-degraded image pairs. AncBench models realistic composite degradations through a comprehensive degradation pipeline that considers material ageing, paper texture variation, blur, staining, water-induced geometric distortion, and local occlusion. Furthermore, we propose AncRestore, a degradation-aware visual-prior routing network for ancient book image restoration. AncRestore first learns degradation representations from degraded images and then dynamically selects and integrates suitable visual priors according to different degradation characteristics. Guided by these degradation cues, a dynamic routing mechanism combines expert-wise and region-wise routing strategies adaptively adjusts the contribution of these priors across different regions, enabling the model to recover both global appearance and local text details. Extensive experiments show that AncRestore effectively suppresses composite degradations while preserving text structures and enhancing restoration quality against other methods, highlighting its application potential for intelligent ancient book image restoration in cultural heritage preservation.

</details>


### [115] [Marrying Capacity and Precision for Efficient 2D Gaussian Image Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13784)
*Lin Dong,Qi Ming,Liuqian Wang,Wentao Chao,Yufei Gao,Chengming Liu,Lei Shi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CP-GS是一个容量-精度耦合的2D高斯泼溅图像表示框架，在共享码率约束下联合优化高斯容量分配与属性精度，在DIV2K和Kodak上取得更优的率失真性能且保持较低优化开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有2D高斯表示在有限预算下的率失真性能取决于高斯容量与参数精度的分配方式，但已有方法通常将这两类资源分开优化，忽略了内容相关的容量需求以及属性间异构的量化敏感性。

Method: 1) 内容自适应高斯表示：在固定高斯预算下，将高斯从冗余区域动态重分配至表示需求更高的区域；2) 分层失真感知表示量化：基于高效渲染的校准，在属性间分配精度，并在异构属性组内进一步细化；3) 容量-精度联合优化：构建不同高斯数量与精度配置的候选表示，依据解码失真与实际序列化大小选择工作点。

Result: 在DIV2K与Kodak数据集上，CP-GS在多种预算下优于代表性2D高斯表示与隐式神经表示，且保持固定的总体表示预算与较低优化成本。

Conclusion: 联合优化空间容量与参数精度有助于实现紧凑的高斯图像表示，验证了容量-精度耦合设计的有效性。

Abstract: 2D Gaussian representations provide an explicit and efficient way to represent images, but their rate-distortion performance under limited budgets depends critically on how Gaussian capacity and parameter precision are allocated. Existing methods typically optimize these two resources separately, overlooking content-dependent capacity demands and heterogeneous quantization sensitivity. We present CP-GS, a capacity-precision coupled Gaussian splatting framework for budget-aware 2D Gaussian image representation that jointly optimizes capacity allocation and attribute precision under a shared rate constraint. CP-GS first constructs a content-adaptive Gaussian representation by dynamically reallocating a fixed Gaussian budget from redundant regions to areas with higher representation demand. It then introduces hierarchical distortion-aware representation quantization, using efficient rendering-based calibration to allocate precision across Gaussian attributes and further refine it within heterogeneous attribute groups. Finally, it jointly optimizes Gaussian capacity and attribute precision by constructing candidate representations with different Gaussian counts and precision configurations and selecting operating points according to decoded distortion and actual serialized size. Experiments on DIV2K and Kodak show that CP-GS achieves improved rate-distortion performance over representative 2D Gaussian and implicit neural representations across a range of budgets, while maintaining a fixed overall representation budget and low optimization cost. These results demonstrate the benefit of jointly optimizing spatial capacity and parameter precision for compact Gaussian image representation.

</details>


### [116] [Lipschitz-Controlled Flow Matching for Robust Image Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13995)
*Sedjro Salomon Hotegni,Konstantin Sonntag,Paul-Christian Bürkner,Sebastian Peitz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LipsFlower通过Lipschitz感知的流匹配提升生成式图像复原的稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流匹配模型能产生高质量图像复原，但其学到的动力学可能放大微小输入扰动，导致重建不稳定；现有方法缺乏对扰动放大机制的定量分析与结构性控制。

Method: 推导Flow-from-Y（以噪声扰动观测初始化ODE）与观测条件后验采样（速度场全程以观测为条件）两类形式的稳定性界；据此设计LipHDiT——Lipschitz感知的沙漏型扩散Transformer作为向量场骨干，包含稳定的近似谱归一化、有界调制与凸残差混合；并在采样插值状态上施加对抗性有限半径增益正则，惩罚对状态及（条件采样下）观测的过度敏感。

Result: 在盲人脸复原任务上评估两种形式，测量对抗鲁棒性与重建感知质量；LipsFlower在降低扰动放大的同时，复原质量与所评估基线保持可比。

Conclusion: Lipschitz感知的流匹配可改善生成式图像复原的稳定性。

Abstract: Flow-matching models can produce high-quality image restorations, but their learned dynamics may amplify small input perturbations and make reconstructions unstable. We introduce LipsFlower to reduce this amplification. We derive stability bounds for Flow-from-$Y$, which initializes the ordinary differential equation from a noise-perturbed observation, as well as for observation-conditioned posterior sampling, which conditions the velocity field on the observation throughout integration. Based on this analysis, we develop LipHDiT, a Lipschitz-aware hourglass diffusion transformer that serves as the vector-field architecture of LipsFlower. LipHDiT incorporates stabilized approximate spectral normalization, bounded modulation, and convex residual mixing. We also use adversarial finite-radius gain regularization at sampled interpolation states to penalize excessive sensitivity to the state and, for conditional sampling, the observation. We test both formulations on blind face restoration, measuring adversarial robustness and the perceptual quality of the reconstructions. LipsFlower reduces perturbation amplification while keeping restoration quality competitive with the evaluated baselines. These results suggest that Lipschitz-aware flow matching can improve the stability of generative image restoration.

</details>


### [117] [SpecValley: Aggregation as Spectral Valley in Dense Heterophilic Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14176)
*Christoph Jahn,Delvin Bacho,Urs Waldmann,Bastian Goldluecke*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文指出异配图节点分类中前置的度归一化聚合本身构成一个"谱谷"，其位置由归一化而非数据决定，恰好落在稠密图特征值聚集处。作者将可学习聚合(SpecValley)与普通谱滤波器分两阶段结合，形式化分析二者交互，证明度归一化响应1−λ在该聚集点处消失，并给出谱能量预算说明聚合移除的信息无法被线性滤波器恢复，最终在异配基准上取得强结果并通过控制消融将增益归因于聚合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作不断设计更具表达力的传播算子，却把传播前的聚合步骤固定为度归一化。作者观察到度归一化本身是一个谱谷：它在λ=1附近衰减固定中频带，而该位置由归一化确定而非数据驱动，且恰是稠密图特征值累积之处，可能精确削弱承载标签的信号。

Method: 从表示而非传播入手，分两阶段：(1) SpecValley——对每个图学习谱谷的位置、锐度与两侧增益，作为可学习聚合；(2) 其后接一个普通谱滤波器。作者形式化刻画聚合算子与下游滤波器的交互，证明度归一化响应1−λ在该特征值累积点处恰好为零，并建立谱能量预算分析；同时逐图度量谱谷所移除的标签能量。

Result: 在多个异配图基准上取得强性能；通过聚合算子与下游滤波器的控制消融实验，将性能增益归因于聚合阶段而非滤波器；谱能量预算表明聚合削去的频带能量无法由任何线性谱滤波器恢复；并量化了每张图上谱谷实际移除的标签能量。

Conclusion: 聚合而非传播是异配图节点分类中被忽视的关键瓶颈；度归一化的固定谱谷有信息损失且不可逆，故应从可学习聚合出发；谱拥挤本身不决定收益，收益取决于谱谷移除多少标签能量。

Abstract: Node classification in heterophilic graphs remains an open problem: aggregating over neighbourhoods can damp precisely the part of the signal that carries the labels. The field has answered with ever more expressive propagation operators, leaving the aggregation step that precedes them fixed by degree normalisation. However, degree normalisation acts as a spectral valley in its own right, attenuating a fixed middle band whose position at lambda equal 1 is set by the normalisation rather than by the data, exactly where the eigenvalues of dense graphs accumulate. We therefore begin with the representations rather than the propagation, in two stages: a learnable aggregation followed by an ordinary spectral filter. The first, SpecValley learns the position, sharpness and flank gains of the valley for every graph. We show formally how the aggregation operator and downstream filter interact. We establish that the response 1 minus lambda of degree normalisation vanishes exactly at that accumulation point. A spectral energy budget shows that what aggregation removes from a band, no linear spectral filter can recover. Crowding alone says nothing about the benefit. That is decided by how much label energy the valley removes, which we measure for every graph. SpecValley achieves strong results across heterophilic benchmarks, and a controlled ablation over aggregation operators and downstream filters attributes the gain to aggregation rather than the filter.

</details>


### [118] [TAMEing the Open-World Personalization: Towards Open-Set Personalized MLLM Assistant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14118)
*Rongpei Hong,Jian Lang,Ting Zhong,Fan Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TAME-O，首个长上下文开放集个性化多模态大模型助手，通过免训练、即插即用的概念调和技能（CR Skill），在持续交互中自动解锁新概念并避免与已知概念混淆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有个性化MLLM研究局限于静态、预定义的个性化概念集合，且需用户手动频繁扩充以容纳新出现的概念，在动态开放世界场景中不可行。

Method: 将传统MLLM个性化重构为开放集（Open-Set）问题，构建TAME-O长上下文开放集个性化助手；核心为免训练、即插即用的概念调和技能（CR Skill），支持在长时间人机交互中自动解锁新概念，并防止与已解锁概念混淆，实现概念的持续累积与已知概念建模的渐进优化。

Result: 在开放集个性化设置下的大量实验验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: TAME-O通过开放集建模与CR Skill，使MLLM能够在长上下文中持续、自动地学习个性化概念，突破静态预定义概念集的限制。

Abstract: Personalizing Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) as intelligent assistants has recently emerged as an active research topic, where an MLLM is expected to go beyond generic, one-size-fits-all replies, and instead generate responses grounded in user-specific objects, entities, and concepts encountered during interactions. Nevertheless, existing studies mainly focus on personalizing MLLMs on a static, predefined set of personalized concepts and require users to manually and frequently extend this set to accommodate newly emerging concepts, which is infeasible in dynamic-world scenarios. To address this limitation, we recast conventional MLLM personalization as an Open-Set problem, and propose TAME-O, the first long-context open-set personalized MLLM assistant. Specifically, TAME-O introduces a training-free, plug-and-play Concept Reconciliation Skill (CR Skill). It enables the assistant to automatically unlock novel concepts during prolonged human-MLLM interactions while preventing confusion with previously unlocked ones, allowing the MLLM to continually grasp more and more concepts and progressively refine the modeling of known ones. Extensive experiments under the open-set personalization setting underscore the efficacy of our method.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [119] [Program-Aided Patchable Skill Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14019)
*Jiahao Wang,Yuqi Xiong,Qing Yang,Zhipeng Xu,Zhenghao Liu,Zulong Chen,Yubin Bao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出PASE框架，用可执行程序承载技能中的过程性逻辑，实现可打补丁式迭代演化，性能超基线2.8%以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体技能多以自然语言文档表示，将过程性逻辑与描述性知识混杂，导致技能难以维护与演化。

Method: PASE（Program-Aided Patchable Skill Evolution）：将技能与可执行程序关联，把可复用的过程性逻辑分离为可编辑程序，从而对可执行组件进行定向更新，减少对自然语言技能文本的反复修改；通过迭代方式演化技能。

Result: 在多模型、多基准实验中稳定优于所有基线，提升超过2.8%；分析显示其演化稳定性与泛化能力更强。

Conclusion: 程序化知识在技能演化中起关键作用，将过程性逻辑与描述性知识解耦是提升智能体技能可维护性与适应性的有效途径。

Abstract: Agent skills have emerged as an effective means of enabling LLM agents to solve complex, interactive tasks by encoding reusable knowledge without modifying model parameters. Recent approaches further improve skill adaptation by distilling reusable experience from agent execution trajectories and evolving skills based on downstream task feedback. However, existing methods primarily represent skills as natural language documents, intertwining procedural logic with descriptive knowledge and making skills difficult to maintain and evolve. To address this limitation, we propose PASE (Program-Aided Patchable Skill Evolution), a program-aided framework that iteratively evolves skills by associating them with executable programs. Unlike prior approaches that encode trajectory-derived experience primarily in natural language, PASE separates reusable procedural logic into editable programs, enabling targeted updates to executable components while reducing the need to repeatedly revise natural language skills. Extensive experiments across diverse models and benchmarks demonstrate that PASE consistently outperforms all baselines, by achieving over 2.8% improvements. Further analyses show that PASE exhibits greater evolutionary stability and generalisation capability, highlighting the critical role of programmatic knowledge in skill evolution. We will release all data and code via GitHub.

</details>


### [120] [Blurring the Block Boundaries: Overlapping Block Denoising for Efficient Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14387)
*Amir Mann,Jonathan Fhima,Shaked Brody,Or Avitan,Asif Zamir,Shahar Tsiper,Niv Nayman*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出免训练、免超参的B3方法，通过在单次前向中并行精化两个块并动态开启下一个块，加速块扩散语言模型推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 块扩散+自编辑的DLM中，每个块的生成速度受限于其最慢的token，且下一块必须等当前块完成后才能开始，造成串行瓶颈。

Method: B3（Blurring the Block Boundaries）：在共享的一次前向计算中并行精化两个相邻块；模型按需在线自校准，仅在已准备好生成下一块时才“开启”该块，无需额外训练或超参数调优，可直接嵌入任意自编辑块扩散模型。

Result: 在10个涵盖不同任务与难度的数据集上，于当前最优DLM上评估：生成质量与基线相当，速度提升11.5–14.5%，并推进了DLM推理的延迟-质量Pareto前沿。

Conclusion: 通过模糊块边界实现块间重叠计算，可在不损失质量的前提下显著降低推理延迟，为DLM推理加速提供通用、即插即用的方案。

Abstract: Diffusion Language Models (DLMs) have emerged as effective alternatives to autoregressive models for latency-critical tasks, enabling faster token generation. Recent advances employ block diffusion with self-editing capabilities, where each block refines tokens iteratively before advancing to the next one. Yet each block is only as fast as its slowest token, and the next block cannot start before it. We suggest Blurring the Block Boundaries (B3), a novel method with which two blocks are refined in parallel within a shared forward pass. B3 calibrates itself on the fly, opening the next block only once the model is ready to start generating it. It is a training-free and hyperparameter-free acceleration method which seamlessly integrates within any self-editing block diffusion model. Evaluated on state-of-the-art DLMs across 10 diverse datasets spanning varied tasks and difficulty levels, B3 results in on par generation quality and 11.5-14.5% speedup compared to the base model, pushing the Pareto frontier for latency-quality trade-offs in DLM inference.

</details>


### [121] [Brasilidade: Measuring Brazilian Identity for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12906)
*José Roberto Homeli da Silva,Felipe Jorge do Nascimento,Lucas Coutinho Freitas,William Alberto Cruz Castañeda,Marcellus Amadeus*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出巴西葡萄牙语LLM评测基准Brasilidade，并发布首个领域（语言、语法、变体）任务；结果显示模型规模不决定表现，中等模型可超越大模型，葡语专用模型总体未达平均，任务难度差异显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对巴西葡萄牙语评测LLM的方法存在局限，缺少标准化、覆盖多领域、能揭示语言细微差别的基准，导致模型在该语言上的不足无法被系统识别与改进。

Method: 构建Brasilidade评测方法学：由跨四个领域的标准化任务组成，本次发布第一领域任务，聚焦巴西葡萄牙语的语言、语法与变体细微差别；并开源项目仓库。

Result: 模型规模本身不决定性能，多个中等规模模型在评测协议中超越更大模型；葡语专用模型总体未高于平均水平，尽管经过针对性训练；不同任务难度分层明显，部分任务对多数模型容易，部分构成极端挑战，部分结果混杂且性能波动大。

Conclusion: 结果凸显了面向巴西葡萄牙语、针对模型局限设计基准的必要性，以指导后续训练周期加以改进。

Abstract: This work presents a new benchmark that aims to address the limitations of current methods for evaluating LLMs in Brazilian Portuguese. We introduced Brasilidade, an assessment methodology comprised of standardized tasks distributed across four different domains, and we are making available the tasks from its first domain, which address nuances of the language, grammar, and variations of Brazilian Portuguese. The results show that model size, by itself, does not determine performance; several medium-sized models outperformed larger models in the protocols evaluated. Specific models for Portuguese, in general, did not perform above average, despite their targeted training. Different task levels revealed significant nuances: while some tasks proved easy for most models, others posed extreme challenges, and some yielded mixed results, with wide variations in performance. This clearly illustrates the need for benchmarks that address the limitations of the models in Brazilian Portuguese, allowing future training cycles to seek to overcome them. Project repository https://github.com/soberania-oficial/brasilidade

</details>


### [122] [Visual Similarity in Adult Word Extension and Artificial Word Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14116)
*Patricio M. Vera*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 视觉相似度（DINOv2编码器距离）只能部分解释命名泛化/词义扩展，教学样例的组成（上位/下位层次）贡献了距离所不能捕捉的部分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨两个对象共享名称时，视觉相似性能解释多少泛化行为，以及教学样例的构成如何额外影响成人词义扩展与人工学习者习得。

Method: （1）重新分析Lewis & Frank (2018)成人词扩展研究的试次级数据（520人），用冻结的自监督视觉编码器DINOv2计算测试图与样例的距离，检验距离、taxonomic level与教学条件对扩展的影响及样例组成效应；（2）用同一编码器构建人工学习者，学习连接两个类别的词，比较近/中/远类别对的命名准确率，并将编码器距离与三个数据集的人类相似性与混淆度指标做相关；另用基于单一儿童经验训练的第二个编码器做复现检验（次要分析依内部分析计划，非外部预注册）。

Result: 成人数据中距离与扩展显著相关（每1个SD，log odds = -3.23）；事后探索分析提示在控制taxonomic level与教学条件后该关联仍在教学域内存在。样例组成影响显著：三个下位样例后仅扩展到1.1%的上位项目，三个上位样例后为77.5%。人工学习者对近/中/远类别对的命名准确率为84.7%、65.4%、53.0%。编码器距离与人类相似性/混淆度相关为 rho = .501–.795，但未能得出人类距离预测学习者难度的确证性证据；儿童经验训练的编码器复现了层级顺序但未复现对级效应。

Conclusion: 视觉相似性在成人与人工系统中都部分组织了泛化，但样例组成携带了所用距离度量无法捕捉的信息，说明泛化受表征相似性与教学输入结构共同塑造。

Abstract: When two objects share a name, how much does their visual similarity explain, and what do the teaching examples add? First, we reanalyzed trial-level data from a large adult word-extension study (Lewis & Frank, 2018), computing each test image's distance to the examples shown in the space of a frozen self-supervised visual encoder (DINOv2). Distance was associated with extension (log odds -3.23 per standard deviation; 520 people), and an exploratory post hoc analysis suggested that this association persisted within the taught domain after controlling for taxonomic level and teaching condition. Example composition also mattered: after three examples, adults extended a word to 1.1% of superordinate items when the examples were subordinate and to 77.5% when they were superordinate. Second, in an artificial learner using the same encoder, words joining two categories were learned less accurately the farther apart the categories were (84.7%, 65.4%, and 53.0% naming accuracy for near, mid, and far pairs). The encoder's distances correlated with human similarity and confusability measures from three datasets (rho = .501-.795), but this representational alignment did not translate into conclusive evidence that human-derived distances predict learner difficulty, and a second encoder trained on one child's experience reproduced the tier ordering but not the pair-level effect. The secondary human data were analyzed under an internal plan fixed before the confirmatory analyses (not externally preregistered). Visual similarity accounts for part of how generalization is organized in both systems, while the composition of examples contributes what the distance we used does not capture.

</details>


### [123] [The Indic Token Tax in 2026: Measuring Tokenizer. Disparity and Its Cost for the Languages of India](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13058)
*Johin Johny Arimbur,Ankur Ashit Ghosh,Shervin Sam*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文量化了九个公开分词器在印度25种语言及印英混合语料上的"分词税"：同一内容在GPT-4o上需英语1.57倍（印地语）至5.01倍（奥里亚语）的token，桑塔利语达13.7倍；仅Sarvam-30B/105B在所有语言上低于2.6倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 商业语言模型API按token计费，分词器对某语言编码效率低会使该语言的成本更高、推理更慢、固定上下文窗口可容纳文本更少。作者希望系统测量并量化这种跨语言的分词不平等（token tax）。

Method: 对9个公开分词器（含GPT-4、GPT-4o、Qwen2.5、DeepSeek-V3、Mistral-Nemo及4个Sarvam模型）进行评测：语言覆盖FLORES-200中印度全部25种语言（2,009句专业翻译、与英语对齐），外加PHINC中13,732对人工翻译的Hinglish-英语社媒句对。此外以官方定价构建成本估算场景，并以公开授权的印地语/马拉地语形态切分集计算词内边界与语素边界的F1。

Result: GPT-4o分词器：印地语1.57倍、奥里亚语5.01倍、Ol Chiki文字桑塔利语13.7倍；GPT-4最高12.8倍。Sarvam-30B/105B是唯一在每种语言上均低于2.6倍（中位1.53）的第三方分词器。成本示例（2026-11-05官方标价、含假设）：GPT-4o上月处理10万次对话的客服机器人，英语约56,640卢比，马拉地语约102,829卢比（属估算而非实测）。形态对齐方面，所有分词器词内边界落在语素边界上的比例不超过35%，边界F1最高0.48。

Conclusion: 主要公共分词器对印度诸语言存在显著分词税，且其分词单元与形态学边界对齐度普遍很低。仅Sarvam系列表现相对均衡。作者在配套论文中提出一种可降低该税的分词器，并开源代码、固定版本产物与原始token计数。

Abstract: Commercial language model APIs bill per token, so a tokenizer that needs more tokens for the same content in one language makes that language more expensive, slower, and able to fit less text in a fixed context window. We measure this disparity for nine public tokenizers, including those of GPT-4, GPT-4o, Qwen2.5, DeepSeek-V3, Mistral-Nemo and four Sarvam models, on all 25 languages of India available in FLORES-200 (2,009 professionally translated sentences aligned with English) and on 13,732 human-translated Hinglish-English social media pairs from PHINC. On GPT-4o's tokenizer the same content needs between 1.57 times (Hindi) and 5.01 times (Odia) the tokens of English, and 13.7 times for Santali in Ol Chiki script; GPT-4's tokenizer needs up to 12.8 times. Sarvam-30B/105B is the only third-party tokenizer that stays below 2.6 times on every language (median 1.53). In an illustrative scenario using official list prices accessed on 5 November 2026 and stated assumptions, a support bot handling 100,000 chats a month on GPT-4o would cost about INR 56,640 in English and INR 102,829 in Marathi; these are estimates, not measured production costs. Against an openly licensed Hindi and Marathi morphological segmentation set, no tokenizer places more than 35% of its word-internal boundaries on morpheme boundaries (boundary F1 at most 0.48). In a companion paper we present a tokenizer that reduces this tax. Code, pinned artifacts and raw token counts are available at https://github.com/HavonAI/indic-token-tax.

</details>


### [124] [HandsOnAid: Building and Evaluating an Offline First-Aid Assistant for Emergency Response Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14059)
*Rezwan Islam Salvi,Saiyma Sittul Muna,Mushfiqur Rahman Mushfique*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 构建基于 4-bit Gemma 2B + QLoRA 微调的端侧急救助手 HandsOnAid，在无网络的 Pixel 7 Pro 上 6.86 秒出首字、14.88 秒出完整回答；微调使均分较基座模型提升 +1.08 至 2.34，最佳配置（4-bit 微调+检索）为 2.51/5。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 紧急场景常无手机信号、附近无救护车，需要能在手机上离线运行的小型语言模型提供急救建议，且其建议必须安全、准确，因此需评估端侧小模型在急救问答上的可行性与安全边界。

Method: 构建 5,550 条急救问答对的 FirstAidQA 数据集，用 QLoRA 对 4-bit Gemma 2B 微调；对比 7 种配置（基座、4-bit/8-bit 微调、长度下限、安全闸门、检索增强等）；评测集为 41 对符合 ANZCOR 指南、经医学审校的问答，采用严格 0–5 分评分量表与 12 条安全覆盖规则；由 4 个前沿 LLM 评审盲评分，并用 45 个隐藏对照题检验评审可靠性；另由医学专业人士对 40 条回答进行盲评；并做关键词覆盖审计与设备端时延测试。

Result: 微调较基座模型均分提升 +1.08 至 2.34；最佳配置为 4-bit 微调 + 检索，得分 2.51/5；医学专业人士盲评结果与 LLM 评审均分接近；关键词审计显示训练语料对 ANZCOR 指南中若干评分量表所要求的细节覆盖有限，可能是低分原因；Pixel 7 Pro 六线程、离线条件下首字显示平均 6.86 秒、完整回答 14.88 秒（10 题平均）。

Conclusion: 端侧 4-bit 小模型可离线、低时延地提供急救建议，微调与检索带来稳定增益，LLM 评审与专家盲评一致，可作为廉价评估代理；但绝对得分偏低，主要瓶颈在于训练语料对指南关键细节覆盖不足，需在数据侧加强而非仅依赖模型侧优化。

Abstract: Emergencies can occur where there is no phone signal and no ambulance nearby. A small language model on a phone can be helpful in such situations, but its advice needs to be safe and accurate. We built HandsOnAid, a 4-bit Gemma 2B model fine-tuned with QLoRA on FirstAidQA dataset, a corpus of 5,550 first-aid question-answer pairs. We compared seven configurations, including the base, 4-bit and 8-bit fine-tuned models, and versions with a length floor, safety gate, or retrieval. We used a 41-pair question-answer bank with medically reviewed references that followed ANZCOR guidelines, and a strict 0-5 score rubric, with twelve safety overrides. Four frontier LLM judges (GPT-5.6 Sol, Claude Opus 5, DeepSeek V4 Pro, GLM-5.3) blindly scored every answer, with 45 hidden controls used to check judge reliability. Fine-tuning improved the mean score by +1.08 over the base model to 2.34. The best configuration scored 2.51 out of 5 which was the fine-tuned 4-bit model with retrieval. A medical professional also conducted a blind evaluation of 40 answers across the base, the 4-bit fine-tuned, and the retrieval configurations, and the scores were similar to the LLM judges' average scores. A keyword audit found limited coverage of several details of ANZCOR guidelines in the training corpus, that are required by the rubric, which suggests that missing information in the training data could be a cause of the low scores. On a smartphone device (Pixel 7 Pro) with six CPU threads and no network connection, the 4-bit fine-tuned model produced its first displayable text in 6.86 seconds and a complete answer in 14.88 seconds on average over ten questions.

</details>


### [125] [A Concept-Based Interpretable Framework for Clinical Text-based Prediction via Token-Concept Attention without Concept Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13564)
*Jifan Gao,Junjie Hu,John Caskey,Madeline Oguss,Simona Cristea,Anoop Mayampurath,Matthew Churpek,Majid Afshar,Guanhua Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种基于定制化交叉注意力瓶颈的框架，在无需概念标注监督的情况下为临床文本预测任务实现基于概念的可解释性，性能接近黑盒模型（AUROC差距在0.02以内）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于概念的解释方法通常需要标注的概念标签来训练模型识别输入中的概念，而临床文本中获取此类标注成本高昂。因此本文希望在没有概念监督的条件下，为语言模型的临床预测任务实现基于概念的解释。

Method: 设计定制化的交叉注意力瓶颈：先用预训练语言模型对临床文本和医学概念的文本描述进行分词与嵌入；再用交叉注意力模块估计每个概念在每个文本token上的存在程度，生成贯穿整份病历的概念相关性分数；对分数进行池化后输入线性预测层，以可解释的方式建模临床结局。

Result: 在两个任务上与黑盒模型对比：私有数据集的胸部创伤严重程度分层任务，以及公开MIMIC-III数据集的院内死亡预测任务。所提框架的AUROC与对应黑盒模型差距在0.02以内，并支持队列级和患者级解释，可刻画整体概念重要性与患者特异的概念贡献。

Conclusion: 该框架无需真实概念存在标签即可实现基于概念的解释，在保持临床预测任务强性能的同时提升了临床文本预测的透明度。

Abstract: Objective: Concept-based interpretation has emerged as a promising approach for explaining blackbox models using human-understandable, high-level concepts. However, most existing methods require annotated concept labels to train the model to identify concepts in the input, and obtaining such annotations in clinical text is often expensive. This study aims to enable concept-based interpretation for language models without concept supervision on clinical prediction tasks. Methods: We propose a novel framework that enables concept-based interpretation without concept supervision through a tailored cross-attention bottleneck. Clinical text and the text descriptions of medical concepts are first tokenized and embedded by a pretrained language model. A cross-attention module then estimates the presence of each concept for each text token, producing concept relevance scores that capture how concepts are involved throughout the note. These scores are pooled and used in a linear prediction layer to model clinical outcomes in an interpretable manner. Results: The performance of the proposed framework was compared against blackbox models across two tasks: chest trauma severity stratification using a private dataset and an in-hospital mortality prediction task using the publicly available MIMIC-III dataset. The proposed framework achieved AUROC values within 0.02 of the corresponding blackbox models. The framework also enabled cohort- and patient-level interpretation, capturing the overall concept importance across the cohort and patient-specific concept contributions. Conclusion: Our proposed framework realizes concept-based interpretation without requiring ground-truth concept presence labels. It improves the transparency of clinical text predictions while maintaining strong performance in clinical prediction tasks.

</details>


### [126] [Are Dense Rewards Worth Their Tokens in GRPO?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13560)
*Vikash Singh,Weicong Chen,Debargha Ganguly,Yanyan Zhang,Mohsen Hariri,Sreehari Sankar,Wang Yang,Chaoda Song,Alexander Nemecek,Osama Zafar,Yuting Shao,Kai Ye,Biyao Zhang,Xinpeng Li,Shouren Wang,Erman Ayday,Jing Ma,Sanmukh Rao Kuppannagari,Vipin Chaudhary*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出"匹配算力"（matched-compute）协议——以生成 token 数与训练 GPU 小时为统一预算，结合预注册实验，重新评估 RLVR 中的稠密过程奖励与提示选择策略。结论是：在匹配算力下，Monte-Carlo 过程奖励由"领先"变为显著落后于 GRPO，而"挑选预测成功率接近 1/2 的提示"带来的增益则稳健成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 RLVR 工作常以"每次更新"的效果比较不同奖励设计，或用单批次的梯度检验来验证奖励，但忽略了过程奖励（如 Monte-Carlo 续写打分）的采样开销——本研究中续写生成占全部生成 token 的 91%。这种忽略导致对方法优劣的判断失真，因此需要一个公平、可控的比较口径。

Method: 提出 matched-compute 协议：给每种训练干预分配相同的生成 token 预算与训练 GPU 小时预算；配合预注册研究与副作用对照，共在六个模型上运行 1,668 次实验。同时检验 per-token 梯度测试作为代理指标的有效性，并与基于"预测成功率接近 0.5"的提示筛选策略进行对照。

Result: 匹配算力后排名发生反转：在 Qwen2.5-1.5B 上，Monte-Carlo 奖励每次更新占优，但等 token 下最终低 14.3 个百分点、等训练时间下低 7.8 个百分点；四种稠密奖励变体在同等训练时间内均未超过 GRPO。per-token 梯度测试给出的排序与训练一致，但系统性高估 1–2 个百分点，幅度与其欲检测的增益相当。提示筛选策略在匹配算力下提升准确率：Qwen3-0.6B +1.7 点、Qwen2.5-1.5B +0.8 点，并在新随机种子与未见问题上复现。"候选提示被预测为更可能失败"这一简单规则在六项预注册测试中的五项上预测出增益出现位置。两类增益均出现在训练早期，且将 GRPO 初始学习率加倍即可接近其效果（差距在 0.5 点以内）。

Conclusion: 训练干预的评估应在匹配算力下进行：该协议推翻了部分表面增益（如 Monte-Carlo 过程奖励），同时确认了另一些（如近 0.5 成功率提示筛选），说明算力预算口径会实质改变方法排序，应成为 RLVR 方法验证的标准做法。

Abstract: RLVR trains language models to reason by rewarding correct final answers. A widely used algorithm, group-relative policy optimization (GRPO), gives every token of a solution the same credit, so dense process rewards have been proposed to credit intermediate steps, for example with a Monte-Carlo reward that scores each step by sampling continuations from it. Comparing such a reward with GRPO per training update, or vetting it with a gradient test on a single batch, leaves out what it costs: in our setting, the continuations alone take 91% of all generated tokens. We introduce a matched-compute protocol that gives every training intervention the same budget, measured in generated tokens and training GPU-hours, and tests it in preregistered studies with controls for side effects. Across 1,668 runs on six models, matching compute reverses the ranking. On Qwen2.5-1.5B, the Monte-Carlo reward leads standard GRPO per update, yet ends 14.3 percentage points lower at equal tokens and 7.8 points lower at equal training time, and none of four dense-reward variants outperforms GRPO even with as much training time. A per-token gradient test orders these rewards as training does but overestimates each by one to two points, as much as the gains it is meant to detect. In contrast, training on the prompts whose predicted success is nearest 1/2 improves accuracy at matched compute, by 1.7 points on Qwen3-0.6B and 0.8 on Qwen2.5-1.5B. These gains replicate on fresh seeds and unseen problems, and a simple rule, that the candidate prompts are predicted to fail more often than not, predicted where they appear in five of six preregistered tests. Both gains come early in training, and standard GRPO with twice the initial learning rate comes within half a point of them. We argue that training interventions should be validated at matched compute, which overturns some apparent gains and confirms others.

</details>


### [127] [PolyRoute: A Twelve-Language and Code-Mixed Benchmark for Typed-Decision Routing, with Baselines and a Leaderboard](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13843)
*Mohammed Nayeem Khan,Sarun Kumar K S*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该文发布 PolyRoute 多语言路由基准（12 语言、9 种文字系统、含 Hinglish/Spanglish/Arabizi 混合文本），用统一三分支部门路由协议评测 typed-decision 编码器，13 个系统对比显示英语根检查点仅 61.2%、官方多语言检查点 77.3%、最优微调 96.6%，TF-IDF+LR 达 91.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: typed-decision 类编码器可单次前向完成文本问答决策，但其非英语行为缺乏统一标尺衡量；现有评测缺少原生语言、混合语与罗马化文本的固定基准，跨语言能力被高估或不可比。

Method: 构建 PolyRoute：322 条原生语言支持消息（12 语言/9 种文字系统）、156 条 code-mixed 与罗马化消息、967 条可选原生训练行，并定义三分支部门路由协议。标签由 Qwen3.8-27B 生成并做盲二次复核（保留 91% 候选）；用 gemma4-31b 独立校验一致性（测试集 97.2%），以测试与训练文本最大字符 5-gram 相似度 0.33 控制泄漏；对 13 个系统排名，并保留 4 种语言做留出评估。

Result: 13 系统排行榜：英语根检查点 61.2%，官方多语言检查点 77.3%，最优微调 96.6%；TF-IDF+逻辑回归 91.9%（强于多数神经检查点）。4 个留出语言上三个多语言系统分别为 77%/90%/93%。code-mixed 文本上英语根 85% 名义领先多语言检查点 79%（p=0.134，不显著）。该测试划分仅在约 4 个点以上的差距才具备区分力。

Conclusion: 多语言能力在原生语言、混合语与罗马化输入上差异显著，简易 TF-IDF 基线可超越官方多语言检查点，说明现有模型跨语言路由能力被高估；同时基准规模有限、统计功效约 4 点、且全部文本与标签均由 LLM 生成，结论需在人工标注与更大样本上复核。

Abstract: Typed-decision encoders answer a question about a text in one forward pass, but their behaviour outside English is rarely measured on a fixed yardstick. We release PolyRoute: 322 native-language support messages in 12 languages and 9 writing systems, 156 code-mixed or romanised messages (Hinglish, Spanglish, Arabizi), 967 optional native training rows, and a protocol for a three-way department-routing decision. Labels come from Qwen3.8-27B with a blind second pass (91% of candidates kept); an independent model, gemma4-31b, agrees on 97.2% of test items, and the largest character 5-gram similarity between any test text and any training text is 0.33. A leaderboard of 13 systems gives 61.2% for the English-root checkpoint, 77.3% for the shipped multilingual one and 96.6% for the best fine-tune; TF-IDF with logistic regression reaches 91.9%. On four withheld languages the three multilingual systems score 77%, 90% and 93%. On code-mixed text the English root (85%) is nominally ahead of the shipped multilingual checkpoint (79%; p=0.134). The split resolves differences only above about 4 points. All texts and labels are LLM-generated.

</details>


### [128] [An AI Assistant for Automating Anti-Corruption Expertise of Draft Legislation Based on the TRACE Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14244)
*Serhii Zabolotnii,Alina Tarasova*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出基于TRACE架构的反腐败专家审查AI助手，用于筛查法案草案中的腐败风险条款，并构建基于乌克兰NAZK结论的公开基准评测；系统在严格指标下优于单体提示基线与盲基线的表现有限，且存在定性错误为主的漏检。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反腐败专家对立法草案的人工审查成本高、效率低，需要自动化工具在法案层面定位应被专家指出的条款并识别其对应的监管缺陷类型。

Method: 流水线由确定性解析核心、模型引导的单条款定性、基于规则的因子赋值与外部人工监督组成。评测构建开放基准：132部草案、474条模型抽取的发现。判定采用"条款兼容+LLM裁判风险一致性"（严格/宽松两档），并与含完整方法论的单体提示及冻结盲基线对比，另设21部留出法案。

Result: 100部草案上，前10个标记的匹配率为0.28（本地Gemma 4 31B）与0.30（Sonnet 5.5），全标记下为0.43–0.46（非盲）；冻结盲基线为0.21（95% CI 0.16–0.26）。漏检多源于条款已定位但风险定性不同；32条法律竞合类发现全部未命中。21部留出法案上，严格指标优于单体提示，宽松指标不优；Opus 5.5相对Sonnet无增益。

Conclusion: 该助手在严格指标下具备一定召回优势，但整体匹配率偏低、定性错误为主要瓶颈。评测结论依赖模型抽取的发现与LLM裁判（两条访问路径在74个标签中一致73个），缺乏人类专家验证，结论稳健性仍受限。

Abstract: We present an AI assistant for automating anti-corruption expertise of draft legislation based on the TRACE architecture, designed to screen bills for corruption risks: pinpoint provisions an expert would criticise and name the regulatory defect in each. The pipeline combines a deterministic parsing core, model-guided single-provision qualification, rule-based factor assignment, and external human oversight. To evaluate the assistant, we constructed an open benchmark from the published conclusions of Ukraine's National Agency on Corruption Prevention (NAZK), comprising 132 draft laws with 474 model-extracted findings. A system flag agrees with a finding when it targets a compatible provision and an LLM judge rates the risk as the same (strict) or partly the same (lenient). On 100 draft laws, the pipeline matches 0.28 of the findings within its top ten flags using the local Gemma 4 31B model, 0.30 with Sonnet 5.5, and 0.43-0.46 across all flags (non-blind; the frozen blind baseline achieves 0.21, 95% CI 0.16-0.26). Across all runs, most misses occur on provisions that the system located but qualified as a different risk, and none of the 32 legal-collision findings was matched. On 21 acts held out from tuning, the pipeline outperforms a monolithic prompt containing the full methodology under the strict measure, though not under the lenient one, and Opus 5.5 adds nothing over Sonnet. The reported scores reflect agreement with model-extracted findings rated by an LLM judge whose two access routes agree on 73 of 74 labels, without human expert validation.

</details>


### [129] [Representational Atoms in Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14168)
*Chenhui Hu,Pengfei Cao,Kang Liu,Jun Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文形式化定义了神经网络中的"基本表示单元"(FRUs)及保真度、稳定性两项评价标准，证明阈值激活稀疏自编码器(TSAE)在适当条件下可高保真、高稳定地识别FRUs，并通过大规模实验验证；稳定性更高的FRUs单义性更强，且可跨层组合恢复与人类概念结构对应的语义组织。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解神经网络内部表征的组织方式，以及刻画其与人类概念结构的关系，是阐明模型机制的两个关键问题；但目前缺乏对"表示单元"的统一定义、评价与识别框架。

Method: 形式化定义基本表示单元(FRUs)，提出保真度与稳定性两项评价标准；理论证明阈值激活稀疏自编码器(TSAE)在适当条件下能高保真、高稳定地识别FRUs；开展大规模实验验证，并分析FRU稳定性与单义性、跨层组合性的关系。

Result: TSAE被证明并经实验验证可高保真、高稳定地识别FRUs；稳定性越高的FRUs单义性越强；FRUs跨层组合可恢复与人类概念结构对应的语义组织。

Conclusion: 论文为神经网络中FRUs的定义、评价与识别提供了统一框架，并为刻画神经网络表征与人类概念结构之间的关系奠定了基础。

Abstract: Understanding the internal representations of neural networks and characterizing their relationship with human conceptual structure are two critical questions for elucidating model mechanisms. The former reveals how learned knowledge is organized in internal representations, whereas the latter provides a basis for interpreting these representations through human conceptual structure. Here, we formally define fundamental representational units (FRUs) and establish two evaluation criteria: fidelity and stability. We further prove that threshold-activated sparse autoencoders (TSAEs) can identify FRUs with high fidelity and stability under appropriate conditions, and validate this result through large-scale experiments. Further analyses reveal that FRUs with higher stability exhibit greater monosemanticity and can be composed across layers to recover semantic organization corresponding to human conceptual structure. These results provide a unified framework for defining, evaluating and identifying FRUs in neural networks, and establish a foundation for characterizing the relationship between neural network representations and human conceptual structure.

</details>


### [130] [A Multi-Word Tokenizer for 204 Languages That Needs Fewer Tokens Than GPT-4o's, Including on English](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13059)
*Johin Johny,Ankur Ghosh,Shervin Sam*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该文训练覆盖约200种语言的分词器Havon v3/v4/v5，通过字节级BPE、保留组合附标的前置分词及多词训练阶段，在FLORES-200上使印度25种语言及多数语言的token数低于GPT-4o的o200k_base，且仅评估压缩率未训练模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 印度诸语言在GPT-4o分词器上同内容需1.6–14倍于英语的token数，导致服务成本更高、速度更慢，故需为多语言（尤其印度语言）从头训练更高效的分词器。

Method: 基于约200种语言的真人书写文本从头训练：v3为262,144词表的字节级BPE，前置分词保留字母与组合附标并结合无空格书写系统的连写块；v4在v3基础上加入SuperBPE式第二多词训练阶段，并在200,019词表规模下对比；v5将v4的纯Python代码样本替换为15种语言、宽松许可代码样本。

Result: FLORES-200 devtest上：v3在印度25种语言全部优于o200k_base（中位比0.74）、204种语言中172种更优，但英语多7%；200,019词表下v4英语少6.1%、161/204语言及印度25语言全部更优（中位0.74），同规模v3英语多9.4%、仅133种更优；262,144词表v4英语少9.6%、182/204更优；524,288词表203/204更优。增益集中于英语、印地语与罗曼语，高棉、缅甸、藏文等无空格文字增益小且丢失子词合并。v5在代码上为GPT-4o的1.03–1.22倍（v4为1.11–1.29），印度语言保持中位0.74（句级bootstrap均显著），英语0.94。

Conclusion: 更精细的字节级BPE前置分词与多词训练阶段可在多数语言上降低token数、缩小与英语的成本差距，代码覆盖多样化亦能改善代码压缩；但结果仅为压缩率评估，尚未用v4或v5训练下游模型，实际效果未知。

Abstract: Languages of India need 1.6 to 14 times as many tokens as English on GPT-4o's tokenizer for the same content, which makes them more expensive and slower to serve. We train tokenizers from scratch on human-written text in about 200 languages. Havon v3 is a byte-level BPE tokenizer with 262,144 tokens and a pre-tokenizer that keeps combining marks with their letters and chunks scripts written without spaces. On FLORES-200 devtest it needs fewer tokens than GPT-4o's o200k_base on all 25 languages of India (median 0.74 of GPT-4o's count) and on 172 of 204 languages, but 7% more on English. Havon v4 adds a second, multi-word training stage (SuperBPE) to v3. At exactly GPT-4o's vocabulary size of 200,019 tokens, v4 needs 6.1% fewer tokens than GPT-4o on English and fewer on 161 of 204 languages and all 25 languages of India (median 0.74); at the same size, v3 needs 9.4% more on English and wins on 133. With 262,144 tokens, v4 needs 9.6% fewer tokens on English and fewer on 182 of 204 languages, and with 524,288 tokens it needs fewer on 203 of 204. The gains are largest for English, Hindi and the Romance languages; scripts without spaces, such as Khmer, Burmese and Tibetan, gain little and lose subword merges. Havon v5 replaces v4's Python-only code sample with permissively licensed code in 15 languages: on code it needs 1.03 to 1.22 times GPT-4o's tokens, against 1.11 to 1.29 for v4, while keeping all 25 languages of India (median 0.74, all significant under a sentence-level bootstrap) and English (0.94). These are compression results only: we have not yet evaluated a model trained with v4 or v5.

</details>


### [131] [Dyship: Leveraging Acoustic Features For Speaker Relationship Classification From Dyadic Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14241)
*Kieran Sturges,Thomas Thebaud,Yaohan Guan,Najim Dehak*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出DyShip框架，基于Seamless Interaction语料库的对话语音，比较五种语音表征用于说话人熟悉度与亲属关系分类，谱特征在熟悉度任务最优（87.81%），WavLM在亲属关系任务最优（85.17%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从二人对话语音中推断说话人之间的社会关系（熟悉度、亲属关系）具有应用价值，但不同语音表征在此类关系推断任务上的相对优劣尚不明确，且已有方法（人类判断、音频语言模型）表现有限，需要系统比较各类表征。

Method: 构建DyShip框架，在Seamless Interaction语料库的对话数据上进行两类分类任务；对比五种表征：频谱图、MFCC、自监督WavLM表征、x-vector以及估计的发音运动特征，并训练分类器评估性能。

Result: 熟悉度分类中谱特征最佳，准确率87.81%；亲属关系分类中WavLM最佳，准确率85.17%；x-vector与发音特征在熟悉度任务较弱，但在亲属关系任务表现突出，x-vector的AUC达0.909；多数分类器超过该数据集上已报道的人类与音频语言模型成绩。

Conclusion: 传统声学特征在关系推断中具有显著潜力，不同表征捕捉的对话线索存在差异，值得进一步研究各表征所编码的会话信息。

Abstract: We introduce DyShip, a framework for classifying speaker familiarity and kinship from dyadic conversations in the Seamless Interaction corpus. We compare five speech representations: spectrograms, MFCCs, self-supervised WavLM representations, x-vectors, and estimated articulatory movements. Spectral features perform best for familiarity classification, reaching an accuracy of 87.81%, while WavLM achieves the highest kinship accuracy of 85.17%. X-vectors and articulatory features yield weaker results on familiarity classifiaction, but shine for kinship classification, with an AUC of 0.909 for x-vectors. Most classifiers exceed previously reported human and audio language model scores on the same dataset. These findings highlight the potential of conventional acoustic features for relationship inference and motivate further investigation of the conversational cues captured by different representations.

</details>


### [132] [Deletion Sensitivity Is a Property of the Model: A Three-Condition Measurement across Seven Open Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13223)
*Hanzhang Jia,Liheng Zeng,Zhiyu Yang,Yi Gao,Bo Ma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文首次对多轮工具调用中"完整推理历史删除"进行直接、盲测、多种子的测量：跨7个开放权重推理模型、3个种子、794个任务、3种渲染条件，测得同一删除操作的影响范围为-4.4至+51.3分，两端均排除零；对同一模型将删除率从0调至100%，读数变化不超过1.5分。由此提出"删除敏感度"（deletion sensitivity）是模型级属性，随模型本身恒定、跨部署不变，并应以先验信息进入路由与聚合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理模型在每轮多轮工具会话中都会写入推理轨迹，后续轮次会读取该历史（ShareGPT 94k对话中73%的用户交互跨多轮）。上游供应商将轨迹绑定到签发方，导致跨签发方转发的请求历史被删除，而这一剥离带来的任务代价此前未被量化，通常作为不可见的默认成本存在。

Method: 采用直接、盲测、多随机种子的受控实验：设定三种渲染条件，覆盖7个开放权重推理模型、3个种子、794个任务，并使用官方检查器评分；通过对比不同渲染条件（含全量保留与完全删除）量化同一删除操作的分数差；对单一模型做删除率0%→100%的剂量—反应实验以检验读数稳定性；并提出"两格筛选"（一次保留运行+一次删除运行，成本为两次运行）用于重新读取重训或重新发布模型的敏感度。

Result: 同一删除操作的得分差异跨度为-4.4至+51.3分，且区间两端均显著排除零，说明既存在受益也存在受损情形。对单一模型进行0%至100%删除率的剂量实验，读数最大变化仅1.5分，表明该测量对删除比例不敏感、而由模型特性决定。删除敏感度在跨部署时保持恒定。

Conclusion: 删除敏感度是可测量的模型级属性，而非随机噪声或部署配置效应；重训或重新发布的模型应被视为新模型，需用两次运行的两格筛选重新读取。建议将删除敏感度作为先验信息纳入请求路由与结果聚合，而不是让它继续作为不可见的默认成本存在。

Abstract: Reasoning models write a reasoning trace at every turn of a multi-turn tool session, and later turns read this history. Across 94k ShareGPT conversations, 73 percent of user interactions span multiple turns. Upstream providers bind each trace to its issuer, so a request forwarded across issuers arrives with its history deleted, and the task cost of this stripping remains unmeasured. To the best of our knowledge, this paper presents the first direct, blind, multi-seed measurement of full reasoning-history deletion in multi-turn tool calling, through three rendering conditions, seven open-weight reasoning models, three seeds, 794 tasks, and the official checker. The same deletion spans -4.4 to +51.3 points with both ends excluding zero, and for the one model re-dosed from 0 to 100 percent deletion, the reading varies by at most 1.5 points. Deletion sensitivity is a model-level property. The reading follows the model itself and stays constant across deployments, and a retrained or re-released model is a new model that a two-cell screen, one retention run and one deletion run, re-reads for the cost of two runs. Deletion sensitivity is thus a measurable property of the model, and the reading must enter routing and aggregation as prior information rather than persist as an invisible default cost.

</details>


### [133] [MISO-BR: An Open Corpus for Misogyny Detection in Brazilian Portuguese](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14036)
*Fábio A. S. Passos,Péricles B. C. Miranda,Ana Paula Cavalcanti*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MISO-BR 是一个开放、可审计的巴西葡萄牙语厌女语料（896 条平衡样本，kappa 0.788），提供可复现基线：经典模型 macro-F1 约 0.65–0.68，微调 BERTimbau 达 0.833，但 Base 与 Large（成本差 2.6 倍）无差异；作者强调可审计性并量化了平衡偏差、来源-标签关联与采样偏差三项局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 巴西葡萄牙语中针对女性用户的网络厌女言论普遍存在，但开放、可审计的专用资源稀缺，限制了该方向的复现研究与公平比较。

Method: 构建 MISO-BR 语料：896 条类别平衡的社交媒体文本，采用词典与目标导向的 X 抓取、从 OLID-BR 导入条目、大模型初筛（公开提示词与解码参数）以及三名标注者依据书面指南的人工标注。在统一协议下比较四种经典分类器（多项式/伯努利朴素贝叶斯、线性 SVM、随机森林）与微调的 BERTimbau Base/Large。

Result: 816 条上标注者间一致性 Fleiss' kappa 为 0.788。经典模型 macro-F1 介于 0.651–0.676，偏精确率而召回率低；Transformer 达 0.833 macro-F1 与准确率（Wilson 95% CI [0.77, 0.88]），领先约 16 个点；Base 与 Large 在测试集上无显著差异，尽管训练成本相差 2.6 倍。

Conclusion: 资源的核心价值在于流程的可审计性而非榜首分数；作者明确指出三项使用限制：人工类别平衡对真实分布下精确率的影响（已量化）、来源与标签之间的关联（可通过公开的 source 字段度量）、以及词典导向采样与自动初筛带来的选择偏差。语料、标注指南、模型与实验脚本均公开。

Abstract: Misogyny is among the most prevalent forms of online hate speech directed at Brazilian users, yet open and auditable resources dedicated to it in Brazilian Portuguese remain scarce. We introduce MISO-BR, an open, documented and versioned corpus of 896 balanced social media texts annotated for binary misogyny, released with its construction protocol and reproducible baselines. The corpus combines lexicon- and target-guided scraping from X with items imported from OLID-BR, a large language model triage stage whose prompt and decoding parameters are released, and manual annotation by three annotators following a written guide. Inter-annotator reliability reaches a Fleiss' kappa of 0.788 over 816 items. Under a single fixed protocol we compare four classical classifiers (Multinomial and Bernoulli Naive Bayes, a linear SVM and a random forest) with fine-tuned BERTimbau Base and Large. The classical models cluster between 0.651 and 0.676 macro-F1 and are precision-oriented but recall-poor. The transformers reach 0.833 macro-F1 and accuracy (Wilson 95% CI for accuracy [0.77, 0.88]), a gap of roughly 16 points, while Base and Large are indistinguishable on the test set despite a 2.6x difference in training cost. We argue that the value of the resource lies less in its headline scores than in the auditability of its pipeline, and we state three limits that users must reckon with: the artificial class balance, whose effect on precision under realistic prevalence we quantify; the association between provenance and label, which the released source field makes measurable; and the selection bias induced by lexicon-guided sampling and automatic triage. The corpus, annotation guide, models and experimental scripts are publicly available.

</details>


### [134] [Inherited Weights: Measuring the Tokenization Premium and the Sovereignty Cost of Language Models for the Global South](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13441)
*Paulo Camara*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 微调模型的决定性属性（词表、知识、文化对齐、记忆数据、许可证）继承自基座；论文给出31语言×7分词器的分词溢价可复现测量（含未知token坍塌校正）、8项黑盒"主权审计"、以及以"每焦耳准确率/校准/选择性风险"替代原始准确率的设计范式论证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 美国与中国之外部署的语言模型多为少数预训练家族的微调衍生品，采购方难以判断其实际继承属性；同时非英语/低资源语言在全局分词器下承受显著分词溢价，且能源与责任约束下的评估目标不当。

Method: (i) 在《世界人权宣言》27条文本上，对2023–2024年发布的7个分词器在31种语言上测量分词溢价，并显式校正未知token坍塌；(ii) 构建无需权重访问的8项黑盒"主权审计"；(iii) 以能源与责任约束为出发点进行范式论证，提出不可被弃答机制虚增的指标；(iv) 报告自研专有架构的初步内部测量及其局限。数据与测量代码公开。

Result: 阿姆哈拉语在旧分词器上溢价达10倍，最新商用分词器仍为6.8倍；南美原住民语言在全局分词器下溢价1.6–2.7倍；以葡萄牙语构建的词表相对所有被测全局分词器，将13种拉丁字母全球南方语言中11种的分词溢价降低，瓜拉尼语与克丘亚语约降24%。

Conclusion: 微调无法摆脱基座带来的词表、知识与许可证约束，语言覆盖不平等可通过本地语料词表部分缓解；买方可借黑盒审计评估模型主权；在能耗与责任约束下，评估应从原始准确率转向每焦耳准确率、校准与选择性风险等弃答无法刷高的指标。

Abstract: Most language models deployed outside the United States and China are fine-tuned descendants of a small number of pretrained families. We argue that fine-tuning leaves the decisive properties of a model inherited from its base: vocabulary, knowledge, cultural alignment, memorized data and license. We contribute (i) an updated, reproducible measurement of the tokenization premium across 31 languages and 7 tokenizers released in 2023-2024, on 27 articles of the Universal Declaration of Human Rights, with explicit correction for unknown-token collapse. Premiums reach 10x for Amharic in older tokenizers and remain at 6.8x in the newest commercial one; Indigenous South American languages pay between 1.6x and 2.7x under global tokenizers. A vocabulary built from Portuguese lowers the premium for 11 of 13 Latin-script Global South languages relative to every global tokenizer tested, by about 24% for Guarani and Quechua. (ii) An eight-test black-box sovereignty audit that a buyer can run without access to weights. (iii) A regime argument: under energy and liability constraints, accuracy per joule, calibration and selective risk should replace raw accuracy as design objectives, with metrics that abstention cannot inflate. We close with preliminary internal measurements from a proprietary architecture built around these objectives, reported with their limitations. Data and measurement code are included.

</details>


### [135] [SELD: Detecting Silent Evidence Loss in Context Compression beyond the Answer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12903)
*Wentao Lei,Li Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出免训练、免标注的"静默证据丢失检测器"SELD，通过比较压缩前后阅读模型对未输出答案的偏好分布来检测"答案不变但证据被删"的隐性问题，并构建SEL-Pairs基准，在五个阅读模型上以83%识别率超越最强基线（70%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 上下文压缩已成长输入语言模型的常规手段，但压缩后的上下文只有在保留支撑答案的证据时才有用。现有校验只比较阅读模型的答案或其最可能词，因此漏掉一类高频失败——静默证据丢失：关键证据被删除而答案不变，导致答案缺乏支撑，对其他复用该上下文的模型构成风险。

Method: 提出SELD，无需训练与标签。核心原理：证据丢失时，阅读模型在其"未说出的答案"上的偏好排序会发生偏移，即使最终答案不变；因此比较压缩前后这些偏好分布即可揭示答案校验看不见的丢失。同时构建SEL-Pairs基准，由三个问答数据集生成长度匹配的"证据删除"与"对照删除"配对样本。

Result: 在SEL-Pairs上对五个阅读模型做定量与定性实验：SELD在多种压缩器、压缩率与模型下均稳定优于强基线及近期基线；在答案保持不变的长度匹配配对中，识别出"删证据"编辑的比例达83%，最强基线为70%。

Conclusion: 即使答案未变，压缩上下文中的证据丢失仍可被检测；以答案不变性为唯一正确性判据并不充分，需要引入基于模型偏好分布的证据保真度校验。

Abstract: Context compression is now routine for language models with long inputs, but a compressed context is useful only if it keeps the evidence for the answer. Existing checks compare the reader model's answer or its most likely words, and thus miss a frequent failure, silent evidence loss: key evidence is removed while the answer stays the same, leaving the answer unsupported and risky for other models that reuse the context. To address this challenge, we propose SELD (Silent Evidence Loss Detector), which needs no training and no labels. Its principle is that when evidence is lost, a reader model's preferences among the answers it leaves unsaid shift even if its answer does not, so comparing these preferences before and after compression reveals losses that answer checks cannot see. We also build SEL-Pairs, a benchmark of length-matched evidence and control deletions from three question-answering datasets.
