Quantitative and qualitative experiments with five reader models on it show that SELD consistently outperforms strong and recent baselines across compressors, compression rates and models, identifying the evidence-removing edit in 83% of matched pairs with unchanged answers, against 70% for the strongest baseline. These results show that evidence loss in compressed contexts can be detected even when the answer is unchanged.

</details>


### [136] [Rethinking in Spikes: Mitigating Hallucinations in MDLMs with Step-Aware Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12901)
*Zhongxing Xu,Zhonghua Wang,Zhe Qian,Shiyan Su,Ming Hu,Xiaocheng Zou,Wei Feng,Mingquan Lin,Yuyin Zhou,Yifan Peng,Hamid Rezatofighi,Zongyuan Ge*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出免训练即插即用解码策略StepRefine：利用去噪步间的熵偏差定位幻觉相关异常步，在熵尖峰处切换为概率加权嵌入的潜在精炼，并由精炼控制器按分布稳定性切回离散去噪，从而缓解多模态扩散语言模型的幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 观察到MDLM迭代去噪中掩码不确定性整体下降，但少数步骤出现局部熵尖峰且与幻觉强相关；作者认为离散token提交丢弃了掩码位置的分布信息，使候选语义与上下文在熵尖峰时无法持续交互，导致幻觉。

Method: 以相邻时间窗口的熵偏差度量识别异常步；核心是步感知的“离散去噪↔潜在精炼”切换：在熵尖峰步使用概率加权嵌入保留多样推理假设并进行多轮连续精炼；精炼控制器监测分布稳定性，抑制冗余循环并切回标准离散去噪。

Result: 在多个多模态基准上、对不同MDLM进行大量实验，StepRefine均显著缓解幻觉。

Conclusion: 熵尖峰可作为幻觉的可解释诊断信号，而将离散提交替换为条件化的潜在精炼可有效保留分布信息；StepRefine作为即插即用策略，无需重训即可提升MDLM解码可靠性。

Abstract: Recent advancements in multimodal diffusion language models (MDLMs) have exhibited strong global modeling capabilities through iterative refinement. We observe that mask uncertainty follows an overall downward trend during iterative denoising, while a few denoising steps exhibit localized entropy spikes closely associated with hallucinations. We argue that anomalous steps can be identified by measuring the entropy deviation of each denoising step from its neighboring temporal window. We hypothesize that discrete token commitment discards the distributional information at masked positions, limiting the continued interaction between candidate semantics and the context during entropy spikes. With this goal, we present StepRefine, a plug-and-play decoding strategy that leverages semantic context to achieve reliable denoising. The core of our method lies in a step-aware switch between discrete denoising and latent refinement. The model employs probability-weighted embeddings at entropy spike steps to preserve diverse reasoning hypotheses and performs multiple rounds of continuous refinement. Meanwhile, a refinement controller monitors distributional stability to reduce unnecessary loops and switch the model back to standard discrete denoising. Through extensive experiments, StepRefine demonstrates significant hallucination mitigation across different MDLMs on multimodal benchmarks.

</details>


### [137] [Synapse-Control: Making Agent Experience Actionable with Lightweight Runtime Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13615)
*Zhiyuan Huang,Shengan Zheng,Chenghu Yang,Song Zhang,Lingyun Li,Chen Yu,Weiquan Huang,Linpeng Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Synapse-Control 是一种智能体运行时，用轻量控制平面（规则筛选 + 1.7B 分类器打分 + 微契约 + Prompt Value Table）替代 Sentry 的生成式失败诊断，在降低推理成本的同时提升任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有系统（如 Sentry）在复用执行经验前需调用大模型生成失败诊断，再检索建议，带来额外推理开销；需要一种低成本的经验复用控制机制。

Method: 控制平面由四部分组成：(1) 规则对相关建议做初筛；(2) 基于教师标注的"状态-建议"对微调的 1.7B 轻量分类器对候选建议打分，而非生成诊断；(3) 微契约（micro-contracts）编码可复用的接口检查（如必填参数、合法选项），不规定完整任务程序；(4) Prompt Value Table 记录历史指导与反馈，通过冷却期与 token 预算检查抑制重复建议。

Result: 在 AppWorld 与 WebShop 上每种方法各 1,308 次尝试中，Synapse-Control 取得 270 次成功（比 Base 高 29.8%），替代了 4,987 次生成式诊断调用，辅助控制时间相对 Sentry 分别减少 50.2% 和 51.0%。

Conclusion: 用轻量判别式控制替代生成式诊断，可在降低成本的同时改善工具型任务的经验复用效果；但成功率为 20.6%（270/1308），绝对性能仍有提升空间。

Abstract: Large language model agents reuse execution experience to improve reliability on complex tool-based tasks. Systems such as Sentry call large models to generate failure diagnoses before retrieving advice, adding inference cost to experience reuse. We present Synapse-Control, an agent runtime whose lightweight control plane selects advice and determines when to send it to the task-executing model. Rules shortlist relevant advice, and a lightweight 1.7B classifier fine-tuned on teacher-labeled state-suggestion pairs scores those candidates instead of generating a diagnosis. Micro-contracts encode reusable interface checks, such as required arguments or valid options, without prescribing complete task programs. A Prompt Value Table records earlier guidance and feedback to limit repeated suggestions through cooldown and token-budget checks. Across 1,308 attempts per method in AppWorld and WebShop, Synapse-Control achieves 270 successes (29.8% more than Base), replaces 4,987 generative diagnosis calls, and reduces auxiliary control time relative to Sentry by 50.2% and 51.0%, respectively.

</details>


### [138] [How Post-Training Influences Verbalized Algorithmic Computation in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13552)
*Niall Dalton*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该文研究后训练（SFT、DPO、RLVR）如何改变语言模型内部信息利用与显式思维链（CoT）的作用：探针可同等精度读取进位状态，但操控内部状态难以控制输出；RLVR生成的CoT对下游更具可迁移性且对进位相关表述更敏感，但敏感性不等于更好的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后训练能提升语言模型推理能力，但其如何改变模型对内部信息的利用以及显式思维链（CoT）的效用仍不清楚，缺乏对训练阶段间差异的机制性刻画。

Method: 以Olmo-3-7B-Instruct在多位数加法任务上的连续SFT、DPO、RLVR检查点为对象，将进位值作为已知中间状态；采用线性探针从内部激活中恢复进位信息，并进行激活操控实验考察因果性；进一步将各阶段生成的CoT跨检查点迁移，比较其对接收模型的准确率贡献，同时用重要性评分比较进位相关语句与随机对照语句的敏感度。

Result: SFT与RLVR检查点的探针恢复进位信息精度相近，但操控该信息不能可靠控制模型输出；RLVR生成的CoT在不同接收检查点上带来的准确率高于SFT或DPO生成的CoT；RLVR后，进位相关语句获得高于匹配随机对照的重要性评分；DPO对进位相关语句表现出类似敏感度，但未带来可比的准确率提升。

Conclusion: 后训练显著改变了书面推理的效用以及对推理内容的自敏感性，但更高的敏感性并不必然对应更好的问题表现，说明内部状态可解码性、因果可控性与显式推理效用之间存在解耦。

Abstract: Post-training improves language-model reasoning, but how it changes the use of internal information and verbalized chain of thought (CoT) remains unclear. We study successive SFT, DPO, and RLVR checkpoints of Olmo-3-7B-Instruct on multi-digit addition, using carry values as known intermediate state. Probes recover carry information from internal activations with similar accuracy in SFT and RLVR, although manipulating this information does not reliably control model outputs. In contrast, the usefulness of verbalized reasoning differs: CoTs produced by RLVR yield higher accuracy across receiving checkpoints than those produced by SFT or DPO. We also show that carry-related statements receive higher importance scores after RLVR than matched random controls. DPO shows similar sensitivity to carry-related statements but without comparable accuracy gains. These results suggest that post-training changes the usefulness of written reasoning and sensitivity to its content in meaningful ways, although greater sensitivity does not necessarily coincide with better problem performance.

</details>


### [139] [From Bias to Positionality: A critical grammar for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14079)
*Dave Murray-Rust,Elisa Giaccardi,Federico Bomba,Maria Luce Lupetti*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文主张"偏见"框架在通用语言模型场景中失效，应改用"位置性"（positionality）描述模型"从何处言说"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: "Bias"已成为理解机器学习系统的默认透镜，但其本质是相对某标准的偏离，需外部提供标准（法律、受保护属性、基准、统计性质）。在决策系统中尚可默认存在这一标准，但常被简写为"biased"而不言明"相对于什么"。在通用语言模型中，无审计决策、无反事实输出，标准无从提供，这一简写彻底失效，因而需要一个更适合的新语法。

Method: 概念分析，并提出"层流式位置性"（laminar positionality）框架：位置性是言说所处的被定位、被明述、被归属且可争议的位置。模型虽非可问责言说者，但当其被"当作在言说"时，就从某处言说。位置性分层叠积于语料、微调、系统提示、应用与实时对话之上；并区分三组因素——创造者的位置性、其意图行为、模型实际呈现的行为，构成矩阵以定位现有研究，分析各层继承关系及否认继承时的断裂与塌陷。

Result: 构建了"创造者位置性×意图行为×实际行为"的分析矩阵，识别出各层从下层继承的内容，以及当继承被否认时出现的"ruptures and collapses"（断裂与塌陷），并据此重新界定模型言说的来源。

Conclusion: 反对厂商"来自无处"及"来自处处"的诱惑性回答；位置性可作为描述模型及其被过度信任的语法，并为模型的制作、解读与问责提供启示。

Abstract: Bias has become a default lens for understanding what machine learning systems do and how we should relate to them. Bias is at root a deviation from a standard; in decision making systems we can supply a standard from outside: a law, a protected attribute, a benchmark, a statistical property. However, even there, biased is often used as a shorthand, without stating what the position taken is - a silent ``relative to''. In a general use of language models, nothing supplies the standard: there is no decision to audit and no counterfactual output, so the shorthand fails. We argue that positionality -- the located, articulated, owned and contestable position from which anything is said -- is the better grammar for the question these models now force: who is speaking, from where? Against the vendors' answer of nowhere and the seductive possibility of everywhere, we argue that when a model is taken as-if speaking, it speaks from somewhere, and this means positionality can describe models, and the way they are believed beyond their warrant, even though they are not accountable speakers. A model's positionality is laminar -- laid down across corpus, tuning, system prompt, application and the live conversation. We separate three sets of factors: the positionality of a model's creators, the behaviour they intend for it, and the behaviour it enacts, and situate current work within this matrix. This lets us highlight what each stratum inherits from the one below, with ruptures and collapses where that inheritance is disavowed. We then return to where models speak from, and discuss the implications for how models are made, read and held accountable.

</details>


### [140] [Actionable Feedback: Improving Model Capabilities from User Edit Data on Clinical Documentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12904)
*Daniel P. Jeong,Yewon Byun,John Giorgi,Peng Xu,Zachary C. Lipton,Michael Oberst*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文指出临床医生对LLM临床文档输出的编辑中有超过45%包含输入不支持的新增信息，直接用其微调会诱导幻觉；作者构建编辑行为分类体系、生成半合成编辑数据并提出简单过滤方法筛选"可操作编辑"，实验证明渐进式过滤既能提升临床对齐指标又能保持对输入上下文的忠实性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM已广泛用于临床文档工作流，医生的后续编辑本可成为可扩展的监督信号；但实际部署数据显示超过45%的编辑输出含输入未支持的用户新增信息，朴素微调会教模型产生幻觉，因此需要区分哪些编辑是可操作的反馈。

Method: （1）基于已部署的AI临床文档产品数据，建立临床医生编辑行为的分类体系；（2）依据该分类体系，在MIMIC-III上的出院小结生成任务中构造半合成医生编辑数据；（3）提出简单的过滤方法来识别可操作（grounded）的编辑，并用专家评审评估其准确性。

Result: 在半合成数据集上的实验表明：对原始编辑输出进行朴素微调会增加无依据（ungrounded）内容；而逐步过滤、趋向grounded编辑的做法可在提升临床对齐指标的同时保持对输入上下文的忠实性。

Conclusion: 临床编辑并非全是可用监督信号，需先过滤掉引入无依据信息的部分；简单的过滤策略即可在利用编辑信号提升对齐性的同时抑制幻觉，为部署场景下的编辑监督提供可行路径。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly being deployed in clinical documentation workflows, where clinicians review and edit their outputs before use. In principle, these clinician edits represent a scalable source of supervision for model training. In practice, not all edits represent actionable feedback: for instance, on data drawn from real-world deployment of an AI clinical documentation product, our review suggests that over 45% of edited outputs contain user-added information that is not directly supported by the inputs to the model. Naively fine-tuning on such edits risks teaching the model to hallucinate. In this paper, we develop a method to address this challenge. Given that real clinician edits on deployed documentation tools are not publicly available, we first develop a taxonomy of clinician-editing behaviors, informed by data from a deployed AI clinical documentation product. Then, we develop a method for generating semi-synthetic clinician-edit data, informed by our taxonomy, on a discharge-summary generation task based on MIMIC-III, a public dataset. Finally, we introduce simple filtering methods for identifying actionable edits, with accuracy evaluated by expert review. We show empirically, on our semi-synthetic dataset, that naive fine-tuning on raw edited output increases ungrounded content, while our approach of progressively filtering toward grounded edits enables improvement on clinician-alignment metrics while retaining faithfulness to the input context.

</details>


### [141] [TimeOmni-o1: Bidirectionally Coupling Time Series Foundation Models and LLMs for Multimodal Reasoning and Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12902)
*Ziyang Zhang,Shenyi Li,Wen Wu,Chao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 TimeOmni-o1，将预训练时间序列基础模型(TSFM)与大语言模型(LLM)双向耦合，使单一架构既能读写时间序列与文本、以文本进行推理，又能利用 TSFM 的预训练预测能力；并构建 500k+ 样本的 MMTR 多模态推理数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实时间序列常伴随文本(如事件报告)，理解(问答)与预测两类任务都需要对序列与文本联合推理，且输出模态不同(文本 vs 序列)，因此需要模型同时读写两种模态。LLM 以数字 token 读取序列、长序列表现差；TSFM 预测强但不读写文本；现有时间序列语言模型把预测放在 LLM 侧，浪费了 TSFM 的预训练预测能力。

Method: 双向耦合 TSFM 与 LLM：方向一，TSFM 将序列压缩编码供 LLM 与文本联合推理；方向二，LLM 的推理结果回馈给 TSFM，作为条件生成预测。构建 MMTR 数据集：含标注推理轨迹的多模态时间序列推理数据，覆盖 12 个领域、两类任务、50 万+ 样本。

Result: 在 MMTR 上，TimeOmni-o1 在多数领域的理解与预测任务上优于现有模型，也优于单独使用的内部 LLM 与 TSFM；增益可迁移到三个公开基准，并超过专用时间序列模型。

Conclusion: 双向耦合 TSFM 与 LLM 可在同一架构中统一序列/文本的理解与生成，并有效复用 TSFM 的预训练预测能力，配合带推理轨迹的多模态数据可显著提升两类任务表现。

Abstract: Time series from real-world systems are often accompanied by text, such as a report on an event that affects them. Two common tasks on these series are understanding, e.g., answering questions about a series, and forecasting its future values. Both tasks require reasoning over the series and the text together. Moreover, understanding outputs text while forecasting outputs a time series, so serving both requires a model that reads and generates both modalities. Large language models (LLMs) read time series as digit tokens and handle long series poorly. Time series foundation models (TSFMs) forecast well from large-scale pretraining but typically neither read nor generate text. Time series language models add a time series modality to the LLM but forecast on the LLM side, leaving the TSFM's pretrained forecasting ability unused. We present TimeOmni-o1, which couples a pretrained TSFM and an LLM bidirectionally. In one direction, the TSFM encodes the series in compressed form for the LLM, which reasons over it together with the text. In the other, the LLM's reasoning is fed back into the TSFM, which produces the forecast conditioned on it. One model architecture thus reads and generates both series and text, reasons in text, and draws on the TSFM's pretrained forecasting ability. To train it, we build MMTR, a multimodal time series reasoning dataset with annotated reasoning traces, covering both tasks across 12 domains with 500k+ samples. On MMTR, TimeOmni-o1 outperforms existing models on both understanding and forecasting across most domains, as well as the LLM and the TSFM inside it when used individually. The gains carry over to three open benchmarks, where it outperforms the dedicated time series models.

</details>


### [142] [AVCC: Audio-Visual Conversation Corpus for Turn-Taking Prediction with a Human Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13957)
*Haotian Qi,Gabriel Skantze*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 将AVCC（31小时、单摄像头+单麦克风录制的二/三人线上对话音视频语料）转化为公开的多方下一说话人预测（NSP）基准，并首次给出人类参照基线与模型对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测“谁在何时开口”是无需等待静音即可自然轮换的对话智能体与社交机器人的前提。现有多方NSP研究各用自带事件定义、每说话人单独配传感器的语料，缺乏与机器人实际配置（单摄像头+单麦克风）相符的公开基准，也没有模型与人类在同一任务上的性能对比。

Method: 基于AVCC构建基准：新增说话人互不重叠的7段视频测试集，在互静音间隙处标注865个hold事件与865个shift事件；提供全语料词级强制对齐转写；在同一事件上采集人类参照数据；评测纯音频的Role-Relative VAP模型（RoleVAP）与多模态MuVAP模型。

Result: 公开了标签、转写、逐响应人类数据及可复现全部数值的脚本；给出了RoleVAP与MuVAP在该基准上的评分以及同一事件上的人类表现参照。

Conclusion: AVCC成为首个匹配单摄像头单麦克风机器人配置、并提供人类参照基线的公开多方NSP基准，为模型与人类表现的直接比较及可复现研究提供基础。

Abstract: } Predicting who will speak next, and when, is a prerequisite for conversational agents and social robots that take turns without waiting for silence. Multiparty next-speaker prediction (NSP) has been studied on corpora with per-speaker sensors, each with its own event definitions. No public benchmark covers the single camera and microphone a robot has, and no model has been compared against how well people perform the same prediction. We turn AVCC, a 31-hour audio-visual corpus of two- and three-speaker online conversation recorded with a single static camera and microphone, into a public NSP benchmark. We add a speaker-disjoint seven-video test partition with 865 hold and 865 shift events at mutual-silence gaps, word-level forced-aligned transcripts for the whole corpus, and a human reference benchmark collected at the same events. We score an audio-only Role-Relative VAP model (RoleVAP) and the multimodal MuVAP model. Labels, transcripts, per-response human data, and the scripts that reproduce every number are released.

</details>


### [143] [BioMem: Agentic Memory for Biomedical LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13426)
*Mingchen Li,Yongkang Xiao,Rumeng Li,Rui Zhang,Hong Yu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了生物医学领域的智能体记忆系统，并提出免训练的混合记忆检索与利用框架HMRU，在多个基准上平均相对提升8.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在生物医学应用中潜力显著，智能体记忆系统有助于跨任务保存和利用信息，但记忆系统能否有效且可靠地保留、检索、更新和利用生物医学信息仍是未解问题。

Method: 在生物医学知识推理、临床预测和长期对话记忆三类场景下，对5种记忆方法与4个LLM进行系统评测，分析记忆构建、证据相关性与对话历史对下游性能的影响；在此基础上提出免训练框架HMRU，联合增强证据可访问性与任务感知利用。

Result: HMRU在生物医学问答与对话记忆基准上取得平均8.6%的相对提升，部分临床预测任务亦有增益；对话答案错误率降低24.9%，并将与全上下文推理的性能差距缩小48.0%。

Conclusion: 研究系统刻画了生物医学智能体记忆的能力与瓶颈，并为构建更有效、可靠的记忆增强LLM系统提供了实践性见解。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated considerable potential in biomedical applications, while agentic memory systems have emerged as a promising approach to preserving and utilizing information across tasks and interactions. However, a fundamental question remains: how effectively and reliably do these systems retain, retrieve, update, and utilize biomedical information? In this study, we systematically investigate agentic memory in the biomedical domain, focusing on task effectiveness, retrieval quality, information fidelity, and long-term robustness. We evaluate five memory approaches across four LLMs in three representative settings: biomedical knowledge reasoning, clinical prediction, and long-term conversational memory. Our analyses reveal how memory construction, evidence relevance, and conversational history shape downstream biomedical performance, highlighting the distinct challenges of retrieving relevant information, preserving its fidelity, and effectively utilizing it for reasoning. Building on these findings, we introduce Hybrid Memory Retrieval and Utilization (HMRU), a training-free framework that jointly enhances evidence accessibility and task-aware utilization to improve biomedical reasoning and long-term conversational memory. HMRU achieves an average relative improvement of 8.6% across biomedical question-answering and conversational memory benchmarks, with additional gains in selected clinical prediction tasks. It further reduces conversational answer errors by 24.9% and closes 48.0% of the performance gap to full-context inference. Our findings provide a systematic characterization of biomedical agentic memory and practical insights into developing more effective and reliable memory-augmented LLM systems.

</details>


### [144] [Mechanistic Interpretability Needs a Logic of Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14483)
*Zhimin Hu,Chen Jiang,Hanbo Xie,N. Apurva Ratan Murty,Sashank Varma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该文比较神经科学与机制可解释性(MI)在从复杂神经系统中推断机制时的共同推理瓶颈，提出五项原则并组织为递归逻辑，以规范机制推断程序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MI已能识别与模型行为相关的神经元、特征、电路、群体结构与动力学模式，但研究重心正从"能观察到什么"转向"这些观察如何支撑机制性解释"；现有工作缺乏严格的机制推断规范。

Method: 概念性/方法论分析：平行对比神经科学与MI的推断实践，归纳反复出现的推断瓶颈，提出五项原则并组织为递归式机制推断逻辑框架。

Result: 识别出跨领域的共性推断瓶颈，并给出一个支持两类科学发现的框架：将局部可解释性发现累积为模型内机制的累积性知识，以及使机制足够精确以生成关于模型之外领域的可检验假设。

Conclusion: 机制推断需要规范化的递归程序；确立模型机制对现实世界的启示，最终仍需MI之外的证据。

Abstract: Neural networks are increasingly studied not merely as predictive systems, but as complex systems whose internal computations are themselves objects of scientific inquiry. Recent progress in mechanistic interpretability (MI) has made it possible to identify neurons, features, circuits, population structures, and dynamical patterns associated with model behavior. As these tools improve, attention is shifting from what can be observed inside a model to how those observations support mechanistic explanations. We examine the parallel efforts of neuroscience and MI to address this question and identify recurring inferential bottlenecks when inferring mechanisms from complex neural systems. We propose five principles and organize them into a recursive logic for a more rigorous procedure of mechanistic inference. These principles provide a framework that supports scientific discovery in two senses: it helps turn local interpretability findings into cumulative knowledge of mechanisms within models, and it makes those mechanisms sufficiently precise to generate testable hypotheses about domains beyond the models. Evidence beyond MI is ultimately required to establish what model mechanisms reveal about the world.

</details>


### [145] [One Byte, One Token: Byteifying a 27B Hybrid-Attention LLM with Self-Generated Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13607)
*Liwei Zhang,ZhiMing Tong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该文将已训练的 27B subword 模型 Qwen3.8-27B 直接改造为纯字节级模型：把词表从 248,320 压缩到 384（256 字节符号+特殊 id），其余权重不动，用两阶段课程（先编码后阅读）在 4.77 GB 有监督字节上继续训练，验证“一对一字节建模”可保留大部分能力，但代价是吞吐显著下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 字节级 BPE 会把中文切碎（Qwen3.8 在中文维基上平均 3.84 字节/token，64% 的 token 是单字），且使模型看不到拼写信息。作者要检验一个已训练好的大模型能否在不加 patch 结构、不加局部编码器的前提下，直接转换为纯字节级（一字节一 token）模型并保留大部分能力。

Method: 以 Qwen3.8-27B（48 层 Gated DeltaNet + 16 层全注意力）为起点，将词表裁剪至 384 项（BPE 词表中已有的 256 个字节符号 + 特殊 id），其余权重完全冻结不改，仅在 4.77 GB 有监督字节上按两阶段课程继续训练：先学编码、再学阅读。监督数据中对话/行为类由原模型 on-policy 生成（字词典、词典、通用对话、代码会话），知识阅读用百科文本（占监督字节 54%）。训练在 8×H200 上耗时 178.6 h。

Result: 同 GPU 与推理栈下，字节模型在 MATH-500、HumanEval+、MMLU-Pro 与非思考 GSM8K 上与原始模型的差距落在配对 bootstrap 区间内；在 C-Eval、思考模式 GSM8K 与非思考 IFEval 上下降 4-6 分。非思考模式下所有字符级任务领先（CUTE 75.2→84.0，EXECUTE-zho 81.6→93.9，自建中文字符套件 54.4→64.0），但汉字读音、笔画数等查表型知识下降，且思考模式无法恢复。吞吐方面：单步速度相同，但每步输出量少 3-5 倍，中文字符/秒约为原来 1/4，64K 字节 prefill 慢 6.5 倍。另记录了四种字节模型失效模式（贪心失控、采样破坏 UTF-8、小词表上惩罚项失效、重复）及对应修复。

Conclusion: 已训练的大模型可在不改结构、不改主体权重的条件下直接转为纯字节级模型，并保留大部分通用能力，且在字符级任务上反而受益；但查表型汉字知识与思考模式增益不可挽回，算术吞吐代价显著（字符/秒约 1/4、长序列 prefill 慢 6.5 倍），需配合专门的解码约束与惩罚修正。

Abstract: Byte-level BPE fragments Chinese text (for Qwen3.8 on Chinese Wikipedia, 3.84 bytes per token on average; 64% of tokens are single characters) and hides spelling from the model. We ask whether an already-trained 27B subword model can be converted directly into a pure byte-level model (one byte, one token), without a patching structure or a local encoder, while keeping most of its ability. Starting from Qwen3.8-27B (48 Gated DeltaNet layers + 16 full-attention layers), we shrink the vocabulary from 248,320 to 384 entries (the 256 byte symbols already in the BPE vocabulary plus special ids), leave every other weight untouched, and continue training in a two-stage curriculum (encoding first, then reading) on 4.77 GB of supervised bytes: all dialogue/behavioral supervision is generated on-policy by the original model itself (character dictionary, word dictionary, general dialogue, coding sessions), and knowledge reading uses encyclopedia text (54% of supervised bytes). Training takes 178.6 h on 8xH200. On the same GPU and serving stack, the byte model's gap to the original falls within the paired bootstrap interval on MATH-500, HumanEval+, MMLU-Pro and non-thinking GSM8K, and it loses 4-6 points on C-Eval, thinking-mode GSM8K and non-thinking IFEval. Without thinking it leads on every character-level task (CUTE 75.2 -> 84.0, EXECUTE-zho 81.6 -> 93.9, our Chinese character suite 54.4 -> 64.0), but lookup knowledge about Chinese characters such as pronunciation and stroke count drops, and thinking does not recover it. The cost is throughput: per-step speed is identical, but each step yields 3-5x less output; Chinese characters per second is about 1/4 of the original, and prefill at 64K bytes is 6.5x slower. We also document four byte-model failure modes (greedy runaway, sampling breaking UTF-8, ineffective penalty terms on a small vocabulary, repetition) and their fixes.

</details>


### [146] [LLM Validation Makes Minimal-Pair Benchmarks Easier for Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13788)
*Guofang Tang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文检验用LLM替代人工做minimal-pair基准标签验证的可行性，发现在BLiMP与QFrBLiMP上，按点估计任何LLM验证器都不优于单个人类评审；且LLM过滤会系统性剔除"人类支持但LM失败"的更难项目，使基准被人为简化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: minimal-pair基准的质量取决于标签本身，而人工验证成本高昂，LLM似乎是很自然的替代方案。但LLM验证是否会引入与人类不同的、非随机的判定偏差，此前缺乏系统检验，尤其缺乏与人类评审的对照以及预注册的严格证据。

Method: 在两个公开了全部强制选择人类判断的minimal-pair数据集（英语BLiMP、魁北克法语QFrBLiMP）上，用三个LLM分别以强制选择、打分、评审团三种方式做验证，并以1人、3人、5人人类评审团为对照，分两阶段预注册。随后在10个开放LM与全部验证器组合上分析被标记项与获批项的人类支持度/难度差异，并在荷兰语BLiMP-NL上用输出前冻结的协议做复现。

Result: 在BLiMP上，强制选择验证器与留出人类多数意见的一致率高于5人评审团，但漏检"人类拒绝其标签"的配对远多于单个评审；按点估计没有验证器价值超过一名评审。误差非随机：两个数据集中，被预注册LLM过滤器标记而人类支持的配对，比其接受的项目难18.9个点，且在全部70个LM-验证器组合中一致出现，也比单个评审标记的更难。因此过滤剔除的正是"LM失败而人类不失败"的项目，其准确率提升超过随机剔除。冻结协议在BLiMP-NL上复现了该差距与提升。

Conclusion: LLM验证会让minimal-pair基准对其本应测试的模型变得更简单，构成一种隐性基准污染/上线前泄露效应；建议以人类评审为基准成本而非替代目标，并公开代码、验证器输出与项目级得分以便审计。

Abstract: Minimal-pair benchmarks are only as good as their labels, and LLMs are an obvious substitute for costly human validation. We test it on BLiMP (English) and QFrBLiMP (Quebec French), the two public minimal-pair datasets releasing every forced-choice human judgment. Three LLMs validate by forced choice, by rating, and as a panel, scored against human panels of one, three and five judges in two pre-registered phases. On BLiMP, forced-choice validators agree with held-out human majorities more often than five-judge panels, yet catch far fewer pairs whose label humans reject than one judge does. By point estimate, no validator is worth more than one judge. The errors are not random. On both datasets, across 10 open LMs, human-supported pairs that the pre-registered LLM filter flags are 18.9 points harder than those it accepts, in all 70 LM-validator combinations, and harder than pairs a single human judge flags. Filtering thus removes items on which LMs fail but humans do not, raising accuracy more than random removal. A protocol frozen before any output replicates the gap and the lift on Dutch BLiMP-NL. LLM validation makes minimal-pair benchmarks easier for the models they are meant to test; we release code, validator outputs and item-level scores.

</details>


### [147] [Do Language Models Feel the Sentiment? Investigating Emotion Vectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13744)
*Ali Hassan Khan,Saad Bin Ather,Muhammad Saif,Maryam Bashir,Hajra Waheed*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究考察Qwen2.5-0.5B/3B-Instruct中情绪向量对情感分类的作用：情绪空间可高精度解码情感，消融会造成显著（但与对照相比增益有限）的损失，基于情绪的校正分类器优于原始模型但未稳定优于仅用margin的基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作表明语言模型内部存在与情绪概念关联的"情绪向量"并影响模型行为，但这些向量在情感分类任务中的实际作用尚不清楚；作者希望在区分"表征中可解码的信息""改变表征带来的因果效应""对分类任务的实用价值"三个层面。

Method: 在Qwen2.5-0.5B-Instruct与Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct上，从GoEmotions抽取8个情绪方向；通过情感探针、激活干预（原始情绪子空间消融，并与各向同性随机子空间匹配消融对照）、以及冻结的监督式情感校正分类器进行评测；主任务为二分类TweetEval（含原始与去噪设定、Holm多重比较校正），探索性扩展到SST-2及20,000条Amazon Polarity评论。

Result: 仅用情绪向量的探针在TweetEval二分类上达88.66%（0.5B）与90.70%（3B）；匹配消融实验中，原始情绪空间移除比匹配的随机子空间移除多造成2.39与1.10个百分点的准确率损失；TweetEval的4项原始与去噪效应在8次比较的Holm校正后均显著。SST-2扩展显示原始超额损失更小（0.66与0.57点），且无效应通过Holm校正。在Amazon Polarity上，情绪校正优于未校正Qwen，但未稳定优于仅margin基线。

Conclusion: 结果区分了模型表征中可获取的情感信息、改变这些表征所产生的效应，以及它们对分类的实际价值；作者明确不主张存在某种独特的"情绪"机制。

Abstract: Emotion vectors are directions in a language model's internal representations that are associated with emotional concepts. Prior work shows that these vectors can influence model behavior, but their role in sentiment classification remains unclear. This study investigates emotion directions in Qwen2.5-0.5B-Instruct and Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct. Eight directions extracted from GoEmotions are evaluated through sentiment probing, activation interventions, and supervised correction. Emotion-only probes achieve 88.66% and 90.70% accuracy on binary TweetEval. In exploratory matched-ablation experiments, raw emotion-space removal causes 2.39 and 1.10 percentage points more accuracy loss than matched isotropic random-subspace removal for 0.5B and 3B, respectively. All four TweetEval raw and denoised effects are significant after Holm adjustment across eight comparisons. An exploratory SST-2 extension shows smaller raw excess losses of 0.66 and 0.57 points, with no effects surviving Holm adjustment. Frozen sentiment-correction classifiers are also tested on 20,000 Amazon Polarity reviews. Emotion-based correction improves accuracy over uncorrected Qwen, but does not consistently outperform margin-only baselines. These results distinguish sentiment information available in model representations, the effects of changing those representations, and their practical value for classification. They do not establish a uniquely emotional mechanism.

</details>


### [148] [OpticalRAG: Pixel-Space Compression for Token-Efficient Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13675)
*Senhao Liu,Yuheng Zhang,Chunyu Wei,Yueguo Chen,Xinran Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 OpticalRAG，将文档渲染为页面图像，用冻结视觉编码器编码并蒸馏为仅16个注入token，在低token预算下实现高保真检索增强生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG受制于LLM的token预算：长文档直接塞入上下文代价高昂，而激进的文本压缩会在单一维度的token序列中不可逆地丢失细粒度信息。作者认为该瓶颈并非源于信息密度本身，而在于压缩"模态"的选择——视觉编码器可将整页渲染文本压缩为约百个视觉token而语义损失很小。

Method: 1) 将文档渲染为页面图像，用冻结视觉编码器编码；2) Encoder-Bridged Retrieval：借助与压缩器共享的投影，把文本查询状态映射到冻结编码器的视觉特征空间，从而用文本查询检索视觉token，无需对比训练；3) Query-Driven Distillation：轻量跨注意力压缩器将检索到的视觉token蒸馏为少量查询条件化的前缀token（可少至16个）注入LLM，避免昂贵的跨模态训练。

Result: 在LongBench-v2、BAMBOO和LooGLE-v2上，仅注入16个token即在这三个基准上均取得最佳的压缩感知准确率（CAP）；在LongBench-v2上相较闭卷的Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct骨干提升5.17分。

Conclusion: 以视觉模态作为压缩载体可突破纯文本压缩的保真度—压缩率权衡，且无需大规模跨模态训练即可在极低token预算下显著提升RAG性能。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) is bottlenecked by the token budget of large language models: feeding long documents into the context window is expensive, while aggressive textual compression discards fine-grained information that is irreversibly lost in a one-dimensional token sequence. We argue that this bottleneck is not fundamental to information density but to the choice of compression \emph{modality}. Modern visual encoders can pack the contents of a rendered text page into roughly a hundred visual tokens with little semantic loss, opening a new compression-fidelity tradeoff that purely textual methods cannot reach. We present \textbf{OpticalRAG}, a framework that renders documents as page images, encodes them with a frozen vision encoder, and distills the retrieved visual tokens into as few as $16$ injected tokens before passing them to the LLM. Two ingredients make this work without expensive cross-modal training: (i) \emph{Encoder-Bridged Retrieval}, which projects text-query states into the visual feature space of the frozen encoder, through a projection shared with the compressor, to retrieve visual tokens from a text query, with no contrastive training; and (ii) \emph{Query-Driven Distillation}, a lightweight cross-attention compressor that condenses the retrieved visual tokens into a small set of query-conditioned prefix tokens. On LongBench-v2, BAMBOO, and LooGLE-v2, OpticalRAG achieves the best compression-aware accuracy (CAP) on all three benchmarks while injecting only $16$ tokens, and improves the closed-book Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct backbone by $5.17$ points on LongBench-v2.Code is available at https://github.com/brighterr/OpticalRAG.

</details>


### [149] [Compute the Interactions, Not the Attributes: What a Fact Layer Must Supply in the Chinese Sexagenary System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13597)
*Huixue Han,Jiaying Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 在干支/八字与棋局等规则化领域，事实层应供给"计算出的交互事实"而非静态属性：直接作答时模型在需组合属性的项目上几乎全错，而查询过滤式的计算事实层可把准确率提升至78–100%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 规则化领域常将语言模型与确定性事实层配对，但"该层应提供哪些事实"缺少实测。本文系统测量这一设计问题，检验静态属性表、完整关系列表与计算型事实层各自的作用。

Method: 以中国干支系统（八字排盘）为测试床，构建682个跨十二类事实类别、值可由表格核验的条目，加160个流年—本命冲合条目；设置闭卷、附参考表、附逐级计算事实等条件。在11个开放权重模型上直接作答测试，并额外用查询过滤式事实层（探索性）与三个闭源推理模型对照；在棋局位置上做预先锁定预测的外部验证。

Result: 直接作答的11个开放权重模型中，干支日正确率无一超过4%，冲合条目无一超过1%；静态属性无一致效应，即使给出含答案的完整关系列表，多数模型仍近于零。查询过滤层将Qwen3-8B与Qwen3-32B提升至78–92%，另四个模型达96–100%；三个闭源推理模型独立计算冲合达68–95%。棋局任务复现同样结论：属性不能替代关系，列出的干扰项被错误归因。

Conclusion: 事实层必须供给"计算出的交互关系"，而非仅静态属性或原始关系列表；呈现方式需依模型与领域而定。该结论在干支与棋局两域一致，为规则化领域的事实层设计提供了可测依据。

Abstract: Language-model systems in rule-governed domains often pair the model with a deterministic fact layer, but which facts that layer should supply is rarely measured. We measure it in the Chinese sexagenary system underlying BaZi chart analysis, where every fact has one table-checkable value: 682 items over twelve fact categories and 160 transit--natal collision items, posed closed-book, with the reference table, and with computed facts at increasing levels. In eleven open-weight models answering directly, errors concentrate where attributes must be combined: none gets more than 4\% of sexagenary days or 1\% of collisions right. Static attributes have no consistent effect, and the complete relation list leaves most direct-answering models near zero although it contains the answer. A query-filtered layer (exploratory) raises Qwen3-8B and Qwen3-32B to 78--92\% and four further models to 96--100\%. Three proprietary reasoning models compute 68--95\% of collisions unaided. On chess positions, under predictions locked in advance, attributes again do not substitute for relations and listed distractors are misattributed. The fact layer must supply computed interactions; how they are presented depends on the model and the domain.

</details>


### [150] [Semantic Chains: From Reachable Facts to Justified Answers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14252)
*Misha Andreev*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该文将上下文建模为逻辑事实图，论证从问题出发寻找可构成"单一链接"文本的根事实与保真边的机制，并给出多链接链条中候选顺序的完全枚举及联合保真条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决"如何从问题中找到为答案提供依据的事实，并将其呈现为连贯陈述序列"这一问题，即答案的论证性生成与可解释性表达。

Method: 形式化建模：上下文为逻辑事实图，事实的表达式预先给定；父事实可表达子事实间关系，保真边（preserving edge）使子事实内容可在父事实完整表示中复原。基于"根事实证成完整答案、保真边路径连接根与问题—答案共享对象"的假设，构造从问题中对象出发的搜索，证明其可达根并覆盖所选展开中的每个事实。

Result: 在模型条件下，该搜索必达根事实且覆盖所选展开的全部事实；根事实可生成一段文本，作为连接问题与完整答案并为其提供依据的单一链接。对多链接链条，给出该类别内候选顺序的完全枚举，并明确了各链接文本何时共同保持论证关系并支撑每一次过渡。

Conclusion: 在给定模型假设下，答案的论证可被系统地从事实图中检索并以链接序列形式呈现，为可解释的答案生成提供了形式化基础。

Abstract: How can the facts that justify an answer be found from a question and presented as a sequence of connected statements? This paper models a context as a graph of logical facts and takes as given which expressions represent each fact. A parent fact can express a relation among its subfacts, and a preserving edge allows the content of a subfact to be recovered within a full representation of its parent. Suppose a root fact justifies the full answer and a path of preserving edges leads from that root to a fact that designates an object shared by the question and the answer. Under the conditions of the model, a search starting from the objects in the question reaches the root and covers every fact in the selected expansion. The root then admits a text that forms a single link connecting the question to the full answer and justifying it. For chains with several links, the paper gives a complete enumeration of candidate orders within the stated class and specifies when the link texts jointly preserve the justification and support every transition.

</details>


### [151] [An Interpretable Stylometric Framework for Early Chinese Buddhist Translator Styles: A Case Study on the Attribution of Disputed Works of An Shigao](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13636)
*Yating Pan,Xinyue Zhang,Jun Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一个可解释的风格计量学框架，用佛教学者审定或复核的语言特征，对早期汉译佛典进行作者归属，并以安世高为案例验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早期汉译佛典的作者归属需要可溯源到文本本身的证据；同时已有方法（如BERT）缺乏可解释性，学者难以检验判定依据。

Method: 构建基于文献学审定特征的可解释风格计量框架，特征包括功能字、句式套语、句末字、句长和词性n-gram；用东汉至西晋七位译者的31部已确证译作建立风格画像，再将8部疑伪经与安世高画像比对。

Result: T1557与T0101与安世高确证语料的风格相似度最高，得分分别为0.783和0.687；该归属结果与同一批疑伪经上已发表的BERT方法一致。

Conclusion: 该框架无需任务特定训练，且允许学者逐项检视支撑每次归属判定的语言特征，兼顾准确性与可解释性。

Abstract: Attributing early Chinese Buddhist translations requires evidence that scholars can trace back to the texts. We develop an interpretable stylometric framework that uses features defined or reviewed by Buddhist philologists, including function characters, sentence formulas, sentence-final characters, sentence length, and part-of-speech n-grams. We construct stylistic profiles from 31 authenticated translations by seven translators from the Eastern Han through the Western Jin, then compare eight disputed texts with An Shigao's profile. Two texts, T1557 and T0101, show the strongest stylistic similarity to the authenticated An Shigao corpus, with scores of 0.783 and 0.687, respectively. The attributions agree with those reported by a published BERT-based approach on the same set of disputed texts. The framework requires no task-specific training and allows scholars to inspect which linguistic features support each proposed attribution.

</details>


### [152] [One Sequence Is Enough: Fingerprinting Language Models from Fixed-Length Integer Sequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14010)
*Fantong Meng,Chengjie Sun,Shuangjia Yang,Zhiyuan Zhao,Yansheng Pei,Xinyang Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出NumPrint基准与SMCT模型，验证单条定长整数序列可同时融合多项数值观测以指纹化识别模型身份。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户依赖模型API和第三方平台声明的模型名称来识别响应模型，但实际模型可能与声明不符，因此需要基于可观测输出的模型身份验证。已有研究表明简单数值选择任务中的偏好可作为模型指纹，但能否用单条定长整数序列融合多项数值观测来识别来源模型尚不明确。

Method: 构建NumPrint基准：使用中、日、英三种提示，要求单次响应中给出1至355之间的294个整数，采集51个模型的5,036条回答；提出Shallow MLP and Causal Transformer（SMCT）模型，捕捉生成序列中的聚合数值偏好与行为模式。

Result: 在51类闭集评估中，SMCT仅凭单条序列达到92.72%准确率，优于所有对比方法；使用2条和4条回答时准确率分别提升至96.06%和97.12%。

Conclusion: 单条整数序列包含有效的模型指纹信息，且增加回答数量可进一步提升模型识别性能。

Abstract: Users of model APIs and third-party platforms often rely on the declared model name to identify the model responding to their requests. However, the actual model may differ from the declared one, motivating identity verification based on observable outputs. Previous studies have shown that preferences in simple numerical-choice tasks can serve as model fingerprints. We investigate whether a single fixed-length integer sequence can combine multiple numerical observations to identify its source model. For this task, we introduce NumPrint, a benchmark of 5,036 answers from 51 models, collected using Chinese, Japanese, and English prompts that request 294 integers between 1 and 355 in one response. We propose the Shallow MLP and Causal Transformer (SMCT) to capture aggregate numerical preferences and behavioral patterns in generated sequences. In 51-class closed-set evaluation, SMCT achieves 92.72% accuracy from a single sequence, outperforming all methods compared in this study. Using two and four answers increases accuracy to 96.06% and 97.12%, respectively. These results show that a single integer sequence contains useful model fingerprints and that additional answers further improve identification.

</details>


### [153] [ARE-bench: Benchmarking Agents' Research Ability in Real-World Researcher Sessions and Workspaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14364)
*Jiahang Lin,Wanying Ren,Wanghan Xu,Huayu Sha,Yang Shu,Zhonghang Lu,Chengjun Pan,Yi Dong,Shichun Liu,Yuyang Hu,Shihan Dou,Honglin Guo,Huijie Lv,Pujun Zheng,Zhe Sun,Yuankai Ying,Yiding Wang,Jiahao Chen,Zhixiong Yang,Dingbo Yuan,Yicheng Zou,Zhecan Wang,Guoxiu He,Xiaoyu Xing,Qi Zhang,Tao Gui,Xuanjing Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 ARE-bench，用于评估研究型智能体能否在真实的、进行中的研究项目里推进工作：既完成当前请求，又遵守先前会话的决策与既有工作区状态。基准包含 75 个任务、7 个研究阶段、33 项需求与 150 项子需求，共 1,173 条二元检查项。最强前沿模型仅在 66.2% 的任务上通过全部检查项；若只查交付契约与通用规范，三个模型约通过 69%，但加入历史与材料/工作区需求后降至 28.4%–40.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究智能体基准多聚焦独立任务与干净、精心整理的工作区，而真实科研场景中用户只要求完成项目中的某一步，请求依赖早期会话的决策，工作区充斥着未完成文件、过期版本与失败尝试。仅以基准分数衡量，无法反映智能体在真实项目中的可靠交付能力，因此需要一个基于真实会话上下文与进行中工作区的评估基准。

Method: 构建 ARE-bench：任务取自招募研究人员自身项目的智能体会话与完整工作区，覆盖 7 个研究阶段、33 项需求、150 项子需求，共 1,173 条二元检查项。每个任务锚定一条真实用户请求，保留其原始需求，并把仍然生效的早期请求归纳为背景；工作区被完整回放到该边界且不做清理，保留此前轮次的产物。检查项由与意图对齐的评分量表编译而成，任何有效解法均可得分。

Result: 在七个前沿模型上，最强模型仅 66.2% 的任务通过全部检查项；Design 与 Execution 对所有模型都是最难的阶段之一。当仅检查交付契约与通用规范时，三个模型约 69% 的任务通过；一旦同时检查历史需求与材料/工作区需求，通过率降至 28.4%–40.0%。

Conclusion: 仅评估交付结果而忽略上下文需求，会高估研究智能体的能力并掩盖模型间差异。研究智能体的评估必须纳入持续项目中的历史决策一致性与工作区状态约束。

Abstract: Research agents achieve increasingly strong benchmark scores, yet these scores do not necessarily translate into reliable delivery in ongoing research projects. Many research-agent benchmarks emphasize standalone tasks and relatively self-contained workflows in clean or curated workspaces. However, researchers ask agents to complete one step within an ongoing project, where requests depend on earlier session decisions and workspaces contain unfinished files, outdated versions, and failed attempts. We introduce ARE-bench to evaluate whether research agents advance ongoing projects while respecting prior decisions and existing workspace state, grounded in real session context and in-progress workspaces. ARE-bench includes 75 tasks built on agent sessions and full workspaces from recruited researchers' own projects, spanning seven research stages, 33 requirements, and 150 sub-requirements, with 1,173 binary checklist items in total. Each task is anchored at a real user request, preserves its requirements, and summarizes the earlier requests still in effect as background. The complete workspace is replayed to that boundary without cleanup, preserving artifacts from earlier turns. Each checklist is compiled from an intent-aligned rubric and credits any valid solution. Across seven frontier models, the strongest passes every checklist item on only 66.2% of tasks, and Design and Execution rank among the hardest stages for every model. Three models pass about 69% of tasks when only delivery contracts and general norms are checked, but just 28.4% to 40.0% once historical and material or workspace requirements are also checked. Evaluating delivery without these contextual requirements can therefore overestimate research agents and mask differences between them.

</details>


### [154] [Measuring Definition Sensitivity in LLM Annotation of Social Science Concepts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13339)
*Bogdan Mamaev,Qizhou Wang,Christopher Leckie,Matteo Vergani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出"定义敏感性"（definition sensitivity）指标：同一批文本用两个不同竞争性定义分别标注，两次标注的分歧量即为定义敏感性，并以改写定义措辞（要求不变）产生的"改写底线"（paraphrase floor）作为对照。在6个概念、3个LLM上，更换定义引起的标注变化始终大于改写措辞，证明LLM标注确实依赖提示中的定义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社会科学中的文本标注传统上依赖昂贵、难扩展的人工流程，LLM被用作低成本标注器。但仇恨言论、假新闻等概念通常存在多个理论上同样成立的竞争性定义，且无法确定模型是按提示中的定义给出标签，还是依据其预训练中固有的概念理解。这一不确定性威胁LLM标注结果的可解释性与有效性。

Method: 让模型对同一批文本标注两次，每次给定不同的竞争性定义，将两次标注的分歧定义为"定义敏感性"；设置对照条件：改写其中一个定义的措辞但保持其判定要求不变，两次措辞间的分歧构成"改写底线"。实验覆盖6个概念与3个LLM。

Result: 在所有6个概念与3个模型组合中，交换竞争性定义所改变的标注比例平均而言均高于改写措辞所导致的变动，即定义敏感性系统性地高于改写底线。

Conclusion: LLM的标注结果依赖于提示中给定的定义，因此任何标注集都应与其生成时所用的定义一并解读；相关研究应同时报告标注器的定义敏感性与改写底线，以提升LLM标注工作的透明度与可比性。

Abstract: In social science research, text annotation is a fundamental task that is traditionally a costly, hard-to-scale manual process. Researchers have used large language models (LLMs) to perform annotation by writing a definition of a concept into a prompt - a rule that determines whether a label should be attributed to a text depending on its content. However, concepts such as hate speech, fake news, and terrorism typically have several theoretically defensible competing definitions. It is also unknown whether the model actually applied the definition to provide the label or the label was based on the model's pretrained notion of the concept. To test this, we have the model annotate the same texts twice, each time with a different definition. We call the disagreement between the two sets of annotations definition sensitivity. As a control, we paraphrase one definition but leave its requirements unchanged - the disagreement between the annotations with the two wordings becomes the paraphrase floor. Across six concepts and three LLMs, a swap of competing definitions always changes more annotations, on average, than a paraphrase. This shows that the annotations depend on the prompted definition, so any set of annotations should be interpreted together with the definition that produced it. The LLM annotator's definition sensitivity and its paraphrase floor should both be reported in such work.

</details>


### [155] [From Relevant Posts to Attributable Spans: Explicit Symptom Evidence for Depression Questionnaire Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12900)
*Sumanth Manduru,Carlotta Domeniconi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SREE：先抽取第一人称、症状特异的文本片段作证据，再用Facility Location目标选代表子集，最后做证据门控推理，以推断标准化抑郁问卷作答。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用社交媒体历史推断抑郁问卷作答，需要与症状语义相关、可归因于用户本人、且足够具体到能支撑单个问卷条目的证据；现有语义相似度检索会引入误导性或不可归因证据（多症状帖、讨论他人经历）。

Method: 提出SREE管线：(1) 抽取第一人称、症状特异的文本跨度；(2) 用受Facility Location启发的目标函数选取代表性证据子集；(3) 在所选证据上执行Evidence-Gated Inference。在BDI-Sen上评证据检索，在eRisk 2019–2021上评证据条件下的问卷推断。

Result: 相较SOTA检索方法，帖子级精确率相对提升70%、F1提升59%，同时保留帖子数至少少30%；在多症状句子上，即使检索预算翻倍仍优于稠密检索；在三个eRisk集合上取得最高条目级精确一致率，序数与聚合指标具竞争力。

Conclusion: 定位可归因证据并在推断问卷作答前控制其构成至关重要。

Abstract: Longitudinal social-media histories offer an opportunity to assess depression, potentially supporting the inference of standardized depression questionnaire responses. However, this task requires evidence that is semantically relevant to a symptom and attributable to the user, while being sufficiently specific to support an individual questionnaire item. Existing approaches rely on semantic similarity to retrieve posts, which can introduce misleading or non-attributable evidence, particularly in multi-symptom posts and discussions of others' experiences. We propose SREE (Symptom Retrieval and Evidence-Grounded InfErence), an evidence-grounded pipeline that extracts first-person and symptom-specific text spans, selects a representative subset using a Facility Location-inspired objective, and performs Evidence-Gated Inference over the selected evidence. We evaluate evidence retrieval on BDI-Sen and evidence-conditioned questionnaire inference on eRisk 2019-2021. Compared with state-of-the-art retrieval approaches, SREE achieves relative improvements of 70% in post-level precision and 59% in F1, while retaining at least 30% fewer posts. On multi-symptom sentences, it also outperforms dense retrieval in precision and F1 even with twice the retrieval budget. Across all three eRisk collections, SREE achieves the highest exact item-level agreement while remaining competitive on ordinal and aggregate metrics. These results highlight the importance of localizing attributable evidence and controlling its composition before inferring depression questionnaire responses.

</details>


### [156] [Mobile AI Factory: Building Specialized Language Models for Your Mobile Device](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13509)
*Evan Li,Krishna Deshpande,Wen Cheng,Shyamnath Gollakota*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 Mobile AI Factory，一个 agentic 系统，可在给定任务与目标手机上自动构建微调+量化的 SLM，无需反复专家调参。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SLM 在移动端具备隐私与离线优势，但只有在针对任务微调并按设备适配规模时才有良好表现，而该过程目前依赖专家反复手工调优，成本高、难以规模化。

Method: 以任务规范与目标手机为输入，由编排 LLM 决策选择调超参、挖掘真实数据或生成合成样本，并用固定规则回滚损害精度的改动；搜索从小模型到大模型遍历基座与量化方案，动态调整精度目标，并基于目标手机在多种推理引擎/后端上的真实测量进行选型。

Result: 在 10 个任务、4 款 Android 手机上评测：未见测试集上，其端侧模型在 5 个任务上超过两个 five-shot 前沿模型（最高 +0.34），另 3 个任务持平；实体抽取任务上搜索效果与穷举训练全部 27 个变体相差仅 0.03，但 GPU 时间节省 19 倍；最优配置随手机变化，缺失手机实测信息时，即便给出完整硬件与模型规格，Claude Opus 5.5 在简单/中等任务中 25%、困难任务中 60% 未满足应用需求。

Conclusion: 端侧 SLM 的自动化构建可行且高效，硬件实测与设备感知搜索对满足应用需求至关重要；agentic 搜索在显著降低算力成本的同时接近穷举最优。

Abstract: Small language models (SLMs) running on mobile devices can keep user data private and work without connectivity. But they perform well only when fine-tuned for the target task and sized for the target device, a process that today requires repeated expert tuning. We present {\it Mobile AI Factory}, an agentic system that builds a fine-tuned, quantized SLM for a given task and phone. Given a task specification and a target phone, an orchestrator LLM chooses whether to tune hyperparameters, mine real data, or generate synthetic examples, while fixed rules roll back changes that hurt accuracy. The factory searches from small to large models across base models and quantizations, adapts its accuracy goal as it searches, and uses real measurements of the target phone across inference engines and backends. We evaluate Mobile AI Factory on ten tasks and four Android phones. On an unseen testset, its on-device models outperform two five-shot frontier models on five tasks, by up to 0.34, and match them on three more. Its search comes within 0.03 of exhaustively training all 27 model variants on an entity extraction task, while using 19$\times$ less GPU time. Phone measurements also matter: the best configuration changes across phones. Without phone measurements, Claude Opus 5.5, given the full hardware and model specifications, misses the app's requirements in 25\% of easy and moderate cases and in 60\% of hard ones. Code is available at: https://github.com/evanly-gh/SLM_Factory.

</details>


### [157] [Understanding Students' Rationales for AI-Mediated Cheating: An Instructor Perspective from Online Forums Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13358)
*Iman Mohammadi,Sina Rismanchian,Ali Keramati,Shayan Salehi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 通过分析201万条r/Professors论坛帖子，揭示了教师所感知的学生AI作弊动机，归纳为四大主题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI的广泛采用引发学术诚信危机。已有研究多聚焦于量化作弊行为，但对教师所认为的学生违规使用AI的动机知之甚少。

Method: 对r/Professors论坛2.01百万条帖子进行定性分析，考察教师如何理解学生对AI介导作弊行为的辩解理由。

Result: 定性分析提炼出四个主要主题：(1) 学术制度博弈（Academic System Gaming）；(2) 数字—学术素养鸿沟；(3) 表现焦虑；(4) 不诚信行为的常态化。

Conclusion: 教师将学生AI作弊动机归因于制度、素养、心理与规范四个层面，为理解学术诚信危机的成因提供了教师视角的定性证据。

Abstract: The extensive adoption of AI has precipitated a crisis in academic integrity. While studies attempt to quantify cheating, less is known about the motivations instructors believe students have for using AI in prohibited ways. We analyze 2.01 million posts on r/Professors to understand how instructors perceive students' justifications for AI-mediated cheating. Our qualitative analyses reveal four primary themes: (1) Academic System Gaming, (2) Digital-Academic Literacy Gap, (3) Performance Anxiety, and (4) Normalization of Dishonesty.

</details>


### [158] [Word-Level Alignment of OCR and Ground Truth under Zoning and Reading-Order Errors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13752)
*Fonseca Cacho,Jorge Ramón*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: The paper revisits the "Zoning Problem" of OCR word alignment by proposing a protocol with reading-order permutations, comparing the 2019 location-aware aligner, a re-anchoring extension, RETAS-style baselines (RETAS-lite, genalog's RETAS, Java RETAS), and a multi-region anchor-based aligner using synthetic noise, historical newspapers, and geometry-derived gold.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OCR post-processing requires word-by-word alignment between OCR text and ground truth, but datasets rarely provide such mapping, and monotone aligners lose their position when zoning errors permute reading order.

Method: A protocol applying OCR-side reading-order permutations with construction-time word-level gold, scoring correct-match F1, cascade length and recovery. Compares the 2019 location-aware aligner, a re-anchoring extension, RETAS-lite, a multi-region aligner combining unique-word and unique n-gram anchors with chunk-to-window assignment, genalog's RETAS and Java RETAS. Evaluations use synthetic character noise with block moves, paragraph swaps, column interleaving, historical newspaper permutations, geometry-derived gold for Tesseract OCR, and a small word-box set.

Result: Synthetic tests reveal where local tracking fails after large displacements and where unique-anchor methods collapse when anchors are scarce; the 2019 method achieved the highest F1 on the synthetic boilerplate page. Historical newspaper and geometry-derived evaluations are limited by approximate gold and the article-order convention. The first multi-region version has quadratic scaling cost.

Conclusion: Local tracking remains fragile under large reading-order changes; anchor-based methods handle many such cases but fail when unique anchors are scarce. Approximate gold, the article-order convention, missing residual annotation, and the lack of modern multi-column word-level ground truth remain limitations.

Abstract: OCR post-processing needs OCR text aligned word by word with its ground truth. Data sets rarely provide that mapping, and a monotone aligner can lose its place when zoning errors permute the reading order. We revisit this problem, the Zoning Problem of our 2019 paper, with a protocol that tests correctness under such permutations. We apply OCR-side reading-order permutations with construction-time word-level gold and score correct-match F1, cascade length and recovery. We compare the 2019 location-aware aligner, a re-anchoring extension of it, a RETAS-style baseline (RETAS-lite), a multi-region aligner that combines unique-word and unique n-gram anchors with chunk-to-window assignment, genalog's RETAS and the original Java RETAS. Experiments use synthetic character noise with block moves, paragraph swaps and column interleaving, historical newspaper reading-order permutations, geometry-derived gold for Tesseract OCR, and a small word-box set. Synthetic tests show where local tracking fails after large displacements and where unique-anchor methods collapse when unique anchors are scarce. On our synthetic boilerplate page the 2019 method had the highest F1 among those tested. Historical newspaper and geometry-derived evaluations carry the limitations of approximate gold and the article-order convention. The first multi-region version has a quadratic scaling cost. Local tracking remains fragile under large reading-order changes; the tested anchor-based methods handle many of those cases but fail when unique anchors are scarce. Approximate gold, the article-order convention, missing residual annotation, and the lack of modern multi-column word-level ground truth remain limitations.

</details>


### [159] [Beyond Passive Imitation: On-Policy Mid-Training Distillation for Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13855)
*Ruize Zheng,Xuyang Liu,Siyu Ren,Xiaobing Tu,Yinggui Wang,Jinkui Ren,Xiantao Zhang,Linfeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文指出标准下一词预测（NTP）在持续预训练中将所有语料词符视为固定目标、忽视其对当前模型的实际效用，导致同一训练数据在不同基座模型上效果相反；为此提出On-Policy Mid-Training Distillation（OPMD），将中训练从离线文本模仿重构为主动的、以查询为驱动的知识获取，在锚点位置采样学生策略轨迹并用冻结教师的稠密词级reverse-KL奖励训练，缓解teacher-forced NTP的训练—生成失配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续预训练（CPT）用于将大模型适配到专门领域，但标准NTP目标对每个语料词符一视同仁，不考虑其对当前模型的效用。作者在相同的1B token数学语料上做受控实验，发现固定训练配方下NTP能提升Llama-3.2-3B的数学基准表现，却显著损害Qwen3-1.7B-Base，说明训练序列的效果依赖于模型本身，被动式领域适配存在根本缺陷。

Method: 提出OPMD：（1）在选定的锚点位置从不断演化的学生策略中采样轨迹；（2）由冻结教师给出稠密的词级reverse-KL奖励；（3）在模型实际访问的状态上训练，而非仅在真实前缀上训练，从而降低teacher-forced NTP固有的训练—生成分布失配。该方法可嵌入CPT流水线，不增加推理阶段开销。

Result: 在MATH-500与Minerva上，OPMD使Qwen学生模型相对其基座性能获得提升，并优于所评估的NTP基线（该基线在两个基准上均退化）。验证了在相同语料与配方下，on-policy目标能避免NTP对特定基座模型的负迁移。

Conclusion: OPMD将中训练从被动的离线域适配转变为模型感知（model-aware）的主动知识获取，为CPT提供了一种不增加推理开销的替代方案，并说明训练目标应依据当前模型的策略分布而非固定语料目标来设计。

Abstract: Continual pre-training (CPT) adapts large language models (LLMs) to specialized domains, yet its standard next-token prediction (NTP) objective treats every corpus token as a fixed target, regardless of its utility to the current model. In a controlled study on the same 1B-token mathematical corpus, we observe sharply different outcomes across base models: NTP improves Llama-3.2-3B's performance on mathematical benchmarks but substantially degrades Qwen3-1.7B-Base's performance, indicating that, under a fixed training recipe, the same training sequences can have model-dependent effects. To address this problem, we introduce On-Policy Mid-Training Distillation (OPMD), which reformulates mid-training from offline text imitation into active, query-driven knowledge acquisition. At selected anchor positions, OPMD samples trajectories from the evolving student policy and applies dense token-level reverse-KL rewards from a frozen teacher, training the model on states it actually visits rather than only on ground-truth prefixes. This on-policy objective reduces the training-generation mismatch of teacher-forced NTP. On MATH-500 and Minerva, OPMD improves the Qwen student over its base performance and outperforms the evaluated NTP baseline, which degrades on both benchmarks. OPMD integrates into CPT pipelines without added inference-time overhead and offers a model-aware alternative to passive domain adaptation.

</details>


### [160] [Alania-2: Turkish text-to-speech with instant and fine-tuned voice cloning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13845)
*Sezgin Saygili,Emre Kaplaner,Oncel Ozgul,Fikri San Koktas*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Alania-2 是一个 488M 参数的土耳其语 TTS 系统，基于 VoxCPM 架构，在 18,195 小时土耳其语语音上从零训练，ASR 词错率降至 1.44%，并给出数据规模、声音克隆与推理加速的工程结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有土耳其语 TTS 在可懂度、文本规范化（数字/缩写/外来名）以及声音克隆稳定性上仍有明显缺陷；作者希望验证"训练数据比模型规模更影响可懂度"这一假设，并系统性地报告克隆与推理部署中的实用经验。

Method: 采用 VoxCPM 架构（语言模型骨干 + 扩散头生成连续音频隐变量），488M 参数，从零在 18,195 小时土耳其语语音上训练，其中 90% 为大模型合成的语音并经自动过滤；引入文本规范化器将数字、缩写、外来名改写为口语化词形；克隆实验比较"仅以参考音频为条件"与"续写参考音频"两种方式，并测试参考时长与 LoRA 适配器；同时优化推理引擎降低首包延迟。

Result: 含数字的 96 句测试中，无规范化器时无一句在合成+ASR 后保留全部关键信息，有规范化器时 86 句通过；Freya-TR-Eval（495 句）WER 1.44%，前代系统 5.99%；内置音色 CER 与 2.3B 模型差距在 0.5 个百分点内，而参数量不足其四分之一；同配方训练的 7.6M 单音色模型可懂度追平所测最强系统；克隆方面，仅条件化使跳词率从 21.9% 降至 1.7%，20–30 秒参考音频效果最佳，约 2 分钟训练的 LoRA 显著提升说话人相似度；单流首包音频延迟降至 32 ms。

Conclusion: 数据质量与规模（尤其是高质量合成数据+过滤+文本规范化）对可懂度的贡献大于模型规模；克隆应采用条件化而非续写、参考音频 20–30 秒、并用轻量 LoRA 提升相似度。作者开源了评估框架与三个土耳其语语音/文本数据集。

Abstract: We present Alania-2, a Turkish text-to-speech (TTS) system with two built-in voices, voice design from text descriptions, and two kinds of voice cloning. Alania-2 is a 488M-parameter model with the VoxCPM architecture, which generates continuous audio latents with a diffusion head on top of a language-model backbone. We train it from scratch on 18,195 hours of Turkish speech, 90% of which is synthetic speech produced by a larger teacher model and filtered automatically. A text normalizer rewrites numbers, abbreviations and foreign names into the words a Turkish speaker would say; without it, none of 96 test sentences with digits kept all its critical parts, such as an amount or a date, through synthesis and speech recognition, against 86 with it. On Freya-TR-Eval, a public benchmark of 495 Turkish sentences scored with automatic speech recognition, Alania-2 reaches a word error rate of 1.44%, compared with 5.99% for our previous system. On its built-in voices, it comes within half a point of character error rate of a 2.3B-parameter model at under a quarter of the size, and a 7.6M-parameter single-voice model trained with the same recipe matches the most intelligible systems we measured, which suggests that training data matters more for intelligibility than model size. We also report practical findings on voice cloning: conditioning on the reference audio alone, rather than continuing it, cuts skipped words from 21.9% to 1.7%; 20-30 seconds of reference audio works best; and a low-rank adapter trained in about two minutes raises speaker similarity well above instant cloning. Finally, we describe inference-engine changes that bring first audio down to 32 ms for a single stream. We release our evaluation harness and three Turkish speech and text datasets.

</details>


### [161] [An evidence-traceable benchmark of clinical reasoning in large language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13562)
*Zhiqun Zhao,Xiaodong Yang,Bruce Gu,Ming Li,Yi Fu,Song Li,Qi Mei,Zhi Liu,Kai Zhang,Kexue Fu,Jing Wu,Xiaofei Lu,Yule Li,Zheng Wang,Haiquan Lu,Xiaoming Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出Med Evidence Bench（MEB）基准，将每条评分标准与可溯源、带版本的医学证据原文片段链接，用证据溯源得分（ETS）评测十个医疗大模型，发现模型在指南知识上表现优异（0.975-0.997），但在标准化与真实场景中证据应用显著下降，指南记忆并不等于患者个体化证据使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医疗大模型基准分数难以解释：评分标准无法追溯到临床证据，导致高分无法说明模型是否真正依据证据作出判断，亟需可溯源、分层级的评测体系。

Method: 构建MEB基准，含1,536道由医生编写的题目，分三层：指南知识、标准化临床场景、真实世界实验室场景；每条陈述医学主张的评分标准均链接至带版本的来源文本片段。对十个大模型计算证据溯源得分（ETS），并分析不同层级排名变化及分环节（识别临床信息、匹配证据、形成建议）表现；以三位医生多数投票为参照，对1,296条标准级判断评估自动评分的宏平均F1。

Result: ETS在指南知识层为0.975-0.997，标准化场景为0.292-0.658，真实场景为0.182-0.447，模型排名随层级变化。真实场景中，识别临床信息得分（均值0.770）显著高于匹配证据（0.165）与形成建议（0.160）。自动评分与三位医生多数投票的一致性为宏平均F1=0.930（95% CI 0.915-0.945），但在证据应用类判断上一致性较低。

Conclusion: 基于中文材料的结果表明，指南知识回忆不能保证患者特异性的证据使用能力；该基准反映的是证据溯源能力而非临床有效性，不构成对临床实际效果的证明。

Abstract: Benchmark scores for medical large language models (LLMs) are difficult to interpret when scoring criteria cannot be traced to clinical evidence. Here we developed Med Evidence Bench (MEB; https://www.medevidencebench.org.cn/), comprising 1,536 physician-constructed items across three levels: guideline knowledge, standardized clinical scenarios and real-world laboratory scenarios. Each criterion stating a medical claim is linked to a versioned source-text span. Across ten LLMs, the Evidence Trace Score (ETS) was 0.975-0.997 for guideline knowledge, 0.292-0.658 for standardized scenarios and 0.182-0.447 for real-world scenarios, and rankings varied across levels. For real-world scenarios, scores for identifying clinical information (mean, 0.770) exceeded those for matching evidence (0.165) or formulating recommendations (0.160). On 1,296 criterion-level judgments, automated grading agreed with the majority vote of three physicians (macro-F1, 0.930; 95% confidence interval, 0.915-0.945), although agreement was lower for evidence application. These findings from Chinese-language materials indicate that guideline recall does not ensure patient-specific evidence use and do not establish clinical effectiveness.

</details>


### [162] [When does text encoding matter for language modeling?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14459)
*Eugene Jang,Catherine Arnett,Terra Blevins*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: UTF-8作为语言模型事实标准的变长编码会造成跨语言不平等，本文系统比较多种编码策略对字节级语言模型性能的影响，并提出按语言定制编码的混合方案Aether。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UTF-8的变长设计导致不同语言的字节序列长度与信息密度差异显著，可能引发跨语言性能不均衡，但编码选择对模型性能的影响此前基本未被系统考察。

Method: 在字节级语言模型上对比UTF-8、其他Unicode编码、语言专用编码及自定义编码；提出Aether混合编码框架：对目标语言使用文字系统专用编码，并通过UTF-8回退保持完整Unicode覆盖；在四种正字法差异显著的语言上进行建模分析。

Result: 信息密度高的字符语言（中文、韩文）从压缩型编码中显著受益；字符单字信息量较低的语言（格鲁吉亚文、泰米尔文）对编码选择基本不敏感。

Conclusion: 文本编码应作为语言特定的设计决策，而非通用默认设置。

Abstract: UTF-8's role as the de facto encoding standard in language models introduces cross-lingual inequities due to its variable-length design. However, the effect that this encoding choice has on model performance remains largely unexamined. In this work, we investigate how text encoding impacts the performance of byte-level language models, comparing UTF-8 with a wide range of other encoding strategies, including other Unicode-based approaches, language-specific encodings, and our own customized encodings. To facilitate our analysis, we propose Aether: a hybrid encoding scheme that uses script-specific encodings for our target setting while maintaining full Unicode coverage through UTF-8 fallbacks, using this framework to model four orthographically diverse languages. Surprisingly, we find that while languages with information-dense characters (Chinese, Korean) significantly benefit from compressive encodings, languages whose characters carry less information individually (Georgian, Tamil) are largely insensitive to encoding choice. In light of these findings, we argue that text encoding should be a language-specific design decision, rather than a universal default.

</details>


### [163] [StaRSE: Compact Russian Sentence Embeddings with a Sign-Coded Static Encoder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14236)
*Boris Malashenko,Ivan Jarsky,Egor Soldatov,Vladislav Luzhnov,Sviatoslav Stumpf,Vladislav Shmatkov,Viacheslav Shalamov,Andrey Stankevich,Valeria Efimova*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: StaRSE：面向俄语的紧凑静态句子编码器，部署时用符号位存词向量方向、单独存词范数，句子经范数缩放后的符号方向均值池化得到嵌入，摒弃 Transformer 推理。RuMTEB 任务均值 51.16，比最强静态基线高 7.98 分；模型 11.3 MiB，CPU 平均延迟 0.08 ms/句。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer 句编码器检索质量强，但在 CPU 上生成嵌入或需高频刷新时推理成本成为瓶颈，需要一种部署期无需 Transformer 推理的低成本方案。

Method: 将词方向存为符号位（±1）、词范数单独存储；句子编码为对范数缩放的符号方向做均值池化。模型从 ruBERT-base 初始化，在俄语相邻句对与任务形态句对上微调，并加一个直接优化符号投影词表的对比后训练阶段。消融考察相邻句监督、词范数、符号投影后训练三项。

Result: RuMTEB 任务均值 51.16，较最强纳入静态基线高 7.98 分；包体积 11.3 MiB，CPU 平均延迟 0.08 ms/句；最接近质量的上下文编码器估计为 55.7 MiB（16-bit 参数）与 1.43 ms/句。

Conclusion: 以符号位量化加范数解耦的静态编码器可在几乎不损失检索质量的前提下大幅降低存储与 CPU 延迟，是有竞争力的俄语高效检索方案。

Abstract: Transformer sentence encoders provide strong retrieval quality, but their inference cost becomes a bottleneck when embeddings must be produced on CPUs or refreshed frequently. We introduce StaRSE, a compact Russian static sentence encoder that removes Transformer inference at deployment time: token directions are stored as sign bits, token norms are stored separately, and sentences are encoded by mean pooling over norm-scaled sign directions. StaRSE is initialized from ruBERT-base, fine-tuned on Russian adjacent-sentence and task-shaped pairs, and post-trained with a contrastive stage that directly optimizes the sign-projected token table. On RuMTEB, StaRSE reaches a task mean of $51.16$, $7.98$ points above the strongest included static baseline. The packaged model occupies $11.3$ MiB and has a mean CPU latency of $0.08$ ms per sentence, compared with an estimated $55.7$ MiB 16-bit parameter footprint and $1.43$ ms mean CPU latency for the closest-quality contextual encoder. Ablations examine adjacent-sentence supervision, token norms, and direct sign-projected post-training.

</details>


### [164] [Mapping Instructors' Attitudes Towards Cheating with AI: An Exploratory Analysis Using Forum Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13366)
*Iman Mohammadi,Ali Keramati,Sina Rismanchian*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究分析 r/Professors 版块约 201 万条帖文与评论，考察 ChatGPT 发布后大学教授对 AI 作弊的信念，归纳其反作弊动机与策略，并据此提出 AI 与学术融合的前景警示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 教学环境中的作弊与学术诚信违规长期存在，涉及管理、知识掌握、学习效果与公平等多层面关切；大语言模型出现后，"对话式作弊"因其可代写学业任务的能力而成为突出问题，但学界对教师自身信念缺乏系统了解。

Method: 以 r/Professors 子版块为数据来源，构建 ChatGPT 发布后讨论作弊的 2.01 百万条帖文与评论语料库，采用话语分析/主题归纳方式，提炼教师劝阻作弊的动机（why）与所采取的策略（how）。

Result: 识别出教师反对作弊的多重动机及对应的干预策略，并对其"为何"与"如何"进行综合归纳，形成关于 AI 与学术整合未来走向的若干警示性判断。

Conclusion: 教师信念与应对实践构成了 AI 融入教学的关键中介；需在诚信规范、评价设计与教学方式上同步调整，以应对生成式 AI 带来的学术诚信挑战。

Abstract: In teaching and learning environments, cheating and the violation of academic integrity have always been one of the challenges, involving different levels of concerns such as administrative, personal knowledge, learning, and equity. The introduction of large language models has brought cheating in dialogue to the forefront, given the apparent capabilities of these models to complete students' academic assignments. This study aims to leverage recent dialogues among university professors about cheating with Generative AI to better understand their beliefs about academic honesty. In doing so, we analyze a corpus of 2.01 million posts (threads and comments) in the r/Professors subreddit discussing cheating after the release of ChatGPT, providing a better understanding of teachers' motivations to discourage cheating and the strategies they use to do so. We synthesize the why and how of addressing cheating to identify caveats about the future of AI and academic integration.

</details>


### [165] [Catch It Where It Happens: Real Time Error Correction During LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13107)
*Rotem Elimelech,Michael Shalyt,Ido Kaminer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出"在线确定性验证"：用代码（如计算机代数系统）在推理步骤写出时实时校验，失败即提示模型从原地续写，无需模型调用或GPU；在ASyMoB上使三个开源模型准确率提升1.4–5.6个百分点，错误率净降1.8–14.9%，token开销仅增6–7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM的推理错误往往表述自信、难以察觉，且单步错误会沿错误前提传播、浪费推理token。现有防护手段要么只校验最终答案并整体重来，要么依赖验证器LLM，既占用GPU又拖慢响应。

Method: 在线、确定性的步骤级验证：识别可由程序判定的推理步骤，在生成过程中逐步骤用代码检查（符号数学场景采用计算机代数系统，毫秒级判定）；检查失败时指出错误位置并让模型就地继续生成，而非回滚重采样。

Result: 在ASyMoB基准、三个开放权重模型上：准确率提升1.4–5.6个百分点，对应错误答案净减少1.8%–14.9%，token消耗仅增加6%–7%。

Conclusion: 验证器与模型解耦，故与LLM能力进步互补而非竞争：新模型可即插即用受益，且随确定性检查器覆盖更多推理步骤类型，收益将持续扩大。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly trusted with mathematical, scientific and engineering work, yet their mistakes are often confidently written and hard to spot. A single wrong step tends to propagate, making the model waste inference tokens following a false premise. Existing safeguards either check only the final answer and restart from scratch, or rely on a verifier LLM that competes for GPU capacity and slows down response time. We propose online, deterministic verification: many kinds of reasoning steps can be validated by code, so a step can be verified in real time, as it is written, without any model call or GPU use. Whenever a check fails, we point out the mistake and let the model continue from the same spot. We demonstrate this approach on symbolic mathematics, where derivations already contain explicit expressions that a computer algebra system can check in milliseconds. Tested on the ASyMoB benchmark with three open-weights models, our online validation improves accuracy by 1.4-5.6 percentage points, corresponding to a 1.8-14.9% net reduction in incorrect answers, while increasing token usage by only 6-7%. Because the verifier is independent of the model, the approach complements progress in LLMs rather than competing with it: every new model can benefit immediately, and the benefit grows as deterministic checkers extend to more kinds of reasoning steps.

</details>


### [166] [You Are What You Read: Indian Political News Media Bias Dataset for Hindi Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13686)
*Kushal Trivedi,Mayank Mahor,Dhruv Sanghvi,Roshni Chakraborty*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文构建了首个面向印地语新闻媒体的多标注者、可解释的媒体偏见数据集，包含来自4家印度主流印地语新闻机构的448篇政治新闻，按四类偏见标注并提供解释，标注一致性达到Kappa≈0.80。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 印度语境、尤其是印地语等印度语言下的媒体偏见理解与检测研究不足。印度语言多样、人口庞大，同一事件常被多家不同语言的新闻机构报道，为偏见提供了空间。

Method: 从四家印度主流印地语新闻机构采集448篇独特的政治新闻文章，由14名专家标注：先判定有偏见/无偏见，再归入选择偏见、框架偏见、标题党偏见、遗漏偏见四类；对每篇有偏见的文章提供文本解释，便于后续半自动化方法在语言模型推理中融入可解释性。

Result: 简单偏见/无偏见分类的Cohen's Kappa为0.81；多标签任务中标题党、选择、框架、遗漏偏见的Kappa分别为0.89、0.75、0.81、0.79，平均0.80，均体现强标注者间一致性。

Conclusion: 该数据集填补了印地语媒体偏见研究的空白，具备高标注一致性与可解释标注，为印度语境下的偏见检测与可解释建模提供了基础资源。

Abstract: Media bias understanding and detection remains an under-researched problem for Indian context, specifically for Indic languages, such as, Hindi, which is widely spoken in India. In this paper, we introduce a comprehensive, multi-annotator, explainable dataset consisting of 448 unique articles from four leading Indian news agencies publishing in Hindi. We focus on Hindi, the most spoken and an official language of India. The study of media bias is particularly relevant in the Indian context due to the country's linguistic diversity and large population, where the same event is often reported by multiple news agencies in different languages, thereby introducing scope for bias. The dataset comprises Hindi political news articles annotated by 14 experts and classified into four bias categories - selection bias, framing bias, clickbait bias and omission bias - when an article is identified as biased or unbiased. For each biased article, annotators also provide a textual explanation, enabling future semi-automated approaches to incorporate explainability when language models reason over the dataset. Cohen's Kappa scores were 0.81 for simple bias/no-bias classification reflecting strong inter-annotator agreement. For the multi-label task, the Cohen's Kappa values are 0.89, 0.75, 0.81, and 0.79 for clickbait, selection, framing, and omission biases, respectively, resulting in an average Kappa of 0.80, which also reflects strong agreement.

</details>


### [167] [On the information rate of phones, syllables and words](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13990)
*Herman Kamper*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文旨在回答"哪种语言单位能以最低比特率编码语音内容"，作者在960小时英语有声书语料上，使用n-gram与Transformer语言模型，比较音素、音节、词、多词组合及中间切分单位的信息率，发现词（偶尔为略大于词的单位）比特率最低，说明在建模能力受限时，介于音节与长短语之间的单位是最高效的表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语音与语言系统如何选择编码单位，以及人类语言加工理论，都依赖于"哪种单位最经济"这一问题。理论上在完美语言模型下所有单位等价，但真实模型容量有限，因此需要在有限建模预算下比较不同单位的信息率，以确定最优编码粒度。

Method: 构建960小时英语有声书的转写语料，将语音内容分别切分为音素、音节、词、多词组合以及音节与长短语之间的中间tokenisation；分别用n-gram语言模型和Transformer语言模型估计各单位的比特率，并进行横向比较。

Result: 在有限建模能力下，各单位的比特率存在系统性差异：以词为单位的比特率最低，略长于词的单位有时表现最佳；音素、音节等短单位以及长多词短语的比特率相对更高。

Conclusion: 当模型容量受限时，大于音节但小于长多词短语的语言单位在信息率意义上提供了最高效的语音内容表示，这对语言系统发展与人类语言加工理论具有启示意义。

Abstract: Which linguistic units minimise the bitrate required to encode speech content: phones, syllables, words, maybe larger units, or something in between? This question has implications for the development of speech and language systems and theories of human language processing. Using a transcribed corpus of 960 hours of English audiobooks, I compare the information rates of phones, syllables, words, multi-word combinations and intermediate tokenisations. While all units are equivalent under a perfect language model, it makes sense to compare these under limited modelling budgets; I use $n$-gram and transformer language models. The lowest bitrates are achieved using words, with slightly longer units sometimes performing best. The results show that, when modelling capacity is limited, units larger than syllables but smaller than long multi-word phrases provide the most efficient representation in terms of information rate.

</details>


### [168] [Framing-Induced Vulnerability in Large Language Models: Evidence from Chinese News Headlines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13531)
*Mo Zhou,Yiwei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文通过生态审计（63,926条中文新闻标题）与受控实验相结合，检验大语言模型对新闻标题的判断究竟基于事实本身还是受标题框架影响。结果显示四种中文模型（DeepSeek、MiMo、Dots、LongCat）在仅改标题框架时对"误导性"与"事件严重性"的评分都会系统性偏移，且方向一致；提供正文可将该严重性偏差降低54%–80%（p < .01），说明仅凭标题的LLM评估存在可测量的稳健性缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM越来越多地承担新闻评估、路由与审核功能，其对标题的判断已成为实质性的"把关"决策。然而当前缺乏证据判断这种判断究竟依据所报道的事实，还是被事实的表述框架（framing）所左右；若模型对表述方式敏感，则仅凭标题的自动化评估存在可靠性风险，而现有部署与治理检查并未将框架敏感性纳入可靠性指标。

Method: 采用生态审计与受控实验相结合的混合设计。生态审计覆盖63,926条中文新闻标题；受控实验包含30个真实事件、30个合成事件与29组自然标题对，在四个中文模型上测试。实验以"仅改变标题框架"为操纵变量，测量感知误导性与事件严重性评分的偏移方向与幅度；同时设置提供文章正文的条件以检验信息补充的缓解效果，并辅以一个较小规模的人类参照研究进行方向性对照。

Result: 仅改变标题框架即可同时改变感知误导性与事件严重性评分，且偏移的方向与排序在DeepSeek、MiMo、Dots与LongCat之间完全一致。情绪强化型标题使严重性评分升高0.65–0.88分，正向重构型标题使其降低0.34–0.52分。提供文章正文后，该严重性偏差降低54%–80%，四个模型的降幅均在p < .01水平上显著。小规模人类参照研究呈现同方向的效应。

Conclusion: 仅基于标题的LLM评估存在可测量的稳健性缺口，模型判断显著受框架而非仅受事实驱动，且该敏感性在多个模型间高度一致。因此框架敏感性应被纳入部署与治理阶段所检查的可靠性属性，实践中应避免或谨慎使用纯标题输入的自动化新闻评估，并考虑以正文等上下文作为偏差缓释手段。

Abstract: Large language models increasingly evaluate, route, and moderate news, so a model's judgment of a headline has become a gatekeeping decision. We ask whether that judgment follows the fact being reported or the way the fact is framed. We combine an ecological audit of 63,926 Chinese news headlines with a controlled experiment. The experiment covers 30 real events, 30 synthetic events, and 29 natural headline pairs, tested on four Chinese models. Changing only the headline frame shifted both perceived misleadingness and event severity, and the direction and ordering of the shifts were the same for DeepSeek, MiMo, Dots, and LongCat. Emotionally intensified headlines raised severity ratings by 0.65 to 0.88 points, whereas positively reframed headlines lowered them by 0.34 to 0.52 points. Supplying the article body reduced this severity bias by 54 to 80 percent, and all four reductions were significant at p < .01. A small human reference study showed the same direction of effect. Headline-only LLM evaluation therefore carries a measurable robustness gap, and framing sensitivity belongs among the reliability properties that deployment and governance check.

</details>


### [169] [Stagewise and interpretable emergence of inside-outside computation in Transformer-based language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14221)
*Jack T. Parley*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究合成上下文无关文法（CFG）中Transformer的学习动力学，揭示其分阶段习得inside-outside解析计算的过程，且各阶段损失可解析计算并对应隐树变量的表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有"事后"研究显示训练充分的Transformer可近似inside-outside等经典解析算法，但其如何被学习得到的理论尚不清楚。作者希望在可变解析树拓扑的合成CFG上、在统计可控的条件下打开理论研究之门。

Method: 在解析树拓扑可变的合成上下文无关文法上训练Transformer，分析分阶段学习曲线，对表示进行探针可视化，追踪inside-outside计算在深度与训练过程中的涌现。

Result: 学习曲线各阶段损失均可解析计算，并可与隐树变量的表示清晰对应（用于消歧、利用更宽上下文信息）；早期阶段仅少数初始层利用bigram/trigram局部信息，随后网络逐步形成编码器-解码器结构，最抽象的隐信息编码于中部，后半部分网络逐步精化最终词元预测。

Conclusion: 该计算组织方式与真实LLM中的发现相似，且这里的合成模型允许对数据统计进行完整解析控制，为理论进展提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Variability in syntactic structure and parsing ambiguity are fundamental aspects of natural language which modern LLMs have to learn to deal with proficiently. Previous "post-mortem" studies have shown that fully trained Transformers indeed approximate canonical parsing algorithms such as inside-outside, but little is established theoretically about how this is actually learned. Here, we make a first step towards addressing this by studying the learning dynamics in synthetic context-free grammars with variable parse tree topology. The stagewise learning curves are fully interpretable, in the sense that all of the loss stages a) can be computed analytically, and b) can be clearly associated to the representation of a hidden latent tree variable, which allows to lift ambiguity and utilize information from wider contexts. By probing representations, we visualize the emergence of inside-outside computation across depth and during training: while during early stages local (bigram/trigram) information is used and involves only a few initial layers, gradually the Transformer develops an encoder-decoder structure where the most abstract latent information is encoded in the middle, and the latter half of the network is used to progressively refine the final token prediction. This organization of computation is reminiscent of findings in real-world LLMs, and importantly applies here to a synthetic model which allows full analytical control of the data statistics, thus opening the door to theoretical progress.

</details>


### [170] [Sckwoky at SHROOM-Visions: Character-Level Hallucination-Span Detection in Image-Conditioned VLMs via Dual-Prompted LoRA Decoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14100)
*Mikhail Kulakov,Ruslan Solikhov,Ivan Bondarenko*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出基于冻结Gemma-4-12B-it加LoRA适配器的字符级幻觉片段检测系统，用于SHROOM-visions 2026四语任务，宏平均字符IoU达0.4404（基线0.1854）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决图像条件VLM回复中的字符级幻觉片段检测问题，并适配本地QA系统中无法额外部署独立验证器的实际场景。

Method: 冻结主干+约10.7M参数LoRA；通过在同一提示内重复候选答案、从第二次出现处抽取隐藏状态，使因果解码器获得近似双向上下文且保持单次非自回归前向；字符级定位器融合中间层隐藏状态，预测幻觉概率、BIO边界与响应级弃权分数；另设分语种分类器为每个片段分配五类幻觉类型之一。

Result: 封闭测试集宏平均字符IoU 0.4404，超过官方基线0.1854一倍以上，四语IoU排名第5–6位；英文调优集消融显示重复答案带来的增益与LoRA适配相当（留出集增益较小且集中于含幻觉答案），打乱图文配对后IoU降至predict-nothing下限或更低。

Conclusion: 重复答案与LoRA均贡献显著增益，检测器确实依赖图文配对信息；当同一模型兼作生成器时可复用基础权重，无需独立验证器，适合本地问答部署。

Abstract: We present our system for the SHROOM-visions 2026 shared task on character-level hallucination span detection in image-conditioned vision-language model (VLM) responses, evaluated across English, French, Italian, and Chinese. The detector is built on a frozen Gemma-4-12B-it backbone with trainable LoRA adapters (approximately 10.7M parameters). Because the backbone is a causal decoder, hidden states read from a single copy of an answer cannot attend to subsequent tokens; we therefore repeat the candidate answer within one prompt and extract features from its second occurrence, giving every position approximate bidirectional context in a single non-autoregressive pass. A character-level locator combines intermediate hidden states to predict hallucination probabilities, BIO boundaries, and a response-level abstention score, while separate language-specific classifiers assign one of five hallucination types to each predicted span. On the closed test set, our system reaches a macro-averaged character IoU of 0.4404, more than doubling the official baseline (0.1854), and ranks 5th-6th on IoU across the four languages.
