On the English tuning split, controlled ablations attribute a gain to the repeated answer that is comparable to that of LoRA adaptation (its held-out gain is smaller and concentrated on hallucinated answers), and they show that the detector relies on the image-answer pairing: shuffling images across examples drives IoU down to the level of the predict-nothing floor or below it in every language. In deployments where the same Gemma-4-12B-it model also generates answers, its base weights can be reused, making the approach practical for local question-answering systems that cannot run a separate verifier.

</details>


### [171] [Point-in-Time Encoders for Earnings Calls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14325)
*Alessio Brini*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对金融文本检索的回测前视偏差问题，作者用412,248份财报电话会议文本，按2010—2024年逐年截断点训练四种预训练配方的"时点快照"编码器（vintage），每份快照只读取截止日期前的文本，再在电话会议自身的问答对上微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实盘检索器可用在全部可用文本上一次性训练的编码器，但回测要求每个向量对应的编码器不能读过该向量日期之后的文本，否则存在look-ahead bias（前视偏差）。本文旨在构建严格point-in-time的文本编码器并评估其对检索、问答与预测任务的影响。

Method: 从412,248份电话会议中，按2010—2024年逐年cutoff、四种预训练配方训练vintage编码器（仅33.8M参数），每份仅见截至cutoff的文本；随后在电话会议问答对上微调。评估涵盖：留出会议上的答案检索、与过去收益和公司规模构建的预测之间的排序相关性、vintage链式续训（后一快照接续前一快照）、基于检索段落的point-in-time语言模型问答，以及收益惊喜与收益预测。

Result: 仅用电话会议训练的33.8M参数vintage在每个测试季度上检索表现均优于所测通用检索器；其向量使"次日收益与基于过去收益及公司规模的预测"之间的秩相关提升约一半。链式续训下，较晚vintage在较早季度的检索优于当时可用的vintage，且point-in-time语言模型继承该增益。2024 vintage对收益惊喜的预测更优，但在无任何vintage读过的会议上该增益置信区间含零；对收益的预测无改善。

Conclusion: 时点vintage编码器对检索类任务价值显著，并可传导至下游point-in-time问答；对收益惊喜预测有边际提升（不稳健），对收益预测则无增益。总体而言，point-in-time快照对检索重要、对收益预测影响甚微。

Abstract: Text encoders turn earnings calls into vectors for search, for language models that answer from retrieved passages, and for forecasting models. A live retriever can use an encoder trained once on all available text, as finance retrievers are, but a backtest needs an encoder that read no text written after the date of each vector. To address this look-ahead bias, from 412,248 calls we train vintages under four pretraining recipes, with yearly cutoffs from 2010 to 2024, and each vintage reads only text dated on or before its cutoff. We then fine-tune each vintage on the question-answer pairs of the calls themselves. The vintages we train on calls alone, with 33.8M parameters, retrieve answers on held-out calls better than the general retrievers we tested in every test quarter. Their vectors also raise by about half the rank correlation between next-day returns and forecasts built from past returns and firm size. When each vintage continues the previous one, a later vintage retrieves better on earlier quarters than the vintage available at the time, and a point-in-time language model that answers from the passages the later vintage retrieves inherits the gain. The 2024 vintage of our families also forecasts earnings surprises better, although the interval of this gain includes zero on calls that no vintage read. It forecasts returns no better, so point-in-time vintages matter most for retrieval and little for forecasts of returns.

</details>


### [172] [Beyond Fluent Responses: Finding User Dissatisfaction in Human-AI Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13894)
*Yuka Saito,Ikumi Numaya,Jun Suzuki,Reina Akama*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究能否从对话文本中检测用户以自我报告方式标注的“不满意”信号，并比较不同输入（用户话语、上下文变化）与检测方法。英文交互中，基于用户话语的模型在AUPRC上最多超过17.8%的标签流行率15个百分点，表明用户话语含有隐性不满线索；相对前一轮用户话语的变化，尤其配合异常检测方法，可进一步提升检测效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 响应评估正从“以响应为中心”的指标（正确性、连贯性）转向“以用户为中心”的指标（用户偏好、感知有用性），因为真实对话中的用户满意度并不总与响应质量一致。然而，用户中心信号能否直接从对话文本中检测尚不清楚，本文即考察文本中用户不满意能否被识别。

Method: 采用带有用户自我报告不满意标签的用户话语数据进行分析；构建基于用户话语的文本检测模型，并以AUPRC为评价指标与标签流行率（17.8%）对比；进一步以“相对紧邻前一轮用户话语的变化”作为特征，并考察异常检测方法在此设定下的效果。

Result: 英文交互中，使用用户话语的模型AUPRC最高比17.8%的不满意标签流行率高出15个百分点，说明用户话语包含隐含不满的线索；以紧邻前一轮用户话语的变化为输入可改善不满意检测，异常检测方法尤为有效。

Conclusion: 用户中心的不满意信号可从对话文本中检测，用户话语本身即含隐性不满线索；建模“话语变化”并结合异常检测是有前景的方向。这为面向真实用户有用的评估技术提供了文本层面的依据。

Abstract: As large language models generate increasingly high-quality responses, recent work on response evaluation has moved beyond response-centered criteria, such as correctness and coherence, toward user-centered criteria, including user preferences and perceived usefulness. This shift is fundamentally important for developing technologies that are truly useful to real-world users. While conventional response evaluation quantifies the quality of the response itself, users' satisfaction in real conversations also depends on their subjective preferences and perceptions of usefulness, which do not necessarily align with response quality. This study investigates whether such user-centered signals can be detected from conversational text. Specifically, we analyze user utterances with self-reported dissatisfaction labels to examine whether user dissatisfaction in text can be detected. In English interaction, models using user utterances exceeded the 17.8% dissatisfaction label prevalence in AUPRC by up to 15 percentage points. These results suggest that user utterances reveal cues to implicit dissatisfaction. Furthermore, changes from the immediately preceding user utterance may improve dissatisfaction detection, with anomaly detection methods appearing particularly effective.

</details>


### [173] [Pterodactyl: Pushing the Limits of Efficient Automated Translation Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14480)
*Evelyn Y. Garland,Carola F. Berger*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍 1020 Group 在 WMT26 自动质量评估共享任务中的参赛系统 Pterodactyl，面向子任务 2（片段级质量分数预测）与子任务 3（无错片段检测），是一种基于 LLM-as-a-judge、以 ROC 分析指导提示工程与模型选择的高可定制、超低成本二分类系统，并针对子任务 3 开发了多个变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向翻译分诊（triage）与数据筛选（data curation）场景，需要一种可高度定制、成本极低的二分类质量评估方案。

Method: 采用 LLM-as-a-judge 框架，通过提示工程与模型选择、并以 ROC 分析作为指导来构建系统，并针对子任务 3 开发多个适配变体。

Result: 摘要未报告具体实验数据或排名结果，仅说明系统在子任务 2 和 3 上提交。

Conclusion: Pterodactyl 为低成本的片段级质量评估与无错片段检测提供了一套可定制方案，体现了以 ROC 分析驱动提示与模型选择的可行性。

Abstract: In this paper, we present 1020 Group's submission to the WMT26 shared task on automated quality evaluation. Our system, Pterodactyl, was engineered specifically for subtasks 2 and 3, that is, segment-level quality score prediction and the detection of error-free segments. Pterodactyl is a highly customizable, ultra-low cost system optimized for binary classification in translation triage and data curation. It is based on an LLM-as-a-judge approach, with prompt engineering and model selection guided by ROC analysis. We also developed several variants of Pterodactyl that were specifically adapted for subtasks 3.

</details>


### [174] [Capacity Allocation at the Source: Sparse Target Optimization for LLM Knowledge Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12995)
*Dongyao Chen,Jiayun Lei,Zhiyuan Ji,Zhiying Deng,Wei Liu,Xiaobo Sun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文关注大语言模型知识编辑中"目标隐状态构造"这一被忽视的上游环节，从理论上建立参数空间干扰与目标隐状态干扰之间的正相关关系，并提出对知识向量施加 ℓ1 正则、剪除冗余"触手"的简单方法，使每次编辑对他知识的干扰更小，在多个LLM与基准上验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有知识编辑方法主要聚焦第二阶段（参数编辑）以保护其他知识，却忽视第一阶段构造理想目标隐状态对模型有限容量空间分配的影响；作者认为目标隐状态的上游构造同样关键，需要探究其与参数干扰的关系并加以优化。

Method: 先理论分析并证明参数空间中的干扰与目标隐状态之间的干扰呈正相关；据此对每个知识向量施加 ℓ1 正则化，剪除其不必要的"触手"分量，仅保留本质成分，从而减少编辑对其它知识的波及，提升多知识共存能力。

Result: 在多个大语言模型与多个基准数据集上的大量实验验证了该方法在减少知识干扰、提升编辑效果方面的有效性（摘要未给出具体数值）；代码已开源。

Conclusion: 目标隐状态的构造是知识编辑中影响容量分配与干扰程度的关键上游因素；通过对知识向量做 ℓ1 稀疏化剪枝即可显著降低跨知识干扰，为知识编辑提供了一条简单而有效的改进路径。

Abstract: Knowledge editing has emerged as a promising approach for efficiently updating embedded knowledge in large language models (LLMs). It first computes an ideal target hidden state that steers the LLM toward the new output, and then edits the model parameters so that the original hidden state is mapped to this target. While most existing methods focus on the second stage of parameter editing in preserving other knowledge, we find that the upstream construction of the target hidden state also plays an important role in allocating the model's limited capacity space. We first establish a theoretical connection between interference in the parameter space and interference between target hidden states, showing that the two are positively correlated. Building on this insight, we propose a simple yet effective $\ell_1$ regularization method that prunes away unnecessary ``tentacles'' of each knowledge vector, retaining only the essential components. With fewer extraneous tentacles, each edit affects less other knowledge, enabling better coexistence. Extensive experiments across multiple LLMs and benchmark datasets verify the effectiveness of our method. Code: https://github.com/wintersdragon-c/STO.

</details>


### [175] [What drives the emergence of symbolic mechanisms in large language models?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13270)
*Melody Zixuan Li,Taylor Webb*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究通过从头训练神经网络并操控架构与数据分布因素，发现数据多样性（而非架构归纳偏置）是符号机制涌现的主要驱动力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作发现大语言模型中存在支持抽象推理的涌现符号机制，但驱动这些机制涌现的因素尚不清楚。

Method: 在抽象序列任务上从头训练神经网络，系统变化架构因素与数据分布因素；采用表征分析、注意力分析和因果中介分析进行检验，并在实体-属性绑定任务上做验证。

Result: 从头训练的Transformer语言模型确实发展出符号机制；在多个数据多样性维度上，多样性越高符号特征越强；相同特征也出现在改造的Transformer架构乃至多层感知机中；在实体-属性绑定任务上观察到一致模式。

Conclusion: 数据多样性而非架构是神经网络中涌现符号计算的主要驱动力，架构归纳偏置并非符号机制发展的必要条件。

Abstract: Recent work has identified a set of emergent symbolic mechanisms that support abstract reasoning in large language models, but it remains unclear what factors drive the emergence of these mechanisms. To address this question, we trained neural networks from scratch on abstract sequence tasks, varying both architectural and data distributional factors, and investigated the effects of these factors on the emergence of symbolic mechanisms. Using representational, attentional, and causal mediation analyses, we first confirmed that transformer-based language models trained from scratch on our task developed symbolic mechanisms. We then found that, across multiple axes of data diversity, greater diversity produced stronger symbolic signatures. Surprisingly, the same signatures emerged in modified transformer architectures and even multilayer perceptrons, indicating that architectural inductive biases are not necessary for the development of symbolic mechanisms. We also observed consistent patterns in an entity-attribute binding task. Together, these findings identify data diversity, not architecture, as the primary driver of emergent symbolic computation in neural networks.

</details>


### [176] [khammoon: From a Linguistic Guideline to a Thai Morpheme Segmenter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13012)
*Warit Sirichotedumrong,Adisai Na-Thalang,Chanakan Wittayasakpan,Kritsadha Phatcharoen,Kunat Pipatanakul*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出以泰语最小意义单位"khammoon"（语素级）替代传统词级分词，消除复合词边界歧义，并证明语素表示在下游任务上不劣于词级基线：制定决策指南（人工一致性κ≈0.99）、用LLM规模化标注并人工纠错，训练1M参数卷积BiLSTM分词器（CPU推理，域外边界F1=0.992），全部资源开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 泰语书写无词间空格，分词是泰语NLP的基础步骤。现有工具主要复现BEST等语料库的词级规范，但泰语"词"缺乏统一语言学共识，这些规范在能产性复合词上表现不佳，产生任意边界并给下游表示带来歧义。

Method: 1) 在语素（khammoon）层面操作，提供显式、一致、无复合歧义的形态表示；2) 将语言学原则形式化为正式决策指南，获得近乎完美的人工标注一致性（κ≈0.99）；3) 用LLM扩展标注，并以人工标签纠正系统性错误；4) 训练轻量级1M参数卷积BiLSTM分词器，面向高效CPU推理。

Result: 下游学习收益显著：基于语素训练的模型在词性标注、命名实体识别和情感分类上达到或超过词级基线；分词器在域外文本上边界F1达0.992。指南、数据集、模型与库均公开。

Conclusion: 语素级切分在无下游性能损失的前提下提升表示质量，说明语言一致性可改善表示；该工作为泰语NLP提供了可复现、可扩展的形态级分析范式与轻量高效工具。

Abstract: Thai is written without spaces between words, making segmentation a foundational step in most Thai NLP pipelines. Existing tools primarily reproduce the word-level conventions of corpora such as BEST; however, because the notion of a "word" in Thai lacks a unified linguistic consensus, these conventions struggle with productive compounding, leading to arbitrary boundaries and ambiguity in downstream representations. Instead, we propose operating at the morphological level, focusing on khammoon, the smallest atomic unit that carries meaning. Segmenting at the morphemic level provides an explicit, consistent morphological representation that eliminates compounding ambiguity. Crucially, this representation benefits downstream learning: models trained on morphemes perform on par with or exceed word-level baselines across part-of-speech tagging, named-entity recognition, and sentiment classification, demonstrating that linguistic consistency improves representation quality without downstream performance penalty. To operationalize this representation, we translate linguistic principles into a formal decision guideline with near-perfect human agreement ($κ\approx 0.99$), scale annotation with an LLM, and correct systematic errors with human labels. Finally, we train khammoon, a lightweight 1M-parameter convolutional BiLSTM segmenter designed for efficient CPU inference, achieving a boundary F1 of 0.992 on out-of-domain text. The guideline, datasets, model, and library are all publicly released.

</details>


### [177] [IntentTwin: Deconfounding Candidate Support and the Oracle-to-Deployment Gap in Offline Web Behavior Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12918)
*Miaobo Hu,Shuhao Hu,Xiaobo Guo,Xin Wang,Bokun Wang,Daren Zha*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文揭示离线Web行为评测中"候选集支持度混杂"：目标OOV而干扰项来自训练支持集时，仅凭支持集归属即可识别正确答案。作者提出支持可识别率(SIR)量化该效应，构建支持均衡候选集，并评测无目标双路提案器；在OPeRA上SIR由1.0000降至0.0000，端到端Exact@1为0.5212。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线基准通常把示范动作插入有限候选集再测重排序，若目标超出训练支持而干扰项均采样自支持集，则"是否在支持集中"这一元信息直接泄露金标动作，使结果无法反映真实行为建模能力，进而误导对部署性能的推断。

Method: 形式化支持可识别率(SIR)指标；构造支持均衡候选集以消除该混杂；提出无目标(target-free)双路提案器，融合支持集检索与开放词表DOM/语言模型提案；并在过滤后的OPeRA、Mind2Web上进行对照评测与200行语义审计（Cohen's κ=0.8173）。

Result: OPeRA(4,282/469/992)上原协议在目标OOV行SIR=1.0000，仅用支持规则的OOV Exact@1亦达1.0000；支持均衡后SIR=0.0000，最强条件重排序器总体Exact@1 0.7214（IV 0.7988，OOV 0.5668）。双路系统Proposal Recall@16 0.6915、端到端Exact@1 0.5212，对比oracle条件Exact@1 0.7450。Mind2Web审计得SIR 0.2380、oracle到proposal差距0.2125。语义审计中0.7150为语义新颖、0.2450为定位符/字符串变体、0.0400歧义。

Conclusion: 候选集支持度构成被忽视的评测混杂，可使离线指标虚高；控制支持集构成与报告候选准确率同等重要。作者建议在离线结果用于部署推断时同时报告SIR并采用支持均衡候选集，以免高估模型泛化能力。

Abstract: Offline web-behavior benchmarks frequently insert the demonstrated action into a finite candidate set and then measure reranking. We show that this protocol can create a candidate-support confound: when the target is outside training support while all distractors are sampled from that support, support membership identifies the gold action without modeling behavior. We formalize this effect with support-identifiability rate (SIR), construct support-balanced candidate sets, and evaluate a target-free dual-path proposer that combines support retrieval with open-vocabulary DOM/LM proposals. On filtered OPeRA (4,282/469/992 train/dev/test steps), the original protocol has SIR 1.0000 on target-OOV rows and a support-only rule reaches 1.0000 OOV Exact@1. Support balancing reduces SIR to 0.0000; the strongest conditional reranker obtains 0.7214 overall Exact@1, 0.7988 on IV rows, and 0.5668 on OOV rows. The target-free dual-path system reaches Proposal Recall@16 0.6915 and end-to-end Exact@1 0.5212, compared with oracle conditional Exact@1 0.7450. On Mind2Web, the same audit yields SIR 0.2380 and an oracle-to-proposal gap of 0.2125. A 200-row semantic audit labels 0.7150 of exact-key OOV targets as semantically novel, 0.2450 as locator/string variation, and 0.0400 as ambiguous (Cohen's $κ=0.8173$). These measurements show why support composition must be controlled alongside candidate accuracy when offline results are used to reason about deployment.

</details>


### [178] [HiCAP: Hierarchical Cultural Adaptation via Personality for Dialogue Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13207)
*Lingzhi Shen,Xiaohao Cai,Yunfei Long,Imran Razzak,Shoaib Jameel*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出HiCAP框架，将人格特质引入文化自适应对话生成，通过人格条件化分层控制机制先从文化基调对齐、再按人格细化语言表达与内容，结合监督微调和指令微调，在CulturalPersonas与CPED上取得最优综合表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对话系统虽关注文化自适应（符合各文化的语言规范与社会期待），但普遍忽视人格特质在塑造文化表达与沟通风格中的作用，文化表达与个体人格的耦合建模不足。

Method: HiCAP采用人格条件化的分层控制机制：先使回复风格与文化基调对齐，再依据个体人格属性细化语言表达与内容；同时融合监督微调与指令微调，配合动态指令模板和多智能体示范，以覆盖文化与人格的多样组合。

Result: 在CulturalPersonas和CPED数据集上，HiCAP在多项对话生成指标上取得最强综合性能，体现出有效的文化适应性与人格建模能力。

Conclusion: 将人格特质纳入文化自适应对话生成是可行且有效的，分层人格条件控制与混合微调策略能够协同提升跨文化的对话表现。

Abstract: Cultural adaptation in dialogue systems refers to the ability to generate responses that conform to linguistic norms and social expectations across different cultures. However, existing dialogue generation approaches pay limited attention to the role of personality in shaping cultural expression and communication style. We therefore propose HiCAP, a novel framework that integrates personality traits into culturally adaptive dialogue generation. HiCAP employs a personality-conditioned control mechanism that hierarchically modulates response style by first aligning it with the cultural tone and then refining linguistic expression and content according to individual personality attributes. To embed these factors, the model combines supervised fine-tuning and instruction-based tuning, supported by dynamic instruction templates and multi-agent demonstrations to capture diverse combinations of culture and personality. Experiments on CulturalPersonas and CPED show that HiCAP achieves the strongest overall performance across various dialogue generation metrics, demonstrating effective cultural adaptability and personality modelling.

</details>


### [179] [Antalia-2 Mini: A 7.6-million-parameter Turkish text-to-speech model trained entirely on synthetic speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13811)
*Sezgin Saygili,Emre Kaplaner,Oncel Ozgul,Fikri San Koktas*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Antalia-2 Mini 是 762 万参数的土耳其语单说话人 TTS 模型，用约 7 倍大的 VoxCPM2 教师蒸馏出约 1,790 h 合成数据训练；在自建评测集上 WER 1.09%、CER 0.22%，与 8.6M 的 EMA Lightning 统计上不可区分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索以极小参数量（7.6M）实现高质量土耳其语 TTS 的可行性，并验证纯合成数据（教师蒸馏）训练路线的有效性与局限，同时提供可复现的公开评测基准。

Method: 字符级输入的单说话人声学模型（7,620,931 参数），输出 48 kHz 音频；训练音频全部来自 VoxCPM2 教师，体量约为学生 300 倍，通过克隆单一合成片段生成数据，自动筛选后保留约 1,790 h；随后用声学模型自身生成的梅尔谱对声码器进行微调（vocoder fine-tuning）以消除声学-声码器域不匹配。

Result: 推理在 Apple M5 笔记本 CPU（2 线程）上达 41× 实时；声码器微调使 Whisper 转写 WER 由均值 3.89% 降至 1.75%（相邻预发布检查点）；在 495 句 Freya-TR-Eval 上（曾用于调参）v1.0.1 取得 WER 1.09%、CER 0.22%；配对 bootstrap 显示与 8.6M 的 EMA Lightning 无显著差异，后者在全频带 UTMOS（英语训练的质量代理指标）上更高。另发现教师缺陷被学生继承：当首词以 ö 开头时教师会破坏片段起始，学生复现该问题，并通过截去开头语音前缀加以掩盖。

Conclusion: 极小参数模型配合纯合成蒸馏数据可在土耳其语上达到与更大模型相当的 ASR 层面可懂度，且 CPU 实时性优异；但评测仅在自建/调参集上进行、缺乏人类听测，且 UTMOS 等质量指标不占优，结论具有明显局限。权重、推理代码与评测框架以 Apache-2.0 公开，训练代码与数据未公开。

Abstract: Antalia-2 Mini is a single-voice Turkish text-to-speech model with 7,620,931 parameters. It reads characters and produces 48 kHz audio at 41 times real time on an Apple M5 laptop CPU (2 threads). Its training audio came only from VoxCPM2 (the teacher), about 300 times larger, cloning one synthetic clip; automatic checks kept about 1,790 h. Fine-tuning the vocoder on the acoustic model's own mel spectrograms cut the word error rate (WER) of Whisper's transcripts from a mean of 3.89% to 1.75% at adjacent pre-release checkpoints. The teacher corrupts the start of a clip when the first word begins with ö, the student learned this, and a spoken prefix we cut from the output hides it. On the 495-sentence Freya-TR-Eval benchmark, where we tuned settings, version 1.0.1 has WER 1.09% and character error rate 0.22% on our public harness. A paired bootstrap cannot distinguish these from EMA Lightning, an 8.6M Turkish model, which scores higher on full-band UTMOS, an English-trained quality proxy. No human listening test exists. Weights, inference code and the benchmark harness are public under Apache-2.0; training code and data are not.

</details>


### [180] [Post-OCR Correction of Mixed-Script Uzbek Text: Byte-Level ByT5 with an Empirical Homoglyph Noise Matrix](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13609)
*Mannon Ochilov,Oybek Narzullayev,Bahodir Zaripov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对乌兹别克语官方/技术文档中因西里尔字母与拉丁字母同形字（homoglyph）导致的OCR错误，提出本地可运行的字节级ByT5纠错系统，将CER从0.0578降至0.0369。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 乌兹别克语正处于文字转写过渡期，同一页面甚至同一句内混排西里尔与拉丁字符，视觉同形字导致大量OCR错误；实测混排页面的同形字错误密度约为单一文字文本的5倍。且工程地质与ICT基础设施报告语料受保密约束不得外传，无法使用云端服务。

Method: 全流程在本地硬件上执行。系统由三部分组成：(1) 字节级ByT5纠错模型；(2) 噪声生成器，其采样权重由359个人工标注的错误事件估计得到，用于合成训练噪声；(3) 基于Levenshtein距离上界的保守解码规则，约束输出偏离程度。

Result: 在5,204对行级样本的Gold集上，字符错误率由0.0578降至0.0369，同形字修复率达91.3%，仅引入84个新错误。

Conclusion: 面向双文字混排场景的字节级建模配合错误分布驱动的噪声生成与距离约束解码，可在完全本地化的条件下有效抑制同形字错误，同时保持低误纠率，适用于敏感语料场景。

Abstract: In Uzbek official and technical documents, a large share of OCR errors is caused not by poor image quality but by characters that are visually identical across the two alphabets in use. Because the script transition is still under way, Cyrillic and Latin appear on the same page and often within a single sentence, and in our measurements the density of homoglyph errors on such pages is close to five times higher than in single-script text. We study this problem in a setting where the corpus of engineering-geological and ICT infrastructure reports may not leave the organisation, so every stage was executed on local hardware. The proposed system combines a byte-level ByT5 model with a noise generator whose sampling weights are estimated from 359 manually classified error events, together with a Levenshtein-bounded conservative decoding rule. On a Gold set of 5,204 line pairs it lowers the character error rate from 0.0578 to 0.0369, restores 91.3% of the homoglyphs and introduces only 84 new errors.

</details>


### [181] [IFRS Compliance Verification: An Open Multi-LLM Benchmark and Content-Conditioned Ensemble Classifier](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13595)
*Aghoghomena Akasukpe,Li Yang,Xinyao Zhou,Chen Shen,Miguel Vargas Martin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出多阶段框架与新基准（19,998条IFRS法规-披露对），通过领域自适应句向量管道与TPE优化的梯度提升树集成分类器融合GPT-4.1、Claude Sonnet 4、Gemini 2.5 Pro，在分歧集上达81.7%加权F1，较最强单模型高10.4分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将企业财务披露与IFRS数百条细粒度条款逐条比对核查是核心审计任务，人工成本高、易错，故引入LLM作为合规助手；但单模型准确率不足以支撑高风险场景，而朴素集成易受各模型系统性偏差影响，可能使聚合准确率低于最强单一标注者。

Method: 构建含19,998条法规-披露对的新基准（11,161条多LLM一致标签 + 8,837条人工标注的分歧样本）；框架将三阶段领域自适应句向量管道（SEC EDGAR文件上的BERT MLM、无监督TSDAE与SimCSE适配、监督对比对齐）与学习型集成分类器结合，后者通过TPE优化的梯度提升树融合冻结嵌入及GPT-4.1、Claude Sonnet 4、Gemini 2.5 Pro的概率特征。

Result: 在分歧集上集成模型加权F1达81.7%，较最强单LLM高10.4个百分点、较多数投票高14.6个百分点，并优于11种无监督聚合基线；检索管道在未见法规上Recall@15为45.3%。

Conclusion: 领域自适应嵌入与学习型异质LLM集成可显著缓解单模型偏差与朴素集成失效问题，提升IFRS合规核查可靠性；数据集、模型检查点、标注平台与分类器实现将公开。

Abstract: Verifying corporate financial disclosures against the International Financial Reporting Standards (IFRS) is a core auditing task in which experts cross-reference disclosures with hundreds of fine-grained regulatory paragraphs: a laborious, costly, and error-prone process that motivates growing interest in large language models (LLMs) as compliance assistants. Yet single-model accuracy remains insufficient for high-stakes use, and naive ensembling is vulnerable to systematic per-model biases that can drag aggregate accuracy below that of the strongest individual annotator. This gap is addressed with a multi-stage framework, supported by a new benchmark of 19,998 IFRS regulation-disclosure pairs comprising 11,161 unanimous multi-LLM labels and 8,837 human annotations on items where the LLMs disagreed. The framework couples a three-stage domain-adapted sentence embedding pipeline (BERT masked language modeling on SEC EDGAR filings, unsupervised TSDAE and SimCSE adaptation, and supervised contrastive alignment) with a learned ensemble classifier that fuses frozen embeddings and LLM probability features from GPT-4.1, Claude Sonnet 4, and Gemini 2.5 Pro via Tree-structured Parzen Estimator (TPE) optimized gradient-boosted trees. On the disagreement set, the ensemble achieves 81.7% weighted F1, exceeding the strongest single LLM by 10.4 points and majority voting by 14.6 points, and outperforms eleven unsupervised aggregation baselines; the retrieval pipeline attains Recall@15 of 45.3% on unseen regulations. The dataset, model checkpoints, annotation platform, and classifier implementation will be publicly released.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [182] [Understanding Mixture-of-Experts Language Models Beyond Expert Boundaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13975)
*Zhenhong Zhou,Mao Zheng,Mingyang Song,Qiankun Li,Kun Wang,Junfeng Fang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出Routing-Aware Magnitude Profiling (RAMP)度量，从神经元层面揭示MoE模型中领域专精计算的组织方式，并据此提出RAMPr剪枝方法，在保持多领域能力的同时显著降低显存并提升吞吐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE专家是否按领域特化存在争议：一方报告专家与领域关联的偏好，另一方将路由主要归因于token身份或句法模式。作者认为仅停留在专家（路由）层面无法澄清分歧，需深入到神经元层面考察领域专精计算的组织。

Method: 提出RAMP（Routing-Aware Magnitude Profiling），一种结合路由权重与神经元幅度的度量以刻画神经元重要性；使用混合领域校准数据识别领域专精神经元集合，并跨多个同领域样本与不同领域计算重叠率；在三个完全开源的MoE系列上追踪预训练各checkpoint与后训练阶段的神经元重要性演化；进一步提出基于RAMP的RAMPr剪枝，跨专家选择神经元以平衡多领域能力。

Result: 同领域样本间领域专精神经元集合重叠率达58-74%，跨领域仅15-24%。预训练checkpoint间神经元重要性持续重组，而专家级重要性排序相对稳定；后训练基本保持神经元排序不变。75%神经元保留率下，RAMPr在六个模型上平均保留91-98%原始性能；50%保留率下减少36-43%驻留GPU显存，批量prefill吞吐提升17-39%。

Conclusion: 领域专精在MoE中真实存在于神经元层面而非专家层面，且其组织在预训练中动态重组、在后训练中趋于固化；神经层面视角可有效指导结构化剪枝，实现显存与吞吐收益而不过度牺牲多领域能力。代码已开源。

Abstract: In Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models, the nature of expert specialization remains controversial. Reports of domain-associated expert preferences contrast with analyses attributing routing primarily to token identity or syntactic patterns. To clarify these differing accounts, we look beyond expert-level routing to investigate the neuron-level organization of domain-specialized computation using Routing-Aware Magnitude Profiling (RAMP), a measure developed for this analysis. Using mixed-domain calibration data, we identify domain-specialized neuron sets with 58-74% overlap across multi-source samples of the same domain, compared with 15-24% across domains. To understand how this organization evolves during training, we trace neuron importance throughout pre-training and post-training across three fully open MoE families. Neuron importance continues to reorganize between pre-training checkpoints despite comparatively stable expert-level importance rankings, while post-training largely preserves neuron rankings. Building on these neuron-level perspectives, we further introduce RAMP-based Pruning (RAMPr) to select neurons across experts for balanced multi-domain capability preservation. At 75% neuron retention, RAMPr preserves average 91-98% of original full performance across six models. At 50% retention, our pruning method can reduce resident GPU memory by 36-43% and improves batched prefill throughput by 17-39% effectively. Our code is available at https://github.com/ydyjya/Neuron-Level-Specialization-in-MoE

</details>


### [183] [TreeAgent: Structure-Aware Memory Agent for Processing Long Hierarchical Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13946)
*Wei Gu,Weiwei Qi,Jiajun Tang,Tianhang Zheng,Bo Chen,Zhibo Zhang,Jinfei Liu,Kui Ren*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TreeAgent 是一种结构感知的记忆型智能体，利用输入的层级结构（如 JSON/HTML/YAML）指导分段与历史信息访问，为四个代表性记忆智能体在 8 个检索与聚合任务上平均提升 3.61%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实用 Web 应用要求智能体处理超出 LLM 上下文窗口的长对话或长文档，现有记忆智能体按固定长度 token 切分输入，不保留结构或层级，对 API 响应、HTML 等结构化数据造成三类问题：同一记录的字段被切断破坏关联；片段缺少层级信息导致结构上下文和位置模糊；逐段记忆更新会丢弃早期同级记录细节，影响后续推理。

Method: TreeAgent 用输入层级同时指导分段式上下文处理与历史信息访问：以完整子树及相邻同级子树组构造片段以保留局部结构关系；给出片段数量的理论保证，用于选择 token 预算以匹配固定长度切分的片段数；为片段附加结构路径以标识祖先与层级位置；并引入从低层子节点片段到高层父节点片段的残差记忆链接，实现跨越顺序更新的直接信息流。

Result: 在 JSON、HTML、YAML、JavaScript 真实应用数据（8K–1M tokens）上，以四种代表性记忆智能体评估八个检索与聚合任务；四个数据集上四类智能体平均得分均提升，平均性能增益 3.61%。

Conclusion: 结构感知的分段与记忆链接机制可有效弥补扁平固定长度切分在结构化长输入上的缺陷，且对多种记忆智能体具有通用增益；理论上的片段数保证使 token 预算选择可与基线切分对齐，便于公平比较与部署。

Abstract: Practical Web applications increasingly require agents to interpret long conversations or documents. For inputs exceeding the context windows of large language models (LLMs), memory agents process segments sequentially and retain past information in bounded memory. However, they typically divide inputs into flat token segments without preserving structure or hierarchy, causing three limitations for structured data such as API responses or HTML documents. First, fixed-length segmentation can split fields of the same record, breaking their relationships. Second, segments lack hierarchical information, obscuring their structural context and positions. Third, successive memory updates may discard details of earlier sibling records needed for reasoning on later segments. To address these limitations, we propose TreeAgent, a structure-aware agent that uses the input hierarchy to guide both segment-wise context processing and access to past information. Specifically, TreeAgent constructs segments from complete subtrees and groups of adjacent sibling subtrees to preserve local structural relationships. We provide theoretical guarantees on segment counts to guide token budget selection toward matching the segment count of fixed-length segmentation. We further attach structural paths to the segments for the agent to identify their ancestors and hierarchical positions. We also introduce residual memory links from the segments of low-level children to the segments of high-level parents, enabling direct information flows beyond successive memory updates. We evaluate TreeAgent with four representative memory agents on eight retrieval and aggregation tasks. The inputs are derived from real-world application data in JSON, HTML, YAML, and JavaScript and range from 8K to 1M tokens. TreeAgent improves the average score across the four datasets for all four agents, with 3.61% performance gain on average.

</details>


### [184] [Decisions by Discrimination: Differential Evidence for Medical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13233)
*Yunpeng Xiao,Yuzhang Xie,Junhao Huang,Carl Yang,Xiao Hu,Kai Shu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出检索增强的"鉴别性证据"框架：为每个选项抽取选项特有的鉴别性证据并组织成简洁提示用于医学选择题作答，在多数据集与多模型上稳定提分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学多选题的核心难点在于干扰项常与正确项共享大量重叠证据，仅靠少数决定性因素区分。现有检索式与推理式方法侧重提供宽泛相关知识或诱导推理路径，未针对"区分易混选项的紧凑鉴别证据"建模，且易引入无关上下文。

Method: 提出检索增强的鉴别性证据框架：为各选项抽取选项特异的鉴别性证据片段，组织为简洁的证据提示，再指导答案选择，使外部知识与鉴别式推理对齐并压缩无关上下文。辅以消融：对比直接使用检索证据块、闭源LLM生成的通用提示、以及答案邻近引导。

Result: 多数据集、多目标LLM上准确率一致提升；消融显示优于直接使用检索证据块与LLM通用提示，且提升不能仅由答案邻近引导解释，与"鉴别证据作为竞争选项推理的紧凑辅助"一致。

Conclusion: 紧凑的鉴别性证据是医学推理的有效辅助信号，为医学问答及其他多候选决策场景提供启示；但方法依赖证据抽取质量与检索覆盖，泛化至开放式临床决策仍需验证。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly studied for clinical decision support, and medical question answering provides a testbed for evaluating this capability. In particular, medical multiple-choice questions (MCQs) reflect a core challenge in clinical decision-making: plausible options often share overlapping evidence, while only a few decisive factors distinguish the correct option from incorrect ones. However, existing retrieval-based and reasoning-oriented methods mainly focus on supplying broadly relevant knowledge or eliciting reasoning paths, rather than identifying the compact differential evidence that separates confusing alternatives. In this work, we present a retrieval-grounded differential evidence framework for medical MCQs. Our method extracts option-specific pieces of differential evidence and organizes them into concise evidence prompts for answer selection. This design better aligns external knowledge with differential reasoning while reducing irrelevant context. Experiments show consistent accuracy gains across multiple datasets and target LLMs. Ablation studies further show that our framework outperforms both directly using retrieved evidence chunks and closed-source LLM-generated general hints, and that its improvements are not solely explained by answer-proximal guidance, but are consistent with differential evidence serving as a compact aid for reasoning over competing alternatives. Our findings also offer insights for medical reasoning and other decision-making settings with multiple possible choices.

</details>


### [185] [Toward Autonomous RTL and Synthesis Optimization: Benchmarking and Self-Improving Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13713)
*Wenji Fang,Yao Lu,Jinghua Wang,Zhiyao Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文提出面向自主RTL与综合优化的三条相互关联路线：真实化基准评测、工具接地（tool-grounded）的RTL优化框架Dr. RTL，以及设计特定综合脚本与算法搜索，并展望AI原生设计自动化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM与自主智能体为硬件设计优化带来新机遇，但现有RTL/综合优化缺乏贴近真实EDA流程的评测基准，且智能体难以与工具链深度耦合、难以复用优化经验。

Method: (1) 构建基于参考与开放式的RTL优化基准，在真实EDA工作流下评估优化空间；(2) 提出多智能体框架Dr. RTL，自动化关键路径分析、RTL重写与实现级评估，并通过可复用优化技能持续进化；(3) 探索智能体驱动的综合脚本调优，以及在中间表示、工艺无关优化与工艺映射中发现可执行综合算法。

Result: 在20个真实RTL设计上，Dr. RTL相较商用综合实现平均WNS提升21%、TNS提升17%，面积减少6%。

Conclusion: 面向AI原生设计自动化，需在验证约束与实现反馈引导下联合探索架构、RTL与EDA算法三个设计空间。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) and autonomous agents are creating new opportunities for hardware design optimization. This paper examines three connected directions toward autonomous RTL and synthesis optimization: (1) Realistic benchmarking. We discuss reference-based and open-ended RTL optimization benchmarks that evaluate optimization opportunities under realistic EDA workflows. (2) Tool-grounded RTL optimization. We present Dr. RTL, a multi-agent framework that automates critical-path analysis, RTL rewriting, and implementation-level evaluation, while continuously improving through reusable optimization skills. Evaluated on 20 real-world RTL designs, Dr.~RTL achieves average WNS/TNS improvements of 21\%/17\% with a 6\% area reduction over commercial synthesis. (3) Design-specific synthesis optimization. We explore agent-driven synthesis script tuning and the discovery of executable synthesis algorithms across intermediate representations, technology-independent optimization, and technology mapping. Finally, we envision AI-native design automation that jointly explores architectural, RTL, and EDA algorithm design spaces, guided by verification constraints and implementation feedback.

</details>


### [186] [Agentic AI Meets Formal Verification in the Era of Agentic Hardware Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13705)
*Wenji Fang,Yuchao Wu,Jing Wang,Zhiyao Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该邀请综述论文探讨AI时代的硬件形式化方法，从"AI赋能形式化"与"形式化保障AI硬件设计"双向视角梳理方法与代表性工作，并展望"证明原生"的硬件开发范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI智能体已能生成并优化硬件电路，但其设计正确性难以保证；同时AI降低了形式化方法的应用门槛。两个趋势交汇，使得"AI与硬件形式化验证如何相互促进"成为亟需系统梳理的问题。

Method: 综述性（invited paper）分析框架，分两条主线：(1) 智能体AI在三个层面推进硬件形式化方法——规约(specification)、证明构造(proof construction)、推理引擎(reasoning engines)；(2) 形式化方法支撑可靠的AI驱动硬件开发——RTL生成、调试、保正确性优化。结合代表性研究与作者近期成果，归纳技术进展与实验洞见。

Result: 给出双向作用的分层化技术图谱，梳理各层面的关键研究进展，并总结作者团队近期工作的实验结果与经验洞见；指出当前方法在规约获取、证明自动化与推理能力上的分工与互补关系。

Conclusion: AI与硬件形式化方法呈现双向赋能关系；未来研究方向指向"证明原生(proof-native)的硬件开发"，即使形式化推理成为自主硬件设计的有机组成部分。

Abstract: As AI agents become increasingly capable of generating and optimizing hardware circuits, ensuring the correctness of AI-generated designs becomes more important. Meanwhile, advances in AI lower the barriers to developing and applying formal methods, creating new opportunities to automate and improve hardware verification. This invited paper presents an overview of the methodologies and applications of hardware formal methods in the AI era. First, we examine how agentic AI advances hardware formal methods across three levels: specification, proof construction, and reasoning engines. Second, we discuss how formal methods support reliable AI-driven hardware development through RTL generation, debugging, and correctness-preserving optimization. We review representative research alongside our recent contributions, highlighting key technical advances and insights from experimental results. Finally, we discuss future research opportunities toward proof-native hardware development, where formal reasoning could become an integral part of autonomous hardware design.

</details>


### [187] [Agent Benchmarks Stop Ranking Before They Run Out of Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13541)
*Zihan Dong,Qishi Zhan,Minxuan Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文指出，智能体基准测试的"饱和"多源于测量方法而非任务本身；作者通过重新评分八个基准，提出用带日期锚点的截断对数比分数（成本作为独立维度）替代单一通过/失败位，以恢复模型间的区分度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据科学与ML工程智能体（如MLE-bench、ALE-Bench、SWE-fficiency、AlgoTune）的任务产出的是一个量（Kaggle指标、竞赛分数或加速比），但当各模型难以区分时，惯常做法是构造更难的版本。作者认为这种饱和很大程度上来自测量方式：多数基准把一次运行压缩为一个通过/失败比特，存在"过于粗糙"和"以参考为上限"两个缺陷，因此需改进评分而非单纯加难任务。

Method: 对八个基准（含SWE-bench Verified、Terminal-Bench以增加广度）的已发布运行结果进行重新评分；分析通过/失败二值化的两类失效：(1)分辨率过粗——SWE-bench Verified前十名之间的差异小于测试误差，纠错后邻居重排幅度不超过随机删除每个智能体六分之一通过样本的影响；(2)以参考为上限——ALE-Bench前十按竞赛分数区分，但以中位人类为参照评分时并列，尽管近半数运行超越该人类。在此基础上推导带日期锚点、并以成本为独立轴的截断对数比分数，并在Terminal-Bench重跑中验证。

Result: 在SWE-bench Verified上，相邻前十智能体的差距小于测试误差，去除误差后重排幅度与随机删除约1/6通过样本相当；ALE-Bench前十虽按竞赛分数有区分，但对中位人类评分时出现并列，而近半运行超过该中位人类；在Terminal-Bench重跑中，通过/失败并列的模型在测试所丢弃的量上相差十倍以上。可见固定任务上提高门槛只是转移问题，分级评分才能修复。

Conclusion: 智能体基准的饱和主要归因于测量粒度过粗和以参考为上限，而非任务本身过易；采用带日期锚点的截断对数比分数并将成本作为独立维度进行分级评分，可恢复模型间的区分度，优于单纯提高任务难度。

Abstract: Data-science and ML-engineering agents are evaluated on benchmarks such as MLE-bench, ALE-Bench, SWE-fficiency and AlgoTune, whose tasks yield a quantity (a Kaggle metric, contest score or speedup). When one stops separating the best models, the usual remedy is a harder version. Re-scoring released runs of eight benchmarks, including SWE-bench Verified and Terminal-Bench for breadth, we find much of this saturation lies in the measurement, not the tasks. Most agent benchmarks reduce a run to one pass/fail bit, which fails in two ways. It is too coarse: neighbouring top-ten agents on SWE-bench Verified differ by less than its tests' errors, and correcting them reorders neighbours no more than deleting a random sixth of each agent's passes. It is capped at the reference: ALE-Bench's top ten, separated by contest score, tie when graded against the median human, although nearly half the runs beat that human. On fixed tasks, a higher bar only moves the problem; graded credit fixes it. We derive a clipped log-ratio score with dated anchors and cost as a separate axis. In a Terminal-Bench re-run, pass/fail-tied models differ over tenfold on the quantity the tests discard.

</details>


### [188] [Seeing Is Not Believing: Counterfactual Lesion Erasure Teaches a Small Medical Vision-Language Agent to Report Only What the Image Shows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13006)
*Bhaskar Gurram*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出"证据敏感性差距"(ESG)指标，用无标注的反事实视图量化VLM是否在"看图"而非"读题回答"；基于此训练的MedScope-4B在内部集上ESG显著优于前沿模型，但不提升真实病变感知，外部泛化与罕见危重发现（气胸）仍存在严重短板。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学视觉语言模型常因问题中提到了某病变就报告该病变（提示词先验/谄媚），而标准基准无法区分"基于图像证据作答"与"基于问题措辞作答"。作者需要一个无需人工标注、能直接测量模型对图像证据敏感度的度量，并检验能否通过训练赋予小模型"证据门控"的作答倾向。

Method: 从公开分割数据集构建三类反事实视图（inpainting擦除病变、其他位置同形状控制编辑、无病变原图），无需人工标注。定义ESG = 擦除后回答"未见/未提及"的比率 − 控制编辑后的该比率；忽略图像或对两种编辑同等反应的模型ESG为0。训练MedScope-4B（Qwen3.5-4B + LoRA + 图像工具），数据为标签衍生的轨迹，按预注册规则选型，单次测试对比9个前沿VLM与6个开源VLM，并用4项预注册检验探查机制（含消融：去掉反事实视图、跨模型复制、未见inpainting工具、外部中心测试）。

Result: 内部507例、5种成像类型：MedScope-4B ESG 0.758，仅9.6%案例仍在擦除后声称病变存在；前沿模型ESG 0.27–0.47、41–62%仍声称存在（均p<0.001），且真实病变准确率无显著下降。去掉反事实视图后ESG降至0.279±0.107（3个种子）。在MedGemma 1.5 4B与两种未见inpainting工具上复制成功。四个外部中心优势保持，但前沿模型检出更多真实病变；全新外部测试中MedScope-4B对48例气胸检出0例，净效用低于gemini-4-argon（−0.36 [−0.50, −0.23]）。机制检验：匹配控制编辑并非必要；3个种子上未确立相对removal-based DPO的全新外部ESG优势；配对视图一致性目标未胜过等算力对照，但该续训本身在每个种子上都降低了未见工具上的擦除后误称（代价之一为灵敏度下降）。

Conclusion: 用擦除与编辑图像训练可让4B级小模型获得前沿模型仅靠提示无法获得的"证据门控"作答倾向，但该倾向不等于感知能力提升：外部验证中真阳性检出与罕见危重发现（如气胸）覆盖不足，净临床效用可能为负。ESG应作为诊断性指标而非能力替代，罕见关键病变需专门的覆盖策略。

Abstract: Medical vision-language models (VLMs) often report a finding because the question names it; standard benchmarks miss this. From public segmentation datasets we build counterfactual views without manual annotation: the lesion erased by inpainting, a same-shape control edit elsewhere, and unedited lesion-free images. The evidence sensitivity gap (ESG) is the rate of absent-or-refer answers after erasure minus the rate after the control edit; it is zero for a model that ignores the image or reacts to both edits alike. MedScope-4B (Qwen3.5-4B, LoRA, image tools) was trained on label-derived trajectories, selected by a pre-registered rule and tested once against nine frontier and six open VLMs; four pre-registered checks then probed the mechanism. On 507 internal cases across five imaging types, MedScope-4B reached ESG 0.758 and claimed an erased lesion in 9.6% of cases, against 0.27-0.47 and 41-62% for the frontier models (all p<0.001), without significant loss of accuracy on real lesions. Without counterfactual views ESG fell to 0.279+-0.107 (three seeds); the effect replicated on MedGemma 1.5 4B and two unseen inpainting tools. At four external centres the advantage held but frontier models detected more real lesions; on a fresh external test MedScope-4B detected 0 of 48 pneumothorax films, with net utility below gemini-4-argon's (-0.36 [-0.50, -0.23]). Matching the control edit was not shown to matter; over three seeds no fresh-external ESG advantage over removal-based DPO was established; a paired-view consistency objective did not beat a matched-compute control, while that continuation alone cut erased-lesion claims on unseen tools at every seed, at a cost in sensitivity at one. Training on erased and edited images gives a small model an evidence-gated disposition that prompting does not give frontier models; it does not improve perception, and rare critical findings need coverage.

</details>


### [189] [ZeroPoint: Lightweight Energy-Based Decision Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12992)
*Javier Marín*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ZeroPoint 将"空答案"设为能量恒为零的固定状态，为各选项能量提供显式参考，使"选择或弃答"成为常规推断的一部分，并用 NLL 损失统一训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准 EBM 只定义相对兼容性：对全部能量加同一输入相关常数不改变预测、Gibbs 分布和损失，因此模型缺乏判断"是否有任何选项可接受"的绝对参考水平，弃答需依赖额外拒绝规则。

Method: 引入能量恒为零的空答案状态；选项的非负能量深度相对空答案度量兼容性；选项与空答案共享同一分布，零深度选项概率为零；用负对数似然统一训练选择与弃答；能量函数为小型多语言编码器，同时读入输入与全部选项并对选项顺序保持不变。

Result: 消融显示收益主要来自"无选项适配"样本的训练；在相同编码器与数据下，固定零点对"有无可接受选项"的区分略优于可学习拒绝类，明显优于阈值化 softmax，且不改变选项间的选择。

Conclusion: 以固定零能量空答案作为显式参考，可用统一推断与 NLL 训练同时完成选择与弃答，避免独立拒绝规则，性能优于拒绝类与阈值化 softmax 基线。

Abstract: Energy-based models (EBMs) assign a scalar energy to every joint value of the observed and predicted variables. In decision models, the prediction is chosen from a finite list of options provided with each input. Inference then reduces to comparing the energies of the options, and training lowers the energy of the correct option relative to the others. A complete decision must also account for the possibility that no option is acceptable. However, the energies learned by a standard EBM define only relative compatibility: adding the same input-dependent quantity to all energies leaves the predictions, the Gibbs distribution, and the usual training losses unchanged. The model therefore has no learned reference level against which to decide whether any option fits. We propose ZeroPoint, an EBM that makes the empty answer a state of the model with energy zero for every input. This fixed state provides an explicit reference for the energies of all listed options, whose non-negative depths measure their compatibility with the input relative to the empty answer. The options and the empty answer share one distribution, in which an option at zero depth receives no probability. The decision to select an option or to abstain is thus part of ordinary inference, trained with the negative log-likelihood loss, rather than a separate rejection rule. The energy function is a small multilingual encoder that reads the input together with all the options and is invariant to their order. Ablations show that most of the benefit comes from training with examples in which no option fits. With the same encoder and data, the fixed zero separates inputs with and without an acceptable option slightly better than a learned rejection class and clearly better than a thresholded softmax, while leaving the choice among options unchanged.

</details>


### [190] [IIT Patna at "ICPR 2026 TVRID Competition on Privacy-Preserving Person Re-Identification from Top-View RGB-Depth Camera"](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12960)
*Abhinav Kumar,Swagatam Pati,Advait Deshmukh,Aditya Mehrotra,Bibek Das,Kumari Priya,Utsav Kumar Nareti,Saba Akram,Chandranath Adak*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文报告ICPR 2026 TVRID竞赛（俯视深度相机隐私保护行人重识别）的赛题设置，并介绍两支IIT Patna前十队伍的方法与成绩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在俯视相机场景下用人体测量形状替代正面外观、并以深度模态进一步削弱颜色身份线索，以实现隐私保护的行人重识别。

Method: 基于4台同步Intel RealSense D455（RGB+Depth）采集的86个身份数据，涵盖平地、上坡、下坡与斜视角，评估同相机进出、上下坡、平-斜视角三类跨场景；方法1与2分别针对Depth与RGB/Depth提出表示与监督策略。

Result: 方法1在Depth上达93.51% mAP / 90.42% CMC@1（28队中第4）；方法2在RGB上99.63%/99.35%、Depth上86.26%/80.34%；'vivek katuri'在RGB赛道为77.67% CMC@1 / 85.20% mAP。

Conclusion: 两方法显著优于57.19%的Depth赛道平均mAP，方法1较方法2高7.3个百分点，表明深度表示与监督对隐私保护Re-ID至关重要。

Abstract: Privacy-aware person re-identification (Re-ID) from top-view cameras replaces frontal appearance with anthropometric shape, while depth sensing further reduces color-based identity cues. The ICPR 2026 TVRID competition evaluates this setting using 86 identities captured by four synchronized Intel RealSense D455 cameras with RGB and Depth streams across flat, ascent, descent, and oblique viewpoints. Performance is measured using mAP and CMC@1 across within-camera in/out, ascent-descent, and flat-oblique scenarios. The competition had 147 registered participants and 33 final submissions. Three teams from IIT Patna participated: 'abhinavkumar03' and 'sjkdgfhl_' ranked 5th and 7th among 31 ranked participants, and 'vivek katuri' reached 77.67% CMC@1 / 85.20% mAP on the RGB track. This paper presents the methods of the two top-10 teams. Methodology 1 (abhinavkumar03) achieves 93.51% mAP and 90.42% CMC@1 on Depth, ranking 4th of 28, while Methodology 2 (sjkdgfhl_) reaches 99.63%/99.35% on RGB and 86.26%/80.34% on Depth. Both substantially outperform the 57.19% track-average Depth mAP, with Methodology 1 yielding a 7.3-point gain over Methodology 2. These results demonstrate the importance of depth representation and supervision for privacy-preserving Re-ID.

</details>


### [191] [Monitoring One Channel Is Not Communication Control: Causal Evidence for Adaptive Reallocation of Joint Message--Route Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12959)
*Liu Hung Ming,Tian Xia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在端到端博弈中，监控可见信道会改变智能体学习到的通信协议与路由策略，但不会消除通信本身的功能价值：部分覆盖导致选择性的路由迁移，冻结干预证明路由具有语义性，而完全覆盖去除了迁移却保留了通信。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望厘清一个关键区分：监控可见信道究竟"消除通信的用处"，还是仅"改变通信的形态"。现有工作常把二者混同，因此需要在无通信监督的端到端设定下，分离"协议是否仍有效"与"信道是否被迁移"这两个问题。

Method: 构建发送者—接收者端到端博弈，双方在无通信监督下同时学习离散协议与路由策略。设置无监控、在线部分覆盖、完全覆盖三类条件，各跑两个独立的20种子批次；采用冻结消息与联合消息—路由打乱等干预检验路由的语义性；并引入修正后的显式静默代价检验严格完全抑制闸门。

Result: 协议形成率高（20/20 与 19/20）。在线部分覆盖相对无监控使影子路由份额提升 .701 [.574,.826] 与 .575 [.412,.737]，而任务准确率维持在 .905/.913，冻结消息的带符号贡献为 .592/.563。完全覆盖消除了大部分选择性迁移但不消除通信。冻结干预显示联合打乱改变 .668 的接收者预测，仅保留路由仍达 .388 准确率（机会水平 .25）。修正静默代价使 18/20 策略完全静默、平均发送降至 .050，但严格完全抑制闸门因两个种子仍保留通信而未通过；语义规避与操作性闸门亦失败。

Conclusion: 结果支持"部分监控下联合消息—路由编码的自适应重分配"，而不支持开放世界中的路由发现、涌现加密或普适防御。监控改变通信形态而非摧毁其功能。

Abstract: Monitoring a visible channel can change how learned agents communicate without removing why communication is useful. We test this distinction in an end-to-end game where a sender and receiver learn a discrete protocol and route policy without communication supervision. In two independent 20-seed batches, 20/20 and 19/20 protocols form. Online partial coverage increases shadow-route share over no monitoring by .701 [.574,.826] and .575 [.412,.737], while task accuracy remains .905/.913 and signed frozen message contribution remains .592/.563. Complete coverage removes most selective migration without removing communication. Frozen interventions, including a fresh pre-outcome confirmation, show that route is semantic: joint message--route shuffling changes .668 of receiver predictions and route alone retains .388 accuracy versus .25 chance. A corrected explicit-silence cost makes 18/20 policies fully silent and lowers mean sending to .050, but its strict complete-suppression gate fails because two seeds retain communication. Failed semantic-evasion and operational gates further bound the result. The evidence supports adaptive reallocation of a joint message--route code under partial monitoring, not open-world route discovery, emergent encryption, or a universal defense.

</details>


### [192] [SetPrint: Black-Box LLM Fingerprinting via Setwise Correspondence of Response Changes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12950)
*Jinmo Han,Ju Yeon Kang,Jimin Hong,Sunghwan Ahn,Yonghyeon Jun,Nam Soo Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SetPrint：一种免训练的黑盒LLM指纹方法，同时利用扰动响应变化的集合内关系与集合间对应关系，在LeaFBench上稳定优于现有免训练方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑盒指纹需仅凭输出判断目标模型是否派生自源模型；扰动查询可得两组响应变化，蕴含集合内关系与集合间对应两类互补信息，但现有方法未充分利用二者。

Method: 训练无关方法SetPrint：将每个响应变化表示为其相对本集合平均变化的偏离，再比较两模型在同一扰动下产生的变化，从而联合建模集合内关系与集合间对应关系。

Result: 在LeaFBench上，SetPrint一致优于现有免训练黑盒指纹方法；消融分析表明性能提升同时来自集合内关系与集合间对应关系两方面。

Conclusion: 联合利用扰动响应变化的集合内与集合间两类信息可有效提升黑盒LLM指纹识别效果，且无需额外训练。

Abstract: Black-box LLM fingerprinting aims to determine whether a target model was derived from a source model when only the output responses to input queries are observable. One way to reveal this relationship is to perturb each query and observe the resulting responses of both models, yielding two corresponding sets of response variations. These sets provide two complementary sources of information: the relations among response changes within each set, and the correspondence of response changes between the two sets. Existing approaches, however, have not fully exploited both. We introduce \textbf{SetPrint}, a training-free method that jointly captures both within-set relations and between-set correspondence. SetPrint expresses each response change relative to the mean change of its own set, and then compares changes from the two models that were induced by the same perturbation. On LeaFBench, SetPrint consistently outperforms existing training-free black-box fingerprinting methods. Further analyses attribute these gains to both within-set relations and between-set correspondence.

</details>


### [193] [Recurrent Modular Structure in Spiking Neural Network Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14262)
*Vikram Ramavarapu,Himanshu Udupi,Ashish Gautam*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出社区循环脉冲神经网络（CoRe SNNs），将循环网络划分为具有固定角色的社区（控制器社区、各动作社区、隐状态社区），动作由脉冲"游走"所到达的社区决定；学习全为局部三因子规则。作者比较三种模块化获得方式：新规则 Map-STDP 驱动的动态模块化、SBM 初始化的部分模块化、sealing 强制的完全模块化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物神经回路既模块化又循环，存在稠密社区并以稀疏连接相连；而用于强化学习的脉冲神经网络（SNN）多为前馈结构，尚不清楚如何在循环 SNN 中获得功能模块、以及模块带来何种收益。

Method: 构建 CoRe SNN：循环网络划分为固定角色的社区，控制器社区接收刺激，每个动作对应独立社区，隐社区承载内部状态，脉冲活动表现为社区间的游走，到达社区即动作。学习为局部三因子规则。三种模块化机制对比：(1) 动态模块化——提出 Map-STDP 新学习规则在训练中促进模块结构；(2) 部分模块化——随机块模型（SBM）初始化部分施加模块性；(3) 完全模块化——sealing 完全强制。

Result: 在 CartPole 上、跨三种脉冲神经元模型：Map-STDP 相对无该规则的同一网络提升学习效果，并在三种模型中的两种上匹敌完全模块化（sealed 社区），且使观测到的脉冲游走更可压缩。将 TD-STDP actor-critic 的前馈 actor 替换为循环动作社区后，在 CartPole 与 LunarLander 上优于前馈网络，在 Acrobot 上与之持平。模块化强度依赖任务：完全模块化在 Acrobot 上收益最大，而 LunarLander 上动态、部分与完全三种机制表现相近。

Conclusion: 循环模块化网络可匹敌甚至超越前馈方法，同时在生物合理性与能效方面有所提升；模块化施加强度应按任务选择。循环社区比前馈 actor 更接近皮层微回路。

Abstract: Biological neural circuits are both modular and recurrent, with dense communities sparsely linked to one another. Spiking neural networks (SNNs) for reinforcement learning are usually feed-forward, and it is unclear how to obtain functional modules in a recurrent SNN or what they buy. In this work, we introduce a class of spiking networks, called Community Recurrent SNNs (CoRe SNNs): recurrent networks partitioned into communities with fixed roles. A controller community receives the stimulus, each action has its own community, and latent communities carry internal state. Spiking activity is then a walk between communities, and the action is the community the walk reaches. All learning is local and three-factor, and the recurrent communities are closer to cortical microcircuits than a feed-forward actor. We compare three regimes for obtaining the communities: dynamic modularity, in which a new learning rule we introduce, Map-STDP, encourages modular structure during learning; partial modularity, in which a stochastic block model (SBM) initialization enforces it partly; and full modularity, in which sealing enforces it fully. On CartPole, across three spiking neuron models, Map-STDP improves learning over the same network without it matches full modularity (sealed communities) on two of the three models, and makes the observed spike walk more compressible. Replacing the feed-forward actor of a TD-STDP actor-critic with recurrent action communities improves on the feed-forward network on CartPole and LunarLander, and matches it on Acrobot. How strongly to enforce modularity depends on the task: full modularity helps most on Acrobot, while on LunarLander the dynamic, partial and full regimes perform alike. Overall, these recurrent modular networks can match or even improve upon feed-forward approaches, with improvements in bio-plausibility and energy efficiency.

</details>


### [194] [When Should Agents Remember? Falsification-Gated Self-Evolution for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14326)
*Runxuan Tang,Haoyu Gao,Yuyan Ding,Junyi Yao,Zihao Zheng,Zecheng Sheng,Liwei Hou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Falsification-Gated Self-Evolution（FGSE）框架，将经验教训建模为带前置条件、行为改变、预期效果与验证器的结构化假设，先在临时状态中经目标迁移、证伪、归档回归三类探针检验，再按收益与风险决定提交、修正或拒绝，从而提升LLM智能体长期自进化的记忆更新可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自进化机制常将单回合经验教训直接写入持久记忆，导致局部成功成为不可靠信号，难以保证知识的可复用性与安全性；核心问题是何时应允许经验驱动的行为更新进入智能体持久状态。

Method: FGSE以验证器为根基：每条候选教训表示为含前置条件、行为改变、预期效果和验证器的结构化假设；仅在临时状态中应用该假设，并通过目标迁移探针、证伪探针和归档回归探针进行检验，最后依据实测增益与风险执行提交、修正或拒绝。

Result: 在Web、App、工具使用与长期记忆类基准上表现强劲：WebArena成功率39.8，AppWorld平均完成度78.8，τ-Retail/τ-Airline的Pass^1分别为0.724/0.487；同时将证伪失败率降至5.0%、回归损害降至1.3%、有害已提交更新降至3.6%。

Conclusion: 将记忆更新作为假设进行检验，可支撑更可靠的长期适应，说明证伪门控机制能有效过滤不可靠的局部经验，兼顾任务性能与更新安全性。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly operate in long-horizon, verifier-rich environments, but current self-improvement mechanisms often write episode-derived lessons directly into memory, making local success an unreliable signal for reusable and safe knowledge. This paper addresses the core question of when an experience-derived behavioral update should be admitted into persistent agent state. We propose Falsification-Gated Self-Evolution (FGSE), a verifier-grounded framework that represents each candidate lesson as a structured hypothesis with an explicit precondition, behavior change, expected effect, and verifier. FGSE applies the hypothesis only in a temporary state, tests it on target-transfer, falsification, and archived-regression probes, and then commits, refines, or rejects it according to measured gain and risk. Across web, app, tool-use, and long-term memory benchmarks, FGSE achieves strong task performance, including 39.8 WebArena success rate, 78.8 AppWorld average completion, and 0.724/0.487 Pass$^1$ on $τ$-Retail/$τ$-Airline, while reducing falsification failures to 5.0%, regression damage to 1.3%, and harmful committed updates to 3.6%. These results suggest that testing memory updates as hypotheses supports more reliable long-term adaptation.

</details>


### [195] [Precision on Demand: Escalating Weight Bits at Uncertain Decoding Steps in a Small Local Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14418)
*Mohammed Nayeem Khan,Logesh L,Aliyas Ahmad Misri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种按需升级的混合精度解码方案：默认用8bit权重的top-4bit解码，仅当低精度输出分布不确定（熵超阈值或top-2 margin过小）时以8bit重算该步；在Qwen3-0.6B与GSM8K上，熵门控对argmax翻转的判别AUC达0.93，16%升级率下得62.7%，占全高精度权重的0.87字节开销，但150题实测尚无法与全高/全低显著区分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有局部解码通常对整段生成为全部权重固定单一精度，缺乏按需分配的机制。作者希望探索"8bit存储、4bit解码、仅在高不确定步回退到8bit"的精度分配策略，以在减少权重读取字节的同时尽量保持精度。

Method: 模拟量化，模型为Qwen3-0.6B，基准为GSM8K；成本以每token读取的权重字节计。门控信号为低精度输出分布的熵（阈值）或top-2 margin。先用36724个重放步评估门控对argmax翻转的判别能力（AUC与在给定升级预算下的召回），再做150题实测评测，比较全高、全低、熵门控三种配置，并做配对检验（McNemar与配对差值CI）。

Result: 重放36724步中，低精度熵对argmax翻转的判别AUC=0.93；15%升级预算下熵门控捕获78%翻转、margin门控94%，而等比例随机门控的期望仅15%（为计算期望，非实跑）。150题实测：全高64.0%、全低55.3%（McNemar p=0.053）；熵门控升级率16%，得62.7%，字节数为全高的0.87。配对差：相对全高-1.3（-8.0~+5.3），相对全低+7.3（0.0~+14.7，p=0.080），数据无法将门控与任一端区分开。相同成本模型下"verified speculation"在相近字节下可达零KL。

Conclusion: 门控信号在离线重放中对argmax翻转有很强的判别力，但在150题的实际评测中样本量不足以给出统计上可分辨的增益，门控效果仍未确证；同时同成本模型下存在可零KL的替代方案，削弱了该方法的必要性。作者明确未运行的项目包括：实际随机门控对照、thinking模式的阶段规则、第二个模型以及实测KL。

Abstract: Local decoding usually fixes one weight precision for the whole generation. We study a different allocation: store 8-bit weights, decode with their top 4 bits, and redo a step at 8 bits only when the low-precision output distribution looks uncertain (entropy above a threshold or a small top-two margin). Quantization is simulated, the model is Qwen3-0.6B, and the benchmark is GSM8K. Cost is counted in weight bytes read per token. On 36724 replayed steps the entropy of the low pass ranks argmax flips with AUC 0.93. At a 15% escalation budget it catches 78% of flips and the margin gate 94%, against the 15% expected for a random gate of equal rate (a computed expectation, not a run). In 150 live problems all-high scores 64.0% and all-low 55.3% (McNemar p=0.053). An entropy gate with a 16% escalation rate scores 62.7% at 0.87 of the all-high weight bytes. Its paired difference is -1.3 (-8.0 to +5.3) points to all-high and +7.3 (+0.0 to +14.7) points to all-low (p=0.080), so the live data do not separate the gate from either end. Verified speculation reaches zero KL at similar bytes in the same cost model. A live random control, a thinking-mode phase rule, a second model and live KL were not run.

</details>


### [196] [Agent Architecture Cost-Success Trade-offs: A Controlled Evaluation across Enterprise-Style SOPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12953)
*Subhrangshu Nandi,Hitesh Arora,Yunfei Bai,Udita Patel,Chirag Shah,Hannah Kwon,Wei Zhang,Weian Chen,Chloe Ruoqi Li,Alberto Sconfienza,Ayush Goyal,Yasmine Lee,Ming Xiong,Prathap Ramachandra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在模型、任务与工具固定的受控条件下比较四种智能体架构（8 个实验臂）于 13 个企业 SOP 上的表现：不存在普适最优架构，架构选择依赖 SOP；额外架构成本必须由任务成功率增益来证明其合理性；实现质量比单/多智能体选择更重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业日益采用大模型智能体执行长程、分支化、工具密集型标准作业流程（SOP），但缺乏在选择竞争性智能体架构时可依据的受控证据，因此需要固定模型、任务与工具、仅变动架构来隔离架构本身的影响。

Method: 控制变量实验：固定模型、任务与工具，比较四种架构——函数调用式单智能体、技能增强智能体、集中式多智能体编排、分诊式群体（共 8 个实验臂），覆盖 13 个企业 SOP；同时提出技能定义质量评分量表，用于构建高质量与低质量相匹配的技能库。

Result: (1) 任务成功率跨度极大：最难 SOP 低于 10%，最易接近 100%；聚合层面无架构具统计可分辨性，增加结构仅在复杂分支 SOP 上呈现方向性（尚不显著）收益。(2) 所有协调型实验臂（多智能体或技能增强）消耗 1.4–4.7 倍 token、2.6–5.8 倍延迟，增益通常微小且少显著，单智能体为高效默认；高质量技能将成功率由 67.4% 提升至 73.1%，劣化技能则低于该基线。(3) 合理实现的多智能体方案不如单智能体，但经针对性重构后成为唯一显著超越基线的实验臂（75.6% vs. 67.4%）。

Conclusion: 架构选择应随 SOP 特性而定，而非追求普适最优；引入多智能体或技能等额外架构需以可观测的任务成功率增益为代价依据，否则单智能体是效率默认；实现质量（同一架构的不同工程实现）对最终性能的影响大于单智能体与多智能体的范式选择。

Abstract: Enterprises increasingly use large language model agents to execute standard operating procedures (SOPs): long, branching, tool-intensive workflows. Yet there is little controlled evidence for choosing among competing agentic architectures. Holding the model, tasks, and tools fixed and varying only the architecture, we compare four designs (a function-calling single agent, a skill-augmented agent, centralized multi-agent orchestration, and a triage swarm; eight arms) across 13 enterprise SOPs, and introduce a skill-definition quality rubric to build matched high and low-quality skill corpora. Three takeaways emerge. (1)~\textbf{There is no universal best architecture; the right choice is SOP-dependent.} On a given SOP, task success ranges from under $10\%$ on the hardest to near $100\%$ on the easiest; no architecture is statistically separable in aggregate, and added structure only plausibly pays off on complex, branching SOPs (a directional, not yet significant, trend). (2)~\textbf{Added architecture cost must be justified by a task-success gain.} Every coordinated arm (multi-agent or skill-augmented) spends $1.4$--$4.7\times$ the tokens and $2.6$--$5.8\times$ the latency of the single agent for gains that are usually small and rarely significant, making the single agent the efficient default; high-quality skills do lift success (to $73.1\%$ from $67.4\%$) while degraded skills fall below it. (3)~\textbf{Implementation quality outweighs the single- versus multi-agent choice.} A plausible multi-agent implementation loses to the single agent, yet a targeted redesign of it becomes the only arm to significantly beat the baseline ($75.6\%$ vs.\ $67.4\%$).

</details>


### [197] [The WoW: A Large-Scale World Knowledge Corpus for Full-Lifecycle LLM Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13487)
*Tianze Sun,Yongyao Duan,Sam Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 WoW（World Knowledge），一个面向基础模型全周期训练的大规模中文教育数据集，以教育知识图谱为分类骨架，系统性采集并补全各学段缺失模块，规模更大、质量更高且含多模态信号；实验显示其在多类基准上显著提升模型性能，并提供基于图谱的交互式数据选择与检索平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有中文教育语料存在覆盖碎片化、缺乏系统性组织的问题，难以支撑基础模型全周期训练的数据需求。

Method: 以教育知识图谱作为分类骨架（taxonomic backbone），系统化收集数据并跨学段补全缺失的教育模块，构建大规模数据集；相较此前版本增强系统性、提升质量、扩大规模，并引入多模态信号，覆盖模型开发的各个阶段；同时提供基于图谱的交互式数据选择与检索平台。

Result: 实验表明 WoW 在多种基准（diverse benchmarks）上显著提升模型性能。

Conclusion: 知识图谱驱动的构建范式可有效提升教育语料的系统性与覆盖完整性，使数据集同时服务于训练与检索研究需求，为社区研究提供可交互的数据基础设施。

Abstract: Existing Chinese educational corpora often suffer from fragmented coverage and a lack of systematic organization. In this paper, we propose WoW (\textbf{Wo}rld Kno\textbf{w}ledge), a large-scale dataset designed for the full-cycle training of foundation models. The construction of this dataset is driven by an Educational Knowledge Graph, which serves as a taxonomic backbone for the systematic collection and completion of missing educational modules across all levels. Compared to previous versions, this iteration provides enhanced systematicity, superior quality, and a larger scale, while incorporating multimodal signals and supporting every stage of model development. Experiments demonstrate that WoW significantly improves model performance across diverse benchmarks. To facilitate community research, we further provide an interactive platform for graph-based data selection and retrieval.

</details>


### [198] [A Cross-Scenario Survey and Analysis of Self-Evolving Multimodal Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14256)
*Jingjing Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该综述系统考察多模态智能体自我改进机制在四类场景间迁移的不对称性，提出以自指差异和“经验生成—反馈获取—更新固化”三阶段循环为分析框架，将各机制重构为以任务结构性资源换取稀缺因素的权衡，并给出可迁移性判定与可证伪的测量方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态智能体自改进机制均在单一场景内设计与评估，其前提条件在跨场景迁移下未被验证，导致机制迁移呈现不对称现象，缺乏统一的结构性解释。

Method: 1）以四条准则与自指差异形式化“自进化多模态智能体”；2）采用经验生成、反馈获取、更新固化三阶段自进化循环作为分析框架，沿“rollout次数上限”和“反馈保真度”两个维度定位四类场景（多模态推理、多模态生成、GUI与Web导航、具身操作）；3）在每个场景内对比自进化循环与固定循环，区分场景固有局限与设计内在局限；4）将每个机制重构为“以任务结构性资源（如输出等价性、可检验结果）换取稀缺因素”的权衡，并判定其在跨场景下的可移植、有条件、受阻或冗余状态。

Result: 发现迁移不对称源于相反迁移方向上稀缺因素与结构性资源组合的差异；同时识别出能够推翻当前尚未检验判定的“匹配预算”测量方式。

Conclusion: 机制跨场景迁移的不对称具有结构性根源，可由稀缺因素—结构性资源的组合差异解释；现有迁移性判定仍属未检验结论，需通过匹配预算的对照测量加以验证或推翻。

Abstract: Recent work shows that multimodal agents increasingly self-improve without recurring human annotation. However, each self-improvement mechanism is designed and evaluated within a single scenario, leaving its preconditions unverified under cross-scenario transfer. This survey investigates the structural source of asymmetric mechanism transfer across four scenarios: multimodal reasoning, multimodal generation, GUI and Web navigation, and embodied manipulation. We first formalize the self-evolving multimodal agent by four criteria and a differentia of self-reference, and adopt the three-stage self-evolving loop of experience generation, feedback acquisition, and update consolidation as our analytical framework to position the scenarios along ceilings on rollout count and feedback fidelity. Comparing self-evolving with fixed loops within each scenario separates the limitations that the scenario imposes from those inherent in the self-evolving design. We then recast each mechanism as a trade-off that raises a scarce factor by presupposing a task-structural resource, such as output equivalence or checkable outcomes, and judge it portable, conditional, blocked, or redundant across scenarios. We attribute the asymmetry to divergent combinations of scarce factors and resources across opposite transfer directions, and identify the matched-budget measurements that could overturn these still untested verdicts.

</details>


### [199] [Topological Dynamics Under Non-Stationary Supervision: Persistent Homology of Neural Network Weight Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13701)
*Mitra Varun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文追踪MLP权重点云的持续同调在稳定标签与随机（非平稳）监督下的演化，发现正确标签模型的归一化fc3 H1持续度在描述性上更高，但校正后未达显著，拓扑特征对条件分类无增量预测价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索监督信号结构（标签正确性、标签稳定性）是否在神经网络权重的高维点云拓扑特征中留下可测量的痕迹，并检验拓扑特征能否预测训练条件。

Method: 同一多层感知机在Fashion-MNIST上用三种监督设置训练：正确标签、固定随机置换标签、每轮重新置换标签；5个配对种子共享初始化与minibatch顺序，共15个模型、各100轮。对原始与行归一化的输入权重点云，在22个检查点计算H0/H1 Vietoris-Rips持续同调，并做条件分类与精确检验（Holm校正）。

Result: 正确标签模型平均真实标签测试准确率89.39%；固定随机标签模型拟合67.94%的指派目标但未泛化到原任务。正确标签模型的时均归一化fc3 H1持续度在描述性上高于两种随机标签条件，但Holm校正后精确比较不显著。早期条件分类平衡准确率：拓扑60.0%、常规诊断66.7%、二者结合60.0%。

Conclusion: 在此设置下拓扑特征未能带来增量预测收益；结论受限于5个种子、单一架构与数据集、282个上限截断的H1距离比较以及未完成的瓶颈分析。

Abstract: We investigate how the persistent topology of neuron- weight point clouds evolves under stable and non-stationary supervision. An identical multilayer perceptron is trained on Fashion-MNIST with correct labels, a fixed random permutation of the training-label vector, or a fresh permutation every epoch. Five paired seeds share initialisation and minibatch order, yielding 15 models trained for 100 epochs. Raw and row-normalised incoming weight clouds are analysed with H0 and H1 Vietoris Rips persistence at 22 checkpoints. Correct-label models reach 89.39% mean true-label test accuracy; fixed-random models fit 67.94% of their assigned training targets without generalising to the original task. Correct-label models have descriptively greater time-averaged normalised fc3 H1 persistence than either random-label condition, but the configured exact comparisons do not reach conventional significance after Holm correction. Early condition classification achieves 60.0% mean balanced accuracy using topology, 66.7% using conventional diagnostics, and 60.0% using both. Thus, no incremental predictive improvement is demonstrated in this setting. The archive contains all 3,300 configured topology cases. Interpretation is limited by five seeds, a single architecture and dataset, 282 capped H1 distance comparisons, and deferred bottleneck analysis.

</details>


### [200] [Megrez: A Knowledge-Guided Agent for Formal Mathematics over MK Set Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13613)
*Tiancheng Xu,Wensheng Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Megrez 是一个面向 Rocq（基于 Morse–Kelley 集合论）数学库的知识引导形式化智能体，通过知识图谱、反馈驱动短程证明循环与三检查验收流水线，实现教材章节级长程形式化的可扩展落地。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用编码智能体在孤立竞赛题上表现优异，但在低资源数学库内进行累积式教材形式化仍面临三大障碍：(1) 教材概念与库中声明的对齐；(2) 长程搜索与实时证明状态的同步；(3) 局部验证的证明可靠整合进不断增长的代码库。

Method: 围绕通用编码智能体协调三项机制：(1) 可源重建的知识图谱，融合形式声明、编译器引用与人工整理元数据，支持中文别名、概念簇、证明依赖与多通道全文本排序检索；(2) 反馈驱动的 1–5 步策略短突发证明循环，配合目标局部支路账本与指数间隔结构化反思；(3) 三检查验收流水线（陈述保真、公理审计、完整构建），结合多语料 Provider 栈与候选绑定的可恢复批处理状态机。

Result: 在点集拓扑语料上完成部署与评测，展现出有效的跨章节形式化能力与可扩展的前提检索效果。

Conclusion: 知识引导 + 短程反馈证明 + 严格验收的组合，可支撑低资源数学库中教科书级累积形式化的工程化推进。

Abstract: General-purpose coding agents equipped with proof-assistant tools have shown strong performance on standalone competition problems, yet cumulative textbook formalization inside a low-resource mathematical library still faces three obstacles: aligning textbook concepts with library declarations, keeping long-horizon search synchronized with live proof states, and integrating locally verified proofs reliably into a growing codebase. We present Megrez, a knowledge-guided formalization agent for Rocq libraries built on Morse--Kelley (MK) set theory. Megrez coordinates a general coding agent through three mechanisms: (1) a source-rebuildable knowledge graph merging formal declarations, compiler references, and curated metadata to support retrieval via Chinese aliases, concept clusters, proof dependencies, and multi-lane full-text ranking; (2) a feedback-driven 1--5 tactic short-burst proving loop paired with a goal-local branch ledger and exponential-interval structured reflection; and (3) a three-check acceptance pipeline (statement fidelity, axiom auditing, and full builds) coupled with a multi-corpus Provider stack and a candidate-bound recoverable batch state machine. Deployment and evaluation on a point-set topology corpus demonstrate effective cross-chapter formalization and scalable premise retrieval.

</details>


### [201] [ExpertCrowd-SWE: Measuring Frontier Coding Agents on Expert-Authored Engineering Tasks in Unfamiliar Codebases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13246)
*Josip Vrdoljak,Akash Singh,Bas Steunebrink,Alex Yarotsky,John Lorenzo,Achille Nicoletti,Sam Pigott*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 EC-SWE 基准：100 个由专家撰写的多文件软件工程任务，用于在大型陌生代码库中评测自主编码智能体；任务附带密封参考解与加权可执行评分器，经校准与难度筛选后评测七个前沿模型（3,495 次运行），顶尖模型得分约 63–67%，无任务被全部模型完全解决。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编码智能体评测缺乏基于真实仓库、由实践工程师撰写、且评分离散粗糙的基准；需要能反映"维护者是否愿意合并"这一真实标准，并能区分当前前沿模型能力的困难任务集。

Method: 构建基准：每个任务为针对真实仓库固定 commit 的变更请求，配密封参考解与任务专属评分器（加权、多为可执行判据、给部分分，并设 must-pass 阻断项与通过/不通过门禁）；任务覆盖 87 个开源项目、11 种语言，工单中位 148 词而参考解中位改动 471 行。每个任务镜像发布前经校准：参考解得 1.0，未改动仓库与惰性改动得 0；近半数任务附带替代、惰性与对抗性控制补丁以检验评分器判别力。最终 100 个任务按对当前前沿模型的难度筛选。评测七个前沿模型，各五次运行（共 3,495 次计分运行），统一平台框架下使用各模型原生工具接口。

Result: Claude Opus 5.5（67.3%）与 GPT-6 Astra（63.7%）领先且差距无统计显著性；随后为 Claude Fable 5.1（46.5%）、Gemini 3.8 Flash（40.0%），以及统计上不可区分的 Qwen 3.8 Max、DeepSeek V4.1 Flash、Grok 4.6（32–34%）。无任务被所有模型完全解决，80 个任务上无模型五次运行均获满分；得分跨度达 35 个百分点。智能体每次运行中位步数 33–166，与得分关系不大。

Conclusion: EC-SWE 提供了区分度高、抗污染的真实仓库编码智能体基准，显示当前前沿模型在专家级多文件变更上仍有显著差距；公开 24 个任务及其环境、评分器与结果，保留 76 个任务以限制数据污染。

Abstract: ExpertCrowd-SWE (EC-SWE) is a benchmark of 100 expert-authored, multi-file software engineering tasks for evaluating autonomous coding agents in large, unfamiliar codebases. Each task is a change request written by a practising engineer against a pinned commit of a real repository, paired with a sealed reference solution and a task-specific grader whose weighted, mostly executable criteria award partial credit; must-pass blockers and pass/fail gates encode what a maintainer would refuse to merge. The tasks span 87 open-source projects and 11 languages. The median ticket is 148 words, while the median reference solution changes 471 lines. Every task image is calibrated before release so that the reference solution scores 1.0 and both the untouched repository and an inert change score 0; nearly half of the tasks also ship alternative, lazy and adversarial control patches that the grader must classify correctly. The final 100 tasks were selected for difficulty against current frontier models. We evaluate seven frontier models, five runs each (3,495 scored runs), in a single platform harness that gives each model its provider-native tool interface. Claude Opus 5.5 (67.3%) and GPT-6 Astra (63.7%) lead without a statistically significant gap, followed by Claude Fable 5.1 (46.5%), Gemini 3.8 Flash (40.0%), and a statistically indistinguishable group of Qwen 3.8 Max, DeepSeek V4.1 Flash and Grok 4.6 (32-34%). No task is fully solved by every model, and on 80 tasks no model earns full credit on all five runs. Scores span 35 points. Depending on the model, agents take a median of 33 to 166 steps per run, with little relation to score. We release 24 tasks with their environments, graders and results, and hold out 76 to limit contamination.

</details>


### [202] [Counting Under Disagreement: Dollar-Weighted Measurement, Selective Machine Labelling and Budgeted Human Review from Disagreeing AI Readers of Administrative Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13215)
*George Koomullil*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文研究"一个文本标签对应多条行政记录、目标是货币总额、且人类标注彼此不一致"情形下的AI辅助测量，给出差分估计量的精确条件，并通过模拟与重放评估两类误差控制规则及两阶段复核策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将语言模型用于行政文本结构化测量时，需以人工判读为锚，但读者间不一致会动摇估计；现有做法缺少在货币总额目标与多样本框下的严格推断条件，也缺乏对"由学习预测决定人工核验对象"这一策略的收益与边界的量化。

Method: 以美国税务申报中121,070条资助用途文本（799,675条记录，1264亿美元）为测试台，用规则分类器与三个语言模型打标，并对651条文本由三名读者在同一编码手册下独立判读。在Poisson抽样下给出固定预测量与"整框预先分折"的交叉拟合预测量之差分估计量的精确条件，并用模拟评估固定样本量程序；评估两类误差控制规则（美元度量下的加权保形p值、基于设计误差估计的Learn-then-Test），在嵌套留出折与含合成真值的重抽样上检验；并重放两阶段复核策略（校准器在第一阶段自拟合）与纯Neyman分配。

Result: 8个预测量自身总额跨112–150亿美元，校正后在40亿美元内一致；两类规则在部署中的误差控制未获确立，基于计数的规则在按美元抽取的样本下不接受任何文本；两阶段复核策略在100条文本预算下将总额RMSE降低28%，而纯Neyman分配在预测量未校准时失效；读者间独立判读使估计总额变动39.3亿美元，为读者内抽样区间的2.2倍，机构层区间在各样本量下均覆盖不足。

Conclusion: 结果同时揭示了用学习预测决定人工核验对象的收益与局限：差分估计与两阶段设计可显著提升货币总额的精度，但误差控制规则的可部署性、分配策略对校准的依赖以及读者不一致导致的区间覆盖不足仍是关键约束；所有主结果均在每位读者下重复验证。

Abstract: We study AI-assisted measurement when one text label applies to many administrative records, the target is a monetary total, and the human readings that anchor the machines disagree. The testbed is 121,070 grant-purpose texts from US tax filings (799,675 records, \$126.4B), labelled by a rule-based classifier and three language models, with a designed sample of 651 texts read independently by three people under one codebook. We state exact conditions for a fixed-predictor difference estimator and for a cross-fitted predictor whose folds are assigned to the whole frame before sampling, under Poisson sampling, and assess the implemented fixed-size procedures by simulation: 8 predictors whose own totals span \$11.2-15.0B agree to within \$0.4B once rectified. Two rules that accept machine labels at a stated dollar-weighted error, weighted conformal p-values under a dollar measure and Learn-then-Test on the design estimate of the error, are evaluated on nested held-out folds and on re-draws of the design with synthetic truths; their error control in deployment is not established, and the count-based rule accepts no text under a dollar-drawn sample. In a replay that charges every human label, a two-phase review policy whose calibrator is fitted on its own first phase reduces the RMSE of the total by 28% at a budget of 100 texts relative to dollar-proportional sampling, while pure Neyman allocation fails with an uncalibrated predictor. Independent readers shift the estimated total by \$3.93B, 2.2 times the within-reader sampling interval, and institution-level intervals under-cover at every size. Every headline result is re-run under each reader. The results identify both the benefits and the limits of using learned predictions to decide what a person should verify.

</details>


### [203] [From Process Models to Composable Agentic Workflows: Combining BPMN, Agents and Enterprise Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13196)
*Christian Fillies,Jan Fillies,Frauke Weichhardt,Gay Anne Wood-Albrecht*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出基于BPMN的Agentic Workflow方法，将SemTalk BPMN模型编译为可执行工作流（LangGraph/Pregel运行时），以显式编排+局部LLM推理构建可复用、可监控的企业级多智能体流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体具备推理、工具调用与协作能力，但复杂智能体应用的决策分散在提示词、工具调用与应用逻辑中，导致工作流隐式、难以理解、监控与改进；同时企业智能体缺乏组织特有的上下文知识。

Method: 1) 将SemTalk BPMN模型转换为基于LangGraph/Pregel运行时的可执行工作流；2) Agentic Task在特定活动处引入LLM推理，而流程控制、确定性决策、系统交互与人工审批仍由常规BPMN机制承担；3) Agentic Participant提供智能体行为或其他可执行流程的可复用接口；4) 递归能力组合（Recursive Capability Composition），把智能体与可执行业务流程封装为其他智能体可调用的Agentic Tools；5) 利用流程模型与知识图谱/本体描述活动、参与者、信息对象、决策及流程间关系，为智能体提供组织特定知识。

Result: 给出三项贡献：(1) 模型驱动执行：BPMN模型到可执行Agentic Workflow的转换；(2) 递归能力组合：将智能体与业务流程暴露为可复用工具；(3) 企业上下文：以流程模型与本体支撑组织特定推理。显式流程模型为开发者与运维者提供工作流结构与执行进度的共享视图，便于调试、监控与优化。

Conclusion: BPMN编排使多智能体系统更易理解与治理：以显式编排约束自主推理、以递归组合实现能力复用、以流程模型/本体注入企业知识，是构建可解释、可运维企业级智能体应用的可行路径。摘要未给出定量评估，尚缺实证性能与可用性验证。

Abstract: LLM-based agents can reason, use tools and collaborate. However, coordinating increasingly complex agentic applications becomes difficult to understand, monitor and improve. Decisions distributed across prompts, tool calls and application logic make workflows implicit. We introduce **Recursive Capability Composition**, enabling agents to execute business processes and expose them as reusable Agentic Tools.
  This paper presents a BPMN-based approach to Agentic Workflows, combining explicit orchestration with autonomous reasoning. SemTalk BPMN models are transformed into executable workflows using a LangGraph/Pregel runtime. Agentic Tasks introduce LLM reasoning at selected activities, while conventional BPMN controls process flows, deterministic decisions, system interactions and human approvals. Agentic Participants provide reusable interfaces for agent behavior or other executable processes.
  BPMN orchestration makes multi-agent systems more understandable and manageable. Explicit process models provide developers and operators with a shared view of workflow structures and execution progress, facilitating debugging, monitoring and optimization.
  Enterprise agents also need organization-specific context. Process Models and Knowledge Graphs describe activities, participants, information objects, decisions and relationships between processes. They provide structured knowledge of how an organization operates, supporting informed agent decisions and actions.
  Our contributions are threefold: 1. **Model-Driven Execution:** Transforming BPMN models into executable Agentic Workflows. 2. **Recursive Capability Composition:** Exposing agents and executable business processes as reusable tools for other agents. 3. **Enterprise Context:** Using Process Models and Ontologies to provide organization-specific knowledge for agent reasoning.

</details>


### [204] [Teaching the Harness: Student-Aware Skill Transfer for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13144)
*Chang Liu,Bo Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 Student-Aware Teaching（SAT）：教师 LLM 为受固定工具、上下文与动作预算约束的学生 LLM 建立学习者模型，经诊断探针验证假设后生成可复用技能，技能须通过开发任务筛选才入库。Claude-Sonnet-5 教 DeepSeek-V4-Flash 时四个主实验均达 86.46% 成功率，优于同框架无画像教师（81.04%）与未受教学生（75.00%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有效教学不仅要理解任务，还要推理学习者本身，即心智理论意义上的"由行为推断他者隐状态"。作者据此提出核心问题：更强的 LLM 若运用该原则，能否把固定的学生教得更好。学生的工具、上下文与动作预算由 harness 固定，因此对某一模型有效的流程可能耗尽另一模型的预算，教师需要按学生特性调整教学。

Method: SAT 分三步：教师构建学习者模型，将经验画像与关于学生局限的假设配对；用诊断探针检验这些假设；针对被诊断出的失败编写可复用技能。技能只有在匹配的开发任务上提升成功率后才被纳入技能库。实验对比了完整 SAT、同一框架但去掉分析性学习者模型的无画像教师、未受教学生，并做了误导性画像、跨模型技能库互换、教师退出后的效果保持等消融。

Result: Claude-Sonnet-5 教 DeepSeek-V4-Flash，四个主实验均达 86.46% 任务成功率，每一轮都优于无画像教师（81.04%）与未受教学生（75.00%）。去掉分析性学习者模型会同时降低预测准确率与成功率；在误导性学习者画像下，48 个候选技能无一通过准入。DeepSeek-V4-Flash 与 Gemini-3.8-Flash 互换技能库时，各自使用自有库表现更好。教师退出后，已保存的技能库仍保持最高 11.1 个百分点的平均增益。

Conclusion: 收益成立需两个条件：学生具备遵循指令的能力，且存在能把指令送达的部署路径。技能库具有可迁移性与一定的持久性，但并非通用——互换后不如自有库，说明技能带有学生特异性；误导性画像会使技能准入完全失效，凸显学习者模型准确性的关键作用。

Abstract: Effective teaching requires reasoning about the learner as well as the task, a capacity that Theory of Mind describes as inferring another agent's latent state from its behavior. We ask whether a stronger LLM teaches a fixed student better when it applies this principle. The student is an LLM agent acting through a harness that fixes its tools, context, and action budget, so a procedure that helps one model can exhaust the budget of another. We introduce Student-Aware Teaching (SAT). The teacher builds a learner model that pairs an empirical profile with hypotheses about the student's limitations, tests these hypotheses with diagnostic probes, and writes reusable skills for the diagnosed failures. A skill enters the library only after it raises success on matched development tasks. With Claude-Sonnet-5 teaching DeepSeek-V4-Flash, SAT reaches 86.46% task success across four primary runs, leading both a profile-free teacher with the same framework (81.04%) and the untaught student (75.00%) in each run. Removing the analytical learner model lowers both prediction and success, and under misleading learner profiles none of the 48 candidate skills passes admission. When DeepSeek-V4-Flash and Gemini-3.8-Flash exchange libraries, each performs better with its own. Saved libraries keep average gains of up to 11.1 points after the teacher leaves. The benefit depends on a student that can follow instructions and a deployment route that delivers them.
